### [1] [SMARtCARE: Privacy-Preserving Agentic AI Systems for Bounded-Autonomy Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31763)
*Srini Ramaswamy,Deveeshree Nayak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SMARtCARE 提出一种四状态临床决策支持架构（Stable、Meta-cognitive、Assisted、Regulated/Revoked），用有损六通道患者历史轨迹指纹来触发元认知升级，避免长上下文临床 AI 遗漏既往住院史；在合成蒙特卡洛与 MIMIC-III/IV 演示数据上验证了状态转换逻辑、可追溯性与隐私保护，但并非临床有效性主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文临床 AI 系统在既往住院记录落在有效推理上下文之外时，可能遗漏相关病史；在 ICU 监测中，这会使早期生命体征漂移看似非特异，即便其与既往恶化模式相似，从而带来中段上下文风险。

Method: SMARtCARE 采用四状态架构：Stable、Meta-cognitive、Assisted、Regulated（Revoked）。系统不自动检索既往记录，而是使用有损的六通道患者既往轨迹指纹；当当前漂移匹配该指纹且既往记录不在上下文中时，触发 Meta-cognitive 升级供临床医生审查，只有临床医生在 Assisted 状态下才进行完整检索。同时设置患者身份守卫，在数据加载、日志与审计层强制正确归因。

Result: 评估包含合成蒙特卡洛研究（验证状态转换逻辑与估计器稳定性，而非临床性能）以及 MIMIC-III 与 MIMIC-IV Clinical Database Demos 的真实数据运行。MIMIC-III 中，14 名两次住院患者中识别出 1 例既往模式复发；MIMIC-IV 中，同一流程在 9 名两次住院患者中未产生指纹匹配，体现了固定规范模式库的关键局限。两次运行中所有记录决策均完全可追溯且归因正确。

Conclusion: 结果支持 SMARtCARE 作为一种可追溯、隐私感知的机制来呈现中段上下文风险，但并非临床有效性声明。

Abstract: Long-context clinical AI systems can miss relevant patient history when prior admissions fall outside the active reasoning context. In ICU monitoring, this can cause early vital-sign drift to appear nonspecific even when it resembles a prior deterioration pattern. SMARtCARE addresses this gap through a four-state clinical decision-support architecture: Stable, Meta-cognitive, Assisted, and Regulated (Revoked). Rather than automatically retrieving prior records, SMARtCARE uses a lossy six-channel fingerprint of the patient's prior trajectory. When current drift matches that fingerprint and the prior record is absent from context, the system raises a Meta-cognitive escalation for clinician review; full retrieval occurs only through clinician action in the Assisted state. A patient-identity guard is designed to enforce correct attribution across data loading, logging, and audit layers. Evaluation combines a synthetic Monte Carlo study that validates the state-transition logic and estimator stability, not clinical performance, with real-data runs on both the MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV Clinical Database Demos. On MIMIC-III, one prior-pattern recurrence was identified among 14 two-admission patients; on MIMIC-IV, the same pipeline produced no fingerprint matches among 9 two-admission patients, which illustrates a key limitation of a fixed canonical pattern library. Across both runs all logged decisions were fully traceable and correctly attributed. The results support SMARtCARE as a traceable, privacy-aware mechanism for surfacing middle-context risk; they are not a clinical efficacy claim.

</details>


### [2] [Witeness Overlap: Directional Provenance Inside Open-Weight Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31784)
*Siyuan Li,Haoxuan Zeng,Xin Luo,Fernando Jia,Florence Li,Zhengyang Geng,Zico Kolter,Tai Sing Lee,Tianqin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Witness Overlap，一种无需提示、无需训练的白盒测试方法，用于判断模型检查点之间的方向性溯源关系（谁先谁后）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重模型被频繁微调、对齐、合并和再发布，溯源审计不仅需要判断检查点是否相关，还需确定先后顺序。现有方法多针对"已知基座"场景，其证据本质是对称的（表征相似度、权重相似度、行为指纹等），只能检测相关性，无法定向A与B之间的父子关系。

Method: 引入局部几何比较：不再直接比较两个检查点，而是引入同家族的第三个检查点作为"见证者"，比较各候选端点周围的几何结构。通过判断哪个候选更像"分支父节点"来推断方向。基于父锚定与子锚定见证重叠分布的经验不对称性，提出Witness Overlap——一种无提示、无需训练的白盒方向性溯源测试，并给出基于SVD的权重降维变体。

Result: 在来自16个家族的176个LLM检查点上，单见证者测试使用Frobenius余弦定向了95.3%的父子决策。作者进一步评估了根识别、兄弟判别、对VLM和扩散家族的泛化，以及链式排序。信号对权重噪声和稀疏剪枝具有鲁棒性，SVD权重降维变体比Frobenius余弦更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 通过引入第三方见证检查点的局部几何比较，可突破对称相似度方法的局限，实现方向性模型溯源；该方法在多种模型家族和扰动条件下均表现稳健，为检查点先后顺序审计提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Open-weight models are often released, fine-tuned, aligned, merged, and re-released, making provenance audits ask not only whether checkpoints are related, but also which checkpoint came first. Many existing model-provenance methods are designed for a base-known audit setting: given a victim or source model, they test whether a suspect model is related to it. Although these audits are framed as source-to-suspect tests, their underlying evidence is often symmetric, relying on representation similarity, weight similarity, behavioral fingerprints, or correlation statistics. Symmetric pairwise comparisons can detect relatedness, but they cannot by themselves orient relationship between checkpoints A and B. We therefore introduce a local geometric comparison: instead of comparing two checkpoints directly, we add a third same-family checkpoint as a witness and compare the geometry around each candidate endpoint. Direction is inferred by asking which candidate behaves more like a branching parent. Motivated by this idea, and by the empirically observed asymmetry between parent-anchored and child-anchored witness-overlap distributions, we propose Witness Overlap, a prompt-free, training-free white-box test for directional provenance. On 176 LLM checkpoints from 16 families, our one-witness test orients 95.3\% of parent-child decisions using Frobenius cosine. We further evaluate root identification, sibling discrimination, generalizations to VLM and diffusion families, and chain-structured ordering. The signal is robust to weight noise and sparse pruning, with a proposed SVD weight reduction variant showing greater robustness than Frobenius cosine.

</details>


### [3] [CP-Agent: A Harness-Engineered Agent for Crystal Plasticity Simulation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31790)
*Samuel Onimpa Alfred,Abhishek Kumar,Veera Sundararaghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CP-Agent，一个基于LLM的智能体，通过harness engineering自动执行晶体塑性（CP）建模的完整工作流，在四个案例中验证了其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体塑性模拟能预测多晶金属力学行为，但常规使用受限于人工配置异构工具、编排多步数据流程以及标定本构参数的高昂成本，阻碍了系统性参数研究的效率，因此需要自动化工作流。

Method: 基于ReAct范式构建LLM智能体，由最小系统提示、类型化工具定义、调度器和带安全边界的迭代循环组成harness；智能体负责工具选择与排序推理，数值搜索交给已有优化器，领域知识编码在工具schema中而非硬编码逻辑。

Result: 四个案例：标定增材制造316L不锈钢的四个滑移参数；用已发表铜基准验证工作流并复现应力-应变与织构演化；反演铜的初始织构（正确识别为弥散初织构）；复现Mg-Zn-Ca合金多道次轧制织构演化（链式执行五次变形道次，复现实验观测的弱化分裂基面织构）。所有案例中智能体均能从任务描述推断正确执行顺序，重复运行稳健，结果具有物理可解释性。

Conclusion: 本研究确立harness engineering为自动化CP建模工作流的系统方法，通过可见推理轨迹保持物理可解释性与可审计性。

Abstract: Crystal plasticity (CP) simulations predict the mechanical behavior of polycrystalline metals, yet their routine use is hindered by the manual effort of configuring heterogeneous tools, orchestrating multi-step data pipelines, and calibrating constitutive parameters against experiments. These bottlenecks impede productivity in systematic parameter studies, motivating interest in automated workflows. This study presents CP-Agent, a harness-engineered LLM-based agent that autonomously executes complete CP modeling workflows from natural-language tasks. Operating under the ReAct paradigm, the agent reasons about tool selection and sequencing while delegating numerical search to established optimizers. The harness comprises a minimal system prompt, typed tool definitions, a dispatcher, and a safety-bounded iteration loop, encoding domain knowledge through tool schemas rather than hard-coded logic. CP-Agent is demonstrated on four case studies: calibrating four slip parameters of additively manufactured stainless steel 316L against tensile data; validating the workflow against published copper benchmarks, reproducing stress-strain and texture evolution; recovering the initial crystallographic texture of copper, where the agent correctly identifies a diffuse initial texture; and reproducing the multi-pass rolling texture evolution of a Mg-Zn-Ca alloy, where the agent chains five deformation passes and recovers the experimentally observed weakened, split basal texture. In all cases, the agent inferred the correct execution sequence from the task statement, robustly across repeated runs, and delivered physically interpretable results. This work establishes harness engineering as a systematic approach to automating CP modeling workflows while maintaining physical interpretability and auditability through visible reasoning traces.

</details>


### [4] [ConflictVLA-Bench: Benchmarking Behavioral Responses of Vision-Language-Action Models to Premise Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31792)
*Liyu Hou,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ConflictVLA-Bench基准，揭示VLA模型面对无效任务前提时普遍存在"失败坚持"（Failed Persistence）现象，即任务失败但行为并未脱离，说明仅凭结果无法评估VLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对前提冲突的评估多聚焦终端任务结果，但任务失败本身无法区分行为性脱离与继续追求目标后出错，因此需要从执行过程层面研究VLA对无效前提的响应。

Method: 构建ConflictVLA-Bench，将冲突rollout与前提一致的参考rollout配对，同时评估结果与执行过程。基于LIBERO，包含2,826个提示条件化冲突任务，涵盖4种冲突家族、4种结构配置和2种提示条件，并在8个VLA模型上进行评测。

Result: 所有8个VLA在无效前提下原目标完成率下降至少17.3个百分点，OpenVLA下降达56.2个百分点；即便模型在一致任务成功、配对冲突任务失败，仍常继续接近原目标、保留早期轨迹结构且动作幅度抑制有限。显式前提检查未能产生一致且协调的行为变化。

Conclusion: 终端的失败既不能证明行为性脱离，也不能证明拒绝；仅凭结果不足以评估VLA模型。失败坚持现象在受评模型中反复出现。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models perform strongly on manipulation tasks, their responses to invalid task premises remain underexplored. Existing evaluations of premise conflicts often focus on terminal task outcomes, yet task failure alone cannot distinguish behavioral disengagement from continued pursuit followed by an execution error. We call the latter pattern Failed Persistence. To study this phenomenon, we introduce ConflictVLA-Bench, which pairs conflict rollouts with premise-consistent reference rollouts and evaluates both outcomes and execution processes. Built on LIBERO, the benchmark contains 2,826 prompt-conditioned conflict tasks spanning four conflict families, four structural configurations, and two prompt conditions. Across all eight VLAs, invalid premises reduce original goal completion by at least 17.3 percentage points, with the reduction reaching 56.2 percentage points for OpenVLA. Crucially, even when models succeed on premise-consistent tasks and fail on their matched conflict tasks, they often continue to approach the original targets, retain early trajectory structure, and show limited action magnitude suppression. Failed Persistence therefore recurs across the evaluated models. Explicit premise checking does not consistently produce selective and coordinated behavioral changes. These findings show that terminal failure alone establishes neither behavioral disengagement nor refusal and that outcomes alone are insufficient for VLA evaluation. Experimental data and additional details are available on the project page: https://github.com/EmbodiedAISurvey/ConflictVLA-Bench

</details>


### [5] [Working with AI: A Design Framework for Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31793)
*Yuqian Lu,Regina Lee,Rui Zhou,Lixin Jiang,Andrew McDaid,Amy Lawrence*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一份白皮书，提出一个用于设计人机协作的实用框架，涵盖人、AI系统、任务、组织和更广泛社会环境五个维度，并通过协作装配系统中协作机器人的用例展示框架的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI（尤其是生成式AI）已成为现代工作的重要组成部分，在工业场景中可支持决策、自动化常规活动、辅助人类并提升生产力。然而，成功的AI采纳不仅取决于技术能力，还取决于人们如何体验和使用它，因此需要回答"如何设计人机协作才能同时有利于人和组织"这一问题。

Method: 提出一个面向人机协作设计的实用框架，综合考虑人（human）、AI系统、任务、组织和更广泛的社会环境五个要素；阐明有效协作的表现形式、影响条件、需满足的要求以及组织应考虑的设计决策；并引入一个人机协作装配系统（含协作机器人）用例，说明如何将设计需求转化为具体协作特征并进行案例研究评估。

Result: 框架及其配套用例展示了设计需求可被转化为具体的协作功能，并通过案例研究加以评估，验证了框架在实践中的可操作性。

Conclusion: 白皮书旨在为设计有效、以人为本且负责任的人机协作提供清晰、实用的指南。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI), particularly GenAI, is becoming an increasingly important part of modern work. In industrial settings, AI can support decision-making, automate routine activities, assist humans, and improve productivity. However, successful AI adoption depends on more than what the technology can do. It also depends on how people experience and work with it. This raises an important question: how should human-AI collaboration be designed so that it works well for both people and organisations? This white paper addresses that question by presenting a practical framework for designing human-AI collaboration. The framework considers the human, the AI system, the task, the organisation, and the wider societal environment. It explains what effective collaboration looks like, what conditions influence it, what requirements should be met, and what design decisions organisations should consider. The report also includes a human-AI collaborative assembly system with cobot use case to demonstrate how the framework can be applied in practice. The use case shows how design requirements can be translated into specific collaboration features and evaluated through a case study. The aim of this white paper is to provide a clear and practical guide for designing human-AI collaboration that is effective, human-centred, and responsible.

</details>


### [6] [DriveHierarchy: A Benchmark for Diagnosing VLM Driving Capabilities from Open-Loop Understanding to Closed-Loop Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31814)
*Chengkai Xu,Jiaqi Liu,Yicheng Guo,Peng Hang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出分层基准 DriveHierarchy，将 VLM 自动驾驶能力分为感知定位、上下文记忆、心智推理与闭环执行四个层级，融合 76,798 组问答对的开环数据与 100 个闭环仿真场景，评测 15 个 VLM，验证能力间结构化且非冗余的关联，并为诊断与优化提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶能力具有复合性，要求系统能定位交通参与者与风险、跨视角跨时间整合上下文、推理未来演化，并在闭环交互中正确行动。现有基准多只评估开环理解或闭环驾驶，缺乏结构化框架来解释这些能力如何组织、如何相互关联，以及如何指导模型诊断与改进。

Method: 构建分层基准 DriveHierarchy，把 VLM 自动驾驶划分为四个层级：感知定位、上下文记忆、心智推理、闭环执行。整合多个开源自动驾驶数据集，形成统一开环基准，含 84,279 帧上的 76,798 组问答对；并基于真实路网搭建闭环仿真平台，支持交互式场景构建，筛选出 100 个具身评测驾驶场景。

Result: 在 15 个 VLM 上的实验表明：DriveHierarchy 能捕捉结构化但非冗余的能力差异，建立开环理解与闭环驾驶之间的联系，并为模型诊断与基准引导的优化提供实用基础。

Conclusion: DriveHierarchy 可作为统一框架，用于评估与改进基于 VLM 的自动驾驶系统，项目已在 GitHub 匿名发布。

Abstract: Evaluating VLM-based autonomous driving remains difficult because driving competence is composite, where a capable system must ground traffic participants and hazards, integrate context across views and time, reason about future evolution, and act appropriately under closed-loop interaction. Existing benchmarks usually assess either open-loop understanding or closed-loop driving but provide limited structure for explaining how these abilities are organized, how they relate, and how they may inform model diagnosis and improvement. We present \textsc{DriveHierarchy}, a hierarchical benchmark that organizes VLM-based autonomous driving into four ranks, spanning perceptual grounding, contextual memory, mental reasoning, and closed-loop execution. To instantiate this hierarchy, we integrate multiple open-source autonomous-driving datasets into a unified open-loop benchmark with 76,798 question-answer pairs over 84,279 frames and develop a closed-loop simulation platform with interactive scenario construction on a real-world road network, from which 100 driving scenarios are curated for embodied evaluation. Experiments on 15 VLMs show that \textsc{DriveHierarchy} captures structured but non-redundant capability variation, relates open-loop understanding to closed-loop driving, and provides a practical basis for diagnosis and benchmark-guided optimization. \textsc{DriveHierarchy} therefore serves as a unified framework for evaluating and improving VLM-based autonomous driving systems. An anonymized project has been released on https://github.com/PerfectXu88/DriveHierarchy

</details>


### [7] [LLM Judge Validation Under Sparse Overlap: From Inference to Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31857)
*Junxuan Li,Arko Mukherjee,Soumyabrata Pal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明标注重叠稀疏性是LLM评判者验证中错误部署决策的一阶决定因素，并提出重叠"数量"与"分配"两个可操作杠杆：ρ≥0.25的最小重叠公式与零成本分层抽样方案，在10个LLM评判者、4个评估矩阵上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用LLM作为评判者（LLM-as-a-judge）时，需估计其与人类的一致性来验证可靠性，但标注预算通常不允许对每个样本进行多重标注，导致重叠稀疏。作者旨在揭示这种稀疏性对部署决策错误的决定性影响，并寻找可行的缓解手段。

Method: 将重叠稀疏性形式化为部署决策错误的一阶决定因素；从理论上推导最小重叠公式（数量维度），并提出零成本的分层抽样分配方案，与随机抽样进行对比。在视觉评估、因果推理和摘要等四类评估矩阵、10个LLM评判者上开展验证。

Result: 在5%成对重叠下，错误决策率高达25%，在十个候选评判者中选出错误最优评判者的概率为65%。理论推导显示，当ρ≥0.25时足以应对非边界情形，而边界情形本质上仍难以解决。分层方案在分层信息有效时将假拒绝率相对随机抽样减半。

Conclusion: 重叠稀疏性是LLM评判者验证中错误决策的首要来源，提升重叠数量与优化重叠分配是两个切实可行的改进方向；最小重叠阈值与分层抽样可显著降低错误率，但边界情形仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Validating an LLM-as-a-judge requires estimating its agreement with humans, yet annotation budgets rarely allow every item to be multiply labeled. We prove that this \emph{overlap sparsity} is the first-order determinant of wrong deployment decisions: at 5\% pairwise overlap, wrong-decision rates reach 25\% and the probability of selecting the wrong best judge among ten candidates is 65\%. The two actionable levers are overlap \emph{quantity} and \emph{allocation}. For quantity, we derive a minimum-overlap formula showing $ρ\geq 0.25$ suffices for non-borderline judges while borderline cases remain fundamentally hard. For allocation, a zero-cost stratified scheme halves false-rejection rates relative to random sampling when strata are informative. We validate on 10 LLM judges across four evaluation matrices spanning visual assessment, causal reasoning, and summarization.

</details>


### [8] [Metro-WM: Long-Horizon Latent Planning with Realisable Sub-Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31868)
*Royson Lee,Fady Rezk,Titouan Parcollet,Timothy Hospedales,Cristina Cornelio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Metro-WM，一种基于真实经验状态检索的层次化目标达成框架，解决潜空间子目标不可物理实现的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 JEPA 的模型预测控制是强大的零样本目标达成规划器，但仅在短规划时域内有效。现有层次化方法通过宏规划器预测潜空间中间子目标来引导微观规划器，然而本文发现不受约束的潜子目标预测存在根本缺陷：最先进的宏规划器经常输出物理上不可实现的子目标。

Method: Metro-WM 从先前经验中检索真实状态作为子目标，而非生成无根基的潜向量。它构建一个图，顶点是离线专家演示或随机动作轨迹中观测到的帧，使不同回合的帧相互连接并拼接成通往目标的路径。在整个图上规划使其对执行误差高度鲁棒：当微观规划器偏离轨道时，能立即从当前状态找到新的最优路径。

Result: 相比次优层次化方法，Metro-WM 的长时域成功率高出最多 37.33 个百分点，速度快最多 10.9 倍，所需离线计算少 13-56 倍，且需调优的超参数更少。额外分析表明其能找到比离线演示更短的路径，优于依赖查询自身演示的 oracle，并在极端稀疏数据集条件下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 用真实经验状态检索替代无约束潜子目标生成，是构建高效、鲁棒长时域层次化规划器的有效方案。

Abstract: Model-predictive control with Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) provides a strong zero-shot goal-reaching planner, but it is only effective over short planning horizons. Hierarchical extensions attempt to bridge this gap by learning a macro planner to predict intermediate latent sub-goals to guide the micro planner. In this work, we demonstrate that unconstrained latent sub-goal prediction is fundamentally flawed. A rigorous evaluation reveals that a leading state-of-the-art macro planner routinely emits physically unrealisable sub-goals. To resolve this, we introduce Metro-WM, a hierarchical framework that issues sub-goals by retrieving genuine states from prior experience rather than generating ungrounded latent vectors. Specifically, Metro-WM constructs a graph whose vertices are observed frames from offline expert demonstrations or random-action trajectories, allowing frames from different episodes to be connected and stitched into routes to the goal. Planning over the full graph also makes the system highly robust to execution errors: if the micro planner drifts off course, Metro-WM instantly finds a new optimal path from the current state. Our experiments show that Metro-WM achieves superior long-horizon success rates of up to 37.33 percentage points over the next best hierarchical approach while being up to 10.9 times faster, requiring both 13-56 times less offline compute and fewer tuned hyperparameters. Additional analysis reveals that Metro-WM finds shorter paths than the offline demonstrations, outperforms an oracle relying on the query's own demonstration, and maintains robust performance under extremely sparse dataset conditions.

</details>


### [9] [IndustryLLM: Failure-Driven LLM Training for Industrial Procurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31871)
*Liang Ding,Zhiang Xu,Yuyang Sheng,Bin Chen,Songlin Bai,Run Zhu,Dingjun Wu,Hui Xu,Yandi Wang,Fulin Shi,Leilei Gan,Linlin Yu,Qihuang Zhong,Keqin Peng,Yalong Li,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 IndustryLLM，一个面向工业采购场景的开源权重大语言模型，通过失败驱动的持续预训练与监督微调配方，结合三值逻辑证据门控接口，在离线与在线实验中均取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业采购场景要求语言模型在严格安全容差下，弥合非正式买家行话、稀疏的商场属性与权威工程标准之间的鸿沟。通用文本规模化训练难以满足该领域对术语规范化、事实准确性与安全性的特殊需求，因此需要领域定制化的适应方案。

Method: 基于 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B-Base（总参数35B，每token约激活3B，视觉编码器冻结）训练开源工业模型。采用失败驱动的 CPT + SFT 配方：CPT 使用约100B token 语料（5B 国标 GB/T 等技术档案、10B 去标识真实工业交易与询价记录、60B 通用回放）；并通过多语域改写（10种体裁、8种风格）、置信度路由的最小事实编辑、面向错误的 QA 合成，重建约20B token 领域子集，解决语域错配与事实脆弱性问题（如"42-luo-mu"→42CrMo、"16674"→GB/T 16674、冲突尺寸规格澄清）。下游部署提出证据门控约束评估接口，强制三值逻辑：未验证的产品证据保持"未知"而非"满足"。

Result: 离线评估中采购查询结构化取得一致提升（No-Think 模式下精确匹配 +2.97 个百分点，95% CI [2.11, 3.86]）；生产环境随机在线 A/B 实验显示 GMV +4.25%、满意询价 +8.3%，延迟从 6-7 秒降至 1.5 秒。模型权重与配置已开源于 Hugging Face。

Conclusion: 通过领域定制化的失败驱动适应配方与证据门控三值逻辑接口，IndustryLLM 有效弥合了工业采购中非规范表达与权威标准之间的差距，在结构化准确率、商业指标与响应延迟上均显著优于基线，为工业部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Industrial procurement requires language models to bridge informal buyer jargon, sparse marketplace attributes, and authoritative engineering standards under strict safety tolerances. We present IndustryLLM, an open-weight industrial language model trained from Qwen3.5-35B-A3B-Base (35B total parameters with ~3B activated per token, with the vision encoder frozen). Rather than relying on generic text scaling, we introduce a failure-driven adaptation recipe spanning continued pre-training (CPT) and supervised fine-tuning (SFT). CPT leverages a curated ~100B-token corpus integrating 5B tokens of national standards (e.g., GB/T) and technical archives, 10B tokens of de-identified real-world industrial transaction and inquiry records, and 60B tokens of general replay. To overcome register mismatch and factual brittleness, we systematically reconstruct an estimated 20B-token domain subset via multi-register rewriting across 10 genres and 8 writing styles, confidence-routed minimal factual editing, and error-targeted QA synthesis (resolving colloquial typos like '42-luo-mu' -> 42CrMo, expanding ambiguous codes like '16674' -> GB/T 16674, and clarifying conflicting dimensional specs). For downstream deployment, we formalize an evidence-gated constraint-evaluation interface enforcing three-valued logic where unverified product evidence remains unknown rather than satisfied. Offline evaluations demonstrate consistent gains on procurement-query structuring (+2.97 percentage points in exact match, 95% CI [2.11, 3.86] in No-Think mode), while randomized online A/B experiments in production yield substantial improvements (+4.25% GMV, +8.3% satisfied inquiries) alongside a latency reduction from 6-7 s to 1.5 s. Model weights and configs are released at https://huggingface.co/alibaba-multimodal-industrial-ai/IndustryLLM.

</details>


### [10] [COUNTERMEM: World-Model Verified Counter-Factual Memory for Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31874)
*Hongji Pu,Ruixiang Tang,Yongfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COUNTERMEM：一种基于强化学习构建并利用可验证反事实记忆的框架，通过世界模型评估"若采取不同行动"的结果，提升智能体任务表现并降低交互成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆框架多通过与事实世界的交互生成记忆（如记住行动反馈以改进未来任务），但很少在记忆构建时提出"若当初采取不同行动会怎样"的反事实问题。而在真实环境中直接获取这类反馈代价高昂，且会改变比较所需的状态。

Method: 在行动失败后，从原始状态的副本或重置出发，利用可执行世界模型（如测试、证明检查器、求解器）评估局部替代方案；存储改进结果，包括原始与修正动作、已验证的结果以及可复用条件。同时学习一个记忆使用策略，决定检索某条记录或跳过记忆，以平衡任务成功率与交互成本，而基础LLM保持固定。记忆与策略在留出评估期间均被冻结。

Result: 在六个领域共12个基准设置上评估。使用gpt-oss-120b时，COUNTERMEM在全部12个基准上同时改进ReAct和Reflexion，平均提升12.6个百分点；在两主干模型的四领域比较中，任务运行token减少7.7%–42.0%（不含离线选择器训练成本）。进一步分析显示，去除验证或持久存储会削弱收益，而将已验证修正应用于不适宜决策时反而可能产生负向效果。

Conclusion: 通过构建和复用经过验证的反事实记忆，并配合学习到的记忆使用策略，可在不改变基础LLM的前提下显著提升智能体性能并降低token开销；验证机制与持久存储是收益的关键来源，修正动作的适用性判断也至关重要。

Abstract: Existing agent memory frameworks mainly create memory through an agent's interaction with the factual world, e.g., remembering feedback from actions taken to improve performance on future tasks. However, these frameworks seldom ask the "what if" question during memory construction: what if a different action had been taken, would the feedback have changed, and how could this feedback become useful memory? Obtaining such feedback directly in an active environment can be expensive and can alter the state needed for comparison. In this work, we introduce COUNTERMEM, a reinforcement-learning framework for constructing and using verified counterfactual memory across tasks. After a failed action, COUNTERMEM evaluates local alternatives from a copy or reset of the original state using executable world models, such as tests, proof checkers, and solvers. It stores improvements with the original and corrected actions, checked outcomes, and conditions for reuse. A learned memory-use policy selects a retrieved record or skips memory to balance task success and interaction cost, while the base LLM remains fixed. Both memory and policy are frozen during held-out evaluation. We evaluate COUNTERMEM on 12 benchmark settings across six domains. With gpt-oss-120b, COUNTERMEM improves both ReAct and Reflexion on all 12 benchmarks across six domains, averaging a gain of 12.6 percentage points over their unaugmented versions. In the four-domain comparison across two backbones, task-run tokens decrease by 7.7-42.0%, excluding offline selector-training costs. Further analyses show that removing verification or persistent storage weakens the gains, while applying verified corrections to unsuitable decisions can reverse them. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [11] [Context-dependent agent evaluation with orthogonal equilibrium learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31897)
*Haorui Ma,Zehua Zang,Jiangmeng Li,Yi Li,Fanjing Xu,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 NashEval 框架，将上下文相关的智能体评估建模为两人博弈，利用去偏的上下文支付矩阵估计与正交损失学习上下文到纳什均衡的映射，从而稳健地识别各上下文下的优胜智能体集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多应用需要在上下文信息（如提示、任务、用户群体）下评估智能体。现有基于得分的方法（如 Bradley-Terry）强加传递性偏好排序，当人类判断具有异质性时无法反映集体偏好。同时，从离线日志学习上下文相关的均衡很困难，因为每个上下文只揭示部分智能体的反馈，朴素的插入式估计会产生偏差。

Method: 受社会选择理论启发，将评估框架化为两名玩家之间的上下文博弈，每位玩家选择智能体上的分布作为策略以获取更高的集体偏好；纳什均衡的支撑集定义了特定上下文的优胜者集合。提出 NashEval 通用框架：首先构造刻画博弈的上下文支付矩阵的去偏估计，随后用定制的正交损失学习上下文到均衡的映射，避免为每个上下文单独求解博弈。

Result: 理论上证明，估计支付矩阵背后干扰函数时的误差仅通过高阶项影响所学均衡的风险（即可利用性）。在多组实验中，NashEval 提升了均衡学习的鲁棒性，并能一致地识别跨上下文表现最佳的智能体集合。

Conclusion: NashEval 通过结合社会选择理论与去偏、正交学习技术，为异质人类反馈下的上下文相关智能体评估提供了稳健的均衡学习方案。

Abstract: Many applications require to evaluate agents under contextual information (e.g., a prompt, task, or user group). We study how to perform such context-dependent agent evaluation from offline feedback. Existing score-based models for this purpose (e.g., Bradley-Terry) impose a transitive preference ordering, which fails to reflect collective preferences when human judgements are heterogeneous. Inspired by social choice theory, we frame evaluation as a contextual game between two players, each selecting a distribution over agents as the strategy to receive greater collective preference than the other. Then, the support of the Nash equilibrium defines a context-specific set of winners. However, learning context-specific equilibria from offline logs is difficult because each context reveals human feedback on only a subset of agents, and, hence, a naive plug-in estimator can therefore be biased. To address these challenges, we propose NashEval, a general framework for robust contextual equilibrium learning. NashEval first constructs debiased estimates of the contextual payoff matrix that characterizes the game. NashEval then learns the context-to-equilibrium mapping with a tailored orthogonal loss, which avoids the need to solve a separate game for each context. We show theoretically that errors in estimating the nuisance functions underlying the payoff matrix affect the risk of the learned equilibrium (i.e., exploitability) only through higher-order terms. Across various experiments, NashEval improves robustness of equilibrium learning and consistently identifies the set of top-performing agents across contexts.

</details>


### [12] [Choir: An Open Protocol for Distributed Multi-Agent Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31903)
*Yidi Qi,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Choir 是一个面向形式化数学的开源分布式协议，将项目分解为任务，让独立贡献者各自运行 AI agent，并完全通过项目的 GitHub 仓库进行协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI agent 虽已能在 Lean 等证明助手中形式化整本教材和重要定理，但这些工作通常是集中式的：由单一团队运行所有 agent 并承担全部计算成本。为降低门槛、实现开放参与，作者提出分布式形式化的思路。

Method: Choir 将项目分解为可由独立贡献者完成的任务；每个贡献者用各自的 agent 和 LLM 订阅运行，并完全通过项目的 GitHub 仓库协调。为保证开放参与的可靠性，每份贡献在合并前都经过确定性 gate 检查。协议支持 Lean 4、Isabelle 和 Rocq，且开源、模块化，项目可替换单个组件或扩展协议。

Result: 论文提出了 Choir 这一开源分布式形式化协议，支持多种证明助手（Lean 4、Isabelle、Rocq），并通过确定性 gate 机制确保贡献质量。摘要未提供具体实验数据或性能指标。

Conclusion: Choir 通过分布式、开放参与的协议设计，改变了 AI 形式化数学的集中式模式，降低了计算成本门槛，并具备模块化和可扩展性。

Abstract: AI agents can now formalize entire textbooks and major theorems in proof assistants such as Lean, but current efforts are typically centralized: a single team runs all agents and bears the full computational cost. We introduce Choir, an open protocol for distributed formalization. Choir decomposes a project into tasks that can be completed by independent contributors, each running their own agent with their own LLM subscription, while coordinating entirely through the project's GitHub repository. To support open participation, every contribution is checked by a deterministic gate before merge. Choir supports Lean 4, Isabelle, and Rocq, and is open source and modular, allowing projects to replace individual components or extend the protocol.

</details>


### [13] [EmailBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on Enterprise Email and Productivity Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31906)
*Mukul Singh,Mansi Uniyal,Devin Devlin,Wen Xie,Big Thadawasin,Ritam Dutt,Vivian Lai,Hyeonsu B. Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EmailBench，一个包含 206 个邮件与生产力场景、16 类任务的邮件智能体基准，采用混合评估协议（258 条静态断言 + 211 条 LLM 评分规则），发现最佳模型仅通过 33.5% 场景，说明工具调用成功不等于任务完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业邮件智能体需要融合信息检索、结构化状态变更、时间推理与多步协调能力，但现有智能体基准多聚焦通用生产力任务，缺少在自包含环境中针对类型化邮件工作流的评测，因此需要一个专门的端到端邮件智能体基准。

Method: 构建 EmailBench：包含 206 个邮件与生产力场景、覆盖 16 类任务；将类型化邮件 API 规范与提供商中立的命名结合，使用确定性的合成 Enron 风格语料，场景主题选择参考交互式原型中的聚合任务意图遥测；评估采用混合协议，即 258 条可执行静态断言加 211 条 LLM 评分规则；在固定单用户语料上评测八种语言模型配置。

Result: 表现最好的配置仅通过 33.5% 的场景，而其工具调用有 99.7% 未出现可见 API 失败；不同任务类别之间的通过率差异显著。

Conclusion: 有效工具执行并不等同于任务完成，二者之间存在明显差距；EmailBench 为邮件智能体提供了自包含的端到端评测环境，未来工作包括扩大工具覆盖、多角色测试以及重复运行评估。

Abstract: Enterprise email agents must combine information retrieval, structured state changes, temporal reasoning, and multi-step coordination. Recent agent benchmarks include productivity tasks, but few center on typed email workflows in a self-contained environment. We introduce EmailBench, a benchmark of 206 email and productivity scenarios across 16 task categories. The benchmark couples a typed email API specification with provider-neutral naming, a deterministic synthetic Enron-inspired corpus, and a scenario suite whose topic selection was informed by aggregate task-intent telemetry from an interactive prototype. Its hybrid evaluation protocol combines 258 executable static assertions with 211 LLM rubrics. We evaluate eight LM configurations on a fixed single-user corpus. The best-performing configuration passes only 33.5% of scenarios despite 99.7% of its tool calls completing without an observed API failure, with pass rates varying substantially across task categories. This gap shows that valid tool execution is not equivalent to task completion. EmailBench provides a self-contained environment for end-to-end email-agent evaluation, with broader tool coverage, multi-persona testing, and repeated-run evaluation as future work areas.

</details>


### [14] [Improving Medical Calculation of LLMs with Embedded Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31908)
*Tianshi Ming,Yingying Zhang,Xian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedCode框架，通过训练LLM生成嵌入式可执行代码来提升医疗计算任务的准确性，并提出wDPO优化方法，在多个模型上取得20-30个百分点的绝对准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在医学考试和问答基准上表现良好，但在需要精确数值输出的医疗计算任务上仍不可靠。这类计算（如药物剂量、器官功能评估、预后评分）涉及高风险临床决策，小错误也可能导致严重后果，因此需要一种更精确可靠的医疗计算方法。

Method: 提出MedCode框架，让LLM根据临床上下文识别相关计算器、提取输入变量，并生成将算术运算委托给确定性解释器的脚本，执行脚本返回计算值、解释和单位。基于MedCalc基准构建SFT和偏好数据集，并额外整理ICU场景计算任务数据集。同时提出加权直接偏好优化（wDPO），自适应强调模型难以区分的偏好对。

Result: 在LLaMA3-8B、Qwen2.5-7B和Mistral-7B上的实验显示，绝对准确率提升20-30个百分点，验证了嵌入式代码生成对医疗计算的有效性。

Conclusion: 嵌入式可执行代码生成能有效提升LLM在医疗计算任务上的精确性和可靠性，MedCode框架结合wDPO方法具有显著效果。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) perform well on medical examinations and question-answering benchmarks, but remain unreliable on medical calculation tasks that require exact numerical outputs. These calculations support high-stakes decisions such as medication dosing, organ-function assessment, and prognostic scoring, for which even small errors can have serious clinical consequences. We introduce MedCode, a framework that improves medical calculation by training LLMs to generate embedded executable code. Given a clinical context, the model identifies the relevant calculator, extracts its input variables, and produces a script that delegates arithmetic operations to a deterministic interpreter. Executing the script returns the calculated value together with an explanation and the appropriate unit. We construct supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and preference datasets from the MedCalc benchmark and additionally curate a dataset for calculation tasks in Intensive Care Unit (ICU) scenarios. We further propose weighted Direct Preference Optimization (wDPO), which adaptively emphasizes preference pairs that are difficult for the model to distinguish. Experiments with LLaMA3-8B, Qwen2.5-7B, and Mistral-7B show absolute accuracy gains of 20--30 percentage points, demonstrating the effectiveness of embedded code generation for medical calculation.

</details>


### [15] [BioDyad: Synchronize Biomedical Discovery and Machine Learning Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31939)
*Xingbo Du,Fadli Aulawi Al Ghiffari,Leonard Song,Loka Li,Duzhen Zhang,Zixiao Wang,Xiuying Chen,Le Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioDyad通过蒙特卡洛图搜索中的双重层级结构耦合生物医学发现与机器学习工程，在BioXArena基准上取得最佳综合得分与任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能生物医学机器学习虽整合了证据获取与可执行程序搜索的进展，但在程序搜索全过程中协调二者仍具挑战：新证据需指导候选构建，执行结果需反馈后续发现与复用，验证需求须匹配搜索预算。

Method: 提出BioDyad，在蒙特卡洛图搜索内设两个层级：科学层级结合先验生物医学指导与迭代发现，并将生物医学方案与执行结果关联存入记忆以供候选复用；工程层级让候选程序依次经历冒烟执行、训练/验证评估直至全数据再训练。

Result: 在76项任务的BioXArena基准、每任务两小时预算、三种匹配LLM后端下评估，BioDyad在惩罚后全任务得分与任务成功率上均优于四种智能体方法及单次基线。

Conclusion: 结果支持通过协调生物医学发现与机器学习工程，将外部知识整合为跨异构生物医学任务的可执行程序。

Abstract: Agentic biomedical machine learning (ML) draws on complementary advances in biomedical evidence acquisition and executable program search. Existing systems connect aspects of these capabilities, but coordinating them throughout program search remains challenging. New evidence must guide candidate construction, execution outcomes must inform subsequent discovery and reuse, and validation demands must fit the search budget. We introduce BioDyad, which couples biomedical discovery and ML engineering through two hierarchies within Monte Carlo graph search. Its scientific hierarchy combines prior biomedical guidance with iterative discovery, then links biomedical plans to execution outcomes in memory for reuse across candidates. Its engineering hierarchy moves candidate programs from smoke execution, through train/validation evaluation, to full-data retraining. We evaluate BioDyad on the 76-task BioXArena benchmark under a two-hour per-task budget with three matched LLM backends. It achieves the highest penalized all-task score and task success rate among four agent methods and a one-shot baseline under each backend. These results support coordinating biomedical discovery and ML engineering to integrate external knowledge into executable programs across heterogeneous biomedical tasks.

</details>


### [16] [Symbolic Guidance for LLM Agents in Distributed Multiagent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31963)
*Ben Rachmut,Ning Zhang,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik,William Yeoh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过符号引导调节智能体自主性，可提升LLM多智能体分布式协调的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益作为自主智能体部署于多智能体系统，但其可靠执行分布式协调协议的能力尚不明确。在AgentsNet等基准中，给予完全推理自主权往往导致复杂领域中的表现不一致或退化，因此需要研究如何调节自主性以改善协调效果。

Method: 提出符号引导分类法（SGT），刻画从开放式自然语言推理到完全规定式算法执行的自主性谱系，中间层级提供部分伪代码引导；基于AgentsNet框架进行实验，比较不同自主性水平下LLM智能体的协调表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，中间自主性水平的表现持续优于无引导智能体和完全规定式规范两种情况。

Conclusion: 自主性调节是基于LLM的分布式协调的关键设计原则，符号引导结合适度自主性可有效提升协调性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in multi-agent systems, yet their ability to reliably execute distributed coordination protocols remains poorly understood. While AgentsNet, a benchmark framework for distributed coordination among LLM agents, enables such coordination, granting full reasoning autonomy often leads to inconsistent or degraded performance in complex domains.
  We hypothesize that coordination can be improved by regulating agent autonomy through symbolic guidance derived from established algorithms. To investigate this, we introduce the \emph{Symbolic Guidance Taxonomy (SGT)}, which characterizes a spectrum of autonomy ranging from open-ended natural language reasoning to fully prescribed algorithmic execution, with intermediate levels providing partial pseudocode guidance. Our results show that intermediate autonomy levels consistently outperform both unguided agents and fully prescriptive specifications. These findings identify autonomy regulation as a key design principle for LLM-based distributed coordination.

</details>


### [17] [Goal-Persistent Coding Agents as Scientific Performance Engineers: A Fixed-Radius Nearest-Neighbor Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31980)
*Xiangyang Ju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通用型编程智能体（Codex、Claude Code）在仓库级案例中自主优化粒子追踪的固定半径最近邻（FRNN）搜索，去除 PyTorch 依赖并生成独立 C++/CUDA 库，同步 NumPy 接口比原 GPU 常驻 PyTorch 接口快 1.6 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏证据表明通用编程智能体能够进行严谨的科学性能工程。作者希望探究：在仅给定可执行目标（精确正确性测试、性能剖析要求、验收标准）而不规定具体代码变换的条件下，现成智能体能否自主完成跨多轮工具调用的持续优化任务。

Method: 采用仓库级案例研究。以粒子追踪中的固定半径最近邻搜索为任务，初始实现依赖 PyTorch 的 CUDA 代码。让 Codex 与 Claude Code 智能体在可执行目标约束下自主开展假设驱动的优化实验，包括移除 PyTorch 依赖、进行性能剖析和迭代改写。并在不同 GPU 架构与软件栈上重复，另进行一次独立优化重跑。

Result: 主轨迹中智能体自主移除 PyTorch 依赖，构建出独立 C++/CUDA 库，精确复现目标参考结果；其同步 NumPy 接口在包含主机传输的情况下仍比原 GPU 常驻 PyTorch 接口快 1.6 倍。不同 GPU 架构与软件栈上观察到类似加速。独立重跑采用了不同假设序列，并在目标负载上取得更好性能。

Conclusion: 目标持续型编程智能体能够充当实验性性能工程师。可执行的科学契约（精确正确性测试、剖析要求、验收标准）既是引导其优化方向的必要条件，也是验证其优化结果的关键。

Abstract: Coding agents can pursue persistent objectives across many tool-use turns, but evidence that general-purpose agents can conduct rigorous scientific performance engineering remains limited. We present a repository-scale case study in which off-the-shelf Codex and Claude Code agents optimize fixed-radius nearest-neighbor (FRNN) search for particle tracking. Starting from a PyTorch-dependent CUDA implementation, the agents follow an executable goal that specifies exact-correctness tests, profiling requirements, and acceptance criteria without prescribing code transformations. In the primary sequential trajectory, they autonomously remove the PyTorch dependency and conduct hypothesis-driven optimization experiments. The resulting standalone C++/CUDA library exactly reproduces the targeted reference result. Its synchronous NumPy interface achieved 1.6-fold speedup over the original GPU-resident PyTorch interface, despite including host transfers. Similar speedups were observed across different GPU architectures and software stacks. An independent optimization rerun followed a different sequence of hypotheses and reached even better performance on the target workload. These results show that goal-persistent coding agents can act as experimental performance engineers, and that executable scientific contracts are needed both to guide and to validate their optimization.

</details>


### [18] [CSI-Agent: LLM-Assisted Few-Shot Adaptation for Cross-Domain Wi-Fi CSI Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31990)
*Tianya Zhao,Chuan Liu,Xuyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CSI-Agent，将跨域 CSI 适配重构为部署时的决策问题，利用 LLM 智能体为不同类别选择保留默认或触发专门动作，在 1-shot 适配下平均 Macro-F1 提升约 16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wi-Fi CSI 支持无设备感知，但跨域部署时模型脆弱，用户或环境变化会导致错误预测。现有方法多将其视为离线模型设计问题，对全目标域施加统一适配，忽略了目标域标注稀缺、且同一域偏移下不同类别的失效方式可能不同。

Method: CSI-Agent 是一个证据搜寻式 LLM 智能体：（1）不处理原始 CSI 或做样本级预测，而是将目标域行为汇总为基于感知的类级证据；（2）从互补 CSI 视角建立强目标自适应默认方案；（3）由 LLM 规划器决定每个类别保留默认还是调用专门动作；（4）通过确定性验证与有界执行减少不可靠干预。

Result: 在四个公开数据集、五种跨域划分（设备、用户、环境及组合偏移）上评估。1-shot 适配下，CSI-Agent 在所有划分上取得最佳目标域表现，平均 Macro-F1 较最强基线提升约 16%。

Conclusion: 将跨域 CSI 适配建模为部署时决策问题、并借助 LLM 进行类级证据驱动的自适应动作选择，能有效缓解跨域脆弱性，优于固定适配方法。

Abstract: Wi-Fi channel state information (CSI) has enabled device-free sensing applications such as human activity recognition. However, CSI sensing models remain brittle in cross-domain deployment, where changes in users or environments can produce incorrect predictions. Existing solutions usually treat this problem as an offline model-design problem, by pretraining a stronger representation or applying one fixed adaptation method to the entire target domain. In practice, labeled target data are scarce and different classes may fail in different ways under the same domain shift. To address this, we propose CSI-Agent, an evidence-seeking LLM agent that reformulates cross-domain CSI adaptation as a deployment-time decision-making problem. Rather than processing raw CSI or making sample-level predictions, CSI-Agent summarizes target-domain behavior into sensing-grounded class-level evidence. It establishes a strong target-adaptive default from complementary CSI views and uses an LLM planner to determine whether each class should retain the default or invoke a specialized action. Deterministic verification and bounded execution further reduce unreliable interventions. We evaluate CSI-Agent on four public datasets using five cross-domain splits covering device, user, environment, and compositional shifts. Under 1-shot adaptation, CSI-Agent achieves the best target-domain performance across all splits and improves the average Macro-F1 by about 16\% compared to the strongest baseline method.

</details>


### [19] [SenseAgent: An LLM Agent for Adaptive Cross-Domain IMU Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32000)
*Tianya Zhao,Chuan Liu,Xuyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SenseAgent，用 LLM 作为运行时规划器，将跨域 IMU 活动识别从固定推理流程转变为闭环感知过程，通过工具选择与验证器提升跨用户、设备、佩戴位置的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IMU 模型在跨用户、跨设备、跨佩戴位置时表现不可靠；现有方法把跨域问题当成静态模型设计任务（预训练更强表征、数据增强或部署前选定一种适配方法），但实际中目标域是逐步观测的、标签稀缺，且不同域偏移需要不同的感知动作。

Method: SenseAgent 不直接用 LLM 分类原始 IMU 信号，而是让 LLM 在感知工具、源域经验记忆、在线目标记忆和验证器之上做运行时规划。它从目标流构建无标签诊断报告，据此决定保留原始推理或调用专门工具（重力感知感知、原型迁移、风格归一化）；验证器检查源校准、目标记忆可靠性和无害性准则后才接受高风险工具决策，并支持少量反馈而不重训主干或替换无标签路径。

Result: 在多个 IMU 数据集和部署偏移上进行评估，结果表明其经验证的路径选择改善了跨域感知，尤其是在更难的佩戴位置偏移和复合偏移下，并能从有限的用户反馈中进一步获益。

Conclusion: 该设计将跨域 IMU 感知从固定推理管线转化为闭环感知过程：诊断目标偏移、选择合适的感知动作并拒绝不安全的适配。

Abstract: Deep learning has improved inertial measurement unit (IMU) sensing for mobile and wearable applications. However, an IMU model trained in one domain often becomes unreliable when it is used with a new user, device, or body position. Existing methods usually treat this problem as a static model-design task: they pretrain a stronger representation, add data augmentation, or select one adaptation method before deployment. In practice, the target domain is only gradually observed, labels are scarce, and different domain shifts require different sensing actions. This paper presents SenseAgent, an LLM-guided sensing agent for cross-domain IMU activity recognition. Instead of asking an LLM to classify raw IMU signals, SenseAgent uses the LLM as a runtime planner over sensing tools, source-domain experience memory, online target memory, and verifiers. The agent builds a label-free diagnosis report from the target stream and uses it to decide whether to keep raw inference or invoke specialized tools, including gravity-aware sensing, prototype transfer, and style normalization. Verifiers check source calibration, target-memory reliability, and no-harm criteria before accepting high-risk tool decisions. SenseAgent also supports scarce feedback without retraining the backbone or replacing the label-free route. This design converts cross-domain IMU sensing from a fixed inference pipeline into a closed-loop sensing process that diagnoses target shifts, selects suitable sensing actions, and rejects unsafe adaptations. We evaluate SenseAgent across multiple IMU datasets and deployment shifts. Results show that its verified route selection improves cross-domain sensing, especially under harder placement and compound shifts, and further benefits from limited user feedback.

</details>


### [20] [What Does the Rank Buy? A Spectral and Distributional Analysis of Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32002)
*Babak Barazandeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出在硬性逐因子范数约束下，LoRA 的秩 r 并不控制容量：可达更新恰为核范数球内秩≤r 的矩阵，而所有分析的复杂度与位移泛函都在秩一更新处取最大，因此秩上限从不生效。秩的真正作用是决定可达更新集合与奇异方向消除的代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA 中较小的秩通常被视为容量控制，认为能得到更简单、泛化更好的模型。作者希望检验这一直觉在实践常用的权重衰减与范数约束理想化下是否成立。

Method: 在硬性逐因子范数预算下刻画 LoRA 可达更新集合，分析线性读出模型类的 Rademacher 复杂度与源分布位移；对比逐因子预算与联合预算的复杂度界；并从谱角度研究秩与预算对消除预训练权重主奇异方向、实现源—目标对齐的影响。

Result: 复杂度与位移泛函均在秩一更新处最大化，秩上限不生效；线性读出模型类对所有 r≥1 相同，Rademacher 复杂度不依赖 r，位移上界与秩无关且紧。联合预算可恢复数据依赖、秩敏感的复杂度界，但仅在特征分布良好散布时有效，最坏情况仍与秩无关。谱方面，消除主奇异方向需要足够秩与预算，给出了最小秩的上下界，并在双侧谱衰减下匹配。

Conclusion: 秩并非控制模型容量的因素，而是决定哪些更新可达以及消除预训练权重主方向的代价。这一视角重新定位了 LoRA 中秩的作用。

Abstract: The rank $r$ in LoRA is widely treated as a capacity control: a smaller rank is assumed to yield a simpler model that generalizes better. We show that, under hard per-factor norm budgets---the idealization of the weight decay and norm control used in practice---this intuition breaks down. The reason is structural: under such budgets, the updates LoRA can reach are exactly the matrices of rank at most $r$ inside a nuclear-norm ball, and every complexity and displacement functional we analyze is maximized over this set by a rank-one update---so the rank cap never binds. The consequences follow directly. The linear-readout model class we study is identical for every $r \ge 1$, its Rademacher complexity carries no dependence on $r$, and the distance the adaptation can move the source distribution obeys a rank-independent upper bound that we show is sharp. If rank does not control capacity, where does it act? We identify two places. Statistically, replacing the per-factor budgets with a joint budget on the product restores a data-dependent, rank-sensitive complexity bound---though the gain appears only for well-spread feature distributions, and the worst case remains rank-free. Spectrally, rank sets the price of adaptation: canceling the leading singular directions of the pretrained weight requires both sufficient rank and sufficient budget. We bound the smallest rank achieving a desired source--target alignment, with upper and lower bounds that match under two-sided spectral decay. Together, these results recast rank as governing which updates are reachable and what cancellation costs---not how much capacity the model has.

</details>


### [21] [Decentralized Master-Mind: Joint Action Refinement through Iterative Intent Denoising in Multi-Agent Pathfinding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32019)
*Valeriy Vyaltsev,Anton Andreychuk,Taisia Zlotnikova,Konstantin Yakovlev,Aleksandr Panov,Alexey Skrynnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DMM用离散迭代的意图精炼替代一次性动作采样，解决去中心化多智能体路径规划中局部有效但联合不兼容的问题，并结合MICPO优化，取得更高成功率与更低代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化多智能体路径规划（MAPF）在部分可观测下需无碰撞到达各自目标。基于专家数据的学习型策略有效，但当同一情境存在多个需协调的联合动作时，独立地从各智能体分布采样会把局部有效选择重组为不兼容的联合动作；即便各智能体分布学得正确，采样机制本身仍会导致失败。

Method: 提出DMM（Decentralized Master-Mind），受扩散模型去噪启发，用跨通信轮次的离散迭代式动作意图精炼替代一次性采样：智能体先初始化随机动作意图，再通过局部通信迭代精炼，在最终承诺前耦合彼此选择。先用模仿学习在专家MAPF解上预训练，再用MICPO（一种面向多智能体多轮精炼、无评论家的组相对强化学习方法）进一步优化。

Result: DMM总体成功率更高、解代价更低。在1,600个MovingAI任务上，MICPO微调后的DMM解出1,598个，覆盖率在被评估方法中最高，解代价接近最强基线；并可扩展到障碍丰富环境中同时行动的超百万智能体。

Conclusion: 轮级意图精炼能在保持去中心化执行的同时改善联合动作协调，说明以迭代精炼替代一次性采样可有效缓解联合动作不兼容问题。

Abstract: Decentralized multi-agent path finding (MAPF) with communication requires agents to reach individual goals without collisions under partial observability. Learnable policies trained on expert data provide an effective approach to this problem. However, when several coordinated joint actions are valid in the same context, independently sampling from per-agent distributions can recombine locally valid choices into incompatible joint actions. This failure can arise from the final sampling mechanism even when the per-agent action distributions are learned correctly. DMM (Decentralized Master-Mind) addresses this by replacing one-shot action sampling with discrete, iterative refinement of action intents across communication rounds, inspired by denoising in diffusion models. Agents initialize random action intents and refine them through local communication, coupling their choices before commitment. DMM is pretrained with imitation learning on expert MAPF solutions and further optimized with MICPO, a critic-free group-relative reinforcement-learning method designed for multi-agent, multi-round action refinement. DMM generally achieves higher success rates and lower solution costs than the evaluated learnable baselines. On 1,600 MovingAI tasks, DMM fine-tuned with MICPO solves 1,598, the highest coverage among the evaluated methods, while achieving solution costs close to those of the strongest baselines. DMM also scales to over one million simultaneously acting agents in obstacle-rich environments. These results show that round-level intent refinement can improve joint-action coordination while preserving decentralized execution.

</details>


### [22] [A Benchmark for LLM's Understanding of Middle School and High School Science Topics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32020)
*Noah L. Schroeder,Yessy Eka Ambarwati,Yuji Zhang,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个符合NGSS标准的中小学科学基准，用于评估LLM在教育场景中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益融入教育，但缺乏与K-12科学课程标准对齐的评估工具；现有基准多评估通用语言或高阶科学推理，无法反映LLM在中学科学课程内容上的表现。

Method: 采用严格的合成数据流程、多评判者验证和项目级心理测量分析，构建涵盖初中与高中、符合NGSS标准的综合基准；对9个开源权重LLM进行系统评估，并引入人工审核者审查LLM生成题目与NGSS标准的一致性。

Result: 若干较小的、可本地部署的模型在多样科学领域和题型上取得高准确率；模型规模并不能稳定预测性能；人工审核发现合成生成题目与NGSS标准并非完美对齐。

Conclusion: 强调教育部署中有意选择模型的重要性，指出human-in-the-loop题目开发的必要性，以及需探索内容知识与教学知识的交叉，并扩展基准以评估LLM在交互式、基于证据的教育反馈方面的能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into educational settings, yet educators lack robust, standards-aligned tools to evaluate their effectiveness in K-12 science contexts. Existing benchmarks predominantly assess general language or advanced scientific reasoning, leaving a critical gap in understanding LLMs' performance on content directly relevant to secondary science curricula. To address this gap, we developed a comprehensive NGSS-aligned benchmark for both middle and high school science using a rigorous synthetic data pipeline, multi-judge validation, and item-level psychometric analysis. Nine open-weight LLMs were systematically evaluated using this benchmark, indicating that several smaller, locally deployable models achieved high accuracy across diverse science domains and question types. Our findings indicate that model size did not consistently predict performance, emphasizing the importance of intentional model selection for educational deployment. We then incorporated a human reviewer into the loop, reviewing the items generated by the LLMs for alignment with NGSS standards. The human review indicated that synthetically generated items were not in perfect alignment with the NGSS standards, indicating the benefits of human-in-the-loop item development, the need to explore the intersection of content and pedagogical knowledge, and the need to extend benchmarks to evaluate LLMs' capacity for interactive, evidence-based feedback in educational scenarios.

</details>


### [23] [Reasoning Concentrates Errors, and Self-Consistency Never Notices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32035)
*Asaad Althoubi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在固定模型权重、仅切换推理模式的情况下，推理会集中模型的错误：两个独立错误答案重合的概率显著上升，答案空间越受限这种集中越明显；聚合后可用的问题多样性下降，导致置信度加权与多数投票等价，且在跨模型跨数据集的280种组合中没有任何方法在纠错后优于简单多数投票。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自一致性假设：当模型不确定时，独立采样会给出分歧答案，因此答案一致可以作为正确性的证据。论文希望检验这一假设是否成立，尤其是在开启推理模式后，模型错误是否仍然保持分散，还是会被系统性集中、从而破坏"一致即正确"的前提。

Method: 固定权重、仅切换推理模式，在5个基准、8个模型、74,944个样本上比较推理与非推理两组。分析维度包括：(1) 两个独立错误答案重合的概率（10个数据集-规模对比，以及对两者都答错的问题子集做9/9比较）；(2) 无界答案空间下不同答案的数量比（0.43–0.65）；(3) 有界答案空间下的选项质量分布；(4) 可用问题多样性/headroom的变化；(5) 在280个方法-数据集-模型组合上比较加权投票与多数投票，并检验6信号学习组合与答案对数概率方向性。

Result: 推理在全部10个数据集-规模对比中提高了两个独立错误答案重合的概率（p = 0.00098），在9/9的两组都答错子集中同样成立。无界答案空间中，推理把不同答案数量压缩到非推理的0.43–0.65；有界答案空间中两臂选项集合相同，推理只是把质量集中在其中（固定权重下位置先验无法解释）。问题多样性在10/10单元中收缩，达2.7倍；按可用headroom归一后两臂都只转化约四分之一。280个组合中，纠错后没有任何置信度加权方法优于简单多数投票；加权投票在98.5%的问题-方法对上与多数投票一致，仅3.7%的剩余情况下正确；答案对数概率在推理关闭时预测正确、开启时预测错误，方向可反转；6信号学习组合在域外无增益。

Conclusion: 推理不是让模型"更不确定从而错误分散"，而是把错误集中到少数答案上，破坏了自一致性"一致即正确"的机制前提；同时推理还收缩了答案多样性可发挥作用的题目空间，使得置信度加权几乎无剩余空间可用。因此置信度信号应在实际决策上评估，而非在区分度上评估。

Abstract: Self-consistency assumes that independent samples disagree when a model is unsure, so agreement is evidence of correctness. Holding weights fixed and toggling only a reasoning mode, over five benchmarks and 74,944 samples, we show that reasoning concentrates a model's errors: the probability that two independently drawn wrong answers coincide rises in all ten dataset-scale comparisons (p = 0.00098), and in nine of nine after restricting both arms to the problems each gets wrong. Where the answer space is unbounded, reasoning cuts the distinct answers produced to 0.43-0.65 of the non-reasoning count; where it is bounded, both arms hold an identical option set and reasoning concentrates mass on it instead, which no positional prior can explain at fixed weights. The aggregate cost is smaller than the mechanism predicts, because reasoning also shrinks the set of problems where answer diversity can decide anything, in ten of ten cells and by 2.7x; normalized for available headroom, both arms convert a quarter of it in domain. Confidence weighting does not recover what is left. Across 280 method-dataset-model combinations on eight models and five benchmarks, not one beats plain majority voting after correction; weighted voting agrees with it on 98.5% of problem-method pairs and is right 56.3% of the time on the rest; and a signal's direction can invert within fixed weights, with answer log-probability predicting correctness when reasoning is off and error when it is on. A learned six-signal combination gains nothing out of domain. Confidence signals should be evaluated on decisions, not on discrimination.

</details>


### [24] [Receiver-Conditioned Latent Communication gives 94% CacheBack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32046)
*Maximillian Rossi,Prajwal Raghunath,Haoqing Xuan,Yusen Zhang,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CacheBack，一种免训练、基于发送方注意力权重的接收方条件化KV缓存压缩方法，在多智能体通信中去除75%冗余状态，同时提升准确率并大幅降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统需跨智能体分发大上下文。文本消息紧凑但需解码且可能遗漏接收方所需证据；近期潜空间通信直接传输KV缓存，虽避免文本生成并提升精度与延迟，但完整KV缓存随单智能体处理上下文及协作智能体数量线性增长，带来远超GPU显存与上下文窗口的内存和上下文开销。

Method: 核心观察：智能体只需发送接收方本地任务所需信息，即接收方条件化通信。接收方将自身信息需求的小型描述传给发送方，用于过滤与压缩发送方的KV缓存。CacheBack是接收方条件化的简单、鲁棒、免训练实现，基于发送方的注意力权重进行筛选。

Result: 在FanOutQA上，CacheBack配合Qwen 3去除智能体本应收到的75%状态，准确率提升14.7个百分点，中位任务完成延迟相对文本通信降低3.2倍。在稠密Transformer、Mamba-注意力混合、滑窗注意力等模型家族上均显示可比提升。

Conclusion: 接收方条件化通信能有效压缩多智能体间的KV缓存传输，CacheBack作为免训练方案在多种模型架构上同时改善精度与效率，为潜空间通信的规模化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems distribute large contexts across agents that communicate to solve a task. Text messages are compact but require decoding and may omit evidence the receiving agent needs. Recent latent communication instead transfers KV caches. This avoids text generation and can improve accuracy and latency. However, a full KV cache grows linearly with both the context an individual agent processes, and the number of agents that coordinate together. This raises memory and context costs, often far exceeding available GPU resources and context window sizes. Our key observation is that agents need only send what the receiving agent requires for its local task -- which we call receiver-conditioned communication. The receiver agent passes the sender a small description of its information needs, which serves to filter and compress the sender agent's KV cache. CacheBack is a simple, robust, training-free instance of receiver conditioning based on the sender's attention weights. On FanOutQA, CacheBack with Qwen 3 removes 75% of the state the agent would otherwise receive, improving accuracy by 14.7 percentage points and reducing median task-completion latency by 3.2x relative to text communication. We show comparable improvements across model families that span dense Transformers, Mamba-attention hybrids, and sliding-window attention.

</details>


### [25] [EngramRAG: Dynamic Usage-Weighted Topology and Synaptic Consolidation for Multi-Hop Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32049)
*Bhavyateja Potineni,Lohit Giri,Anu Jain,Vadim Kutsyy,Rajasekhar Pentakota*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EngramRAG，一种基于互补学习系统原理的自适应记忆架构，通过唤醒态快速反射与梦境态异步巩固双过程协同，在 LoCoMo 长程对话基准上大幅超越稠密向量 RAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多会话 LLM 智能体的记忆架构存在三大缺陷：关联盲区（无法遍历多跳关系依赖）、脚手架失忆（时间衰减驱逐核心人格不变量）与静态拓扑停滞（图结构不可变、忽略使用动态）。

Method: EngramRAG 耦合低延迟唤醒态反射与异步梦境态巩固，包含四项机制：(1) 使用调制个性化 PageRank（U-PPR），转移概率经 Hebbian 可塑性自适应，将持久实体提升为高中心性认知宏枢纽；(2) 巩固激活的拓扑衰减（CATD），按拓扑承载权重而非挂钟新近度缩放保留半衰期，并设冷启动宽限期（N_grace≥4）；(3) 有向 SUPERSEDES DAG 过滤，抑制事实变更中的过时状态；(4) 三源混合检索，经动态 RRF 融合稠密向量、BM25 与 U-PPR。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 基准 10 段长期对话的全部 1,982 个 QA 对上，Recall@5 相对提升 +38.9%（53.21% vs. 38.29%，p<0.001），MRR 提升 +43.1%（0.4203 vs. 0.2937），显著优于 Okapi BM25（48.66%）与孤立静态图检索（8.50%）；时序推理 Recall@5 达 62.33%（比稠密向量高 16.67 点）。受控变异测试中，SUPERSEDES 将分裂脑幻觉从 70.0% 降至 0.0%；90 天模拟下脚手架保留率 100.0%，交互检索反射延迟 26.21ms。

Conclusion: EngramRAG 通过类脑双过程记忆架构有效缓解关联盲区、脚手架失忆与静态拓扑停滞，在长程对话记忆的召回、排序、时序推理与抗幻觉方面全面领先，且保持毫秒级交互延迟。

Abstract: As autonomous LLM agents are deployed across multi-session environments, conventional memory architectures suffer from Associative Blindness (inability to traverse multi-hop relational dependencies), Scaffolding Amnesia (temporal decay evicting core persona invariants), and Static Topology Stagnation (immutable graphs ignoring usage dynamics). Grounded in Complementary Learning Systems (CLS) principles, we propose EngramRAG, an adaptive memory architecture coupling a low-latency Waking State reflex with an asynchronous background Dreaming State consolidation cycle. EngramRAG introduces: (1) Usage-Modulated Personalized PageRank (U-PPR), where transition probabilities adapt via Hebbian plasticity to promote persistent entities into high-centrality Epistemic Macro-Hubs; (2) Consolidation-Activated Topology Decay (CATD), which scales retention half-life by topological load-bearing weight rather than wall-clock recency, protected by a cold-start grace period (N_grace >= 4); (3) Directed SUPERSEDES DAG filtering to suppress obsolete state during fact mutations; and (4) Triple-source hybrid retrieval fusing dense vectors, BM25, and U-PPR via dynamic Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF). Evaluating on all 1,982 QA pairs across 10 long-term conversations in the LoCoMo benchmark, EngramRAG achieves +38.9% relative improvement in Recall@5 (53.21% vs. 38.29%, p < 0.001) and +43.1% in MRR (0.4203 vs. 0.2937) over dense vector RAG, significantly outperforming Okapi BM25 (48.66%) and isolated static graph retrieval (8.50%). On temporal reasoning, EngramRAG reaches 62.33% Recall@5 (+16.67 points over dense vectors). In controlled mutation tests, SUPERSEDES suppresses split-brain hallucinations from 70.0% to 0.0%, while 90-day simulations show 100.0% scaffolding retention under a 26.21ms interactive retrieval reflex.

</details>


### [26] [Contract monitoring: governing AI via separation of powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32061)
*Enric Boix-Adsera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种AI安全框架，通过契约约束智能体行为，用监控者和裁决者分工及非对称算力实现契约执行，并可实证测量统计安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体的行为范围需要被限定以确保安全，但如何让智能体遵守许可动作、并对其行为提供可验证的安全保证，是当前缺乏系统方法的问题。作者希望建立一种可执行、可度量的安全机制。

Method: 将工作者智能体绑定到规定其许可动作的契约；通过给监控智能体与裁决者分配不同职责，以及对监控者与工作者分配非对称的计算资源，来执行和具体化这些契约。

Result: 该框架支持对统计安全保证进行实证测量，并可适用于多种场景，包括代码安全与"逃出盒子"等情形。

Conclusion: 契约+职责分工+非对称算力的组合为AI安全提供了一种可执行且可度量的框架，具有较广的适用性。

Abstract: We propose an AI safety framework that binds worker agents to contracts specifying their permitted actions. We show how these contracts can be enforced and specified by assigning distinct responsibilities to monitor agents and judges, and asymmetric computational resources to monitors and workers. Our framework allows us to empirically measure statistical safety guarantees. The framework applies to a wide range of settings, including code security and escape-the-box scenarios.

</details>


### [27] [Toward Interactive Understanding of Code APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32081)
*Dhananjay Ashok,Jesse Thomason,Jonathan May*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PAU 基准，要求模型仅通过黑盒 API 交互理解 Python 代码片段功能，发现前沿模型存在过度自信与探索不足问题，并借鉴非对称 Actor-Critic 范式微调模型，使 Qwen3-8B 达到 GPT-5-mini 水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成与理解方法通常依赖对相关代码的读取权限，但在面对外部 API 时这一假设不成立。作者希望研究更一般的"无监督工具理解"问题，即仅通过交互来理解以 Python 方法形式实现的工具，因此构建了 PAU 基准。

Method: PAU 基准为模型提供黑盒、API 级别的代码片段访问权限，模型需用探索性输入查询 API 并从输出中提炼洞见，最终描述代码片段的真实功能。作者分析了常见错误模式，并借鉴机器人学习中常用的非对称 Actor-Critic（AAC）范式对模型进行后训练，以提升交互式代码理解能力。

Result: 即使前沿模型在 PAU 上也难以取得高表现，最佳模型 Claude-4-Opus 在超过 45% 的 PAU 测试集上理解失败。错误模式分析显示模型过度自信，常高估当前假设质量，导致探索不足和过早终止。经 AAC 调优的 Qwen3-8B 模型能进行更主动的 API 探索，性能匹配 GPT-5-mini。

Conclusion: 仅通过交互理解黑盒 API 是一项具有挑战性的任务，现有模型因过度自信而表现受限；引入 AAC 范式进行后训练可有效增强模型的主动探索能力，显著提升交互式代码理解性能。

Abstract: Empowered by advances in Language Model agents, systems have made substantial strides in code generation and understanding. However, these approaches often rely on read access to the relevant code, an assumption which does not hold when dealing with external APIs. In this work, we introduce the PAU (Python API Understanding) benchmark, where we provide models with black-box, API-level access to code snippets. Models must query the API with exploratory inputs and draw insights from the resulting outputs, with the goal of describing the snippet's true functionality. By treating the code snippets as external tools that must be understood via interaction alone, PAU studies the more general problem of unsupervised tool understanding, specifically for tools implemented as Python methods. Despite recent progress in coding agents, even frontier models struggle to achieve high performance on PAU, with the best model (Claude-4-Opus) failing to understand over 45% of the PAU test set. An investigation into the common error modes reveals that models are overconfident; they often overrate the quality of their current hypothesis, leading to insufficient exploration and premature termination. Finally, we take inspiration from the Asymmetric Actor Critic (AAC) paradigm, frequently used in robot learning, to post-train models for interactive code understanding. Models trained with AAC conduct more active exploration of the APIs, with an AAC-tuned Qwen3-8B model matching the performance of GPT-5-mini.

</details>


### [28] [Memory as Middleware for Self-Improving AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32091)
*K. R. Jayaram,Vatche Isahagian,Vinod Muthusamy,Gegi Thomas,Punleuk Oum,Gaodan Fang,Ashwath Vaithinathan Aravindan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将AI智能体的记忆视为中间件问题，主张构建可插拔的一流记忆层，并通过六大系统挑战及参考实现ALTK-Evolve来阐述这一愿景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体默认在会话间无状态，导致操作上"失忆"，每次会话缺乏对以往失败、修复、偏好和成功策略的持久知识，从而重复犯错、丢弃经验。现有的主要解决方案是"定制记忆"，即把检索、持久化和学习逻辑硬编码到单个智能体中并绑定单一存储引擎，造成记忆无法被替换、共享、隔离或独立推理的碎片化局面。

Method: 作者将智能体记忆界定为中间件问题，类比数据访问、消息传递和持久化等已成为中间件关注点。通过提出六大系统挑战来展开这一愿景：双向可插拔性、宿主原生介入、多租户隔离、写路径一致性、带来源的联邦共享，以及生命周期治理。并给出参考实现ALTK-Evolve。

Result: 提出了ALTK-Evolve——一个面向自我改进智能体的记忆中间件参考实现，用以支撑并阐明更广泛的记忆中间件研究议程。

Conclusion: 智能体记忆应被视为一等、可插拔的中间件层，而非绑定于单个智能体的定制逻辑。作者呼吁围绕记忆中间件展开更广泛的研究议程。

Abstract: AI agents are stateless across sessions by default and therefore operationally amnesic: each session begins with little durable knowledge of prior failures, repairs, preferences, or successful strategies. As a result, agents repeat the same mistakes and discard hard-won experience. The dominant fix is \emph{bespoke memory}---retrieval, persistence, and learning logic hand-wired into one agent and bound to one storage engine. This creates a fragmented landscape where memory cannot be swapped, shared, isolated, or reasoned about independently of the agent that owns it. We argue that this is a middleware problem: agent memory deserves a first-class, pluggable layer, just as data access, messaging, and persistence each became middleware concerns.
  We develop this vision through six systems challenges: two-sided pluggability, host-native interposition, multi-tenant isolation, write-path consistency, federated sharing with provenance, and lifecycle governance. We present ALTK-Evolve, a reference implementation of memory middleware for self-improving agents, and use it to motivate a broader research agenda for future memory middleware.

</details>


### [29] [GameBoyWorlds: A Testbed for Self-Improvement in Embodied Video Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32093)
*Dhananjay Ashok,Adam Shen,Aslan Huo Feng,Chinmay Khanna,Jun Rui Huang,Raghav Sarmukaddam,Surendira Balaji Natarajan,Xiaotong Cui,Xincan Zhang,Thomson Yen,Hongseok Namkoong,Jonathan May,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GameBoyWorlds，一个用于评估智能体在电子游戏中自我改进能力的测试平台，包含任务执行（Execution）和通关（Playthrough）两部分。结果显示，当前主流自我改进方法表现不佳，前沿模型在 500 个任务中完成率不足 50%，且先进多模态智能体流水线甚至无法在游戏通关中到达首个主要里程碑，凸显了自我改进智能体的巨大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管借助专家指导智能体已能在交互环境中操作，但其能否真正从自身经验中自主学习和自我改进仍不清晰。现有研究缺乏系统评估这类自我改进方法的测试平台。

Method: 提出 GameBoyWorlds 测试平台：1）GameBoyWorlds-Execution，涵盖 5 个不同游戏系列，智能体仅能访问专门训练游戏，无演示、文档或奖励，需通过自主探索和从自身经验推断可操作知识来扎根环境，测试时需完成评估导航、交互和游戏机制理解的短时程任务；2）GameBoyWorlds-Playthrough，测试两款玩家自制宝可梦游戏的端到端通关，要求智能体从自身经验中学习并自主提升。

Result: 前沿模型在 500 个任务中完成不足 50%，主要因多模态环境扎根失败。世界建模与自主技能发现等当代自我改进方法均告失败，基于好奇心探索编写指南的新策略仅取得部分成功。在 Playthrough 中，具备多模态记忆和分层子目标的先进智能体流水线在两款游戏中均未能达到首个主要里程碑。

Conclusion: GameBoyWorlds 是一个富有挑战性的自我改进智能体目标，当前方法仍存在明显不足，表明自主学习和自我改进能力尚待突破。

Abstract: Powered by expert guidance, agents can operate in interactive environments; however, it is unclear whether they can learn autonomously from their own experience. To evaluate such self-improvement methods, we introduce GameBoyWorlds, a testbed for agentic self-improvement in video games. GameBoyWorlds-Execution evaluates task execution on a collection of 5 distinct game series. Agents are allowed access to dedicated training games but are provided no demonstrations, documentation, or rewards. Agents must ground themselves in the environment through self-directed exploration and by inferring actionable knowledge from their own experience. At test time, agents must complete short-horizon tasks that evaluate their ability to navigate, interact, and engage with game-specific mechanics in unseen games. Out-of-the-box frontier models complete fewer than 50% of the 500 tasks due to failures in multimodal grounding, establishing that self-improvement methods have room to push performance. We demonstrate that contemporary approaches to self-improvement are lacking, with world modelling and autonomous skill discovery failing, and a novel strategy that uses curiosity-based exploration to write guides achieving only partial success. GameBoyWorlds-Playthrough tests end-to-end game completion in two fan-made Pokémon games. We show that while frontier models have been pre-exposed to official releases such as Pokémon Red, they lack essential information on the games in our testbed. Instead of relying on their parametric knowledge to succeed, agents must learn from their own experience and autonomously improve over the course of the playthrough. We show that a sophisticated agentic pipeline with multimodal memory and hierarchical subgoals fails to reach even the first major milestone in both games, establishing GameBoyWorlds as an ambitious target for self-improving agents.

</details>


### [30] [Artificial intelligences and human scientists exhibit complementary strengths in theory building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32562)
*Ke Li,Spyros I. Zoumpoulis,Phanish Puranam,Philip Parker,Matthew Eshbaugh-Soha,Izzy Gainsburg,Michael Gilead,Igor Grossmann,Britt Hadar,Yoel Inbar,Almog Simchon,Robb Willer,Rui Ai,Ruicheng Ao,Gavin J. Bala,Matthew Bidwell,Shuang Cai,Kai Chang,Skyler Y. Chen,Cory J. Clark,Irmak Dai,Abhinandan Dalal,Connor Douglas,Alexis Du,Zhehang Du,Leyun Feng,Isabel Fernandez-Mateo,Linnea Gandhi,Cyrille Grumbach,Anmol Gupta,Vansh Gupta,Maria Hademer,Jay H. Hardy,Chen Kai Huang,Jacob Xiangyu Jin,Ufuk Keskin,Na Hyun Kim,Mert Kobaş,Byounghoon Koh,Gabrielle Lamont-Dobbin,Gregory Lanzalotto,Sun Young Lee,Dingzhe Leng,Chenjun Li,Weiyuan Li,Zeyuan Li,Zhongyuan Liang,Ning Liu,Peihong Liu,Yuhan Liu,Jiuyao Lu,Wanteng Ma,Nicolas Martinet,Natnael Mulat,Christina A. Nguyen,Khai Nguyen,Quang Minh Nguyen,Naja Pape,Chanwoo Park,Stefanos Poulidis,Jeffrey Sanchez-Burks,Michael Schaerer,Isabelle Solal,Yanbo Song,Junghyo Sun,Qingyao Sun,Rui Sun,Roderick Swaab,Kevin Tan,Dequn Teng,Michelle A. Vaccaro,Robin Vigerbaeck,Xiaomeng Wang,Randol H. Yao,Duygu Yilmaz,Shun Yiu,Ecem Yucesoy,Allen Zang,Ruijia Zhang,Xilan Zhang,Yichi Zhang,Zhanhao Zhang,Eric Luis Uhlmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究比较了25个大语言模型与13位资深研究者及60位博士学者在社会科学高阶认知任务（理论构建、预测新实证结果、依据新证据修正理论）上的表现，发现AI在大多数任务上超越多数人类个体，但人类理论更多样、聚合后预测增益更大，且AI的理论复杂带有装饰性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI（尤其是大语言模型）在社会科学高阶认知任务上能否媲美或超越人类科学家，以判断AI在理论构建、预测与理论修正等核心科研环节中的能力与局限。

Method: 以性别与种族不平等的学术话语为研究领域，将25个大语言模型与13位资深研究者、60位博士学者进行比较，评估其在理论构建、对新实证结果的预测以及依据新证据修正理论等任务上的表现，并由不知来源的独立评分者评定理论质量。

Result: AI在大多数任务的个体表现上超过多数人类；人类理论更多样，聚合后预测准确性提升更大；AI理论阐述更详尽（含更多理论路径与潜在变量）且被独立评分者评为更高质量，但其复杂性部分只是装饰性的，并未带来更准确的预测；人类以更简洁的理论实现了更高的预测效率；AI比人类更倾向于依据新证据修正理论，而人类则以对先前预测误差敏感的筛选方式更新信念。

Conclusion: AI凭借更强的信息处理能力，特别适合需要应对复杂性的任务；但人类思想的更大多样性对于群体智慧与集体创造力不可或缺。

Abstract: We investigate the effectiveness of artificial intelligences (AI)-specifically large language models (LLMs)-relative to human scientists at high-level cognitive tasks in social science such as theory formulation, predictions of novel empirical results, and theory revision in response to new evidence. The research domain was academic discourse regarding gender and race inequality. Our findings, comparing 25 LLMs with 13 senior researchers and 60 doctoral scholars, reveal that the AIs outperformed most humans individually on most of the present tasks, while human theories were more diverse and exhibited greater gains in predictive accuracy from aggregation. AI-generated theories were more extensively elaborated, involving additional theoretical paths and latent variables, and were rated as higher quality than human theories by independent raters blinded to source. However, this theoretical complexity was in part ornamental, in that it was not associated with more accurate predictions about empirical patterns in data; in contrast, human scientists achieved greater predictive efficiency with simpler theories. The AIs were significantly more likely than human scientists to revise their theories to incorporate new evidence; human scientists updated their beliefs in a selective way that is sensitive to prior prediction errors. We speculate that the superior processing capacity of artificial intelligences makes them especially well-suited to tasks requiring grappling with complexity, but that the greater diversity of human ideas is essential to wise crowds and collective creativity.

</details>


### [31] [Residual Streams Read, Recurrent States Remember: The Global Workspace in Mamba Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32102)
*Wenlong Wang,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文探讨全局工作空间理论能否扩展到状态空间语言模型，通过对 Mamba-1/2/3 拟合 Jacobian 透镜，发现循环状态可作为工作空间内容的互补载体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局工作空间的解释通常针对 Transformer 表征，尚不清楚是否能延伸到状态空间语言模型（如 Mamba）。作者希望验证 Mamba 的循环状态是否也承载可读、可用、可因果干预的工作空间内容。

Method: 在 Mamba-1、Mamba-2、Mamba-3 的残差流与循环状态上拟合 Jacobian 透镜，沿用原始 1000 条提示的配方；比较残差透镜、状态独读、联合残差-状态读出与 logit 透镜；开展时序图与词表实验；提出 sign-guarded steering（符号保护引导）并与坐标交换法在匹配言语报告试验上对比。

Result: 联合残差-状态读出在六个任务族中至少五个上优于残差透镜；Mamba-2 上状态独读在全部六个族上超过残差与 logit 透镜，归一化联合读出在五族上优于两个分量；时序图与词表实验显示当残差可见性变化时早期内容仍可被状态读出；sign-guarded steering 在五个模型的匹配言语报告试验中提升目标前五成功率；但增益不能稳定扩展到关系型答案，受保护编辑常直接输出被编辑概念，且 Mamba-3 的联合编辑可能破坏仅状态重定向的成功。

Conclusion: 循环状态提供了工作空间内容的互补载体，其恢复、持续与因果使用需要分别测量；对于关系型答案，状态读出的可解释性与可干预性仍有限。

Abstract: Can the global-workspace account of transformer representations extend to state-space language models? We fit Jacobian lenses to the residual streams and recurrent states of Mamba-1, Mamba-2 and Mamba-3, using the original 1000-prompt recipe. Joint residual--state readouts improve recovery of known intermediate concepts over the residual lens on at least five of six task families in every tested Mamba checkpoint. On Mamba-2, state alone exceeds residual and logit lenses on all six families; a normalised joint readout improves on both components on five. Temporal maps and word-list experiments show earlier content remaining state-readable as residual visibility changes. We also propose sign-guarded steering, which improves target top-five success over coordinate exchange on matched verbal-report trials in five models. Recurrent state alone supports this verbal access. These gains do not extend consistently to relational answers: guarded edits often output the edited concept itself, and Mamba-3's joint edits can disrupt successful state-only redirection. Recurrent state thus provides a complementary carrier of workspace content, whose recovery, persistence and causal uses require separate measurements.

</details>


### [32] [CLAIRE: A Schema-Grounded Hybrid Workflow for Healthcare Administrative Form Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32787)
*Garapati Keerthana,Manik Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出并评估了CLAIRE混合工作流，用于医疗行政表单的字段填充，在合成基准上实现1.000的成功率与字段准确率，去除验证环节后压力测试成功率降至0.500。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗行政人员需将电子健康记录、转诊、理赔系统、服务提供者名册和工作队列中的结构化信息转录到动态表单中，该过程耗时且易出错，需要一种既能借助语言模型辅助映射、又不让其授权不受支持或高风险操作的自动化方案。

Method: CLAIRE将流程解耦为字段状态发现、源到字段映射、确定性验证、有界纠正、升级和审计追踪。实验使用五个合成医疗行政模式、1000条源记录、四种界面变体、两个数据质量套件和六个对比方法，共24000个基准回合；另设严格输出审计（Qwen2.5-1.5B与7B的直接映射）及基于追踪的操作模拟（6000回合）。

Result: 在合成基准条件下，完整CLAIRE在两个套件中的回合成功率、字段准确率、必填字段完成率和依赖完成率均为1.000；移除验证后压力套件成功率降至0.500。模拟中，干净与验证压力回合100.0%达到可供人员审核的草稿，升级挑战回合为68.6%，不支持的情形被阻断。基于场景的节省为每例149.7-165.5秒（非实际观测的员工时间）。

Conclusion: 研究支持以模式为基准、验证优先的医疗行政自动化范式：语言模型组件仅辅助映射，不授权不受支持或具后果性的操作。

Abstract: Healthcare administrative staff transfer structured information from electronic health records, referrals, claims systems, provider rosters, and work queues into dynamic forms. We developed and evaluated CLAIRE (Clinical Language and Agentic Intelligence for Reasoning and Entry), a hybrid workflow that separates field-state discovery, source-to-field mapping, deterministic validation, bounded correction, escalation, and audit tracing. We tested five synthetic healthcare administrative schemas, 1,000 source records, four interface variants, two data-quality suites, and six comparators, yielding 24,000 benchmark episodes. A separate strict-output audit evaluated direct mappings from Qwen2.5-1.5B and Qwen2.5-7B, and a trace-derived operational simulation covered 6,000 episodes. Under the evaluated synthetic benchmark conditions, full CLAIRE achieved 1.000 episode success, field accuracy, required-field completion, and dependency completion in both suites; removing validation reduced stress-suite success to 0.500. In the simulation, 100.0% of clean and validation-stress episodes reached a staff-reviewable draft, compared with 68.6% of escalation challenge episodes, unsupported cases were blocked. Scenario-based savings were 149.7-165.5 seconds per case, not observed staff times. The findings support schema-grounded, validation-first healthcare administrative automation in which language-model components assist mapping but do not authorize unsupported or consequential actions.

</details>


### [33] [Escaping Alignment: A Physical Trap Model of Best-of-N Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32116)
*Marco Biroli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了Best-of-N越狱攻击成功率随N呈幂律增长的既有观点，提出一个由四个可解释安全性机制参数决定的障碍模型，用以刻画完整的(N, M)攻击面及其对温度的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作认为Best-of-N越狱的攻击成功率随N呈幂律增长，但作者发现幂律指数会随N漂移，且存在源于对抗数据集有限尺寸效应的指数交叉，说明幂律观点不准确。此外，现有研究较少系统探索完整的双预算(N, M)攻击面及其对生成温度T的依赖。

Method: 提出一个简单的障碍模型（barrier model）：每个提示具有基线安全水平，每个增强对应一个随机热激活障碍。模型由四个数字（每个背后对应一种可解释的安全机制）确定整个(N, M)攻击面。

Result: 该模型能够将预测从N ≤ 100外推到N = 10^4，将五个不同模型坍缩到同一缩放函数上，并能在某温度拟合后预测其他温度下的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: Best-of-N越狱的攻击成功率并非简单幂律，而是可由一个含四个可解释参数的障碍模型统一描述，该模型具备外推能力、跨模型泛化能力以及对温度的预测能力。

Abstract: Best-of-$N$ jailbreaking (BoN) bypasses safeguards of aligned models by drawing $N$ independent augmentations of an unsafe prompt and sampling $M$ completions of each. Previous works have shown that the attack success rate (ASR) seems to follow a power-law in $N$, which we challenge. The exponent drifts with $N$, with an exponential crossover which is a finite-size artifact of the adversarial dataset. Little work has been done to explore the entire two-budget ($N, M$) attack surface as well as its dependence on the generation temperature $T$. We introduce a simple barrier model where each prompt has a baseline safety level and each augmentation a random thermally activated barrier. Then four numbers, each backed by an interpretable safety mechanism, determine the entire ($N, M$) attack surface. They extrapolate predictions from $N \leq 100$ to $N = 10^4$, collapse five distinct models on the same scaling function and predict ASR at different temperatures from the one they were fitted at.

</details>


### [34] [PainterBench: A Figural Divergent-Thinking Benchmark for Tool-Using Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34195)
*Shane K. A. Dalumura Hettige,Jonas Oppenlaender*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PainterBench基准，将人类"不完全绘画任务"移植到智能体设定下，用于评测多模态大模型的图形发散思维能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图形发散思维（把给定形状片段发展为原创绘画）是人类创造力评测的重要维度，但缺乏面向智能体、多轮工具使用的标准化评测。

Method: 智能体通过工具调用在画布上作画，每轮观察结果；画布含不可擦除的起始形状。任务开放式，智能体自行决定何时完成。评测14个多模态语言模型，开展主研究与6项敏感性分析，收集2700幅画作，并对所有画作及300幅人类参考画作进行众包创意性与可识别性评分；同时提出自动评分器ViDrA-adapted。

Result: 图形发散思维在14个模型中差异显著，GPT-6 Astra画作最具创意；相较人类画作，智能体画作创意性更高但可识别性更低；ViDrA-adapted在随机留出测试集上预测人类创意评分的相关系数达r=0.85。

Conclusion: PainterBench可有效评测智能体的增量视觉规划与创造力迁移能力，并公开画作、过程快照、工具调用轨迹、刺激库、评测框架、众包评分（N=72000）及模型权重。

Abstract: Figural divergent thinking is the ability to develop a given shape fragment into an original drawing. In humans, this ability is assessed with incomplete-drawing tasks. We introduce PainterBench, a benchmark that ports the incomplete-drawing task to the agentic setting. The agent draws on a canvas through tool calls and observes the result after every turn. The canvas includes a starting shape which cannot be erased, and the agent's goal is to incorporate this shape into the most original drawing it can produce. The task is open-ended, and the agent itself decides when the drawing is finished. The benchmark tests incremental visual planning over a short horizon and the transfer of creative ability from pretraining to multi-turn tool use. We evaluate 14 multimodal language models from small to frontier scale. Across the primary study and six sensitivity analyses, we collect 2,700 drawings and crowdsource creativity and recognizability ratings for every drawing and for 300 human reference drawings. We also present ViDrA-adapted, an automated scorer that predicts human creativity ratings of agent drawings (r = 0.85 on random held-out test split). Figural divergent thinking varies widely across the 14 models, and GPT-6 Astra produces the most creative drawings. Relative to the human drawings, the agent drawings score higher in creativity but lower in recognizability.
