We release the final drawings, per-round canvas snapshots, tool call traces, stimulus bank, benchmark harness, crowdsourced ratings (N = 72,000), and ViDrA checkpoint.

</details>


### [35] [READ-Bench: Benchmarking Historical Instance Retrieval for Time-Series Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32123)
*Gerardo Pastrana,Haojun Li,Dhruv Mehta,Anoushka Vyas,Sina Khoshfetrat Pakazad,Henrik Ohlsson,John Paparrizos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 READ-Bench，一个面向多元时间序列故障诊断的历史案例检索基准，覆盖12个数据集。研究发现预训练表征在检索上并不显著优于经典与符号基线，而重排序阶段少量已标注案例监督（高斯过程重排序器）是决定性因素；据此提出的融合方法在全部12个数据集上提升NDCG@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列诊断系统很少依赖检索相关历史案例，即便使用检索，也仅通过下游预测间接评估。缺乏对"历史案例检索"本身的直接、标准化评测，因此需要构建一个明确以故障/事件类型定义相关性（而非信号形态）的检索基准。

Method: 构建 READ-Bench：跨12个诊断数据集、以多元时间序列为核心，相关性按共享故障或事件类型定义。将检索建模为"基础检索器+重排序器"两阶段，在统一协议下评估经典距离、符号检索器、自监督与基础模型嵌入器及其融合，以及标签感知重排序器与语言模型重排序器，并变化监督、污染与语料规模进行显著性检验。最终提出将正常残差评分嵌入器与动态时间规整（DTW）分支通过倒数排名融合（RRF）结合，再以高斯过程重排序器重排。

Result: 在通道独立接口下，预训练表征在单独检索上相对强经典与符号基线无统计显著优势。关键因素是在重排序时使用少量已解决案例监督，即高斯过程重排序器在嵌入空间中传播少量近邻标签，其效果远超更复杂的表征或语言模型推理，且在污染条件下与全语料规模下依然成立。最终方法在全部12个数据集上相对其自身检索阶段提升NDCG@10，重排序贡献+0.11，相对最强单一基础检索器提升+0.16。

Conclusion: 有效的时序诊断检索并不取决于更复杂的表征，而取决于在重排序阶段注入少量已解决案例监督；简单的嵌入+DTW融合配合高斯过程重排序即可在全部数据集上稳定提升检索质量。

Abstract: Time-series diagnostic systems rarely rely on retrieving relevant historical cases, and when they do, retrieval is evaluated only indirectly through downstream prediction. We introduce READ-Bench, a benchmark for historical-case retrieval across 12 diagnostic datasets, centered on multivariate time series, that defines relevance by shared fault or event type rather than signal shape, so visually different traces of the same fault count as relevant while similar-looking traces of different faults do not. Treating retrieval as a base retriever followed by a reranker, we evaluate classical distances, symbolic retrievers, self-supervised and foundation-model embedders, and their fusion, plus label-aware and language-model rerankers, under one protocol that varies supervision, pollution, and corpus scale with significance testing. Under a common channel-independent interface, pretrained representations offer no statistically detectable advantage over strong classical and symbolic baselines for search alone. The decisive factor is a small amount of resolved-case supervision at reranking, namely a Gaussian-process reranker that propagates a few neighbor labels in embedding space, which helps far more than more sophisticated representations or language-model reasoning and holds under pollution and at full corpus scale. Guided by these findings, we fuse a normal-residual-scored embedder with a dynamic time warping leg via reciprocal-rank fusion, then rerank with the Gaussian-process reranker, improving NDCG@10 over its own search stage on all 12 datasets, by +0.11 from reranking and +0.16 over the strongest single base retriever.

</details>


### [36] [PastForward: Faster On-Device GUI Agents via Computational Experience Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32166)
*Taehwan Park,Changmin Lee,Hayeon Lee,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PastForward 通过验证的细粒度复用，加速边缘设备上的 GUI 智能体，在保持任务成功率的同时实现 1.63-2.36 倍的动作步延迟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘设备上运行 GUI 智能体可保护敏感屏幕和交互历史，但每步推理的计算成本高，部署困难。现有系统要么每步做完整 VLM 推理，要么复用与先前任务匹配的粗粒度知识，难以在动态移动环境中有效利用历史执行，且需要微调或离线探索。

Method: PastForward 利用日常任务执行中积累的计算经验进行经验证的细粒度复用。解码时，检索先前输出序列作为设备自适应的多 token 提案，并在一次 VLM 前向传播中验证。跨动作步时，利用先前的 GUI 转移在设备执行当前动作时开始下一步推理，仅当预测屏幕与观察到屏幕匹配时才保留早期计算，并向前传递可复用的 KV 状态。

Result: 在基于真实移动使用模式的 AndroidWorld 工作负载上，使用多种 VLM 骨干在服务器和边缘平台评估，设备端实现 1.63-2.36 倍动作步延迟加速，同时维持任务成功率。

Conclusion: PastForward 通过细粒度、经验证的计算经验复用，有效降低了边缘设备上 GUI 智能体的推理延迟，兼顾隐私与性能。

Abstract: Running GUI agents on edge devices can keep sensitive screens and interaction histories local, but the computational cost of inference at every action step makes deployment challenging. Existing GUI agent systems either perform full vision-language model (VLM) inference at each action step or reuse coarse-grained knowledge matched to prior tasks. However, dynamic mobile environments and user tasks make it difficult to fully utilize prior task executions without additional fine-tuning or task-specific offline exploration. To address this challenge, we present PastForward, a system that accelerates GUI agents through validated, fine-grained reuse of computational experience accumulated during ordinary task execution. During decoding, PastForward retrieves prior output sequences as device-adaptive multi-token proposals and verifies them in a single VLM forward pass. Across action steps, it uses prior GUI transitions to begin next-step inference while the device executes the current action, retains the early computation only when the predicted screen matches the observed screen, and carries reusable KV states forward. We evaluate PastForward on AndroidWorld workloads derived from real mobile usage patterns using multiple VLM backbones across server and edge platforms. On device, PastForward achieves action-step latency speedups of 1.63-2.36$\times$ while maintaining task success rates.

</details>


### [37] [Noisy Test-Time Reinforcement Learning for Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32172)
*Xikai Yang,Hieu Trung Nguyen,Dunyuan Xu,Yuzhi Zhao,Jinpeng Li,Wenao Ma,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NTRL-Code框架，利用测试阶段的未标注噪声数据实现代码大模型的鲁棒自进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实用户指令往往模糊且易出错，而现有鲁棒性微调依赖昂贵的成对干净-噪声样本及复杂噪声模拟，限制了代码大模型的鲁棒性提升。

Method: 采用保守自去噪获取更干净的语义锚点进行目标估计；设计基于抽象语法树（AST）的结构聚合机制，从多个候选程序中估计代理目标；并在原始噪声提示上以结合格式有效性、代码相似度与抗重复信号的混合奖励优化策略。

Result: 在三个基准（涵盖字符级、词级、段落级扰动）上的大量实验表明，NTRL-Code带来稳健且一致的性能提升，稳定了多种基座模型的预测。

Conclusion: NTRL-Code仅依赖测试阶段的未标注噪声数据即可提升代码大模型鲁棒性，为噪声场景下的自进化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance across various code-related tasks. However, unlike carefully curated datasets that are typically high-quality and error-free, real-world user instructions are often vague and error-prone, posing significant challenges to the robustness of code LLMs. Furthermore, robustness-oriented fine-tuning relies on paired clean-noisy samples, which are costly to curate and require sophisticated noisy simulation techniques. To address these challenges, we propose the Noisy Test-time Reinforcement Learning framework (NTRL-Code), which enables robust self-evolution of code LLMs using only unlabeled noisy data during the testing stage. Specifically, NTRL-Code uses conservative self-denoising to obtain a cleaner semantic anchor for target estimation, and employs an abstract-syntax-tree (AST)-based structural aggregation mechanism to estimate a proxy target from multiple candidate programs. The policy is then optimized on the original noisy prompts with a hybrid reward that combines format validity, code similarity, and anti-repetition signals. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks, each incorporating character-level, word-level, and paragraph-level perturbations, demonstrate that NTRL-Code yields robust and consistent improvements, stabilizing the predictions of various base models. Our code is available at https://github.com/Xikai97/NTRL-Code.

</details>


### [38] [AI Harness: Certification under Proposal-Conditioned Information for Foundation-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32184)
*Hailin Zhong,Shengxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文刻画了"提案—历史"相关性在模型—工具边界上对认证的必要性：将历史条件化的提案过程坍缩为仅依赖状态的模型，会保覆盖率但破坏可认证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署中的基础模型智能体通常只在模型输出语义提案后才被运行时介入，此时提案既是动作候选，又是历史条件过程生成的决定时观测。既有做法常把这一结构坍缩为仅依赖状态的提案包络，作者希望弄清这种坍缩何时无害、何时致命。

Method: 在有限鲁棒接口下比较坍缩模型的生存核与历史增广核的物理投影；以"每个提案条件化的坍缩纤维是否保有共同的鲁棒安全介入"作为无损判据；用精确有限信念与标准安全/可达不动点把单步结论推广到时序，并进行受控的模型在环测试。

Result: 坍缩模型的生存核包含于历史增广核的物理投影中，且坍缩无损当且仅当共同动作条件成立；即使提案与历史字母表为常数规模，该间隙也可达到最大。同一条件给出对偶结果：观测当前提案可在其区分需要不兼容介入的潜在模式时恢复鲁棒可行性。时序上区分了无限期运行可行性与有限最坏情况已验证进展。

Conclusion: 贡献不是一个全新的不动点演算，而是给出认证中提案—历史相关性何时必要、何时可安全忽略的刻画；移除遥测或效果验证、或限制介入权限时，模型在环测试复现了预测的障碍。

Abstract: Foundation-model agents are often modeled as policies over an observed state. In deployed systems, however, a runtime may intervene only after the model has emitted a semantic proposal, making the proposal both an action candidate and a decision-time observation generated by a history-conditioned process. We show that collapsing this structure into a state-only proposal envelope can preserve proposal coverage while destroying certifiability. In a finite robust interface, the viability kernel of the collapsed model is contained in the physical projection of the history-augmented kernel, and the collapse is lossless exactly when every proposal-conditioned collapsed fiber retains a common robust-safe intervention. This gap can be maximal even with constant-size proposal and history alphabets. The same common-action condition yields a dual result: observing the current proposal can restore robust feasibility when it separates latent modes requiring incompatible interventions. We extend these one-step results over time using exact finite beliefs and standard safety and reachability fixed points, separating indefinite operational viability from finite worst-case verified progress. Controlled model-in-the-loop tests reproduce the predicted obstructions when telemetry or effect verification is removed or intervention authority is restricted. Thus, our contribution is not a new fixed-point calculus, but a characterization of when proposal--history correlation at the model--tool boundary is necessary for certification.

</details>


### [39] [CoMemBench: Benchmarking Collaborative Memory Boundaries across Multi-Agent Workflow Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32192)
*Sen Zhao,Ruiqi Kong,Zuyu Zhang,Lifeng Shen,Xinyu He,Ding Zou,Xu Zhang,Qinghua Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CoMemBench，一个基于执行的多智能体协作记忆基准，用于衡量工作流拓扑条件下的信息共享与隔离边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体工作流需要跨智能体共享与任务相关的信息，同时隔离无关、过时、未验证或不兼容的信息，这种“任务条件下的信息范围”即协作记忆边界。现有记忆基准主要评估保持与检索，多智能体基准侧重协调与端到端完成，拓扑条件下的记忆边界基本未被测量。

Method: 从有源依据的依赖图构建跨四个领域的 800 个复合工作流，包含节点局部规范、可验证的产物交接、原生评估器和配对的隔离挑战；并设计完成度、验证节点进展、必需交接可靠性、隔离鲁棒性和 token 成本等指标。

Result: 实验揭示了共享—隔离权衡：更宽的上下文提升信息可用性，但可能削弱隔离；系统排名在不同拓扑和产物违规情形下发生变化。

Conclusion: CoMemBench 填补了拓扑条件下协作记忆边界的评测空白，表明共享与隔离之间存在结构性张力，需针对不同拓扑进行权衡设计。

Abstract: Multi-agent workflows require task-relevant information to be shared across agents, while irrelevant, stale, unverified, or incompatible information must remain isolated. We call this task-conditioned scope of information a collaborative memory boundary. Workflow topology determines which intermediate artifacts are applicable to which downstream workers and when they cease to be valid, thereby providing a structural stress dimension for sharing and isolation. Existing memory benchmarks primarily evaluate retention and retrieval, whereas multi-agent benchmarks emphasize coordination and end-to-end completion, leaving topology-conditioned memory boundaries largely unmeasured. We introduce CoMemBench, an execution-grounded benchmark for collaborative memory sharing and isolation across multi-agent workflow topologies. It constructs 800 composite workflows across four domains from source-grounded dependency graphs, with node-local specifications, verifiable artifact handoffs, native evaluators, and matched isolation challenges. CoMemBench measures workflow completion, verified node progress, required-handoff reliability, isolation robustness, and token cost. Experiments reveal a sharing-isolation trade-off: broader context improves information availability but can weaken isolation, while system rankings shift across topologies and artifact violations.

</details>


### [40] [Instruct, Not Answer: Using Instruction Privileges in On-Policy Context Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32201)
*Hantao Yu,Sandy Han,Udaya Ghai,Ferhat Erata,Joe Lilien,Aman Goel,Ali Torkamani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在按策略上下文蒸馏（OPCD）中，使用针对学生常见错误设计的通用指令作为特权信息，比实例级金标准答案更具可迁移性，能在分布外（OOD）场景下大幅提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OPCD 研究多默认使用实例级金标准答案或金标准示范作为特权信息，但这类特权会损害训练性能，尤其在分布外（OOD）场景下表现不佳。作者希望找到一种更可迁移的特权信息形式来替代实例级金标准。

Method: 作者设计针对训练样本中学生常见错误的通用指令作为特权信息，替代实例级金标准。在 OPCD 框架下，教师模型以该通用指令为条件，最小化特权教师与学生模型在学生对生成 token 上的 KL 散度。每条指令仅几句话，且对所有训练样本统一应用。

Result: 在自动形式化任务中，匹配的格式指令作为特权在 OOD 准确率上大幅超越金标准。在使用 ProverQA、ProofWriter、ProntoQA 数据集，以及 Qwen3-Thinking 和 Olmo3-Thinking 系列模型的 8 组实验中，有 7 组里匹配指令特权在 OOD 上比金标准高出 4 至 17 个点，同时在域内性能与金标准持平。

Conclusion: 通用指令在源域和目标域样本上同等适用，比实例级金标准在 OPCD 中更具可迁移性，同时能保持域内性能。这为 OPCD 中特权信息的设计提供了新思路，即用简洁通用指令替代信息量更大的实例级金标准。

Abstract: On-Policy Context Distillation (OPCD) has recently emerged as a powerful technique for transferring context to student models and for self-improvement. In OPCD, the teacher is conditioned on privileged information, and the goal is to minimize the Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence between the privileged teacher and the student, evaluated on student-generated tokens. Many existing studies show that using instance-specific gold answers or gold demonstrations as the default privilege can hurt training performance, especially out-of-distribution (OOD). In this work, we instead design general instructions that target common student mistakes observed on the training samples, and show that such simple instructions can outperform gold as the OPCD privilege. In autoformalization tasks, using a matched formatting instruction as the privilege could outperform gold in OOD accuracy by a large margin. In 7 out of 8 experiments using ProverQA, ProofWriter, and ProntoQA as datasets, and Qwen3-Thinking and Olmo3-Thinking families as models, matched instruction privileges outperform gold in OOD by 4 to 17 points, while remaining on par with gold in-domain. Each instruction is only a few sentences (and thus contains much less information compared to all instance-specific gold) and is applied uniformly to every training sample. These results indicate that a general instruction, which applies equally to source and target domain examples, can be substantially more transferable than instance-specific gold in OPCD while maintaining in-domain performance.

</details>


### [41] [Witness: Discovery, Deciphering, and Epiphany in Interactive Puzzle Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32208)
*Guanghan Ning,Ping Liu,Linyi Li,Huangjie Zheng,Arjun Neervannan,Huu Nguyen,Michael Sklar,Deniz Zorlu,Nicolai Ouporov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出WITNESS环境与WitnessBench基准，研究语言模型在隐藏规则发现类交互谜题上的能力上限，以及强化学习能否提升其在未见规则上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化科学需要智能体通过与环境交互来推断陌生环境的规则。交互式规则发现谜题为此提供受控研究场景：智能体需通过实验推断隐藏规则，并利用推断结果达成目标。论文关注两个问题：当前语言模型在此类谜题上的瓶颈是什么，以及强化学习能否提升其在新规则上的表现。

Method: 提出WITNESS：一个2D网格谜题环境，具有真值ASCII观测和对规则的受控访问。通过智能体流水线生成游戏，构成强化学习训练套件WitnessGym，以及包含公开验证集与私有测试集的WitnessBench。验证集分别测试已训练规则原语的新组合，以及训练中未出现的原语。在统一测试框架下评估18个前沿专有及开放权重模型，并进行强化学习训练实验。

Result: 在统一框架下，18个前沿模型中最佳者仅解出私有测试关卡槽位的24%，得分对观测接口与智能体配置敏感。提供真值规则后，Opus-5的验证集RHAE-L5（前五关相对人类动作效率）从59.9提升至97.8；而27B开放权重模型仅提升2.1分，即使给出规则仍表现受限。在WitnessGym上进行强化学习使27B模型的私有测试RHAE-L5从2.1升至5.4，并在四个外部发现类基准上平均提升4.1分。

Conclusion: 规则获取是Opus-5等前沿模型的主要难点，而较小模型在基于规则的执行上进一步受限；在隐藏规则谜题上进行强化学习可迁移到更广泛的规则任务和训练之外的现实任务。基准已公开于 https://witnessbench.ai 。

Abstract: Automated science needs agents that can work out the rules of an unfamiliar environment by interacting with it. Interactive rule-discovery puzzles offer a controlled setting for studying this ability: an agent infers hidden rules through experimentation and uses what it has inferred to reach a stated goal. We ask what limits current language models on these puzzles and whether reinforcement learning (RL) improves performance on rules held out from training. To study both, we introduce WITNESS, a 2D grid-based puzzle environment with ground-truth ASCII observations and controlled access to rules. An agentic pipeline generates games for WitnessGym, the RL training suite, and WitnessBench, comprising public validation and private test games. The validation set separately tests new compositions of trained rule primitives and primitives absent from training. Under a shared harness, the best of 18 frontier proprietary and open-weight models solves only 24\% of private test level slots, with scores sensitive to the observation interface and agent configuration. Providing ground-truth rules raises Opus-5's validation RHAE-L5 (relative human action efficiency over the first five levels) from 59.9 to 97.8, whereas a 27B open-weight model gains only 2.1 points and remains limited even with the rules provided. RL on WitnessGym raises the 27B model's private test RHAE-L5 from 2.1 to 5.4 and yields a mean gain of 4.1 points on four external discovery benchmarks. Together, these results point to rule acquisition as a major difficulty for frontier models like Opus-5 while smaller models further struggle on rule-based execution, and indicate that RL on hidden-rule puzzles transfers to broader rules and real-world tasks beyond training. Benchmark is available at: https://witnessbench.ai

</details>


### [42] [Fracast-0: Fractal Weight Sharing for a Time Series Foundation Model with Only 85K Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32209)
*Tianxiang Zhan,Huanyao Zhang,Yuanpeng He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Fracast-0，一种利用时间自相似性跨尺度复用同一算子的概率预测基础模型，仅 85,001 参数即可实现多域、多尺度概率预测，并在 GIFT-Eval 上取得最小参数量与较优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型需同时保持多域广度、概率输出与多时间尺度建模能力，但若每个尺度使用独立表示，参数量会随之膨胀，因此需要一种能在多尺度下压缩参数的建模方式。

Method: 利用时间自相似性在多个尺度间复用同一算子：使用无参数探测器提取显著季节结构；编码器在几何膨胀阶梯上应用共享局部块并进行尺度条件化；解码器将上下文聚合状态与显式季节未来状态结合，并在另一阶梯上复用第二个块，最终输出九个分位数；在六个语料库上进行预训练。

Result: 在 85,001 参数内保持多域广度；在 97 个 GIFT-Eval 配置上无需逐数据集微调，成为 28 个评估检查点中参数量最小者，并在参数量-精度聚合平面上保持非支配，MASE 为 0.808，WQL 为 0.564；相比 TinyCast 参数量减少 42.0%，而 TinyCast 的 MASE 和 WQL 分别低 4.2% 和 3.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，跨尺度权重复用是进一步压缩时间序列基础模型的可行路径。

Abstract: Time series foundation models must preserve multi-domain breadth, probabilistic output, and multiple temporal scales, but parameter count grows when each scale receives a separate representation. We introduce Fracast-0, a probabilistic forecasting foundation model that exploits temporal self-similarity to reuse one operator across scales. A parameter-free detector extracts significant seasonal structure. The encoder applies a shared local block along a geometric dilation ladder with scale conditioning, while the decoder combines context-gathered states with an explicit seasonal future state and reuses a second block along another ladder before emitting nine quantiles. Pretraining across six corpora preserves multi-domain breadth within 85,001 parameters. On 97 GIFT-Eval configurations without per-dataset fine-tuning, Fracast-0 is the smallest of 28 evaluated checkpoints and remains non-dominated in the aggregate parameter-accuracy plane with MASE 0.808 and WQL 0.564. It uses 42.0% fewer parameters than TinyCast, whose MASE and WQL are 4.2% and 3.3% lower. These results support cross-scale weight reuse as a practical route to further time series foundation model compression.

</details>


### [43] [A bilingual AI audiologist built through rubric-guided playbook induction outperforms human audiologists in a blinded evaluation of simulated cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32220)
*Linkai Li,Changgeng Mo,Hanlin Yu,Congxi Lu,Shangqiguo Wang,Matthew B Fitzgerald,Shan X Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双语AI听力学家系统，通过通用大模型结合评分表引导的策略归纳、多模态听力图解读与检索增强，无需微调主干模型，在58例模拟病例上全面超越17名执业听力学家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 听力学会诊需要结构化病史采集、听力图解读与以患者为中心的沟通，但真实病例材料稀缺，缺乏可用于训练与评估的资源。

Method: 将通用大语言模型与评分表引导的playbook归纳、多模态听力图解读、检索增强接地相结合，不微调语言模型主干；基于21项评分表和AI患者模拟器，从73个训练病例（43英文、30中文）中归纳出19条会诊策略，并在58个独立模拟病例（30中文、28英文）上进行预设的、来源盲法对比。

Result: AI听力学家在每一个病例上均优于人类听力学家（58/58；5分制综合评分平均配对Δ=+1.35，Cohen's d=1.84，P=4.5×10⁻²⁰），在21项评分指标中的20项及两种语言上均胜出；组件消融显示playbook贡献最大。

Conclusion: 该系统为低数据医学领域的专科会诊智能体提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Audiology consultation requires structured history-taking, audiometric interpretation and patient-centred communication, yet real-world case material is scarce. We present a bilingual AI audiologist pairing a general-purpose large language model with rubric-guided playbook induction, multimodal audiogram interpretation and retrieval-augmented grounding, without fine-tuning the language-model backbone. Using a 21-item rubric and an AI patient simulator, we induced a 19-rule consultation policy from 73 training cases (43 English, 30 Chinese) and evaluated the system on 58 independent simulated cases (30 Chinese, 28 English) in a pre-specified, source-blinded comparison with 17 practising audiologists. The AI audiologist outperformed human audiologists on every case (58/58; mean paired $Δ$ = +1.35 on a 5-point composite, Cohen's d = 1.84, $P = 4.5 \times 10^{-20}$), on 20 of 21 rubric items and in both languages. Component ablation identified the playbook as the largest contributor, offering a practical route to specialist consultation agents in low-data medical domains.

</details>


### [44] [RAO-Nav: Probing Omni-Language Models for Zero-shot Semantic Audio-Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32224)
*Qilang Ye,Meng Liu,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RAO-Nav，一种将全模态语言模型直接用于零样本语义视听导航（SAVN）的部署框架，并辅以测试时潜空间导航推理模块LNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专用模型即使经过大量任务训练，仍难以实现通用的多模态导航，作者探究OLM能否直接胜任零样本SAVN。

Method: 构建RAO-Nav部署流程，利用OLM中隐含的视听知识实现"听、看、推理、行动"；并提出可嵌入解码空间的测试时潜空间导航推理（LNR）模块以激发模型内在思维。

Result: 在公开SAVN基准上，无需任何训练数据即超越现有最先进基线；同时提出新的全局导航指令设定来评估OLM作为具身导航智能体的能力。

Conclusion: OLM可被直接用于零样本SAVN并取得领先表现，LNR有助于提升目标相关观测检索与导航决策，具备成为具身导航智能体的潜力。

Abstract: We explore whether Omni-Language Models (OLMs) can be directly applied to zero-shot Semantic Audio-Visual Navigation (SAVN). Recent work demonstrates that even state-of-the-art specialized models still struggle to achieve generalist multimodal navigation, despite extensive task-specific training. In this paper, we introduce RAO-Nav, short for Reasoning All-in-One OLM, a deployment pipeline for zero-shot SAVN. By leveraging the rich implicit audio-visual knowledge encoded in OLMs, the embodied agent is enabled to ``hear'', ``see'', ``reason'', and ``act'' in the environment. To further elicit the built-in thinking ability of OLMs, we propose a test-time Latent Navigation Reasoning (LNR) module that can be seamlessly integrated into the decoding space. LNR encourages the model to retrieve more target-relevant observations and make effective navigation decisions. Through comprehensive experiments, we show that our framework surpasses existing state-of-the-art baselines on public SAVN benchmarks without using any training data. Moreover, we introduce a new \emph{Global Navigation Instruction} setting to further evaluate the ability of OLMs to serve as embodied navigation agents. Code: https://github.com/rikeilong/OmniAV\_Nav.

</details>


### [45] [Certifying Interventional Agreement Among Observationally Equivalent Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32247)
*Sourena Khanzadeh,Daniel Platnick,Marjan Alirezaie,Hossein Rahnama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Interventional Separation Selection (ISS)，通过可容许干预反复查询真实系统并淘汰与结果矛盾的候选模型，直到在给定成本界限内无干预可区分剩余候选，从而对连续变量上的因果抽象提供精确且可验证的停止条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测等价模型在干预下可能分歧，因为干预会产生观测数据中从未出现的输入，观测学习无法给出干预一致性的保证，因此需要一个无需知道真值即可验证的干预选择方法。

Method: ISS 反复用可容许干预查询真实系统，淘汰被结果否定的候选项，成本界限内无可分干预即停止；对连续变量的无限版本空间，用混合整数线性规划精确判定停止条件，一致性到容差范围。

Result: 在三位数字彩色 MNIST 因果抽象任务上，普通卷积网络达到零留出误差，但与基于形状的标签在单数字编辑上 26% 分歧，与基于色相的标签一样频繁；ISS 平均每图像 13.6 次干预即可认证每个网络所感知的抽象，证书在所有可容许干预上有效。

Conclusion: ISS 能提供观测学习无法给出的干预一致性保证；但当网络绕过所有候选抽象依赖的单元时，针对该单元的干预证书可能悄然失效，二十次随机验证干预可反驳 69% 的此类证书。

Abstract: Observationally equivalent causal models can still disagree about what happens under intervention, because interventions create inputs that never occur in observational data. We introduce Interventional Separation Selection (ISS), which repeatedly queries the true system with an admissible intervention on which the surviving candidate models disagree, discards the candidates the outcome contradicts, and stops once no intervention within a cost bound separates the survivors. If the true system is among the candidates, this stopping condition certifies that every survivor agrees with it on every admissible intervention within the bound, a guarantee that no observational learner can give, however much data it sees. The stopping condition depends only on the survivors, so it can be checked without knowing the truth. For continuous variables the candidates form an infinite version space, and mixed-integer linear programs decide the stopping condition exactly over all of it, with agreement holding up to a tolerance. On a three-digit colored MNIST causal abstraction task in which ink hue tracks digit size, plain convolutional networks trained on examples reach zero held-out error, yet disagree with shape-based labels on 26% of single-digit edits, as often as hue-based labels do. Auditing the causal abstractions of networks observed only on such images, ISS certifies what each network perceives with 13.6 interventions per image on average, and each certificate, checked against every admissible intervention, holds whenever the network's true abstraction is among the candidates. When a network bypasses a unit that every candidate abstraction relies on, certificates covering interventions on that unit can be silently void, and twenty random validation interventions refute 69% of them.

</details>


### [46] [Why Directly Learning Periodic Trajectories Can Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32254)
*Kaixin Zheng,Anita Layton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 周期性解算子学习中的数据记录与表示方式会影响可学习性：不对齐轨迹泛化差，相位对齐改善同分布泛化但固定时间窗对超范围周期仍有大误差；作者用"频率差随时间累积导致目标相位随参数快速变化"统一解释，并提出解耦学习对齐归一化波形与周期的表示来规避误差下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性解算子学习需要决定仿真数据如何记录与表示。直觉上"积分足够长使瞬态衰减、窗口足够宽包含至少一个完整周期"的保守做法，却可能让轨迹变得难以学习，即使底层周期轨道随系统参数规则变化。作者要探究这种困难的根本机制并寻找可行表示。

Method: 作者分析两类失败（不对齐轨迹的泛化差、相位对齐但固定物理时间窗对训练范围外周期的误差），提出共同机制：频率差随时间累积，使目标相位随参数快速变化；无法跟踪该变化的预测器会产生总体MSE下限，并针对固定窗口预测推导逐样本下界。随后研究一种简单表示：分别学习对齐归一化波形与其周期，在ODE假设下建立解耦目标的正则性，并在ODE系统与一个PDE案例中实验验证。

Result: 不对齐轨迹即便在训练分布内也泛化很差；相位对齐显著改善同分布泛化，但固定物理时间窗训练的模型对训练范围外周期的轨迹误差仍很大。上述失败可由频率差累积机制统一解释，并给出相应的MSE下限（含固定窗口的逐样本下界）。解耦学习对齐归一化波形与周期的方法在实验中避免了这两类失败。

Conclusion: 周期性解的算子学习不应盲目采用"长积分+宽窗口"的保守数据记录方式；分离并分别学习相位对齐的归一化波形与周期，可在ODE系统及PDE案例中同时规避泛化差与超范围周期误差，为周期解学习提供了更合适的表示。

Abstract: Operator learning of periodic solutions requires deciding how simulation data should be recorded and represented. A natural choice is to integrate long enough for transients to decay and record a window wide enough to contain at least one full period of all trajectories. We find that these conservative choices can make the resulting trajectories difficult to learn, even when the underlying periodic orbits vary regularly with system parameters. Unaligned trajectories generalize poorly even within the training distribution. Phase alignment substantially improves in-distribution generalization, but models trained on a fixed physical-time window still have large errors on trajectories with periods outside the training range. We explain both failures through a common mechanism: frequency differences accumulate over time, so the target phase varies rapidly with the parameters. Predictors that cannot track this variation incur a population MSE floor in both settings; for fixed window prediction, we also derive a per-sample lower bound. We then study one of the simplest representations that escape these floors: learning an aligned, normalized waveform and its period separately. We establish regularity of the decoupled targets under ODE assumptions and show experimentally that this approach avoids both failures in ODE systems and a PDE case study.

</details>


### [47] [Clarify the User or Verify the World? Uncertainty Routing for Proactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32255)
*Zhaofeng Li,Xuan Zhang,Xiaokui Xiao,Yang Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PROUR框架，将LLM智能体的主动信息获取问题形式化为在ACT、CLARIFY、VERIFY三种动作间的不确定性路由，通过分解不确定性信号并训练带模式条件信息增益奖励的查询生成器，在τ-bench上取得平均28.17%成功率，超越最强基线4.57%且减少2.17步交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动式方法通常只专注于用户澄清或环境验证中的一种，未能在每个决策点上明确判断应使用哪种信息源来消解不确定性，导致信息获取方式与不确定性来源不匹配。

Method: 将问题形式化为ACT、CLARIFY、VERIFY之间的不确定性路由；把动作不确定性分解为两个信号：跨多种合理解读的分歧（指示用户侧歧义，触发CLARIFY）与每种解读内部的残余熵（指示世界侧证据缺失，触发VERIFY）。查询生成器采用模式条件的信息增益奖励训练，CLARIFY面向用户目标识别，VERIFY面向下一步动作识别。

Result: 在τ-bench的零售与航空任务上平均成功率28.17%，较最强先前方法提升4.57%，交互步数减少2.17步；所学策略无需再训练即可泛化到更强的任务智能体及τ³-bench的事务型领域。

Conclusion: 面向信息源对齐的不确定性消解对主动式智能体具有明显收益，能有效提升任务成功率并减少交互开销，且具备跨智能体与跨领域的泛化能力。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents must decide not only whether additional information is needed, but also which source can resolve the uncertainty. Existing proactive approaches often specialize in either user clarification or environment verification, without explicitly determining the appropriate information source for each decision. We formulate this problem as uncertainty routing among ACT, CLARIFY, and VERIFY, and propose PROUR, a proactive uncertainty routing framework. PROUR decomposes action uncertainty into disagreement across plausible user-goal interpretations, which signals user-side ambiguity, and the entropy remaining within each interpretation, which signals missing world-side evidence. To acquire information from the routed source, a query generator is trained with a mode-conditioned information-gain reward, targeting user-goal identification under CLARIFY and next-action identification under VERIFY. On $τ$-bench, PROUR achieves 28.17% average success rate across retail and airline, outperforming the strongest prior method by 4.57% while using 2.17 fewer interaction steps. The learned policy further generalizes to stronger task agents and transactional domains of $τ^3$-bench without retraining, demonstrating the benefit of source-aligned uncertainty resolution for proactive agents.

</details>


### [48] [LAM: Efficient Lossy Agent Memory Framework With A Retrieval-Score Error Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32256)
*Baixi Sun,Le Chen,Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury,Xiaolong Ma,Chih-Hsuan Yang,Mingze Xia,Syed Zawad,Sheng Di,Rajkumar Kettimuthu,Huihuo Zheng,Rajeev Thakur,Venkatram Vishwanath,Feng Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LAM（Lossy Agent Memory）系统，通过确定性去重规则、保留缓存前缀的内存管理与成本预测模型，在减少22.47%观测token的同时保留99.984%证据，并借助调度方式实现71.4x-91.6x的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent记忆随读取输入、推理和工具调用不断增长，导致推理成本上升并最终超出上下文窗口。基于LLM的摘要方法虽能压缩历史，却增加延迟，且无法对信息损失给出显式上界。

Method: LAM由三部分组成：一是带替换界的确定性去重规则（该界约束检索分数的扰动，而非排名不变的保证）；二是保留缓存前缀、并将压缩与推理重叠执行的内存管理器；三是可在部署前估算压缩成本的性能模型。

Result: 在600条agent轨迹上，LAM移除22.47%的观测token，同时保留99.984%已测量的gold-patch证据。在固定删除集下，性能模型预测：在预填充前移除记录相较从已预填充上下文中删除记录，可获得71.4x-91.6x的端到端加速。

Conclusion: 该加速收益来自调度策略而非删除规则本身，且适用于任何保留前缀的测试方案。

Abstract: Agent memory grows as agents read inputs, reason, and call tools. Longer histories increase inference cost and eventually exceed the context window. LLM-based summarization reduces this history but adds latency and provides no explicit bound on information loss. We propose LAM, a Lossy Agent Memory system with three components: a deterministic deduplication rule with a substitution bound on retrieval scores - a bound on score perturbation, not a certificate of unchanged ranking; a memory manager that preserves the cached prefix and overlaps compaction with inference; and a performance model that estimates compaction costs before deployment. On 600 agent trajectories, LAM removes 22.47% of observation tokens while retaining 99.984% of the measured gold-patch evidence. At a fixed deletion set, the performance model predicts a 71.4x-91.6x end-to-end speedup from removing records before prefill instead of deleting them from a prefilled context. That benefit comes from the schedule rather than the rule and applies to any prefix-preserving test.

</details>


### [49] [Prefill-Free Cross-Family KV Cache Transfer for Heterogeneous Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32259)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Woosang Lim,Haneul Yoo,Sungjoo Yoo,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy,Murali Annavaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 HeteroFold，一种免预填充的跨模型家族 KV 缓存传输方法，在保持发送方和接收方冻结的前提下，实现异构多智能体 LLM 系统的高效通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体 LLM 系统常组合异构模型扮演不同角色，但基于文本的通信要求每个接收方重新预填充发送方已处理过的共享上下文，造成冗余计算。复用发送方的 KV 缓存可避免这种冗余，但跨模型家族的免预填充传输需解决分词、模型深度和 KV 表示的差异。

Method: HeteroFold 保持发送方与接收方均冻结，通过三步实现传输：对齐模型结构、将发送方缓存映射到接收方空间、并进行校准以保持接收方行为。

Result: 在六个传输方向上，HeteroFold 在四个长上下文基准上均取得最佳缓存传输性能，并在多数短上下文设置中领先；在多智能体基准上匹配文本通信效果。32K 上下文下，Llama-3.1-8B→Ministral-3-14B 传输比 Native Prefill 快 10.7 倍，比最先进的免预填充基线 Dense Latent 和 KV Ridge 快 1.18–1.47 倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明 HeteroFold 能在无需接收方预填充的情况下实现高效的跨家族 KV 复用。

Abstract: Recent multi-agent LLM systems increasingly combine heterogeneous models for specialized agent roles. However, text-based communication requires each receiver to prefill shared context already processed by the sender. Reusing the sender's key-value (KV) cache avoids this redundancy, but prefill-free transfer across model families must handle differences in tokenization, model depth, and KV representations. To address these issues, we propose \textit{HeteroFold}, a prefill-free cross-family KV cache transfer method that keeps both the sender and receiver frozen. HeteroFold aligns model structures, maps the sender cache into the receiver space, and calibrates it to preserve receiver behavior. Across six transfer directions, HeteroFold achieves the best cache-transfer performance on all four long-context benchmarks and most short-context settings. It also matches text-based communication on the multi-agent benchmark. At 32K context length, Llama-3.1-8B$\rightarrow$Ministral-3-14B transfer is $10.7\times$ faster than Native Prefill and $1.18$--$1.47\times$ faster than the state-of-the-art prefill-free baselines, Dense Latent and KV Ridge. These results show that HeteroFold enables efficient cross-family KV reuse without receiver prefill.

</details>


### [50] [When Does a Skill Add Value? Task-Conditional Gain Prediction for Selective Skill Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32274)
*Anjie Xu,Zhiyu Zhang,Ruiqing Ding,Fengli Xu,Leye Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SkillDelta 框架，通过同一智能体在有/无技能下的配对执行历史，预测任务条件下的技能增益，从而在行动前决定是否启用技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体技能常无收益甚至损害性能并增加 token 成本，因此需要在执行前预测某技能对该任务是否有帮助。

Method: 基于同一智能体带技能与不带技能的配对执行构建 SkillDelta；用局部预测器将历史增益迁移到新任务，无需重训智能体；并从支持覆盖、表示失配与执行噪声三方面分析其对预测误差和决策遗憾的影响。

Result: 在五个基准、三个目标智能体上，配对历史在 15 个设置中的 12 个里提升了对观测增益的排序；在相同期望技能使用率下，15 个设置全部优于随机激活，平均绝对成功率提升 4.3%。优势主要来自任务组间分配，组内选择价值在 ToolQA 上最明显。

Conclusion: 配对历史可用于任务条件的技能增益预测，按预期使用率分配技能能稳定提升成功率，但组内细粒度选择收益有限。

Abstract: Agent skills are expected to improve task performance. Yet we find that they often provide no benefit, and can even hurt performance while incurring additional token costs. Can we predict whether a skill will help before the agent acts? We introduce SkillDelta, a framework for predicting task-conditional skill gains from paired executions of the same agent with and without the skill. A local predictor transfers these historical gains to new tasks without retraining the agent. Under explicit transfer assumptions, our analysis links support coverage, representation mismatch, and execution noise to prediction error and decision regret. Across five benchmarks and three target agents, paired history improves observed-gain ranking over skill-assisted outcomes alone in 12 of 15 settings. At matched expected skill-use rates, SkillDelta improves success over random activation in all 15 settings, with an average absolute gain of 4.3%. Most of this advantage comes from allocation across task groups. Evidence for additional within-group selection value is strongest on ToolQA and weaker elsewhere. Code is available at https://github.com/TankTechnology/skilldelta.

</details>


### [51] [GLIDE: Generalized Layer-wise Intrinsic Distributional Evaluation for Heterogeneous LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32295)
*Wei Zhu,Yiming Wang,Rui Wang,Lixing Yu,Kun Yue,Zhiwen Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GLIDE：面向LLM智能体的广义逐层内在分布评估方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体需要可靠的步级评估来比较候选分支并有效分配计算。但轻量评估存在困难：外部验证器带来额外推理成本，智能体自评分数可能校准不准，尤其在异构智能体生成候选时更明显。

Method: 提出GLIDE，从逐层残差一致性中提取内在步级证据，衡量局部残差更新是否持续支持候选步引起的全局残差变化；将该证据对照生成智能体近期分数分布进行校准，转化为悲观奖励，兼顾绝对残差证据与智能体相对位置；奖励为MCTS分支选择提供跨智能体价值信号，归一化预测不确定性引导自适应分支。

Result: 在多跳推理、序列决策和符号逻辑实验中，GLIDE提升了任务性能、步级排序质量和计算效率，且无需外部验证器或任务特定监督。

Conclusion: GLIDE为LLM智能体提供了一种无需外部验证器的轻量步级评估方案，可有效支持分支选择与计算分配。

Abstract: LLM agents require reliable step-level evaluation to compare candidate branches and allocate computation effectively. However, lightweight evaluation remains challenging. External verifiers introduce additional inference cost, while agent-produced confidence or self-evaluation scores can be miscalibrated, especially when candidates are generated by heterogeneous agents. We propose \textbf{G}eneralized \textbf{L}ayer-wise \textbf{I}ntrinsic \textbf{D}istributional \textbf{E}valuation (\textbf{GLIDE}) for LLM agents. \textsc{GLIDE} derives intrinsic step evidence from layer-wise residual coherence, which measures whether local residual updates consistently support the global residual change induced by a candidate step. It calibrates this evidence against the recent score distribution of the generating agent and converts it into a pessimistic reward that jointly accounts for absolute residual evidence and agent-relative standing. The reward provides a cross-agent value signal for MCTS branch selection, while normalized predictive uncertainty guides adaptive branching. Experiments on multi-hop reasoning, sequential decision making, and symbolic logic show that \textsc{GLIDE} improves task performance, step-level ranking quality, and computational efficiency without external verifiers or task-specific supervision.

</details>


### [52] [Agentsensus: Consensus-Compressed Shared Memory for Multi-Agent Story Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32297)
*Yu Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Agentsensus，一个采用统一长期记忆的故事世界模拟框架，将同一事件的记录合并为所有见证者共有，并链接语义相关记忆，从而显著降低存储冗余、实现记忆共享与关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准智能体故事世界设计为每个角色分配私有记忆流，导致同一共享事件被每位见证者各存一份，产生大量存储重复。

Method: Agentsensus 采用统一长期记忆：同一事件的记录合并为所有见证者共同拥有，并将语义相关的记忆记录相互链接。在四个世界（两部中国古典小说、哈姆雷特、一个真实世界冲突时间线）上，以等粒度协议运行 40 至 80 轮，与三种逐角色记忆设计对比。

Result: 相比最接近的基线，Agentsensus 写入条目少 22–44%；是唯一实现记忆共享（14–28% 的记录被多个角色持有，部分被 10 个角色持有）与关联（94–99%）的设计，且模拟质量评判与基线相当甚至更好。消融实验表明合并是关键：禁用合并使存储膨胀 3.1 倍，共享率降为零。共享随模拟时长累积而非早饱和，在 10、20、40 轮时由 6% 升至 9% 再到 14%。

Conclusion: 统一且可合并、可链接的记忆结构能有效消除故事世界模拟中的记忆重复，在提升存储效率的同时带来自然的记忆共享与关联，且不损害模拟质量，其收益随模拟时长持续增长。

Abstract: A agentic story world is a dynamic system simulating who learned what, when, and from whom -- yet the standard design gives each character a private memory stream. A shared event is therefore stored once per witness, large duplication will be incurred in terms of storage. We present Agentsensus, a story-world simulation framework in which there is an unified long-term memory. Records of the same event merge into one owned by all its witnesses, and semantically relevant memory records are linked. We evaluate on four worlds -- two classical Chinese novels, Hamlet, and a real-world conflict timeline -- run for 40 to 80 rounds against three per-character memory designs under an equal-granularity protocol. Agentsensus writes 22-44% fewer entries than the closest baseline and is the only design whose memory becomes shared (14-28% of records held by more than one character, some by 10) and linked (94-99%), at judged simulation quality indistinguishable or even better than the baselines. An ablation attributes this to the merge itself: disabling it multiplies the store by 3.1x and takes sharing to exactly zero. Sharing also compounds with the horizon rather than saturating early, rising 6% to 9% to 14% as one world is re-run at 10, 20 and 40 rounds.

</details>


### [53] [Train4Merge: A Controlled Single-Teacher Study of RL vs. SFT Teachers for OPD-Based Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32303)
*Jingyuan Huang,Zuming Huang,Yucheng Shi,Zhongzhi Li,Xiaoming Zhai,Wei Chu,Ninghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在单教师在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中，用强化学习（RL）训练的教师比用监督微调（SFT）训练的教师更"会教"，学生表现更好且参数空间更接近共享初始化，更易被追随。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师在线策略蒸馏中，领域专家从同一检查点训练后合并为一个模型。但一个上游选择很少被审视：每个专家该用监督微调（SFT）还是强化学习（RL）来构建。同等强的教师不一定同等会教，作者想探究这一训练方式选择的影响。

Method: 采用受控的单教师OPD实验（多教师OPD的基础模块）：在Agentic、Reasoning、Perception三个任务上，从Qwen3.5-9B训练表现相当的SFT教师与RL教师，各自指导学生（学生也从同一模型初始化）。

Result: 在最佳检查点上，RL指导的学生在Agentic、Reasoning、Perception上分别比SFT指导的学生高出4.27、1.50、0.86个百分点，并恢复了更多教师相对基础模型的性能增益。Agentic中最明显：最佳SFT学生仅恢复教师增益的44.44%，而最佳RL学生恢复115.00%，甚至超过教师。

Conclusion: RL教师比SFT教师在参数空间上更贴近共享初始化，因此学生更容易跟随。教师的教学能力不仅取决于其自身表现，还取决于它与学生初始化的距离。

Abstract: Domain experts trained from a shared checkpoint can be merged into one model through on-policy distillation (OPD), where they act as teachers supervising a student on its own trajectories. One upstream choice is rarely examined: whether to build each expert with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) or reinforcement learning (RL). Yet equally strong teachers need not be equally good teachers. We probe this choice through controlled single-teacher OPD, a building block of multi-teacher OPD: in Agentic, Reasoning, and Perception, comparably performing SFT and RL teachers are trained from Qwen3.5-9B, each guiding a student initialized from it. At their best checkpoints, RL-guided students outperform SFT-guided students by 4.27, 1.50, and 0.86 percentage points in Agentic, Reasoning, and Perception, respectively, and recover more of their teachers' performance gains over the base model. The contrast is clearest in Agentic, where the best SFT-guided student recovers only 44.44% of its teacher's gain, whereas the best RL-guided student recovers 115.00%, surpassing its teacher. Our analysis points to an explanation: RL teachers stay much closer to the shared initialization in parameter space than SFT teachers and are therefore easier for their students to follow.

</details>


### [54] [PhiFold: Towards Dynamic Protein Design with Physics-Structured Covariance Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32309)
*Yutian Liu,Mujie Lin,LanqianZhang,Meng Fan,Chang Liu,ZhiweiNie,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhiFold提出一个联合生成蛋白质骨架与其二阶动力学的框架，用残基位移协方差表示动力学，将动力学分解为三个可解释分量，实现紧凑且物理约束的平衡动力学表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型通常把动力学视为结构生成后的下游属性，通过模拟或预测估计；而以MD轨迹作为生成目标并不理想，因为随机、路径依赖的轨迹过度指定了潜在的平衡系综。

Method: PhiFold联合生成蛋白质骨架及其二阶动力学（残基位移协方差）。不预测二次规模的全协方差，而是分解为三个可解释成分：局部柔性、低秩集体运动表示、残基级集体参与度，并组装为具有精确边缘一致性的正定协方差矩阵。

Result: 在生成的蛋白质上，PhiFold改善了局部涨落和长程残基耦合的恢复，同时在主导集体运动子空间上保持竞争力；还能在保持骨架可设计性的同时实现残基柔性的双向控制。

Conclusion: 通过将结构生成与平衡动力学的显式表示统一，PhiFold为不仅按"看起来如何"、还按"如何运动"来设计蛋白质奠定了基础。

Abstract: Protein design is moving beyond structural correctness toward function-aware design, yet existing generative models typically treat dynamics as a downstream property estimated through simulation or prediction after structure generation. Using MD trajectories as a generative target is also undesirable because stochastic, path-dependent trajectories over-specify the underlying equilibrium ensemble. We introduce PhiFold, a framework for jointly generating protein backbones and their second-order dynamics, represented by residue-displacement covariance. Rather than predicting the quadratically sized full covariance, PhiFold decomposes dynamics into three interpretable components: local flexibility, a low-rank collective-motion representation, and residue-wise collective participation. These components are assembled into a positive-definite covariance matrix with exact marginal consistency, yielding a compact and physically constrained representation of equilibrium dynamics. Across generated proteins, PhiFold improves recovery of local fluctuations and long-range residue coupling while remaining competitive on dominant collective-motion subspaces. It further enables bidirectional control of residue flexibility while preserving backbone designability. By unifying structure generation with an explicit representation of equilibrium dynamics, PhiFold lays a foundation for designing proteins not only by how they look, but also by how they move.

</details>


### [55] [Delayed Supervision for Test-Time Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32312)
*Jinha Kim,Taksh Kothari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"延迟监督"（delayed supervision）作为测试时记忆的外层训练目标：在长间隔无关事件后提出基于模拟器的问题并监督其答案，从而提升LaCT、DeltaNet等模型的长期记忆保持与更新能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时语言模型在序列处理中以紧凑记忆形式运行（如LaCT的非线性fast-weight、DeltaNet的关联delta规则、RWKV-7的广义delta规则状态更新）。但仅用下一词预测训练，并不显式要求某个事实在经历大量后续记忆更新后仍可被访问，因此模型可能缺乏长期保留与修订事实的能力。

Method: 在后训练阶段采用延迟监督：在经过一段无关事件的长间隔后，才提出基于模拟器的问题，并将答案与常规下一词预测一同监督。问题在一次性分支上评估，其答案不进入后续事件流。构造上区分"保持"（事实在延迟期间始终有效）与"修订"（事实须以最新值作答）。在TextWorld训练轨迹上评估LaCT-760M与plain DeltaNet-1.3B，并使用共享的BABILong与RULER评测面板，另单独报告RWKV-7对比。

Result: 相较仅事件训练，延迟QA使BABILong提升：LaCT +5.48个百分点，DeltaNet +1.32个百分点；单针RULER分别提升+1.45与+3.27个百分点。RWKV-7对比在其各自面板上报告+4.60与+7.00个百分点的增益。

Conclusion: 结果表明延迟语义监督是训练可用测试时记忆的一种实用外层目标；但仍未明确收益中有多少具体来自"延迟"本身，而非一般的问答监督与答案终止监督。

Abstract: Test-time language models adapt a compact memory while processing the input sequence. This
  perspective encompasses nonlinear fast-weight learning in LaCT, associative delta-rule
  updates in DeltaNet, and generalized delta-rule state updates in RWKV-7. Training these
  models to predict the next token does not explicitly require a fact to remain accessible
  after many subsequent memory updates. We study delayed supervision for this test-time memory:
  during post-training, ask a simulator-grounded question only after a long interval of
  unrelated events, and supervise its answer alongside ordinary next-token prediction.
  Questions are evaluated on disposable branches, so their answers never enter the continuing
  event stream. The construction distinguishes retention from revision: a retained fact must
  remain valid throughout the delay, whereas a revised fact must be answered with its latest
  value. We evaluate this approach on LaCT-760M and plain DeltaNet-1.3B using TextWorld
  training trajectories and shared BABILong and RULER evaluation panels, and include a
  separately reported RWKV-7 comparison. Relative to event-only training, delayed QA improves
  BABILong by 5.48 percentage points for LaCT and 1.32 points for DeltaNet, and single-needle
  RULER by 1.45 and 3.27 points, respectively. The RWKV-7 comparison reports gains of 4.60 and
  7.00 points on its own panels. These results support delayed semantic supervision as a
  practical outer training objective for usable test-time memory, while leaving open how much
  of the benefit derives specifically from delay rather than general question-answering and
  answer-termination supervision.

</details>


### [56] [RLHarness: Co-evolving Procedural Skills with Reinforcement Learning for Long-horizon Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32326)
*Ziqiao Shang,Zian Xu,Ji-Chen Yan,Weiming Wu,Ziyi Jia,Jie Meng,Tao Huang,Shan Huang,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RLHARNESS框架，将技能、选择与执行协议、少样本演示及任务契约组织为统一版本化的Harness，并与策略学习交替进行演化，从而解决多模态推理中的程序冷启动问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推理需在长决策链中保留视觉证据并选择合适流程，而仅靠终端验证器的强化学习只表明最终答案对错，无法指导"如何产出"，导致策略需边发现可复用推理程序边学习执行，形成程序冷启动问题。固定技能库假设指导始终与演化策略兼容，而只更新技能又会使其触发条件、执行协议和演示过时或不一致。

Method: RLHARNESS将技能、选择与执行协议、少样本演示和任务契约统一为版本化的Harness，并交替进行Harness演化与策略学习：先用探索-蒸馏Harness构建初始Harness及版本对齐的验证轨迹用于SFT与DAPO I；首个RL块后，用后RL重构Harness基于新的成功-失败rollout重建技能、协议与演示，再以DAPO II让策略适配重构后的程序。

Result: 在MetroMap/TravelMap上准确率从16.25%/27.50%提升至62.00%/50.00%；在Fee-VL/Cancel-VL上F1从37.13%/45.50%提升至65.81%/65.51%。四个任务均仅在重构与DAPO II之后取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 演化的Harness通过持续更新策略所学习执行的外部程序，与强化学习形成互补，是提升多模态推理性能的关键。

Abstract: Multimodal reasoning requires models to preserve visual evidence through long decision chains while selecting appropriate procedures across diverse scenarios and rules. When learning is guided only by terminal verifiers, reinforcement learning (RL) reveals whether a final answer is correct but not how it should be produced. The policy must therefore discover reusable reasoning procedures while learning to execute them, creating a program cold-start problem. Skills can externalize successful procedures, reduce repeated exploration, and provide inspectable guidance. However, a fixed Skill Bank assumes that this guidance remains compatible with an evolving policy, while updating Skills alone can leave their triggers, execution protocols, and demonstrations stale or mutually inconsistent. We introduce RLHARNESS, which organizes Skills, selection and execution protocols, few-shot demonstrations, and task contracts into a unified, versioned Harness and alternates Harness evolution with policy learning. An Exploration-Distillation Harness builds the initial Harness and version-aligned verified traces for SFT and DAPO I. After the first RL block, a Post-RL Reconstruction Harness rebuilds Skills, protocols, and demonstrations from fresh success-failure rollouts, and DAPO II adapts the policy to the reconstructed program. RLHARNESS improves Accuracy from 16.25%/27.50% to 62.00%/50.00% on MetroMap/TravelMap and raises F1 score from 37.13%/45.50% to 65.81%/65.51% on Fee-VL/Cancel-VL. All four tasks achieve their best results only after reconstruction and DAPO II, showing that an evolving Harness complements RL by continually updating the external program that the policy learns to execute.

</details>


### [57] [HyperReCo: Retrieving and Connecting Evidence with Hypergraph Neural Networks for LLM Multi-hop Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32327)
*Zicheng Zhao,Linhao Luo,Junnan Dong,Haoran Luo,Xiaoli Li,Shirui Pan,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperReCo框架，用超图神经网络实现查询相关的证据检索与连接，并通过梯度引导超路径解码为LLM多跳推理提供显式证据关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超图检索方法依赖预定义的结构扩展或扩散，难以捕捉查询相关的交互，可能遗漏相关证据；同时检索到的证据之间的连接关系是隐式的，需要LLM自行重建后才能推理。

Method: 将每篇文档表示为在其抽取实体上的超边，共享实体连接各超边；通过超图消息传递并对文档与实体进行联合监督，学习查询相关的交互以检索互补证据；提出梯度引导超路径解码（GGHD），利用梯度归因解释学到的交互并转化为显式超路径。

Result: 在六个基准上实验，HyperReCo在三个多跳QA数据集上均取得对比方法中最佳的检索性能，并具备较强的下游QA表现；案例研究与进一步分析验证了解码超路径对连接检索证据的有效性。

Conclusion: HyperReCo通过超图神经网络实现查询相关的证据检索，并以梯度引导解码生成显式超路径，帮助LLM组合互补事实进行多跳推理，兼顾检索与下游问答效果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities, with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) supporting complex multi-hop reasoning by retrieving evidence distributed across documents. Graph-based approaches exploit connections among evidence, and hypergraph-based retrieval further preserves higher-order entity associations within documents and connects documents through shared entities. However, existing hypergraph retrievers often rely on predefined structural expansion or diffusion, which may miss query-dependent interactions needed to identify relevant evidence. They also leave connections among retrieved evidence implicit, requiring LLMs to reconstruct these connections before reasoning. Therefore, we propose HyperReCo, a framework for retrieving and connecting evidence with a hypergraph neural network (HyperGNN). We represent each document as a hyperedge over its extracted entities, with shared entities connecting the hyperedges. Through hypergraph message passing with joint supervision over documents and entities, the HyperGNN learns query-dependent interactions to retrieve complementary evidence. We further introduce Gradient-Guided Hyper-Path Decoding (GGHD), which uses gradient attribution to interpret the learned interactions and translate them into explicit hyper-paths that help LLMs combine complementary facts for multi-hop reasoning. Experiments on six benchmarks show that HyperReCo achieves the best retrieval performance among the compared methods on all three multi-hop QA datasets, together with strong downstream QA performance. Case studies and further analyses demonstrate the utility of decoded hyper-paths for connecting retrieved evidence.

</details>


### [58] [Enabling Timely Guidance before Skill Retrieval: Retaining Helpful Warm Tips in Agent Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32339)
*Feng Liang,Yupeng Li,Runhao Zeng,Francis C. M. Lau,Xiping Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 TipsWarm 机制，通过维护预算化的技能关键点池（warm tips）并按轮次选择性注入上下文，让 LLM 智能体在行动前即可获得可迁移的技能指导，在三个编码与迭代任务基准上取得最高成功率且保持时间高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复用技能能帮助 LLM 智能体解决复杂任务，但智能体必须在投入无效方法之前就获得指导。现有技能机制通常只暴露元数据、按需加载全文，导致有用的指导在智能体决定检索前不可用；通用记忆方法维护开销大，而将指导一直保留在对话上下文中又可能反复暴露无关或有害建议。

Method: TipsWarm 作为现有技能机制的补充，维护一个有预算限制的技能衍生关键点池（warm tips），并在每条消息轮次中按需选择性注入上下文。其核心是将事件触发的 LLM 评估与低成本的逐轮筛选相分离，从而在使可迁移技能指导随时可用的同时控制维护成本。

Result: 在三个编码与迭代任务执行基准上，与近期技能方法和通用记忆基线相比，TipsWarm 取得了最高的任务成功率，同时保持时间高效。

Conclusion: TipsWarm 通过预算化 warm tips 池与评估/筛选分离设计，解决了技能指导可用性与维护成本的矛盾，验证了前瞻性、选择性注入技能关键点对提升 LLM 智能体任务表现的有效性。

Abstract: Reusable skills help LLM-based agents solve complex tasks, but the agent must receive guidance before it commits to an ineffective approach. Existing skill mechanisms often expose only metadata and load full content on demand, leaving useful guidance unavailable until the agent decides to retrieve it. General memory methods can incur substantial maintenance overhead, while keeping guidance in conversation context risks repeatedly exposing the agent to irrelevant or harmful advice. We propose TipsWarm, a mechanism that complements existing skill mechanisms by maintaining a budgeted pool of skill-derived keypoints, or \textit{warm tips}, for selective injection into the context of every message turn. By separating event-triggered LLM assessment from inexpensive per-turn screening, it makes transferable skill guidance readily available while controlling maintenance costs. In three coding and iterative task-execution benchmarks, TipsWarm achieves the highest task success rate while remaining time-efficient, compared to recent skill and general memory baselines.

</details>


### [59] [ALLOT: Budgeted Hybrid-Memory Routing for Knowledge Updates in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32344)
*Shanfeng Huang,Zhou Fang,Song Xiao,Hai Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ALLOT是一个混合记忆路由框架，将学习到的写入优先级与硬性参数预算分离，仅在检索不足时才进行参数适应，从而大幅减少参数写入量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的参数适应成本高昂，而在检索已足够的情况下，对每个事实更新都进行等价的参数训练是一种浪费，因此需要按增量价值分配适应能力。

Method: 提出ALLOT框架：一个记忆感知路由器结合冻结文本表示、检索置信度与关系元数据；单一排序支持多种写入预算，并将所有事实保留在外部记忆中，从而分离学习到的写入优先级与硬性参数预算。

Result: 在CounterFact与Qwen3-4B上，20%参数写入预算下达到0.760准确率，恢复78.4%的预算匹配oracle增益，参数写入比全量双写少80%；联合加入检索与关系特征使归一化oracle增益提升6.2个百分点。Qwen3-0.6B共享存储结果以6-14.5%参数写入达到双写水平，跨基准迁移保留约88%域内增益。

Conclusion: 应按适应容量的增量价值进行分配，而非将每个事实更新视为等价有价值的训练目标。

Abstract: For large language models (LLMs), parametric adaptation is costly when retrieval already suffices. We introduce ALLOT, a hybrid-memory routing framework that separates learned write priority from a hard parametric budget. A memory-aware router combines frozen text representations, retrieval confidence, and relation metadata; a single ranking supports multiple write budgets while preserving all facts in external memory. On CounterFact with Qwen3-4B, ALLOT reaches 0.760 accuracy at a 20% parametric-write budget and recovers 78.4% of the budget-matched oracle gain, with 80% fewer parametric writes than dual-writing every fact. At this budget, jointly adding retrieval and relation features to text improves normalized oracle gain by 6.2 percentage points. Complementary Qwen3-0.6B shared-store results achieve dual-write-level accuracy with 6-14.5% parametric writes, and cross-benchmark transfer retains approximately 88% of in-domain gain. These results support allocating adaptation capacity according to its incremental value rather than treating every factual update as an equally valuable training target.

</details>


### [60] [From Trajectories to Grounded Preferences: Process Preference Synthesis via Interaction Element Graphs for Web PRMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32351)
*Yangzhe Peng,Xiaoyang Wang,Yiyang Zhao,Lijun Wu,Kun He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SURFPRM框架，通过交互元素图合成高质量对比偏好数据，将Grounded Minimal Contrastive Pairs占比从24.19%提升至74.60%，显著增强Web智能体过程奖励模型的决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过多策略采样合成的偏好训练数据严重缺乏Grounded Minimal Contrastive Pairs (GMCPs)——即竞争候选动作针对真实页面元素且动作类型相同的数据。在WebArbiter基线中GMCPs仅占24.19%，导致过程奖励模型(PRMs)训练时依赖元素幻觉、动作类型不匹配等浅层捷径，而非学习真正的上下文决策语义。

Method: 提出SURFPRM图引导的过程偏好合成框架。该框架将网页演示构建为持久的交互元素图(Interaction Element Graph)，作为环境接地的负动作提议机制，在空间、时间及时空混淆轴上系统合成对比负动作，并据此构建了SURFPRM-DATA数据集。

Result: GMCP占比从24.19%提升至74.60%。在六个开源骨干模型(3B至9B参数)上，基于SURFPRM-DATA训练的PRM在WEBPRMBENCH上平均优于基线模型，并可媲美领先的专有LLM。在WEBARENA-LITE的下游奖励引导轨迹搜索中，SURFPRM为GPT-4o(+14.21%)和GPT-4o-mini(+12.83%)策略提供步级指导，显著提升复杂网页任务成功率。

Conclusion: SURFPRM通过图引导的对比偏好数据合成有效解决了对比过程奖励模型中GMCPs稀缺的问题，提升了PRMs的上下文决策语义学习能力，并在多骨干、多基准及下游任务中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Comparative Process Reward Models (PRMs) provide critical step-level guidance for autonomous web agents by evaluating state-conditioned preferences between candidate actions. However, existing preference training data synthesized via multi-policy sampling suffers from a severe scarcity of Grounded Minimal Contrastive Pairs (GMCPs)-where competing candidates target genuine on-page elements with identical action types. In representative baselines preference data (namely, WebArbiter), GMCPs account for merely 24.19%, biasing PRMs during training to rely on shallow shortcuts (such as element hallucinations and action type mismatches) rather than acquiring genuine contextual decision semantics. To address these challenges, we propose SURFPRM, a graph-guided process preference synthesis framework for comparative Web PRMs. SURFPRM structures web demonstrations into a persistent Interaction Element Graph that acts as an environment-grounded negative action proposal mechanism, systematically synthesizing contrastive negative actions across spatial, temporal, and spatiotemporal confusion axes. This elevates the GMCP proportion from 24.19% to 74.60%, producing the curated SURFPRM-DATA dataset. Across six open-source backbones (3B to 9B parameters), PRMs trained on SURFPRM-DATA outperform baseline-trained models on average on WEBPRMBENCH and rival leading proprietary LLMs. In downstream reward-guided trajectory search on WEBARENA-LITE, SURFPRM provides step-level guidance for both GPT-4o (+14.21%) and GPT-4o-mini (+12.83%) policies, yielding substantial improvements in complex web task success rates.

</details>


### [61] [AuthorityLens: Rethinking LLM-Based Agent Systems Through the Lens of Authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32378)
*Shaojin Chen,Huihao Jing,Wun Yu Chan,Wenbin Hu,Jiaxing Li,Wu Pandy Pui Ching,Kshitij Bhatia,Xinlei He,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AuthorityLens 框架，从"系统权限""权限分离""主体权限"三个维度度量 LLM 智能体系统中实际实现的权限结构，并在 Codex、OpenCode、Gemini CLI 上验证名义配置与实现权限的错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在真实系统中被赋予对重要资源和决策的权限，常与人类及其他 LLM 参与者共事且各有不同权限。然而工作流角色、权限设置与审查机制未必反映实践中真正实现的权限结构，缺少统一方法来测量这种"实然"权限分布。

Method: 以"权限组合"为起点，从实现每个结果所需的最小参与者组合及可容许运行时状态出发，沿三个维度度量系统：系统被授权做什么（System Authority）、行使该权限所需联合参与程度（Authority Separation）、各参与者持有多少权限（Principal Authority）。将该框架应用于 Codex、OpenCode 和 Gemini CLI 的 13 种运行配置。

Result: 名义配置与实现权限并不清晰对应：Codex 中 Full Access 仅略微改变系统权限，却把权限大幅集中于执行的 Assistant；OpenCode 的 Build 与 Plan 配置尽管工作流与根级权限不同，系统权限与权限分离相同；Gemini CLI 中基于模型的审查提高了权限分离但不改变系统权限。主体权限还能区分"权限复制"与"权限分离"：派生或委派的智能体可成为同一权限的替代持有者而不增加所需的联合参与。

Conclusion: AuthorityLens 提供了一个统一框架，用于度量和比较不同智能体系统中实际实现的权限结构。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed with authority over consequential resources and decisions in real systems. These agents often operate alongside human and LLM-based participants who hold different forms of authority. Yet workflow roles, permission settings, and review mechanisms do not necessarily reflect the authority realized in practice. We introduce AuthorityLens, a framework for measuring a system's authority structure. Starting from an authority portfolio, we evaluate a system along three dimensions: what the system is authorized to do (System Authority), how much joint participation is required to exercise that authority (Authority Separation), and how much authority each participant holds (Principal Authority). We derive these measurements from the minimal combinations of participants sufficient to realize each outcome across admissible runtime states. We apply AuthorityLens to Codex, OpenCode, and Gemini CLI across 13 operating configurations over a common portfolio of agent operations. We find that nominal configurations do not map cleanly onto realized authority. In Codex, Full Access changes System Authority only marginally while substantially concentrating authority in the executing Assistant. OpenCode's Build and Plan configurations have the same System Authority and Authority Separation despite different workflows and root-level permissions. In Gemini CLI, model-based review increases Authority Separation without changing System Authority. Principal Authority further distinguishes authority replication from authority separation: spawned or delegated agents can become alternative holders of the same authority without increasing the required joint participation. Together, these results demonstrate that AuthorityLens provides a unified framework for measuring and comparing realized authority structures across agent systems.

</details>


### [62] [Reward Hacking and Agent Containment Failure: A Monte Carlo Study Based on the 2026 Hugging Face Incident](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32390)
*Murat Ozer,Bulent Erenay,Ibrahim Berber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 建立五阶段概率风险模型，用蒙特卡洛模拟比较四种控制配置下智能体造成外部网络安全事件的概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026年7月对Hugging Face生产基础设施的入侵事件表明，当能力较强的智能体遇到薄弱的围堵边界时，奖励黑客行为可能演变为外部网络安全事件。因此需要量化评估不同控制措施对这类风险的抑制作用。

Method: 构建连接五个阶段的概率风险模型：奖励黑客、围堵逃逸、可用访问权、持久化、检测失败。对每种控制配置进行10万次蒙特卡洛模拟；输入分布用于表达显式不确定性及比较分析，而非预测现实频率。进行敏感性分析，并对模型中每个系数施加独立±25%扰动、重复300次抽样以检验排序稳健性。

Result: 在既定假设下，分层控制对模拟外部事件概率的降低幅度显著大于单独使用网络隔离或监控，且该排序在系数扰动下依然成立。敏感性分析显示，智能体能力以及监控、授权与凭据控制方面的弱点对模型风险影响最大。

Conclusion: 研究支持将具备网络能力的智能体评估视为敌对安全区域，间接外联通道、共享基础设施、凭据和评估产物都必须处于智能体有效权限之外。人类时间贴现与指标博弈仅作为短视优化的行为类比，不推断AI智能体具有满足感或人类动机。

Abstract: The July 2026 intrusion into Hugging Face production infrastructure showed how reward hacking can become an external cybersecurity incident when a capable agent encounters weak containment boundaries. This study develops a probabilistic risk model linking five stages: reward hacking, containment escape, usable access, persistence, and failure of detection. A Monte Carlo simulation evaluates 100,000 runs under each of four control configurations. Input distributions represent explicit uncertainty and are used for comparative analysis rather than real-world frequency prediction. Under the stated assumptions, layered controls reduce simulated external-incident probability substantially more than network isolation or monitoring used alone, an ordering that holds under independent plus/minus 25% perturbation of every coefficient in the model across 300 draws. Sensitivity analysis shows that agent capability and weaknesses in monitoring, authorization, and credential control exert the greatest influence on modeled risk. Human temporal discounting and metric gaming provide a behavioral analogy for short-horizon optimization, but the study does not infer that AI agents experience gratification or human motivation. The results support treating cyber-capable agent evaluations as hostile security zones in which indirect egress, shared infrastructure, credentials, and evaluation artifacts must remain outside the agent's effective authority.

</details>


### [63] [SCLATE: a Substrate for Continual-Learning Agent Training and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32391)
*Youngmok Jung,Sirajul Salekin,Henry Tran,Javier Movellan,Zhao Huang,Manjot Bilkhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SCLATE，一个统一事件调度与 rollout 引擎的执行底座，让基准与未经修改的智能体共享同一时间线，压缩长周期场景评估，并支持在真实 harness 与记忆系统上进行后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习智能体由模型、harness 与记忆组成，需在长多会话跨度上运行，涉及会话启停、定时任务、记忆固化等智能体侧事件。但现有基准与训练框架只调度自身事件，导致每组基准-智能体配对都要自建调度循环，难以统一评估与训练。

Method: 设计 SCLATE 执行底座：基准与未经修改的智能体均通过适配器把各自事件加入同一个开放事件调度器；采用混合模拟时钟在共享时间线上运行事件，智能体工作时按实时推进、空闲间隙跳过，从而把一个月场景压缩到数小时。同时作为 rollout 引擎，原样运行任意智能体的 harness 与记忆，通过容器内代理记录每次模型调用的 token 与 log 概率。

Result: 移植七个基准，在十种模型上对十种未经修改的 harness 与记忆配置进行横向对比：额外添加的记忆系统并不总能稳定优于 harness 的原生记忆；同一 harness 与记忆下不同模型的使用方式差异很大。随后通过未经修改的 harness 与记忆对 Qwen3.5-4B 进行后训练，模型学会同时使用二者，读取文件行数减少 6.8 倍，SWE-bench Verified 通过率提高 16.7 个百分点，记忆记录更丰富，留出的 MetaClaw 准确率最高提升 11.8 个百分点。

Conclusion: SCLATE 以共享事件调度和混合模拟时钟统一了持续学习智能体的评估与训练，支持原样运行 harness 与记忆；实验表明记忆系统收益不确定、模型差异显著，而借助该底座进行后训练可显著提升工具使用效率、任务通过率与记忆质量。

Abstract: Continual-learning agents are systems of models, harnesses, and memory operating over long multi-session horizons. Evaluating and training them requires interleaving tasks with agent-side events such as session stop and start, crons, and memory consolidation. Yet existing benchmarks and training frameworks schedule only the benchmark's own events, leaving each benchmark and agent pair to build a custom scheduling loop. We present SCLATE, an execution substrate where benchmarks and unmodified agents each add their events to one open event scheduler through an adapter. A hybrid simulated clock runs these events on a shared timeline, flowing in real time while the agent works and skipping idle gaps, which compresses a month-long scenario into hours. SCLATE also serves as a rollout engine that runs any agent's harness and memory unmodified, recording the tokens and log probabilities of every model call through an in-container proxy. We port seven benchmarks to SCLATE and compare ten unmodified harness and memory configurations head to head on ten models. The comparison shows that an added memory system does not reliably beat the harness's native memory and that models differ widely in how they use the same harness and memory. We then post-train Qwen3.5-4B through unmodified harnesses and memory systems. The model learns to use both, reading 6.8x fewer file lines with a 16.7-point higher SWE-bench Verified pass rate, and writing richer memory records, while its held-out MetaClaw accuracy rises by up to 11.8 points.

</details>


### [64] [Beyond Scripted Search: Sample-Efficient Reward Discovery via Agentic Black-box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32394)
*Minghao Li,Rui Tan,Ruihang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出ARBO框架，用LLM智能体在运行时基于持久化评估历史构建奖励搜索策略，从而提升低层强化学习控制的奖励函数搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为低层RL控制设计稠密奖励函数仍很困难。近期方法用LLM迭代生成与精炼奖励函数，但每个候选奖励都需完整跑一次RL训练，导致样本效率成为复杂控制任务奖励搜索的核心瓶颈。

Method: 提出Agentic Reward Black-box Optimization（ARBO）框架：LLM智能体以持久化工作区形式维护评估历史，并在运行时构建搜索策略。评估历史包含两部分：(1) 评估预言机维护的观测，包括候选得分、逐项训练曲线、错误回溯；(2) 智能体维护的信念，记录诊断与下一步意图。智能体通过工具查询这两类信息并生成下一批奖励候选，而非依据固定提示一次性生成。

Result: 在四个控制域中，在共享评估预算下，ARBO相较基线均值在操作成功率上提升29.9%，在电网评分上提升192.8%。消融实验考察了各组件贡献及对骨干模型选择的敏感性。

Conclusion: 通过让LLM智能体动态构建搜索策略并利用结构化评估历史，ARBO有效缓解了奖励搜索的样本效率问题，在多个控制任务上显著优于基线。

Abstract: Designing dense reward functions for low-level reinforcement learning (RL) control remains difficult. Recent work uses large language models (LLMs) to iteratively generate and refine reward functions using policy-training feedback within scripted search algorithms. However, evaluating each candidate requires a full RL training run, making sample efficiency a central challenge for reward search on complex control tasks. To address this limitation, we propose an Agentic Reward Black-box Optimization (ARBO) framework, in which an LLM agent builds the search strategy at run time from an evaluation history maintained as its persistent workspace. The evaluation history comprises two components: observations maintained by the evaluation oracle, including candidate scores, per-term training curves, and error tracebacks; and an agent-maintained belief that records diagnoses and intended next steps. The agent queries both with tools and generates the next batch of reward candidates, rather than generating them in a single pass from a fixed prompt. Across four control domains, ARBO achieves gains of 29.9% in manipulation success rate and 192.8% in power-grid score over baseline means under a shared evaluation budget. Ablations examine each component's contribution and sensitivity to backbone choice.

</details>


### [65] [Memory as a cache: Exact context reuse and deletion by construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32395)
*Shengyao Wang,Jiang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SMem 通过块局部编码与交叉注意力读取，使 KV 缓存天然可精确组合与删除，在几乎不损失困惑度的情况下实现更快的解码与编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Transformer 的 KV 缓存将每个 token 表示与其整个前缀纠缠，导致一段文本一旦编码就无法在不同前缀下复用，也无法在不重算后续内容的情况下删除，精确复用仅限于共享前缀的场景。

Method: 提出 SMem 架构，其上下文表示本身就是缓存：块局部编码器独立于其他块将每个块映射为记忆行，读取器通过交叉注意力在它们的并集上条件生成。该设计对任意参数设置都能在固定块索引处精确组合记忆，删除一个块是精确的 O(b) 更新（b 为块 token 数），记忆状态与编辑路径无关。此外 SMem 可与 RoPE 组合。

Result: 在 4 倍训练上下文下，SMem 能检索超出任何训练长度窗口的植入针（31 与 63 块距离处精确匹配 0.14-0.28），而学习位置、RoPE 和 Block-Attention 式 Transformer 得分至多 0.02。全缓存上下文仅需计算一个块，耗时近恒定 3.1-6.2 ms，冷预填充则随上下文增长；批量解码存储的 KV 行减少 34-38%，带宽受限时快 1.4-1.7 倍；删除在 512 块时比后缀重算快 8.5 倍，4096 块时快 452 倍。代价是与参数匹配、位置方案相同的 Transformer 相比，困惑度差距在 -4.7% 到 +2.8% 之间（负值有利 SMem），在 160M-1.5B、FineWeb-Edu 两种配方及学习率搜索下验证。SMem 与 RoPE 组合后，在 160M 与 410M 上匹配或领先匹配 Transformer，并弥补 SMem 相对 RoPE Transformer 差距的 29-59%。

Conclusion: 去除前缀纠缠使缓存可精确组合与编辑，同时在 160M-1.5B 规模上保持与匹配 Transformer 可比的困惑度，并显著提升解码与编辑效率。

Abstract: The KV cache of a transformer entangles every token's representation with its entire prefix: a passage encoded once cannot be reused under a different prefix or removed without recomputing everything after it, so exact cache reuse is limited to shared prefixes. We present SMem, an architecture whose context representation is a cache by construction. A block-local encoder maps each block to memory rows independently of other blocks, and a reader conditions generation on their union through cross-attention. For every parameter setting, memory composes exactly at fixed block indices, deleting a block is an exact $O(b)$ update for $b$-token blocks, and the memory state is independent of the edit path. At $4\times$ the training context, under the shared recipe, SMem retrieves planted needles beyond any trained-length window (exact match 0.14-0.28 at distances of 31 and 63 blocks), where learned-position, RoPE, and Block-Attention-style transformers all score at most 0.02. A fully cached context is served by computing one block alone at a near-constant 3.1-6.2 ms, whereas cold prefill grows with context; batched decode stores 34-38% fewer KV rows and runs 1.4-1.7$\times$ faster when bandwidth-bound; and deletion beats suffix recomputation by 8.5$\times$ at 512 blocks and 452$\times$ at 4096 blocks (32-256$\times$ the trained length, probing the cost model rather than a served regime). The cost is a perplexity gap of -4.7% to +2.8% (negative favors SMem) against a parameter-matched transformer with the same positional scheme, at 160M-1.5B on FineWeb-Edu across two recipes and a learning-rate search. SMem also composes with RoPE: at 160M and 410M the composite matches or leads the matched transformer and closes 29-59% of SMem's gap to a RoPE transformer. Dropping prefix entanglement thus keeps perplexity comparable while making the cache exactly composable and editable.

</details>


### [66] [Function Over Form: Distributional Orthogonalization in Mixture-of-Experts with Replica Expert Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32398)
*Jinfan He,Yunzhuo Liu,Kai Zhang,Weidong Han,Key,Rayying*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出分布正交化损失（DO-loss）与副本专家机制（REM），通过动态路由行为正则化和两级负载均衡策略，缓解专家坍缩与表示冗余，提升MoE模型容量利用率与下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构被广泛用于解耦激活计算量与总参数量以支撑LLM扩展，但专家坍缩和表示冗余导致模型容量未被充分利用，现有方法多关注静态权重多样性而忽视动态路由行为。

Method: 提出DO-loss，将每个专家的token分配历史表示为高维二值负载签名，通过惩罚签名重叠来防止专家坍缩并促进功能特化；同时引入REM，采用全局批次级副本专家放置调整与微批次级实时token分发的两级策略改善负载均衡。

Result: 在4.8BA0.5B和30BA3B两种MoE模型的下游任务上，该方法优于所评估的路由算法，并保持相当的训练效率。

Conclusion: 从静态权重多样性转向动态路由行为的正则化，并结合系统层面的负载均衡机制，可有效缓解专家坍缩与表示冗余，提升MoE模型容量利用率，兼顾性能与训练效率。

Abstract: The scaling of LLMs increasingly relies on MoE architectures to decouple active computation from total parameter count. However, the efficacy of MoE is often constrained by expert collapse and representation redundancy, both leading to underutilization of model capacity. To address these challenges, this paper proposes Distributional Orthogonalization Loss (DO-loss), an auxiliary regularization that shifts the focus from static weight diversity to dynamic routing behavior. By representing each expert's token assignment history as a high-dimensional binary load signature, DO-loss penalizes signature overlap to prevent expert collapse while encouraging functional specialization. To align this algorithmic design with system efficiency, we further introduce the Replica Expert Mechanism (REM), which improves load balancing through a two-tiered strategy: adjusting replica expert placement at the global-batch level and performing real-time token dispatching at the micro-batch level. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that our method outperforms the evaluated routing algorithms on downstream tasks for both 4.8BA0.5B and 30BA3B MoE models, while maintaining comparable training efficiency.

</details>


### [67] [Opening LLM Judges: Recovering Preference Signals Beyond the Final Verdict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32407)
*Sourabrata Mukherjee,Sunayana Sitaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究发现LLM评判者给出错误判决时，其内部激活往往已包含正确答案信息，只是未能反映到输出中；一个简单探针即可大幅恢复该信号，且表面特征可预测的偏差程度能预示信息恢复的收益大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判者被广泛用于评估模型输出，但其判决可能不可靠（受位置、长度等表面特征影响）。作者希望弄清：当评判出错时，正确的判断所需信息究竟是模型内部根本没有，还是存在于内部表征中却未反映到输出。这一区分对判断是否值得从内部恢复信号至关重要。

Method: 作者在64个开放权重评判模型和14个数据集上开展研究，对其中41个评判者进行因果干预（在运行中途编辑激活以观察判决是否改变）。他们训练简单探针（不更新权重）读取评判者激活，并在残差化剔除长度、位置等表面特征后测量探针性能，同时用表面特征单独预测人类标签的分数作为诊断指标。

Result: 在LLMBar上（表面更好的答案实为更差的答案），50个评判者的判决与人类标签一致率仅0.456（即使平均两种答案顺序后仍如此）；同一评判者激活上的小探针达到0.846，残差化表面特征后为0.686（打乱标签为0.507）。该差距在8个基准和多个模型家族中成立，但并非普遍适用。事前计算的表面特征预测人类标签的分数能预测探针收益大小（Spearman rho = 0.90）。在单答案评分的评分标准任务上，无表面线索可利用，读取内部信息无优势。干预表明，中途编辑激活已能改变判决，早于直接读出判决，并定位了携带位置和长度偏差的通路。在相同标签预算下，恢复的信号可让评判者标记可能出错的情况，并为偏好学习提供更好的标签。

Conclusion: 错误的判决并不意味着评判者缺乏判断所需的信息。一个简单诊断（表面特征预测人类标签的程度）可以显示何时值得从内部表征中恢复信号，从而改进评判并产出更好的偏好学习标签。

Abstract: LLM judges are widely used to evaluate model outputs, but their verdicts can be unreliable: a judge may favor the worse answer for its position, length, or other surface features. When a judge is wrong, is the information needed to judge correctly absent from the model, or present in its internal representations but not reflected in the output? We study this across 64 open-weight evaluators and 14 datasets, including causal interventions on 41 judges (editing activations mid-run to see whether the verdict changes). On LLMBar, built so the superficially better answer is the worse one, the verdicts of 50 judges agree with human labels only 0.456 of the time, even after averaging both answer orders. Yet a small probe on the same judges' activations, with no weight updates, reaches 0.846, and 0.686 once surface features such as length and position are residualized out (0.507 with shuffled labels). The gap holds across eight benchmarks and model families, but is not universal: a score of how well surface features alone predict the human label, computed before any probe is trained, predicts the size of the gain (Spearman rho = 0.90). On rubric tasks that score one answer at a time, leaving no surface cue to exploit, reading the internals gives no advantage. The interventions also show that editing activations mid-network already changes the verdict, before it can be read off directly, and locate the pathways carrying position and length bias. At the same label budget, the recovered signal lets a judge flag cases where it is likely wrong and yields better labels for preference learning. A wrong verdict, then, does not mean the judge lacks the information, and a simple diagnostic shows when it is worth recovering.

</details>


### [68] [Carnator: Fast Text-to-Video Generation with Generation-Native Compatibility-Guided Cross-Request Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32420)
*Xingkun Yin,Xuebin Tang,Mingkun Xu,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Carnator：一种跨请求加速框架，通过从模型内部状态提取"生成原生"兼容性证据，实现更精准的历史计算复用，在三个文生视频骨干网络上实现最高2.17倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer生成质量高，但迭代去噪导致推理延迟巨大，限制了交互式应用和大规模服务。现有加速方法多针对单请求，只能利用单条生成轨迹内的冗余；近期跨请求复用虽提供额外收益，但通常基于粗粒度语义相似度判断可复用性，混淆了"语义相关"与"生成级计算兼容"，导致激进复用会接受不兼容的历史计算，保守复用又浪费了加速空间。

Method: Carnator通过轻量级早期探测（early probe），从模型演化的内部扩散状态中构建"早期签名"（Early Signature），并基于风险感知的兼容性决策来评估复用有效性。同一证据还用于刻画复用范围：定位目标特定计算，并指导历史潜在轨迹与稀疏注意力连接性的联合复用。

Result: 在三个文生视频骨干网络上，Carnator相比所评估的跨请求基线实现了更高的缓存命中端到端加速，尽管其缓存接受更严格（更具选择性），最高可达2.17倍加速，同时保持有竞争力的生成质量。

Conclusion: 从模型内部状态中提取的"生成原生"兼容性证据，比粗粒度语义相似度更能准确刻画跨请求复用的有效性与范围；据此可实现更选择性但更高收益的缓存复用，为视频扩散模型的高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers produce high-quality videos, yet iterative denoising incurs substantial inference latency, limiting interactive and large-scale serving. Most existing acceleration methods focus on individual requests, thereby restricting efficiency gains to redundancy within a single generation trajectory. Recent cross-request reuse offers an additional source of savings, but existing approaches often infer reusability from coarse semantic similarity. This conflates semantic relatedness with generation-level computational compatibility, so aggressive reuse may accept incompatible historical computation while conservative reuse leaves substantial acceleration unrealized. We present \emph{Carnator}, a cross-request acceleration framework that addresses this challenge by extracting and using generation-native compatibility evidence directly from the model's evolving internal states. Specifically, \emph{Carnator} performs a lightweight early probe to construct an Early Signature from internal diffusion states, assessing reuse validity through risk-aware compatibility decisions. The same evidence characterizes reuse scope by localizing target-specific computation and guiding joint reuse of historical latent trajectories and sparse attention connectivity. Across three text-to-video backbones, Carnator consistently achieves higher cache-hit end-to-end acceleration than the evaluated cross-request baselines despite more selective cache acceptance, reaching up to 2.17$\times$ speedup while maintaining competitive generation quality.

</details>


### [69] [MergeHEIR: Mitigating Multimodal Hallucinations as the Tax of Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32422)
*Jinyu Li,Hao Fang,Zhiming Zhang,Jiawei Kong,Bin Chen,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 MergeHEIR 框架，用于缓解模型合并带来的"合并税"——即合并后幻觉率上升的问题，同时保留各专家能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并可将多个任务专家整合为单一模型，但作者发现这会带来"合并税"：在 8 种合并方法中，合并后检查点的幻觉率均高于各专家平均值。直接将现有幻觉缓解方法套用于合并后模型会破坏继承的能力，形成幻觉缓解与专长保留之间的张力。

Method: 提出 MergeHEIR 后合并适配框架：利用小型专家任务校准集，从合并检查点采集任务相关激活，通过 SVD 构建逐层零空间投影器，并周期性地将累积的后合并位移投影到零空间上，以保留继承的专长。理论层面给出最小失真与最大维度保证，刻画阈值控制的"适配-保留"权衡，并将扰动保证扩展到有限校准数据之外。

Result: 在三种 MLLM 配置与 8 种模型合并方法共 24 组配对比较中，MergeHEIR 持续降低幻觉，同时大体保留继承专长，展现出更优的"幻觉-保留"权衡。

Conclusion: MergeHEIR 能有效缓解合并导致的幻觉上升，并在幻觉缓解与专长保留之间取得更有利的平衡。

Abstract: Model merging consolidates task-specialized experts into a single deployable model. However, we show that such capability consolidation incurs a merging tax of increased hallucination: across 8 model-merging methods, every merged checkpoint exhibits a higher hallucination rate than the average of its constituent experts.
