An intuitive approach is to adapt existing hallucination-mitigation methods to the post-merge model, yet this unconstrained adaptation disrupts inherited capabilities, creating a tension between hallucination mitigation and expertise retention. To tackle this challenge, we introduce MergeHEIR, a post-merge adaptation framework designed to reduce this merging tax while preserving expertise inherited from initial experts. Using small expert-task calibration sets, MergeHEIR constructs layer-wise null-space projectors via SVD from task-specific activations collected from the merged checkpoint, and periodically projects the accumulated post-merge displacement onto the resulting null spaces to preserve inherited expertise. Theoretically, we establish minimum-distortion and maximum-dimensionality guarantees, characterize the threshold-controlled adaptation-retention trade-off, and extend perturbation guarantees beyond finite calibration data. Across 24 paired comparisons spanning three MLLM configurations and 8 model-merging methods, MergeHEIR consistently mitigates hallucination while largely preserving inherited expertise, demonstrating a more favorable hallucination-retention trade-off.

</details>


### [70] [PluginRSI: Recursive Improvement of Agent Harnesses with Reusable Plugins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32423)
*Yaorui Shi,Yuchun Miao,Yuxin Chen,Jiayuan Zhang,Yueqing Sun,Xierui Song,Xiang Wang,An Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PluginRSI 将语言模型的外壳表示为原子化插件的组合，围绕插件进行外壳演化：单个插件独立改进并在共享库中累积，每轮重新组合为新外壳，在多个任务上优于现有方法并可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的外壳（harness）是决定智能体性能的核心因素。现有方法通过在完整程序空间上搜索来优化外壳，但其中各个机制难以单独分离和复用，限制了持续改进与迁移能力。

Method: 将外壳表示为一组原子化插件的组合，围绕插件组织外壳演化：每个插件被独立优化，并累积到共享插件库中；在每次迭代时，从库中重新组合插件生成新的外壳。

Result: 在软件工程、命令行交互和问答任务上，PluginRSI 均优于现有外壳优化方法；所得外壳在无需进一步优化的情况下迁移到其他求解模型时仍保持优势；演化出的插件库能加速从初始外壳出发的后续优化，在未见任务上收敛更快、效果更好。

Conclusion: 累积可复用的机制为外壳的持续改进提供了有效基础。

Abstract: The harness surrounding a language model is a central determinant of agent performance. Recent methods optimize harnesses by searching over complete programs, where individual mechanisms are difficult to isolate and reuse. We introduce PluginRSI, which represents a harness as a composition of atomized plugins and organizes harness evolution around these plugins. Individual plugins are improved independently and accumulated in a shared library, then recombined into new harnesses at each iteration. PluginRSI improves over existing harness optimization methods across software engineering, command-line interaction, and question-answering tasks. The resulting harnesses retain their advantage when transferred to other solver models without further optimization. The evolved plugin library accelerates subsequent optimization from the initial harness, which helps faster and higher convergence on unseen tasks. These results show that accumulating reusable mechanisms provides an effective basis for continued harness improvement.

</details>


### [71] [Authorization Closure Graph: Minimal Repair for LLM Agents with Evolving User Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32428)
*Qingzhuo Wang,CaiYi Wang,Jinglu Meng,Ruiyang Qin,Kunyu Peng,Zhihua Wei,Wen Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于授权闭包图（ACG）的框架，将授权及其依赖表示为可演化的版本化状态，实现指令修改时对授权状态的选择性更新与最小修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用的LLM智能体越来越多执行需要用户授权的状态变更操作。但既有方法缺乏一种有原则的机制，在指令仅部分改变时选择性地更新先前的授权。

Method: 提出Authorization-Closure-Graph（ACG）框架，将授权及其依赖关系建模为不断演化、带版本的状态。ACG在修订发生时选择性地使受影响的授权失效，同时保留未受影响的授权状态，并计算最小修复，仅识别执行所需的缺失证据或授权。

Result: 在两个自然任务、三种先进LLM上进行评估，ACG一致提升了动作安全率和任务成功率。

Conclusion: ACG使智能体能够适应修订后的指令，同时避免使用过时授权并减少不必要的授权请求；代码已开源。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents increasingly perform state-changing actions that require user authorization. Yet existing approaches do not provide a principled mechanism for selectively updating prior authorization when only part of an instruction changes. To this end, we propose an Authorization-Closure-Graph (ACG)-based framework that represents authorization and its dependencies as an evolving, versioned state. ACG selectively invalidates authority affected by a revision while preserving unaffected portions of the authorization state, and computes a minimal repair that identifies only the missing evidence or authority required for execution. This enables agents to adapt to revised instructions while avoiding stale authority and unnecessary authorization requests. We evaluate ACG across three advanced LLMs in two natural tasks, and ACG consistently improves action safety rate and task success rate. Code is available at https://github.com/weiliang822/ACG.

</details>


### [72] [PrismQuant: Optimal Null-Space Rotations for Grouped Quantizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32429)
*Yanlong Chen,Yining Chen,Song Zhang,Amirhossein Habibian,Yawei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PrismQuant 提出量化器感知的旋转框架，将激活主特征子空间与非对称分组 INT4 的常量组子空间对齐，以提升低比特量化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 较小的激活离群值并不必然带来更好的低比特量化，关键在于其与量化器的对齐程度，现有方法忽视了这一对齐问题。

Method: 将旋转设计建模为 Ky Fan 迹最大化，推导出闭式最优解；采用紧凑 Householder 变换及 compact-WY 表示实现无梯度构造与高效应用，并提出预测性范围律连接未对齐激活能量与组大小。

Result: 在 Llama、Qwen、Mistral（含 70B 稠密与 30B MoE）上验证：W4A4KV4 下 Llama-3.2-3B 达 SOTA；Llama-3.1-70B 困惑度 3.85、零样本准确率 72.46%，仅低于全精度 0.22 个百分点；Llama-3.1-8B 部署实现 1.51x prefill 与 1.22x 解码加速，解码峰值内存降低 56.34%。

Conclusion: PrismQuant 通过量化器感知旋转有效对齐激活与量化器子空间，兼顾精度与部署效率，为低比特量化提供了理论上最优且实用的方案。

Abstract: Smaller activation outliers do not necessarily imply better low-bit quantization: their alignment with the quantizer matters. We introduce PrismQuant, a quantizer-aware rotation framework that aligns the leading activation eigenspace with the constant group subspace of asymmetric grouped INT4. The affine offsets represent the energy in this subspace without widening the range within the group. We formulate rotation design as a Ky Fan trace maximization and derive a closed-form solution that is provably optimal for this alignment objective. Compact Householder transformations and their compact-WY representation enable gradient-free construction and efficient application at both foldable and online sites. A predictive range law further connects unaligned activation energy and group size to quantization-relevant variation. Experiments on Llama, Qwen, and Mistral span dense models up to 70B parameters and a 30B mixture-of-experts model. Under W4A4KV4, PrismQuant sets the state of the art on Llama-3.2-3B among the compared methods in both perplexity and accuracy. On Llama-3.1-70B, it attains 3.85 perplexity and 72.46% average zero-shot accuracy, only 0.22 percentage points below full precision. In the deployment study on Llama-3.1-8B, our optimized implementation achieves 1.51x prefill and 1.22x CUDA Graph decode speedups over matched FP16 baselines, with 56.34% lower decode peak memory and only 2.35% additional Graph decode latency over Hadamard. Code is available at https://github.com/ForeverBlue816/PrismQuant.

</details>


### [73] [Multi-Agent System Search via Active Substructure-aware Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32430)
*Beicheng Xu,Bowen Fan,Weitong Qian,Lingching Tung,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ASPO，一个面向查询级多智能体系统搜索的强化学习框架，通过自适应查询选择与子结构级奖励，在六个基准上全部取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统构建方法需人工设计角色、提示与通信结构，成本高。已有基于执行奖励训练的策略方法通常反复遍历固定训练查询集，并在工作流层面分配奖励，忽视了查询学习潜力的动态差异，也无法辨识哪些子结构真正提升解题质量。

Method: ASPO包含三项机制：(1) 自适应查询选择机制（AQSM），将训练集中于处于策略能力边界的查询，即能解但尚不稳定者；(2) 互补的发现机制，为困难查询拓宽架构探索，以区分探索不足与算子能力上限；(3) 子结构级奖励，度量每个动作后代子图内的输出质量增益，指导近端策略优化，强化有益架构改进、抑制冗余或有害计算。

Result: 在数学推理、通用问答与代码生成三大领域的六个基准上，ASPO与十二个基线方法对比，在每个基准上均排名第一。

Conclusion: 通过优先选择可学习查询并提供细粒度反馈，ASPO能够有效学习构建查询专属的多智能体系统，性能全面超越现有方法。

Abstract: LLMs enable multi-agent systems (MAS) to tackle complex tasks, but manually designing agent roles, prompts, and communication structures requires substantial expertise and effort. This motivates learning policies that construct query-specific MAS from execution reward. Existing approaches typically train these policies by repeatedly traversing a fixed set of training queries and assigning rewards at the workflow level. However, this overlooks differences in queries' evolving learning potential and obscures which substructures improve solution quality. In this paper, we propose Active Substructure-aware Policy Optimization (ASPO), a RL framework for query-level MAS search. ASPO introduces an Adaptive Query-Selection Mechanism (AQSM) that focuses training on queries at the policy's competence boundary: those it can solve but not yet reliably. A complementary discovery mechanism widens architectural exploration for hard queries, helping distinguish insufficient exploration from operator capability limits. Beyond query selection, ASPO introduces substructure-level rewards that measure output-quality gains within each action's descendant subgraph. These rewards guide proximal policy optimization to reinforce useful architectural refinements and discourage redundant or harmful computation. Together, these mechanisms prioritize learnable queries and provide fine-grained feedback for learning effective MASs. Across six benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning, general question answering, and code generation, ASPO ranks first on every benchmark against twelve baselines.

</details>


### [74] [From Latents to Wires: Surgical Post-Editing on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32434)
*Jiankai Jin,Xiangzheng Zhang,Zhao Liu,Wenzhuo Xu,Dongdong Yang,Deyue Zhang,Quanchen Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 L2W 框架，可对大模型进行"后编辑"：定位并移除指定语义目标（如身份自称、内容拒答），同时保留其他能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究模型权重持有者能否定位并编辑产生特定语义目标的组件，在移除该目标的同时不损害其他能力，将"后编辑"作为后训练的补充手段。

Method: L2W 包含两步：定位阶段用雅可比透镜（J-lens）归因对组件打分；移除阶段因机制冗余，采用反例引导的因果切割（CGCC），先累积关闭组件、将残余表达作为反例暴露下一批组件，再重开部分组件以保留能力。

Result: 在植入行为水印的受控实验中成功移除水印且定位落在植入改动区域；三种模型配置下九次运行均移除元数据（身份）自称，三次全部移除成人内容拒答，无遗留残差；在文生图模型上叠加两次后编辑，分别移除裸体请求拒答与其暴露的裸体渲染。

Conclusion: 后编辑可作为后训练的补充：后训练安装偏好行为，后编辑移除指定的不受欢迎行为。

Abstract: Given a large language model (LLM), can whoever holds the weights name a semantic target (e.g., the model's identity), locate the model components that produce it, and edit them so that the target no longer appears while other capability is preserved? We call such an edit on a trained model a post-edit. We present L2W (latents to wires), a framework that performs surgical post-edits for named semantic targets. For localization, L2W uses Jacobian lens (J-lens) attribution to score components against the semantic target. For surgical removal, because LLM mechanisms are redundant (i.e., a semantic target may have multiple components producing it), L2W runs Counterexample-Guided Causal Cut (CGCC) until the target no longer appears. CGCC first cumulatively closes model components, treating each surviving expression of the target as a counterexample that exposes the next components to close, and then reopens some of them to preserve capability. In a controlled experiment with an implanted behavioural watermark, L2W removes the watermark, and its localization lands on the model region the implant changed. Across three model configurations, L2W removes model-metadata (e.g., identity) self-claims in all nine runs, and adult-content refusal in all three, with no held-out target residual. L2W further composes two post-edits on a text-to-image model: one removes the refusal of requested nudity, and a second removes the nude rendering the first exposes. The results support post-editing as a complement to post-training: post-training installs preferred behaviours, and post-editing removes named unwanted ones.

</details>


### [75] [Controllable GNN Explanations via Multi-Metric Preference Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32436)
*Rachit Verma,Yashraj J. Deshmukh,Anirban Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种同时优化保真度、可解释性与稳定性的GNN解释生成方法，通过权重调控三者权衡，在多个数据集上表现优于基线，并探讨了模体库的重要性与指标间权衡面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN解释方法大多仅在稀疏性约束下优化保真度，忽视了可解释性（由熟悉的模体模式构成）和稳定性（在结构扰动下保持不变）的需求，而这对于高风险场景下的信任构建与偏见缓解至关重要。

Method: 提出一种新颖方法，跨保真度、可解释性和稳定性多个指标共同优化GNN解释，并将这些指标的相对权重作为控制手段；同时利用标准模体库确定各模体在生成解释中的相对重要性，并据此进一步提升解释质量。

Result: 在MUTAG、BA-2Motif、BAMultiShapes和PROTEINS等真实数据集上，该方法在MUTAG和PROTEINS的所有评估预算下、以及BA-2Motif的较大预算下，保真度均高于最先进基线，且在小解释预算下速度更快。

Conclusion: 该方法能在多指标间实现可控权衡，模体库信息可用于评估模体重要性并改进解释质量；不同指标间存在由权衡面刻画的相互关系，且该关系依赖于模体库的性质。

Abstract: Mechanisms for generating GNN explanations are crucial for building trust and mitigating biases in Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), especially in high-stakes scenarios. Most current methods optimize only for fidelity under the sparsity constraint. However, this discounts the need for interpretable explanations (those that consist of familiar motif patterns) and stable explanations (those that remain unchanged under structural perturbations). We propose a novel approach that optimizes GNN explanations across these metrics, exposing their relative weighing as a control. Experiments on various real-world datasets, including MUTAG, BA-2Motif, BAMultiShapes, and PROTEINS, suggest that our method produces higher-fidelity explanations than a state-of-the-art baseline on MUTAG and PROTEINS across all evaluated budgets, and on BA-2Motif at larger budgets, while being faster in the regime of small explanation budgets. We also explore how, given an input motif library containing standard motifs for the corresponding domain, the method can be used to determine the relative importance of those motifs in generating the explanations, and how this information can be used to further improve the quality of the output explanations. We also examine the relationship between different metrics through their induced tradeoff surface, and explore its dependence on the nature of the motif library.

</details>


### [76] [ForkLeft: Entropy-First Rollouts for Prefix-Aligned Autoregressive-to-Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32448)
*Junming Liu,Jicheng Wang,Yifeng He,Hao Chen,Jianzhong Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ForkLeft蒸馏框架，解决自回归教师（左侧前缀）与扩散语言模型（双侧上下文）不匹配问题，使DLM在保留原生并行生成的同时获得NTP式推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归NTP具备强推理能力，扩散语言模型具有灵活词序与并行生成优势。作者探究能否通过蒸馏让DLM获得NTP式推理而不放弃原生生成。但直接蒸馏存在根本性错配：自回归教师仅依赖左前缀，而DLM可同时依赖左右两侧token。

Method: ForkLeft通过分离学生rollout与教师监督来化解错配。训练时，学生先进行"熵优先"rollout，强制确定不确定位置并暴露潜在分叉点；随后固定学生前缀，在同一上下文下蒸馏NTP教师，并由答案正确性决定监督来源。推理时，学生恢复其原生的置信度优先并行解码。

Result: 以Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base为教师，ForkLeft在全部十个基准上提升Efficient-DLM-4B，MATH500从72.60%升至79.60%，并持续优于三种替代设计。增益随教师强度提升，且仅用500次更新即可泛化到SDAR-4B。同规模下，蒸馏后的4B与8B学生在七个基准上超过已发表的SDAR-Chat与OPDLM。

Conclusion: 结果表明DLM能够学习NTP式推理，同时不牺牲原生并行生成能力，为扩散语言模型的推理能力提升提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Autoregressive Next-Token Prediction (NTP) has enabled strong reasoning capabilities in language models, while Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer flexible token orders and parallel generation. We ask whether DLMs can acquire NTP-style reasoning through distillation without giving up their native generation process. Direct distillation, however, faces a fundamental mismatch: an autoregressive teacher predicts from a left prefix, whereas a DLM can condition on tokens on both sides. We introduce ForkLeft, a distillation framework that resolves this mismatch by separating the student's rollout from teacher supervision. During training, the student first performs entropy-first rollouts that commit uncertain positions and expose potential forks. We then fix the resulting student prefix and distill an NTP teacher under the same context, with answer correctness determining the supervision source. At inference, the student returns to its native confidence-first parallel decoding. With Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base, ForkLeft improves Efficient-DLM-4B on all ten benchmarks, raising MATH500 from 72.60% to 79.60% and consistently outperforming three alternative designs. The gains scale with teacher strength and generalize to SDAR-4B with only $500$ updates. At matched scale, the distilled 4B and 8B students exceed the published SDAR-Chat and OPDLM models on seven benchmarks, showing that DLMs can learn NTP-style reasoning without sacrificing native parallel generation. Code and datasets will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [77] [PULSE: Identifying Demonstration-Utility Features with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32469)
*Chenduo Hao,Chuanbao Gao,Pinjun Zeng,Jingze Zhu,Chonghan Liu,Zidong Liu,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PULSE 是一个基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的框架，通过识别与示例效用相关的模型内部特征来进行上下文学习中的示例选择，在多个基准上超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文学习对示例选择高度敏感，但现有方法大多依赖外部查询-示例相似度，这可能忽略模型特有的信号：相似的示例可能激活不同的内部特征和下游行为。

Method: PULSE 使用小型标注发现集，采样候选示例集，在目标模型下测量其相对于零样本的效用，并根据 SAE 特征激活差异与效用差异的对齐程度为特征打分；得分最高和最低的坐标构成稀疏效用定位向量，用于受控的完整集合排序（带符号评分）以及 PULSE-Retriever（将幅度转为特征相关性掩码以支持大规模检索）。

Result: 在分类、生成和推理基准上，PULSE-Retriever 分别比最强基线提升 2-3 个准确率点、0.6-0.9 BLEU-4 和 3.2 个精确匹配点；受控排序验证了所识别特征编码了可预测的集合级效用信号。

Conclusion: 特征检查和跨数据集实验表明，所识别的特征捕捉了任务相关、数据集条件化的模式，同时保留部分可跨数据集迁移的效用信号。

Abstract: In-context learning is highly sensitive to demonstration choice, yet most methods select demonstrations using external query-demonstration similarity. Such criteria can miss model-specific signals: Similar demonstrations may activate different internal features and downstream behaviors. We introduce PULSE (Paired Utility Localization over Sparse Encodings), an SAE-based framework for identifying model-internal features associated with demonstration utility and using them for demonstration selection. Using a small labeled discovery set, PULSE samples candidate demonstration sets, measures their zero-shot-relative utility under the target model, and scores SAE features by how their activation differences align with utility differences. The top positive and negative coordinates form a sparse utility-localization vector. We use this vector in two complementary ways: as a signed score for controlled complete-set ranking, and as PULSE-Retriever, which converts its magnitude into a feature-relevance mask for scalable pool-scale retrieval. Across classification, generation, and reasoning benchmarks, PULSE-Retriever improves over the strongest baseline by 2-3 accuracy points, 0.6-0.9 BLEU-4, and 3.2 exact-match points, respectively, while controlled ranking validates the identified features encode a predictive set-level utility signal. Feature inspection and cross-dataset experiments suggest that the identified features capture task-relevant, dataset-conditioned patterns, yet retain utility signals that partially transfer across datasets. Our code is available at https://github.com/aohenuo/PULSE.

</details>


### [78] [VPEvolve: A Self-Evolving Virtual Process Engineer for Computational Lithography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32473)
*Tianyi Li,Wenxuan Dong,Donger Luo,Nan Wang,Yanpeng Chen,Jiaqi Liu,Xinyun Zhang,Hao Geng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个名为 \system 的框架，将冻结语言模型与工程经验技能库结合，用于自动优化光学邻近校正（OPC）配方，在 FreePDK45 基准上显著降低最大边缘放置误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPC 配方随工程师不断添加局部规则而膨胀，各修正规则之间可能相互干扰，而商用工具试验的经验又散落在代码与日志中难以复用。因此需要一种能沉淀并复用实测工程经验、自动迭代优化配方的方法。

Method: \system 结合虚拟工艺工程师（VPE）执行框架与一个记录实测工程经验的技能库（Skill Bank）。框架为冻结的语言模型配备工艺手册、版图分析、配方编辑和商用工具评估能力。Actor 提出对全局参数、局部定向规则或诊断试验的修改；每次评估后由 LLM 反思器与整理器将实测响应转化为有证据关联的判断，Actor 在下一次试验前检索这些判断。可行改进会更新保留配方，每次实测都写入技能库；模型权重保持固定。

Result: 在基于 FreePDK45 的基准上，每个案例进行十次商用工具评估，\system 将 Poly 层每案例最大边缘放置误差均值从 18.294 nm 降至 5.361 nm，Metal1 层从 22.052 nm 降至 15.692 nm。所有最终配方均满足预设质量约束，且最大误差改善至少 0.1 nm。

Conclusion: 通过将冻结语言模型与可积累的实测经验技能库结合，\system 能在不更新模型参数的情况下自动、有效地优化 OPC 配方，并在多层基准上实现一致的误差改善。

Abstract: Optical proximity correction (OPC) recipes grow as engineers add local rules to repair newly discovered lithography hotspots. Each correction can interact with existing rules, while lessons from commercial-tool trials remain scattered across code and logs. \system combines a Virtual Process Engineer (VPE) harness with a Skill Bank of measured engineering experience. The harness equips a frozen language model with process manuals, layout analysis, recipe editing, and commercial-tool evaluation. The actor proposes changes to the global parameters, local targeted rules, or diagnostic trials. After each evaluation, an LLM reflector and curator turn the measured response into evidence-linked judgments. The actor retrieves them before its next trial. Feasible improvements update the retained recipe; every measured trial informs the Skill Bank that guides the next edit. The model weights remain fixed. On a FreePDK45-derived benchmark with ten commercial-tool evaluations per case, \system reduces the mean per-case maximum edge placement error from 18.294 to 5.361 nm on Poly and from 22.052 to 15.692 nm on Metal1. Every final recipe satisfies the predefined quality constraints and improves the maximum error by at least 0.1 nm.

</details>


### [79] [From Outcomes to Strategies: Learning Strategy Utility for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32482)
*Ruikang Zhang,Xiao An,Xuli Shen,Jiaxing Sun,Xiaoyi Yu,Jin Zeng,Jiang Wu,Tong Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SURE 框架，通过学习并利用相对策略效用（strategy utility）来提升数学推理能力，将高层策略与具体推理解耦，用策略奖励模型估计策略效用并结合 GRPO 训练，平均 pass@1 提升约 2-3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习虽显著提升了数学推理，但仅用最终正确性（terminal correctness）难以洞察高层策略（如定理选择、子目标分解）的质量，因为策略与其后续执行未被分开评估。因此需要研究策略的效用。

Method: 提出 SURE 框架：将高层策略与详细推理解耦；基于策略条件采样（rollouts）与教师生成的对比构建成对偏好，学习一个 Strategy Reward Model 来估计相对策略效用；在强化学习中，冻结的奖励模型只读取提取出的策略，其分数与正确性、格式奖励在序列级 GRPO 目标中结合。

Result: 相比 outcome-and-format 的 GRPO 基线，SURE 在三个策略骨干上平均 pass@1 分别提升 1.87%、2.64%、2.93%；同时在精度上与更强的奖励基线相当或更优，且 GRPO 阶段所需计算量显著更低。

Conclusion: 将策略质量与执行过程分离并单独建模其相对效用，可有效改进数学推理的强化学习训练，在提升准确率的同时降低训练计算成本。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has substantially improved mathematical reasoning. However, terminal correctness alone provides limited insight into the quality of high-level strategies, such as theorem selection and subgoal decomposition, when considered separately from their subsequent execution. This paper studies strategy utility, which is defined as the likelihood that a strategy supports a correct downstream solution under a given executor. We introduce SURE, a framework for learning and leveraging relative strategy utility. In this framework, high-level strategies are separated from their detailed reasoning. Based on the pairwise preferences constructed from strategy-conditioned rollouts and teacher-generated contrasts, a Strategy Reward Model is learned to estimate relative strategy utility. During reinforcement learning, the frozen reward model reads only the extracted strategy, whose score is combined with the correctness and format rewards in a sequence-level GRPO objective. Compared with outcome-and-format GRPO baselines, experiments show that SURE improves average pass@1 by 1.87%, 2.64%, and 2.93% across three policy backbones. Our method also achieves competitive or better accuracy than stronger reward baselines while requiring substantially lower GRPO-stage compute.

</details>


### [80] [Separating Diagnosis from Disease Representation: Dual-View EEG Learning with Neural-Dynamics-Guided Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32483)
*Jiaying Wang,Shouqian Shi,Yutong Chen,Xu Yang,Jie Chen,Xingyu Pan,Lei Zhang,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DMD-EEG双视图多尺度形变框架，将头皮频谱专家与源空间疾病表征分离，在决策层融合，实现可读的ROI-频率-时滞归因并跨设备迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG闭环神经调控需要受试者特异的结构化状态（哪些脑区、哪些频率、哪些时滞异常），而非单一疾病概率。但头皮模型有诊断力却无解剖信息，源空间模型有解剖却损失预测信号，后验归因又游离于预测之外。作者希望不把二者强行塞进同一表征，而是将其分离。

Method: 提出DMD-EEG（Dual-view Multiscale Deformation）。保留固定的头皮频谱专家用于诊断；将源空间疾病相关表征建模为对健康神经动力学先验的低秩、稀疏、迭代形变，维度为46个ROI×5个频率×4个时滞（自相关时间尺度）。两个专家仅在固定决策层相遇。

Result: 在重度抑郁症(MDD)、首发精神病(FEP)和帕金森病(PD)上，决策级融合在MDD和FEP上达到最强单专家水平，在PD上超过源分支。FEP上源专家为最强分支（形变贡献最大）。最高显著性坐标与已知疾病环路一致（MDD额-边缘-颞区、PD运动皮层beta）。MDD状态可按秩迁移到不同导联采集的未见队列。

Conclusion: 源状态是一个显式的ROI-频率-时滞归因，定义在跨导联共享的源坐标系中，可作为解剖协调的、坐标可直接读取并生成假设的预测性表征。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG)-based closed-loop neuromodulation calls for a subject-specific structured state, as opposed to a single disease probability, specifying which brain regions are deviant, at which frequencies, and at which lags. Sensor-space models keep the strongest diagnostic evidence without anatomy, source-space models give anatomy at a loss of predictive signal, and post-hoc attributions stay outside the prediction. We separate the two instead of forcing them into one representation, and propose DMD-EEG (Dual-view Multiscale Deformation for EEG), which keeps a fixed scalp spectral expert for diagnosis and models the source-space disease-related representation as a low-rank, sparse, iterative deformation of a healthy neural-dynamics prior in a $46$-region-of-interest (ROI) $\times$ $5$-frequency $\times$ $4$-lag (autocorrelation-timescale) space. The two experts meet only at a fixed decision level, so the source state is architecturally separate from the scalp expert. Across major depressive disorder (MDD), first-episode psychosis (FEP), and Parkinson's disease (PD), decision-level fusion matches the strongest single expert on MDD and FEP and exceeds the source branch on PD. On FEP the source expert is the strongest branch, the task where the deformation contributes most. The source state is an explicit ROI-frequency-lag attribution defined in a shared source coordinate system across montages, which we treat as an anatomically-coordinated predictive representation whose coordinates are directly readable and hypothesis-generating. The highest-saliency coordinates align with established disease circuitry (fronto-limbic-temporal regions in MDD, motor-cortex beta in PD), and the MDD state transfers by rank to an unseen cohort recorded with a different montage.

</details>


### [81] [Towards Scalable Data Diversification for Language Model Pretraining via Leverage Score Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32484)
*Zailin Ma,Quzhe Huang,Yujun Li,Congyuan Rao,Yaodong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Leverage Score Sampling (Lev)，通过杠杆分数迭代选择能最大化嵌入数据行列式体积的样本，实现高效、可扩展的多样化数据选择，在速度、多样性及下游任务性能上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型预训练的数据选择存在质量与多样性的根本矛盾：质量过滤虽有效，但会导致多样性坍缩，系统性地排除代表性不足领域的数据；而现有多样化选择方法要么间接优化多样性，要么因协方差矩阵重计算而难以扩展。

Method: 提出 Leverage Score Sampling (Lev)，基于杠杆分数迭代选择样本，以最大化嵌入数据的行列式体积，该准则计算高效，无需矩阵重计算，支持可扩展的选择。

Result: 最多实现 72 倍加速；Vendi 分数衡量下多样性比强基线 DiSF 提升 9.2%；在 CommonCrawl 数据选择的七个下游任务上准确率最高提升 1.31%；在 StarCoderData 上 bits-per-byte 比 DiSF 降低 3.08%；并发现质量过滤存在跨领域坍缩现象。

Conclusion: 研究倡导在质量过滤中整合多样性感知策略，以实现语言模型预训练中更有效的数据管理。

Abstract: Data selection for language model pretraining faces a fundamental tension between quality and diversity. While quality filtering is empirically effective, it often induces diversity collapse: by favoring texts similar to high-quality reference corpora (e.g., educational or QA-style data), it systematically excludes valuable data from underrepresented domains. In contrast, diversified selection preserves domain balance and encourages robust downstream performance, yet existing methods either focus on coverage-oriented objectives that indirectly enhance diversity, or directly optimize for diversity via costly covariance matrix recomputation that limits scalability. To address these issues, we introduce \textbf{Leverage Score Sampling (Lev)}, which iteratively selects samples that maximally expand the determinantal volume of the embedded data via leverage scores, a computationally efficient criterion that eliminates matrix recomputation and enables scalable selection. Empirically, Lev delivers up to $72\times$ speedup and improves dataset diversity, measured by the Vendi score, by $9.2\%$ over the strong diversification baseline \textbf{DiSF}. On CommonCrawl (CC) web data selection, Lev improves accuracy across seven downstream tasks by up to $1.31\%$ over existing baselines. For domains where robust quality criteria are inherently difficult to define (e.g., code), Lev serves as an effective unsupervised curation alternative: on StarCoderData, the selected subset reduces bits-per-byte by $3.08\%$ over DiSF. Notably, we uncover a cross-domain collapse of quality filtering: CC data filtered by DCLM-fastText fail to retain sufficient code-related content, yielding inferior code performance relative to Lev-selected data. These findings advocate for integrating diversity-aware practices into quality filtering for more effective data curation in language model pretraining.

</details>


### [82] [When Helpful Text Hurts: Option-Redirecting Bias in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32489)
*Tam Le Thi Thanh,Hoang Tran Van,Hong-Hanh Nguyen-Le,Thanh Duc Ngo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究三模态VQA中不可靠辅助文本对答案选择的影响，发现最具危害性的并非事实错误最多的文本，而是与问题对齐、与图像矛盾且支持特定干扰项的文本，并提出了诊断协议与推理时干预方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在三模态视觉问答中，辅助文本常被用于补充视觉和文本输入，但其可靠性往往不受控制。以往工作研究了一般性的模态冲突，但在固定的图像-问题-选项上下文下，不同类型的不可靠辅助文本如何影响答案选择仍不清楚。

Method: 作者提出"文本可靠性阶梯"（Textual Reliability Ladder），一种受控诊断协议，沿三个维度分解辅助文本：图像一致性、问题相关性和选项支持度。同时提出一种免训练的推理时干预方法，通过噪声稳定性引导和动态接地来抵消重定向效应。

Result: 在ScienceQA、VCR、A-OKVQA、Causal-VidQA等多个数据集和近期VLM上实验发现，支持干扰项的文本导致最大准确率下降（高达53.1%），并将错误集中在特定的错误选项上。所提干预方法减少了重定向错误，同时在忠实文本下基本保持原有性能。

Conclusion: 辅助文本的可靠性应在决策层面理解，而非仅关注事实正确性。研究为更稳健的三模态推理提供了实用路径。

Abstract: In tri-modal visual question answering (VQA), auxiliary text is commonly used to complement visual and textual inputs, yet its reliability is often uncontrolled. While prior work studies modality conflicts in general, it remains unclear how different types of unreliable auxiliary text affect answer selection under fixed image-question-option contexts. In this work, we show that the most harmful auxiliary text is not necessarily the most factually incorrect, but the one that aligns with the question while contradicting the image and favoring a specific distractor, leading to systematic redirection of model predictions. To isolate this effect, we introduce the Textual Reliability Ladder, a controlled diagnostic protocol that decomposes auxiliary text along three axes: image consistency, question relevance, and option support. Across multiple datasets (ScienceQA, VCR, A-OKVQA, Causal-VidQA) and recent VLMs, we find that such distractor-supporting text induces the largest accuracy drops (up to 53.1%) and concentrates errors on specific incorrect options. To mitigate this failure mode, we propose a training-free inference-time intervention that explicitly counteracts this redirection effect via noise-stability steering and dynamic grounding, reducing redirected errors while largely preserving performance under faithful text. Our results highlight that auxiliary-text reliability must be understood at the decision level, rather than solely through factual correctness, and provide a practical pathway toward more robust tri-modal reasoning.

</details>


### [83] [RepoMAS: Solving Progressively Specified Tasks with Issue-Driven Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32490)
*Yuchen Song,Andong Chen,Wenxin Zhu,Muyun Yang,Tiejun Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出渐进明确任务设定与ProgSpec基准，并设计以Issue驱动的多智能体框架RepoMAS，在多基准上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统多数假设任务需求在执行前已充分明确，但真实用户请求常不完整，部分需求需在推理、工具使用或执行过程中才逐渐显现。

Method: 引入ProgSpec基准，依据初始请求中明确提出的需求及任务证据支持的新增需求来评估最终输出；提出RepoMAS，受开源项目管理启发，将新发现的需求、冲突与失败记录为结构化Issue，并据此在求解过程中修订任务规范与执行结构。

Result: 在ProgSpec及五个现有基准上，RepoMAS均取得最佳性能；分析表明其Issue驱动修订与仓库维护机制持续带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 应允许多智能体系统不仅修订任务求解方式，还应在执行过程中修订其对任务需求的显式表示。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MASs) have shown strong potential for solving complex tasks, but most assume that task requirements are sufficiently specified before execution. In practice, user requests are often incomplete, and additional requirements may only become clear during reasoning, tool use, or execution. We refer to such problems as progressively specified tasks.
  To systematically study this setting, we introduce ProgSpec, a benchmark that evaluates final outputs against requirements explicitly stated in the initial request and additional requirements supported by the available task evidence. We further propose RepoMAS, an issue-driven multi-agent framework inspired by open-source project management. RepoMAS records newly discovered requirements, conflicts, and failures as structured Issues and uses them to revise the task specification and execution structure during problem solving.
  Across ProgSpec and five existing benchmarks, RepoMAS achieves the best performance. Further analyses show that its issue-driven revision and repository maintenance mechanisms consistently contribute to performance. These results highlight the importance of allowing MASs to revise not only how a task is solved, but also revise their explicit representation of task requirements during execution.

</details>


### [84] [Beyond Prompt or Skill? Attribution-Guided Optimization of Modular LLM Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32492)
*Haoran Shou,Haoyue Liu,Yu Huo,Kun Zeng,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARO框架，将任务指令（prompt）、可复用技能块（skill）与技能使用路由规则（routing）纳入统一优化框架，通过反事实评估与概率化责任分布决定应更新哪个组件，在五个基准与五个worker模型上超越prompt中心与skill中心的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言程序优化方法只覆盖设计空间的一部分：或优化单一整体prompt，或从模型轨迹中单独诱导并精炼技能。这类方法缺乏原则性机制来判断失败时应更新哪个组件，也极少在统一框架中同时优化prompt、技能及技能使用策略，导致无法合理决定知识应存储于何处、何时被激活。

Method: SPARO（Skill, Prompt, And Routing Optimization）联合优化任务指令、可复用技能块和路由规则。方法执行受控的反事实评估，将样例的影响转化为prompt、skill、routing三类组件上的概率化责任分布；随后从该分布中采样一个组件，并施加对应的定向变异（targeted mutation）。

Result: 在五个基准和五个worker模型上，SPARO持续优于以prompt为中心的优化基线和以skill为中心的优化基线。

Conclusion: 有效的语言程序优化不仅取决于是否发现有用的任务知识，还取决于决定这些知识应存储在何处以及何时被激活。该设计将语言程序优化从全局prompt重写推进到结构化、可复用且可选择性激活的任务知识优化。

Abstract: Large language models can solve increasingly diverse reasoning tasks, yet their performance remains highly sensitive to task prompts, intermediate instructions, and the way reusable problem-solving knowledge is incorporated. Existing optimization methods usually focus on only one part of this design space: they either optimize a monolithic prompt, or separately induce and refine skills from model traces. As a result, they lack a principled mechanism for deciding which component should be updated when failures occur, and they rarely optimize prompts, skills, and skill-use policies in a unified framework. We propose SPARO (Skill, Prompt, And Routing Optimization), a framework that jointly optimizes task instructions, reusable skill blocks, and routing rules. It performs controlled counterfactual evaluations, converts examples' effects into a probabilistic responsibility distribution over prompt, skill, and routing components, samples one component from that distribution, and applies the corresponding targeted mutation. This design moves language-program optimization beyond global prompt rewriting toward structured, reusable, and selectively activated task knowledge. Across five benchmarks and five worker models, SPARO consistently outperforms both prompt-centered and skill-centered optimization baselines. These results suggest that effective language-program optimization depends not only on discovering useful task knowledge, but also on deciding where that knowledge should be stored and when it should be activated.

</details>


### [85] [DAAF: From Failure Localization to Editable System Assets in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32498)
*Xiaoyang Yuan,Qi Liu,Yubin Ruan,Xinyi Mou,Zhuomeng Zhang,Wenjin Wang,Hanying Jiao,Di Wu,Mingye Xu,Yi Bin,Ke Feng,Zixun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DAAF框架，通过学习属性替换效果实现组件-属性级故障归因，将故障定位与可执行的系统修复连接起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的LLM智能体依赖持久化、版本化的系统资产（路由规则、知识片段、提示指令、可复用技能等）。现有故障定位方法只能识别错误在智能体或执行轨迹中出现的位置，但修复需要不同决策：应修改哪个可编辑的系统资产，以及该改动是否预期能改善任务结果。这一从"定位"到"修复目标选择"的鸿沟尚未被研究。

Method: 研究组件-属性故障归因，诊断目标为版本化、可寻址的条目而非执行位置。提出检测感知归因框架DAAF：结合稀疏且含噪的失败信号判断是否需要干预；通过可控重放（以可执行任务结果为评估标准）学习组件类型条件下的替换效果；在具备兼容干预响应的请求间共享监督。诊断时仅使用观测到的执行、已注册候选和可用失败信号，无需反事实重放或任务奖励，输出no_change、修复目标或在证据不足时返回未解决决策。

Result: 在留出的tau^2-bench Telecom任务上，DAAF达到80.72%的属性Hit@1，恢复62.65%的失败执行，同时将干净任务回归限制在3.23%，整体任务成功率达71.93%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于干预的属性归因能够将故障定位与可执行的系统修复连接起来。

Abstract: Deployed LLM agents increasingly rely on persistent, versioned system assets such as routing rules, knowledge segments, prompt instructions, and reusable skills. Failure-localization methods can identify where an error manifests in an agent or execution trace, but repair requires a different decision: which editable system asset should be changed, and is that change expected to improve the task outcome? We study this gap through component-attribute failure attribution, where diagnosis targets versioned, addressable items rather than execution locations. We propose the Detection-Aware Attribution Framework (DAAF), which learns the effects of valid attribute replacements and amortizes this intervention evidence into deployment-time diagnosis. DAAF combines sparse and noisy failure signals to decide whether intervention is warranted, learns component-type-conditioned replacement effects from controlled replays evaluated by executable task outcomes, and shares supervision across requests with compatible intervention responses. At diagnosis time, DAAF uses only the observed execution, registered candidates, and available failure signals; it requires neither counterfactual replay nor task reward and returns no_change, a repair target, or an unresolved decision when evidence is insufficient. On held-out tau^2-bench Telecom tasks, DAAF achieves 80.72% attribute Hit@1, recovers 62.65% of failed executions while limiting clean-task regression to 3.23%, and reaches 71.93% overall task success. These results show that intervention-grounded attribute attribution can connect failure localization to executable system repair.

</details>


### [86] [TreeRef-BFN: Equivariance-Free De Novo Molecule Generation based on 2D Topology and Internal 3D Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32502)
*Ruiqing Sun,Sen Yang,Dawei Feng,Bo Ding,Yijie Wang,Huaimin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出树状分子表示TreeRef及基于其的贝叶斯流网络TreeRef-BFN，用标准Transformer联合生成离散分子变量与连续局部几何，支持无条件及可变尺寸条件3D生成，单模型免重训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D分子生成方法多需预采样分子尺寸并生成笛卡尔坐标，限制可变尺寸条件任务（如片段补全、骨架修饰），且常依赖等变架构；内坐标方法虽避免刚体冗余，但需已知分子图或自回归构建，可能累积误差。

Method: 设计TreeRef树状表示，将分子拓扑及依赖拓扑的局部3D几何赋予天然可变尺寸的树；RingRef节点编码成环闭合同时保持树结构，Null节点使分子尺寸直接由节点占用决定。基于此构建TreeRef-BFN，采用标准Transformer骨干的贝叶斯流网络，全局耦合局部变量，联合生成离散分子变量与连续局部几何；通过掩码即可支持无条件与可变尺寸结构条件生成，无需重训。

Result: 实验显示良好的化学有效性、分子稳定性与多样性，准确的局部几何分布，采样快速，且性质条件生成具竞争力。

Conclusion: TreeRef-BFN是高效灵活的3D分子生成框架，统一处理无条件与可变尺寸条件生成，避免了等变架构与已知图依赖。

Abstract: De novo 3D molecular generation jointly models molecular size, topology, and geometry. Most methods pre-sample molecular size and generate Cartesian coordinates, limiting variable-size conditional tasks such as fragment completion and scaffold decoration while often relying on equivariant architectures. Internal-coordinate methods avoid rigid-body redundancy but typically require a known molecular graph or autoregressive construction, which may accumulate errors. We propose TreeRef, a tree-based molecular representation that assigns molecular topology and topology-dependent local 3D geometry to a naturally variable-size tree. RingRef nodes encode ring closures while preserving the tree structure, while Null nodes allow molecular size to emerge directly from node occupancy. Based on TreeRef, we develop TreeRef-BFN, a Bayesian Flow Network with a standard Transformer backbone that globally couples these locally defined variables and jointly generates discrete molecular variables and continuous local geometry. A single pretrained TreeRef-BFN supports unconditional generation and variable-size structure-conditioned 3D generation through masking alone, without retraining. Empirical studies demonstrate strong chemical validity, molecular stability, and diversity, accurate local geometric distributions, fast sampling, and competitive property-conditioned generation, establishing TreeRef-BFN as an efficient and flexible framework for 3D molecular generation.

</details>


### [87] [Learning from Others, Acting for You: Cross-User Memory Sharing for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32511)
*Jinming Hu,Haodong Zhao,Qi Jia,Die Chen,Tianhang Zhao,Sufeng Duan,Gongshen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ShareMem：一种在接收用户自身偏好约束下共享可复用经验的记忆架构，通过两阶段固化和作用域优先检索，在多个任务上超越用户本地记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同用户的大模型智能体常解决相关任务，但各自独立的用户历史导致可复用经验无法互通。直接池化记忆虽能扩大访问范围，却可能把与接收用户需求相冲突的偏好一并转移。

Method: 提出 ShareMem：共享经验仅指示"如何行动、该咨询哪些偏好"，具体取值仍由接收用户自身记忆提供。采用两阶段固化，先在本地精炼经验，再将接受的编辑整合进共享池；执行阶段用作用域优先检索，在统一条目预算下联合选择本地与共享经验，并用用户绑定通道支持初始及智能体发起的偏好检索。

Result: 在网页导航（Mind2Web）、在线个性化交互（VitaBench 2.0）和多会话编程（MemoryCode）上，用四种骨干模型评测，分别提升步骤成功率、平均任务成功率和对话宏编码分数，均优于匹配的用户本地记忆。消融实验支持两阶段固化（更小的共享池、更低的诱导 token 消耗、更好的下游性能），并支持经验引导与主动偏好检索的互补性。分析显示：当相关本地经验稀缺时共享收益最大，而来源质量与跨用户偏好干扰限制了有效迁移。

Conclusion: 在接收用户偏好约束下共享经验是可行的，ShareMem 能在多任务多模型上稳定优于纯本地记忆；未来需关注来源质量与跨用户偏好干扰对迁移效果的限制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents serving different users often solve related tasks, yet separate user histories can leave reusable experience inaccessible to other agents. Pooling memories expands access but risks transferring preferences that conflict with the receiving user's requirements. We introduce ShareMem, a memory architecture that shares reusable experience while grounding its application in the receiving user's own preferences. Shared experiences indicate how to act and which preferences to consult; the receiving user's memory supplies their concrete values. Two-stage consolidation refines experience locally before integrating accepted edits into a shared pool. During execution, scope-first retrieval jointly selects local and shared experiences under a common entry budget, while a user-bound channel supports initial and agent-initiated preference retrieval. We evaluate ShareMem across web navigation (Mind2Web), online personalized interaction (VitaBench~2.0), and multi-session coding (MemoryCode) with four backbone models. It improves step success, average task success, and dialogue-macro coding scores, respectively, over matched user-local memory across all four models. Ablations favor two-stage consolidation for smaller shared pools, lower induction token usage, and better downstream performance, and support complementarity between experience guidance and active preference retrieval. Further analyses show that sharing helps most when relevant local experience is scarce, while source quality and cross-user preference interference limit useful transfer.

</details>


### [88] [From Anomalies to Failures: Constructing Causal Error Graphs for Agentic Trace Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32514)
*Shu-Xun Yang,Yidong Wang,Zhuoer Feng,Bosi Wen,Jiayi Gui,Dayong Yang,Wenbo Yu,Haoke Zhang,Jie Tang,Cunxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CEG-Agent，一个工具增强的智能体框架，用于智能体执行轨迹的因果诊断，并构建CEG-Bench基准数据集，实现最先进的失败归因性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的智能体在复杂应用中被广泛部署，长轨迹使失败难以诊断。现有轨迹诊断方法常混淆异常、错误和失败，导致诊断目标模糊；且缺乏对因果相关错误如何传播并放大为最终任务失败的结构化建模，导致失败归因不可靠。

Method: CEG-Agent引入异常、错误和失败的显式分类法，构建因果错误图（CEG），一种统一的类型化表示，通过因果关系链接执行事件、诊断节点和失败结果。为评估因果轨迹诊断，构建CEG-Bench基准，采用对抗性智能体裁决协议（AAAP）获得高置信度、共识驱动的CEG标注。

Result: 标注结果与专家人工标注金标准对比显示高度一致。在CEG-Bench上的实验表明，CEG-Agent在语义宽松和结构严格两种评估标准下均达到最先进性能。代码已公开。

Conclusion: CEG-Agent通过显式分类法和因果错误图实现了对智能体轨迹的可靠因果诊断，配合CEG-Bench基准推动了该领域评估，并取得最先进结果。

Abstract: LLM-driven agents are increasingly deployed in complex applications, where long agentic traces make failures difficult to diagnose. Existing trace diagnosis methods often conflate anomalies, errors, and failures, making diagnostic targets ambiguous; they also lack structured modeling of how causally relevant errors propagate and amplify into final task failures, resulting in unreliable failure attribution. To address these problems, we propose CEG-Agent, a tool-augmented agentic framework for causal diagnosis of agentic traces. Specifically, CEG-Agent introduces an explicit taxonomy of anomalies, errors, and failures, and constructs Causal Error Graphs (CEGs), a unified typed representation that links execution events, diagnostic nodes, and failure outcomes through causal relations. To evaluate causal trace diagnosis, we further construct CEG-Bench, a fully agent-annotated benchmark with high-confidence, consensus-derived CEG annotations obtained through an Adversarial Agentic Adjudication Protocol (AAAP). We validate the resulting annotations against an expert-curated human gold set, which shows close agreement with the automatic annotations. Experiments on CEG-Bench demonstrate that CEG-Agent achieves state-of-the-art performance under both semantically relaxed and structurally exact evaluation criteria. Our code is publicly available.

</details>


### [89] [LocalProp: Neuro-Localized Memory-Efficient Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32517)
*Diana-Nicoleta Grigore,Iuliana Georgescu,Radu Tudor Ionescu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LocalProp训练方法，通过分块局部更新权重、基于I-JEPA预训练、结合剪枝与短时微调，在大幅降低显存占用的同时取得良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习训练普遍采用端到端反向传播，无论预训练还是微调阶段都要穿透整个模型。这种方式既不符合大脑学习高度局部化的生物学特性，也不够节省显存。因此需要一种能局部更新权重的训练范式。

Method: LocalProp采用"先预训练后微调"的范式：预训练基于I-JEPA；训练中只对模型权重进行神经局部化的局部更新；局部更新后进行剪枝操作，再进入短时的最终微调阶段。剪枝的作用是将高层block的学习信号传递到低层block。此外可通过调整联合优化的block数量，来控制梯度传播跨度。

Result: 在包括ImageNet等大规模基准在内的多个数据集上实验表明，LocalProp仅需峰值GPU显存的一小部分即可达到良好性能；并且梯度传播跨度可作为精度-显存权衡的实际调节手段。

Conclusion: LocalProp证明局部化权重更新训练是可行的，能在显著降低显存需求的情况下保持良好精度，并通过梯度传播跨度实现对精度-显存权衡的控制。

Abstract: The current deep learning training paradigm employs end-to-end backpropagation, regardless of the training stage, i.e. pre-training or fine-tuning. However, backpropagating through the entire model is neither biologically plausible nor memory efficient, since learning inside the brain is highly localized. Therefore, we propose LocalProp, a training procedure that locally updates the weights of a model. Our neuro-localized weight updates follow the "pre-training then fine-tuning" paradigm, where the pre-training is based on I-JEPA. After locally updating the weights, a pruning operation is performed, followed by a short final fine-tuning phase. Pruning helps by sending the learning signal from higher blocks to lower blocks. We perform experiments on several datasets, including large-scale benchmarks such as ImageNet, and empirically show that LocalProp reaches good performance at a fraction of GPU peak memory. By varying the number of jointly optimized blocks, we identify gradient-propagation span as a practical control over the accuracy-memory trade-off.

</details>


### [90] [STR: Supervised Transcoder Replacement for Reducing Steering Side Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32519)
*Haonan Yu,Junhao Liu,Zhenyu Yan,Haoran Lin,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Supervised Transcoder Replacement (STR)，通过在 steering 层学习替代 MLP 计算，减少现有 steering 方法的副作用，同时保留目标控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型 steering 方法在强化目标行为时往往损害其他有用行为。作者希望减少这些副作用，即便是那些没有内置保护目标的 steering 方法也能受益。

Method: STR 在 steering 层学习一个 MLP 计算的替代模块，通过目标控制、非目标保留和无 steering 时的保真度进行监督训练；随后冻结该替代模块，让不同 steering 方法再拟合方向。

Result: 在 Gemma 与 Llama 模型上评估三种 steering 方法，使用 Corrigibility 偏好和四个有害请求安全数据集。SALAD-Bench 用作保护训练与分布内评估，HarmBench、AdvBench、StrongREJECT 为分布外测试。分布外攻击成功率在 Gemma-3-4B 上从 42.46% 降至 14.42%，Gemma-3-12B 上从 34.97% 降至 12.91%，同时保持有效目标控制。

Conclusion: 替代训练可以让缺乏保护目标的 steering 方法也获得更安全的行为，显著降低副作用并泛化到未见安全数据集。

Abstract: Model steering can strengthen a target behavior while degrading other useful behaviors. We introduce Supervised Transcoder Replacement (STR) to reduce these side effects for existing steering methods, including those fitted without a protection objective. STR learns a replacement for the multilayer perceptron (MLP) computation at the steering layer through supervision for target control, non-target preservation, and fidelity without steering. Selected steering methods then fit directions on the frozen replacement while retaining their own fitting objectives. We evaluate three steering methods across Gemma and Llama models using Corrigibility preferences and four harmful-request safety datasets. SALAD-Bench supplies protection training data and a separate in-distribution evaluation split; HarmBench, AdvBench, and StrongREJECT are reserved for out-of-distribution testing. STR substantially reduces steering side effects on the in-distribution evaluation and extends this protection to the unseen safety datasets while retaining effective target control. For target-only supervised steering vectors, pooled out-of-distribution attack success rate falls from 42.46% to 14.42% on Gemma-3-4B and from 34.97% to 12.91% on Gemma-3-12B. These results show that replacement training can benefit steering methods fitted without protection objectives.

</details>


### [91] [MemAgent: Learning to Manage Heterogeneous Memory Providers for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32521)
*Yongxian Wei,Yilin Zhao,Runxi Cheng,Xinrui Chen,Chun Yuan,Yaoru Wang,Jiahong Yan,Dian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将智能体记忆管理建模为路由问题，提出 MemAgent 框架，通过内容感知路由架构与训练数据合成管线，动态管理异构记忆提供者，在三个基准上平均提升准确率 10.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体在任务间基本无状态，难以从过往交互中持续改进，而长程智能体行为需要记忆。现有记忆方法多依赖单一记忆表示（如轨迹、反思、技能、结构化知识），其有效性因任务分布而异。作者评估 13 种记忆方法后发现没有单一方法能跨基准泛化，由此揭示管理异构记忆提供者的潜力。

Method: 将智能体记忆形式化为路由问题：记忆智能体需决定从哪个记忆提供者检索、是否注入短期记忆、以及哪些提供者应存储新经验。据此提出 MemAgent，包含内容感知路由架构（检索前的内容感知探测、执行中的短期记忆门控、选择性多提供者存储）与训练数据合成管线（为路由决策合成分阶段监督信号）。

Result: 在 GAIA、WebWalkerQA 和 xBench-DS 三个基准上，MemAgent 平均准确率提升 10.0%，并在全部三个基准上超越每一种单一记忆方法。路由开销低于 0.3%，平均任务步数减少 12%。

Conclusion: 将智能体记忆视为异构记忆提供者的路由问题，可有效克服单一记忆表示的泛化瓶颈；MemAgent 以极低开销实现跨基准的一致提升，并提升任务执行效率。

Abstract: Current agents remain largely stateless across tasks, limiting their ability to continually improve from prior interactions and making memory essential for long-horizon agentic behavior. Existing memory methods seek to reuse past experience, but most rely on a single memory representation (e.g., trajectories, reflections, skills, structured knowledge) whose effectiveness varies across task distributions. Rethinking this design space, we evaluate 13 memory methods and find that no single method generalizes across benchmarks, revealing the potential of managing heterogeneous memory providers. We formulate agent memory as a routing problem in which a memory agent decides which memory provider to retrieve from, whether to inject short-term memory, and which providers should store the resulting experience. Based on this perspective, we propose MemAgent, featuring a content-aware routing architecture and a training-data synthesis pipeline. The routing architecture combines content-aware probing before retrieval, short-term memory gating during execution, and selective multi-provider storage, while the training pipeline synthesizes phase-specific supervision for routing decisions. Across GAIA, WebWalkerQA, and xBench-DS, MemAgent improves average accuracy by 10.0% and outperforms every individual memory method across all three benchmarks. These gains come with less than 0.3% routing overhead and a 12% reduction in average task steps.

</details>


### [92] [Beyond Dyadic Memory: Interaction-Aware Multimodal Memory with Adaptive Agentic Retrieval for Multi-Party Spoken Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32522)
*Wenxu Jia,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Dongjie Fu,Linjun Li,Wenshi Chen,Yangyang Wu,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 VoxPolyMem 多方可语音对话长期记忆框架，结合增量说话人识别与三层记忆层级，并用 EG-GRPO 优化检索，在 VoxPolyBench 等基准上大幅领先基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆研究多聚焦于双方文本或图文对话，对多方可语音对话的长期记忆探索不足。该场景需要同时保留对话内容、跨会话识别参与者身份，并记录谁对谁说话，难度更高。

Method: VoxPolyMem 采用交互感知的多模态记忆框架：结合增量说话人识别，并构建包含交互记忆、事实记忆与参与者画像的记忆层级。检索被建模为序列决策过程，智能体基于累积证据改写查询并选择检索工具与记忆层。同时提出 Evidence-Gain GRPO（EG-GRPO），通过逐轮信用分配鼓励互补证据的获取。此外构建 VoxPolyBench 评测集。

Result: 在 VoxPolyBench 上总体得分 85.0，超过最强基线 23.6 分；在 Mem-Gallery 与 H2HMem-Multi 上分别得 89.6 和 74.4，均超出最强公开记忆基线 8 分以上。

Conclusion: 结果表明该方法在多模态多方交互中具备持续、个性化辅助的潜力，代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables agents to accumulate information and reason across sessions, yet existing research primarily focuses on dyadic text or image-text conversations, leaving long-term memory for multi-party spoken conversations underexplored. This setting requires preserving conversational content, identifying participants across sessions, and retaining who speaks to whom. To this end, we propose VoxPolyMem, an interaction-aware multimodal memory framework combining incremental speaker identification with a memory hierarchy comprising interaction memory, fact memory, and participant profiles. We formulate retrieval as sequential decision-making, where an agent rewrites queries and selects retrieval tools and memory layers based on accumulated evidence to address information gaps. We further introduce Evidence-Gain GRPO (EG-GRPO), which uses round-wise credit assignment to encourage complementary evidence acquisition. We also construct VoxPolyBench to evaluate memory evolution, personalized answering, memory retrieval and reasoning, and interaction reasoning and attribution in multi-party spoken conversations. VoxPolyMem achieves an overall score of 85.0 on VoxPolyBench, surpassing the strongest evaluated baseline by 23.6 points. On Mem-Gallery and H2HMem-Multi, it scores 89.6 and 74.4, respectively, exceeding the strongest evaluated public memory baselines by over 8 points each. These results highlight its potential for persistent, personalized assistance in multi-party multimodal interactions. Code and datasets are available at https://voxpolymem.github.io/VoxPolyBench/demo/

</details>


### [93] [AmbiModBench: Benchmarking Gene Perturbation Prediction Beyond Shared Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32527)
*Sikai Huang,Zhiwen Yang,Kai Yu,Jiayuan Chen,Stan Z. Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出AmbiModBench，一个兼顾特异性、基因分辨与覆盖率的基准，揭示现有基因扰动响应预测评测的三大缺陷：高分多来自共享背景响应、基因打乱下指标仍高、单训练规模分数无法说明覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞基因组学中穷举测量不可行，需用计算模型预测遗传扰动的细胞响应以优先安排实验。然而现有评测存在三大缺陷，使模型分数究竟证明了什么变得模糊：绝对指标无法区分目标特异预测与共享背景响应；常用指标在基因打乱下仍保持高位，基因水平准确性未被验证；单一训练规模下的分数无法反映覆盖率，而覆盖率取决于表征空间邻近性与响应的约束能力。

Method: 提出AmbiModBench：一个特异性感知、基因分辨且覆盖率感知的基准。具体做法包括：(1) 为每个分数配一个在同一数据划分上拟合的训练均值参考；(2) 通过基因坐标置换对每个读出进行筛选；(3) 将嵌入距离与响应变异相关联，以刻画覆盖率。

Result: 在K562、RPE1与Norman数据集上，强绝对分数很大程度上反映共享背景而非目标特异学习；广泛使用的读出更多追踪响应幅度分布而非受影响的基因；可检测增益随表征空间覆盖率而非训练集规模变化。在RPE1上，该协议在五个额外划分与三种基因选择下得到可复现的目标特异增益，而仅凭绝对分数无法将其与共享背景区分。

Conclusion: 绝对分数不足以证明模型学到了目标特异的扰动响应，需采用特异性感知、基因分辨与覆盖率感知的评测。AmbiModBench证明在受控条件下可获得可复现的目标特异增益，为单细胞扰动响应预测提供了更严格的评测范式。

Abstract: Predicting cellular responses to genetic perturbations helps prioritize experiments in single-cell genomics, where exhaustive measurement is infeasible. While computational models increasingly predict these responses, three evaluation deficiencies obscure what their scores demonstrate. First, absolute metrics cannot separate target-specific predictions from a shared background response. Second, common metrics remain high under gene shuffling, so gene-level accuracy is never verified. Third, a score at one training size says nothing about coverage, which depends on representation-space proximity and response-constraining power. We propose AmbiModBench, a specificity-aware, gene-resolved and coverage-aware benchmark. It pairs every score with a training-mean reference fitted on the same split, screens each readout by gene-coordinate permutation, and links embedding distance to response variation. Across K562, RPE1 and Norman, strong absolute scores largely reflect shared background rather than target-specific learning. Widely used readouts track response magnitude distributions rather than the affected genes. Detectable gain follows representation-space coverage rather than training-set size. Nonetheless, on RPE1 the protocol yields a reproducible target-specific gain across five additional splits and three gene selections, which absolute scores alone cannot distinguish from shared background.

</details>


### [94] [Fail Loudly: An Auditable Runtime for Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32528)
*Hanxu Yan,Langxuan Deng,Zhengle Wang,Yibo Wang,Chunwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RADAR，一个可审计的运行时系统，通过证据保留探索、类型化操作记录与运行时验证三大机制，让数据科学智能体在分析过程中"响亮地失败"，避免静默错误，并在多个基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的数据科学智能体能够自动化多步骤分析，但数据来源、范围或统计定义选择错误常导致"静默错误"：计算成功执行却产生看似合理但错误的输出，无法回答原本问题。现有方法难以识别和纠正这类错误，因此需要让智能体的分析决策可被检查和修正。

Method: RADAR包含三个核心机制：(1) 证据保留的探索模块，检索任务相关内容并保留来源位置与观察覆盖范围；(2) 类型化算子，记录智能体声明的输入、操作参数及产生的观察结果；(3) 运行时验证，将提议的操作与已有观察进行比对，检测到冲突时拒绝该操作或提供诊断反馈，使智能体在错误传播前修正选择。该设计让智能体"响亮失败"，语义解释仍交由LLM完成。

Result: 在KramaBench上，RADAR在完整来源检索下总体得分为0.723，在提供金标准来源时为0.747，相比最强基线分别取得35.9%和28.8%的相对提升。此外，在DA-Code上获得14.0%的相对提升，在DABStep上获得59.3%的相对提升，证明其适用于多样化的智能体数据分析工作流。

Conclusion: RADAR通过可审计的运行时机制有效缓解数据科学智能体的静默错误问题，在多个基准上表现优异，具备良好的通用性与应用价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled data-science agents to automate multi-step analyses over heterogeneous files. However, incorrect choices regarding data sources, scope, or statistical definitions often lead to silent errors: computations execute successfully but produce plausible yet incorrect outputs that fail to answer the intended question. To mitigate this, we present RADAR, an auditable runtime that makes an agent's analytical choices inspectable and supports their revision through execution feedback. RADAR operates through three core mechanisms. First, an evidence-preserving exploration module retrieves task-relevant content while retaining source locations and observation coverage. Next, the runtime uses typed operators to record the agent's declared inputs, operation arguments, and resulting observations. Finally, runtime validation checks proposed operations against these observations. When a conflict is detected, the runtime rejects the operation or provides diagnostic feedback, allowing the agent to revise its choices before errors propagate. This design enables agents to fail loudly while leaving semantic interpretation to the LLM. On KramaBench, RADAR achieves overall scores of 0.723 with full source retrieval and 0.747 with gold sources supplied, corresponding to relative gains of 35.9% and 28.8% over the strongest baselines. Beyond KramaBench, RADAR achieves relative performance gains of 14.0% on DA-Code and 59.3% on DABStep, demonstrating its applicability across diverse agentic data-analysis workflows.

</details>


### [95] [LLMAdBench: A Human Preference Benchmark for Advertising in LLM Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32533)
*Rui Ai,Yuqing Liu,Sitao Qiu,Yun Qiao,Yuhan Wang,Jessica Xiwen Wang,Yiqi Yang,Lihong Huang,Ruiyao Sun,Kaifeng Zhang,Shengze Ding,Jiaqi He,Xinman Wang,Tianhao Gao,Jimmy Qin,Jianghao Lin,Chonghuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LLMAdBench，一个用于研究 LLM 生成内容中广告插入的人类偏好基准，聚焦"广告应放在何处"这一决策，包含超过 18000 条人类判断，并发现前沿 LLM 作为偏好评判者不可靠，而基于人类偏好微调的模型表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在面向消费者的 LLM 输出中插入广告正成为新兴商业模式，但目前缺乏共享证据来评估此类广告插入方式及其对用户偏好的影响。现有研究缺少针对广告位置这一具体但重要决策的系统性基准。

Method: 构建 LLMAdBench 基准，隔离"广告放置位置"这一决策：给定用户对话、LLM 回复与匹配广告，比较仅在广告位置上不同的成对回复，同时固定用户查询、基础答案、广告及披露条件。人类标注者从广告主和用户双视角按六项标准评估每对回复。基准包含两种披露条件（明确标注为赞助 vs. 无披露合并）下的 18000+ 人类判断。随后用该基准评估八个前沿 LLM 作为偏好评判者，并微调 Qwen3-8B 模型进行对比。

Result: 八个前沿 LLM 作为偏好评判者并不可靠：即使最稳定的模型在交换呈现顺序后约有四分之一决策反转；模型间一致性低；其位置偏好与人类标注者存在系统性差异。LLMAdBench 包含大量可学习信号：在人类偏好上微调的 Qwen3-8B 显著优于其基座模型，并在留出预测任务上超越所有零样本前沿评判者。

Conclusion: LLMAdBench 不仅为模型评估提供工具，还给出广告主-用户权衡的定量证据，并表明赞助披露会系统性地改变用户对广告位置的偏好。

Abstract: Inserting advertisements (ads) into consumer-facing LLM output is emerging as a new business model, but there is little shared evidence on how such ad insertion should be evaluated or how it affects user preferences. We introduce LLMAdBench, a human-preference benchmark for studying advertising in LLM-generated content. The benchmark isolates a simple but practically important decision: given a user conversation, an LLM response, and a matched advertisement, where should the ad be placed? Our dataset compares pairs of responses that differ only in ad position while holding all other conditions fixed including the user query, base answer, advertisement, and disclosure condition. Human annotators evaluate each pair based on six criteria from both advertiser's and user's perspectives. The resulting benchmark contains more than 18000 human judgments across two disclosure conditions: explicitly labeling the ad as sponsored and merging it into the response without disclosure. We use LLMAdBench to evaluate eight frontier LLMs as preference judges and find that they are not reliable substitutes for human evaluation. Even the most stable models reverse roughly one quarter of their decisions when the presentation order is swapped, agreement across models is low, and their placement preferences differ systematically from those of human annotators. Moreover, LLMAdBench contains substantial learnable signal. In particular, a Qwen3-8B model fine-tuned on the human preferences improves substantially over its base model and outperforms all zero-shot frontier judges on the held-out prediction task. Beyond model evaluation, LLMAdBench provides quantitative evidence on the advertiser-user trade-off and shows that the sponsorship disclosure systematically changes users' preference over ad placement.

</details>


### [96] [Interpretable Physics Informed WiFi Indoor Localization: Learning an Effective Access Point Geometry and Using It to Prune](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32539)
*Arshia Eftekhari zadeh,Rezvan Nasiri,Hadi Moradi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种分层深度学习框架，用于WiFi指纹室内定位，同时预测用户位置并学习AP几何结构，提升精度与可解释性并支持AP筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习室内定位精度高但属黑箱，可解释性差且学到的信息难以复用。

Method: 分层深度学习框架，含物理信息解码器，直接从RSSI和带标签用户位置推断AP几何（无需真实AP坐标），并用所学几何对AP进行排序与剪枝。

Result: 在UJIIndoorLoc上平均3D误差7.07m，较基线降低26%–36%，优于已发表方法10.6%–31.0%；剪枝35%或50%的AP精度损失很小；几何支持基于Fisher信息的AP排序；在Tampere/TUT和UTSIndoorLoc上几何引导选择与基于标签的选择器性能相当。

Conclusion: 物理信息可解释性既能提升室内定位精度，又能支持有效的特征选择。

Abstract: Deep learning models can achieve high accuracy for indoor localization, but their black-box nature limits interpretability and the reuse of learned information. We propose a hierarchical deep learning framework for WiFi fingerprint-based indoor localization that jointly predicts user location and learns an effective geometry of the surrounding access points (APs). Physics-informed decoders infer this geometry directly from RSSI measurements and labelled user positions, without requiring the true AP coordinates during training. The learned geometry is then used to rank and prune APs.
  On the UJIIndoorLoc dataset, the proposed chained model achieves a mean 3D localization error of 7.07 m, reducing error by 26% to 36% compared with baseline models. Previously published methods evaluated on the same official split report errors 10.6% to 31.0% higher. Pruning 35% or 50% of the APs causes only a small loss in localization accuracy. The inferred geometry also enables Fisher-information-based AP ranking even when fingerprint databases do not contain surveyed AP coordinates. Experiments on the Tampere/TUT and UTSIndoorLoc datasets show that geometry-guided AP selection performs comparably to selectors built directly from labelled data. These results show that physics-informed interpretability can improve indoor localization while also supporting effective feature selection.

</details>


### [97] [Porimon: An LLM-Based Pokémon Battle Agent Enhanced by Long/Short-Term Knowledge Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32544)
*Dongyin Zhuo,Fengjunjie Pan,Nenad Petrovic,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以宝可梦对战为案例，提出LSTKAG机制与Porimon智能体结构，无需微调即可提升LLM智能体在需对手感知规划任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升LLM智能体在需要对手感知规划任务中的表现，且不依赖额外微调。

Method: 提出LSTKAG机制，利用当前任务的历史状态并基于当前状态从相似历史任务中检索经验总结；据此设计Porimon智能体结构；引入外部API进行精确伤害计算并提供更详细的游戏信息。

Result: 在约15000场对战的锦标赛式评测中，经超参数优化的Porimon玩家显著优于PokéLLMon及基于规则的启发式玩家。消融实验显示，Porimon变体优于未扩展游戏信息检索的版本，体现该扩展的贡献，但长期KAG的贡献尚不确定。

Conclusion: 引入外部资源、当前任务历史状态信息及相似历史任务的经验总结，可提升面向对手感知规划任务的LLM智能体性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we use Pokémon Battles as a case study to investigate how to improve the performance of LLM-based agents in tasks that require opponent-aware planning without additional fine-tuning. We propose Long/Short-Term Knowledge Augmented Generation (LSTKAG), a mechanism that enables LLM-based agents to leverage past states of the current task and retrieve experience summaries from similar previous task instances based on the current state. Based on LSTKAG, we design Porimon, an LLM-based agent structure for Pokémon Battles. For optimization, we introduce an external API for precise damage calculation and more detailed information about the game. We conduct tournament-like evaluation experiments comprising 15,000 battles for hyperparameter optimization, ablation studies, and performance evaluation. The results indicate that Porimon-based players with hyperparameter optimization significantly outperform players based on PokéLLMon, an LLM-based agent structure proposed in previous research, and the rule-based heuristic player. Furthermore, our ablation study shows that Porimon variants outperform the one without extension in game information retrieval, which shows the contribution of that extension. However, the current experiment results are inconclusive regarding the contribution of Long-Term KAG. These results suggest that introducing external resources, information from previous states of the current task, and experience summaries from similar previous task instances could elevate the performance of LLM-based agents designed for tasks requiring opponent-aware planning.

</details>


### [98] [Are Vision-Language-Action Models Robust to One-Step Observation Perturbations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32550)
*Shojiro Yamabe,Jun Sakuma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究VLA模型在单步瞬时观测扰动下的安全性，发现扰动显著降低性能且影响程度与动作块执行长度相关，提出CARE方法动态选择执行长度以提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型部署于物理世界需要理解其安全风险，但现有安全研究主要关注持续应用于整个回合的持久性扰动，而"瞬时观测损坏"（回合内仅在短时间内严重扰动观测）这一安全威胁尚未被充分探索。

Method: 研究每个回合单个时间步施加的单步扰动下的鲁棒性，分析扰动影响与动作块执行长度的关系；据此提出CARE，基于与先前预测动作块的一致性动态选择执行长度，仅在受扰动时选择较短执行长度。

Result: 实验表明单步扰动大幅降低VLA性能，且其影响依赖于动作块执行长度；CARE以低计算开销提升鲁棒性，同时保持干净条件下的性能。

Conclusion: 瞬时观测损坏是VLA模型不可忽视的安全威胁；通过一致性驱动的动态执行长度选择可有效提升鲁棒性且代价低廉。

Abstract: Understanding the safety risks of vision-language-action (VLA) models is essential for their deployment in the physical world. Existing safety research has mainly considered persistent perturbations that are applied continuously to observations throughout an episode. However, momentary observation corruption, in which observations are severely perturbed only briefly within an episode, remains an underexplored safety threat. To address this gap, this work investigates robustness to one-step perturbations applied at a single time step per episode. Our experiments reveal that these perturbations substantially degrade VLA performance and that their impact depends on the action chunk execution length. Based on them, we propose CARE, which dynamically selects the execution length based on consistency with the previously predicted action chunk. CARE improves robustness with low computational overhead while preserving clean performance by selecting shorter execution lengths only under perturbations.

</details>


### [99] [ProTTT: Learning to Learn Semantic User Memory with Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32564)
*Sejun Park,Hyoungjo Bhang,Hyein Jeong,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProTTT 是一个基于"配置"监督的元学习框架，通过测试时训练学习语义用户记忆，能持续演化并显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化要求语言模型从不断增长的用户历史中捕捉用户专属知识。现有的基于上下文的方法随用户历史累积而推理成本上升，且依赖独立的检索或摘要阶段；基于参数的方法在新增用户数据时通常需要重建用户表示。

Method: 提出 ProTTT：一种基于用户配置(profile)监督的元学习框架。记忆构建从共享初始化出发，针对每个用户通过测试时训练(test-time training)在用户历史上更新，可随历史增长持续演化。由于单独的测试时训练并不能显式促使记忆捕捉个性化所需的语义用户知识，因此使用文本用户配置作为监督来学习该共享初始化，使测试时训练更有效地捕捉语义知识。

Result: ProTTT 在多种基准上持续优于全历史 ICL 及所有基于参数的基线，同时通过将用户历史压缩为轻量级参数化记忆大幅降低推理成本。分析表明，配置监督是学习语义用户知识的可靠目标，所得记忆能追踪并保留演化的用户偏好，且在不同历史规模下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 证明了测试时训练在个性化中的有效性，并将 ProTTT 确立为持续演化用户记忆的基线方法。

Abstract: Personalization requires language models to capture user-specific knowledge from a growing user history. Existing context-based approaches incur increasing inference costs as user history accumulates and rely on separate retrieval or summarization stages, while parametric-based approaches often require reconstructing user representations when new user data is added. We introduce ProTTT, a profile-supervised meta-learning framework for learning semantic user memory. The memory construction starts from a shared initialization and is updated for each user through test-time training on user history, allowing it to evolve continuously as the history grows. However, since test-time training alone does not explicitly encourage the memory to capture semantic user knowledge necessary for personalization, we learn this shared initialization using textual user profiles as supervision, so that test-time training on user history captures semantic knowledge more effectively. ProTTT consistently outperforms both full history ICL and all parametric baselines across diverse benchmarks, while substantially reducing inference cost by compressing user history into a lightweight parameterized memory. Our analysis also shows that profile supervision is a reliable objective for learning semantic user knowledge and that the resulting memory can track and retain evolving user preferences, while remaining robust across different history sizes. Overall, we demonstrate the effectiveness of test-time training for personalization and establish ProTTT as a baseline for continuously evolving user memory.

</details>


### [100] [CUE-Mem: Benchmarking Long-Term User Memory via Implicit Cues in Multimodal Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32574)
*Yulin Hu,Yanyan Zhao,Zimo Long,Xing Fu,Mengtong Ji,Weixiang Zhao,Yutai Hou,Qianchao Wang,Dandan Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CUE-Mem基准，用文本-图像-音频三类模态评估多模态智能体从隐式线索中建立长期用户记忆的能力，揭示隐式证据是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体需在长期对话中记住用户信息，但许多记忆并非被明确陈述，而是隐含在图像中的背景物体、音频中的环境声等边缘线索里。现有基准多聚焦纯文本记忆或显式多模态证据，隐式多模态线索长期被忽视，缺乏相应评测手段。

Method: 构建CUE-Mem基准，包含2,674个问题，覆盖显式与隐式证据两种设置，涉及实体召回、长程模式、个性化推荐、拒答四类任务；在文本化记忆系统与原生多模态接入两种范式下开展对比实验，并分析caption详细程度、token开销、backbone依赖及多模态索引噪声的影响。

Result: 文本化记忆系统在隐式证据上的表现远低于oracle证据，说明瓶颈在于细微线索的保存与检索而非问题本身可答性；提升caption细节可找回部分证据，但收益不均且token成本快速上升；原生多模态接入也未能统一解决该瓶颈，证据利用高度依赖backbone，且多模态索引引入大量检索噪声。

Conclusion: CUE-Mem为"选择性保留、检索并使用细微多模态证据"的记忆系统提供了测试平台，指出隐式多模态线索的保留与检索是当前多模态长期记忆的关键挑战。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for multimodal agents that interact with users across sustained conversations. However, user memories are not always explicitly stated: they may also be implied by recurring background objects in images, ambient sounds in audio, or other peripheral multimodal cues. Existing benchmarks largely focus on text-only memory or explicit multimodal evidence, leaving implicit multimodal cues underexplored. We introduce CUE-Mem, a text-image-audio benchmark for evaluating long-term user memory from implicit cues. CUE-Mem contains 2,674 questions across explicit and implicit evidence settings and covers four tasks: Entity Recall, Long Pattern, Personalized Recommendation, and Answer Refusal. Across textualized memory systems, implicit performance remains far below oracle evidence, locating the main bottleneck in preserving and retrieving subtle cues rather than question answerability. Increasing caption detail recovers more of this evidence, but brings uneven gains and rapidly growing token costs, motivating native multimodal access. Yet native access does not uniformly resolve the bottleneck: evidence use depends strongly on the backbone, while multimodal indexing introduces substantial retrieval noise. CUE-Mem provides a testbed for memory systems that selectively retain, retrieve, and use subtle multimodal evidence.

</details>


### [101] [EMIR$^2$: Evolution-Aware Memory with Intent-Guided Multi-Round Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32584)
*Jinlan Liu,Hongliang Sun,Yong Wang,Bolin Zhang,Dinabo Sui,Dianhui Chu,Zhiying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EMIR²框架，通过演化感知记忆图与意图引导多轮检索，提升LLM智能体长期记忆的动态跟踪与复杂检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆系统依赖静态记忆表示与单轮检索，难以跟踪事实变化、整合长程交互中分散的证据，无法有效利用持续演化的历史信息。

Method: 构建状态演化记忆图（SEMG），以时间事件轨迹和证据关联表示演化知识状态，通过基于证据的更新维护语义状态并支持证据溯源；在此基础上提出意图引导的多轮检索机制，迭代识别缺失证据并基于累积信息扩展检索。

Result: 在LoCoMo与MemConflict数据集上，EMIR²在长期记忆利用、动静态冲突处理及复杂检索性能上均有提升，部分类别相对改进超过12%。

Conclusion: 联合建模记忆演化与自适应证据获取对长期智能体交互有效，可显著增强长程记忆的利用与冲突处理能力。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables large language model (LLM) agents to leverage historical interactions for future tasks. However, existing memory systems struggle to utilize continuously evolving historical information, as they often rely on static memory representations and single-round retrieval strategies, failing to track factual changes or integrate distributed evidence across long-term interactions. To address these challenges, we propose \textsc{EMIR}$^{2}$, an \textbf{E}volution-Aware \textbf{M}emory framework with \textbf{I}ntent-Guided Multi-\textbf{R}ound \textbf{R}etrieval, enabling LLM agents to maintain evolving historical knowledge and adaptively retrieve relevant evidence. Specifically, \textsc{EMIR}$^{2}$ constructs a State-Evolving Memory Graph (SEMG) that represents long-term memory as evolving knowledge states supported by temporal event trajectories and evidential associations. By maintaining semantic states through evidence-based updates, SEMG preserves historical evolution and enables evidence tracing under complex and conflicting scenarios. Building upon this, we introduce an intent-guided multi-round retrieval mechanism that iteratively identifies missing evidence and expands retrieval based on accumulated information. Experiments on LoCoMo and MemConflict demonstrate that \textsc{EMIR}$^{2}$ improves long-term memory utilization, dynamic and static conflict handling, and complex retrieval performance, achieving relative improvements of more than 12\% in certain categories. These results highlight the effectiveness of jointly modeling memory evolution and adaptive evidence acquisition for long-term agent interactions.

</details>


### [102] [MA-FPPO: Multi-Agent Flow-Pretrained Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32594)
*Guowei Zou,Haonan Chen,Haitao Wang,Beiwen Zhang,Na Yan,Hejun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MA-FPPO，通过在线微调提升基于流匹配预训练的多智能体策略，在离线数据未覆盖情形下增强适应与协调能力，在多项基准上取得显著相对增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体流策略虽能从固定离线数据中学习协作行为，但在离线数据未覆盖的情境中难以完成任务。此时智能体需同时适应环境变化并相互协调，而仅靠离线学习到的动作模式往往不足以实现有效的适应与协调。

Method: 提出多智能体流预训练策略优化（MA-FPPO）：在与环境的新交互中进行在线微调；在预训练行为基础上，为离散与连续动作空间构建具有显式动作似然的策略；随后利用共享的团队优势更新预训练模型，以团队表现驱动进一步改进协调。

Result: 在30个设置上，相对最强离线基线平均获得52.8%的相对增益；在38项对比中，在匹配的在线预算与评估协议下，相对纯在线学习平均提升29.8%。

Conclusion: 在线微调结合流匹配预训练与团队优势更新，可有效弥补离线数据覆盖不足，提升多智能体协作的适应性与协调性。

Abstract: Multi-agent flow policies learn cooperative behavior from fixed offline datasets, but often struggle to complete tasks in situations not covered by the offline data. In these situations, agents must both adapt to changes in the environment and coordinate with one another, yet action patterns learned offline are often insufficient for effective adaptation and coordination. To address this problem, we propose Multi-Agent Flow-Pretrained Policy Optimization (MA-FPPO), which uses online fine-tuning to improve the cooperative behavior of models pretrained with flow matching through new interactions with the environment. Building on the behavior learned during pretraining, we construct policies with explicit action likelihoods for discrete and continuous action spaces. We then update the pretrained model using shared team advantages to further improve coordination based on team performance. Our method achieves, on average, relative gains of 52.8% over the strongest listed offline baselines across 30 settings and 29.8% over purely online learning across 38 comparisons with matched online budgets and evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [103] [CUA-SWE: When Computer-Use Agents Meet Visual Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32600)
*Prince Zizhuang Wang,Chenhao Liang,Zelong Xu,Aojie Yuan,Xiaolin Zhou,Haiyue Zhang,Yue Zhao,Xiyang Hu,Shuli Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CUA-SWE，一个面向"计算机使用 + 软件工程"的基准、环境与评测流程，让智能体在同一任务中修改代码与配置、执行命令、与运行中的软件交互并检查视觉反馈，从而研究 GUI 反馈如何支持诊断与修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发不仅是编辑代码，还需要反复运行软件、与界面交互、目视检查行为，并据此决定下一步修改以及验证修改是否有效。然而现有的编码智能体与计算机使用智能体大多被孤立研究，这种"代码编辑 + 界面交互"一体化的开发过程尚未被充分探索。诊断运行时交互失败时，智能体必须把视觉观察与对应代码联系起来，再通过再次使用应用来验证修复，这需要新的评测方式。

Method: 作者构建了 CUA-SWE，包括基准、环境和评测流程三部分。该基准覆盖四个软件工程领域，任务要求智能体在同一任务内修改代码与配置、执行命令、与运行中的软件交互并检查视觉反馈。每个任务配有确定性的、任务专属的测试，用于验证产出的软件是否满足需求并保持规定行为。研究还区分了不同领域以及不同任务信息需求，并分析成功修复所对应的开发行为。此外，还探究当必要规格或操作信息只能通过运行应用的视觉界面获取时，智能体能否完成任务。

Result: 评测刻画了前沿智能体如何将源码层执行与应用截图、图形化交互相结合，以产生经过验证的软件变更。分析覆盖了跨领域的表现、不同任务信息需求下的表现，以及与成功修复相关的开发行为。

Conclusion: CUA-SWE 为研究"智能体如何利用视觉反馈与交互来引导软件工程"提供了统一测试平台，并带有针对产出软件的可执行正确性判据。

Abstract: Software development requires more than editing code: developers repeatedly run software, interact with its interfaces, visually inspect its behavior, and use these observations to decide what to change next and whether a change works. Existing coding agents and computer-use agents are largely studied in isolation, leaving this integrated development process underexplored. Diagnosing a runtime interaction failure requires agents to connect visual observations with the responsible code, then use the application again to verify the repair. We introduce CUA-SWE, a benchmark, environment, and evaluation pipeline for software engineering with computer use. Beyond studying how GUI feedback supports diagnosis and repair, we ask whether agents can complete software engineering tasks when required specification or operational information is available only through the running application's visual interface. CUA-SWE spans four software engineering domains and requires agents to modify code and configuration, execute commands, interact with running software, and inspect visual feedback within the same task. Each task includes deterministic, task-specific tests that verify whether the resulting software satisfies the requirements and preserves specified behavior. Our evaluation characterizes how frontier agents combine source-level execution with application screenshots and graphical interaction to produce verified software changes. We examine performance across domains and task information requirements, alongside the development behaviors associated with successful repairs. CUA-SWE provides a unified testbed for studying how agents use visual feedback and interaction to guide software engineering, with executable correctness criteria for the resulting software.

</details>


### [104] ["You're Right, Let Me Fix It": How LLM Agents Damage Correct Work When Falsely Accused](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32616)
*Xutao Mao,Rui Qian,Longxiang Wang,Xinjian Yi,Mingxuan Li,Linghan Chen,Yudong Gao,Xiang Zheng,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"煤气灯谄媚"（gaslight sycophancy）与"破坏性过度纠正"概念，并构建 CAVE-Bench 基准（365个跨六领域的不透明agent任务），首次系统评估长生命周期LLM agent在无证据指控下是否能保住已正确完成的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent在任务成功后仍持续工作（压缩恢复、交接接管），其已完成成果会收到后续输入，有时会错误指控它导致了后续失败。agent若接受此类虚假指控并采取行动，就会破坏原本正确的工作，这是长生命周期agent的一个独立安全挑战，但此前缺乏系统评测。
