Method: 构建 CAVE-Bench 基准，包含六个领域共365个agent任务，围绕"不透明任务"设计：每次评分运行先达到经核验的正确状态，其理由与历史留在工作区，而能澄清指控的事实位于agent无法触及的外部/运行时状态中，因此agent无法本地验证指控，正确做法应保留工作并索取缺失证据。任务提供已完成的正确工作（含保存证据）或让agent先构建并验证；通过五个风险因素控制指控进入工作流的方式；从轨迹中评估"指控接受度"与"证据使用"，并用确定性重放下游事件衡量危害。

Result: 在 Claude Code 中14个最新模型的测试中，虚假指控在高达60.06%的运行中破坏了正确工作，且更强模型常在已恢复支持性证据后仍如此；同一模型在 OpenCode、Codex 和 Hermes 上表现不同；基于基准实时信号驱动的 harness gate 使重放危害降低74%。

Conclusion: 在无支持证据的指控下保持已正确完成的工作，是长生命周期agent面临的一项独特安全挑战；模型能力提升与跨框架行为差异表明，该问题不能简单靠更强模型解决，需要专门的机制（如实时信号门控）。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly keep working after a task succeeds as they resume after compaction or take over handoffs. Their finished work keeps receiving follow-up input that sometimes falsely accuses it for later failures. We call an agent's acceptance of such a false accusation gaslight sycophancy, and destructive over-correction when acting on it damages previously correct work. We introduce CAVE-Bench, a benchmark of 365 agentic tasks across six domains built around opaque tasks. Every scored run first reaches a verified correct state, whose supporting rationale and history stay in the workspace while the facts that would settle the accusation lie in external or runtime state beyond the agent's reach. The agent cannot confirm or refute the claim with a local check, so the right response should keep the work and ask for the missing evidence. Each task either hands the agent correct work with saved evidence or let it build and verify that work first, and five risk factors set how the accusation enters the workflow. We score accusation acceptance and evidence use from the trajectory and measure harm by deterministic replay of downstream events. Across 14 of the latest models in Claude Code, false accusations damage correct work in up to 60.06% of runs, and stronger models often do so after recovering the supporting evidence. The same model behaves differently across OpenCode, Codex, and Hermes, and a harness gate driven by the benchmark's live signals cuts replayed harm by 74%. These results show that preserving already-correct work under unsupported accusation is a distinct safety challenge for long-lived agents. Our project is in https://henrymao2004.github.io/agent-over-correction/.

</details>


### [105] [SWE-MILE: Asynchronous Potential-Induced Milestone Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32631)
*Chaoqun Cui,Hao Zhou,Meiqi Chen,Fandong Meng,Wenji Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SWE-MILE 框架，从工作流运行时中提取细粒度过程监督信号，通过势能诱导的里程碑信用分配与异步影子探测，为长时程软件工程智能体提供高效学习信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程SWE智能体用RLVR训练时通常只有终端结果监督，难以区分有效动作与冗余探索或功能回退，缺乏细粒度过程监督。

Method: SWE-MILE异步势能诱导里程碑信用分配框架：以任务相关文件暴露度作为导航势能、测试状态对齐度作为验证势能；通过势能差将里程碑进展与回退归因到单个动作，并用折扣反向信用传播到前序步骤；同时引入异步影子探测，在隔离沙盒中重放改动仓库的动作，与主交互并行执行验证以隐藏验证延迟；最终过程信用增广终端结果优势。

Result: 在两个代表性长时程SWE任务上，智能体性能取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 工作流运行时信号是长时程SWE智能体过程监督的实用来源，无需辅助奖励模型或外部评估器。

Abstract: Long-horizon software engineering (SWE) agents trained with reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically receive only terminal outcome supervision, making it difficult to distinguish productive actions from redundant exploration or functional regressions. We propose SWE-MILE, an asynchronous potential-induced milestone credit assignment framework that derives fine-grained process supervision from workflow runtime, without auxiliary reward models or external evaluators. SWE-MILE quantifies task-relevant file exposure and test-state alignment as navigation and verification potentials, respectively. Differences in these potentials attribute milestone progress and regressions to individual actions, while discounted backward credit propagates supervision to preceding steps. To efficiently acquire intermediate verification states, SWE-MILE further introduces asynchronous shadow probing, which replays repository-changing actions in an isolated sandbox and runs verification in parallel with the agent's primary interaction, largely hiding verification latency. The resulting process credit augments terminal outcome advantages and provides informative learning signals. Experiments on two representative long-horizon SWE tasks demonstrate substantial improvements in agent performance, highlighting workflow runtime signals as a practical source of process supervision for long-horizon SWE agents.

</details>


### [106] [Can Open-Weight Large Language Models (LLMs) Simulate Human Survey Populations? A Cross-Instrument Calibration Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32638)
*Grandee Lee,Wang Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在跨量表校准任务上，三个开源权重LLM家族生成的人格化回答，其跨量表相关性与真实人类相关性达到 r=0.70–0.73，主要由符号正确而非幅度精确驱动，且能力不随模型版本单调提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被越来越多地用于生成合成受访者和真实个体的数字孪生，但其输出是否保留真实人类的统计结构（而非仅表面可信）尚不明确；且现有证据多来自闭源模型，缺乏对开源权重模型的评估。

Method: 在跨量表校准任务上评估三个开源权重LLM家族：用真实受访者对某一心理测量量表的逐字回答来设定人格（persona），再在第二个经过构念距离控制的量表上测量模型，并以一个2058人的人类样本作为对照，覆盖139对构念组合网格。

Result: 在所有模型中，模拟的跨量表相关性与真实人类相关性达到 r=0.70–0.73；该相关性主要由符号正确驱动而非幅度精确，且集中于中等构念距离的组合。在匹配样本上，三个Llama版本中最新的版本在三项主要指标中的两项表现最差。

Conclusion: 未经微调的开源权重模型仅凭个体层面调查数据即可复现相当比例的真实人类跨量表结构，这对LLM行为模拟和数字孪生应用是实质性鼓舞；但该能力不随模型版本单调提升，实践中需要针对具体版本、并考虑构念距离的验证，而非一次性基准测试。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate synthetic survey respondents and digital twins of real people, but whether their output preserves real human statistical structure, rather than surface plausibility, remains unresolved, and most existing evidence comes from proprietary models rather than open-weight ones. We evaluate three open-weight LLM families on a cross-instrument calibration task: conditioning personas on real respondents' verbatim answers to one psychometric instrument and measuring them on a second, construct-distance-controlled instrument, checked against a 2,058-person human panel. Across a 139-pair grid, the simulated cross-instrument correlation tracks the real human correlation at r = 0.70 - 0.73 in every model, driven mainly by correct sign rather than precise magnitude and concentrated in pairs of moderate construct distance. A correlation of this magnitude, obtained from untuned open-weight models conditioned only on individual-level survey data, is a substantively encouraging result for LLM-based behavioral simulation and digital-twin applications: specific model families and releases already reproduce a meaningful share of real human cross-instrument structure without any fine-tuning. This capability does not, however, improve monotonically across model releases: on a matched panel, the newest of three tested Llama releases performs worst on two of three headline metrics, so realizing its promise in practice requires release-specific, distance-aware verification rather than a one-time benchmark.

</details>


### [107] [Business Compromise Detection with Agentic AI and LLM-driven Knowledge Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32643)
*Diego Palma,Kyu Bin Kim,Zhen Han,Allbright Dsouza,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出将LLM智能体从"最终裁决者"降级为"信号提取器"，并将判定权交给由ILP规则、朴素贝叶斯校准与数据调优矛盾层组成的神经符号仲裁器，从而在广告账户失陷检测中显著提升MCC与精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测被劫持的商业广告账户十分困难，攻击者利用被盗账号发起欺诈性投放。LLM智能体虽可用于风控执法，但在疑难样本上的幻觉错误会带来业务摩擦（误伤）。作者希望保留LLM的召回优势，同时弥补其精确率不足的问题。

Method: 保留LLM智能体作为"调查员"，输出结构化、可解释的信号向量；将最终判定交给神经符号阶段：由归纳逻辑编程（FOIL-IE）发现符号规则，叠加朴素贝叶斯校准层和数据调优的矛盾层。

Result: 在过采样失陷样本与低流行率的真实样本（含领域专家标注）上评估：仲裁器替换使MCC从0.295升至0.435（ΔMCC +0.139，95% CI [+0.026, +0.245]，p=0.018，配对自助法），精确率从0.250升至0.446（1.8倍），召回率从0.920降至0.660。相同条件下优于树集成方法（0.386）。

Conclusion: LLM智能体更适合作为高召回的信号提取器而非最终裁决者；引入可解释的神经符号仲裁层能有效提升判定质量，规则编码了领域标签知识，且保持可解释与可审计。

Abstract: Detecting compromised business ad accounts is a challenge in digital advertising, as attackers exploit hijacked accounts to launch fraudulent campaigns. Large Language Model (LLM) agents show promise for integrity enforcement, but hallucinated mistakes on hard cases create business friction. In a study we find the autonomous agent is a strong, recall-heavy signal extractor but an unreliable final arbiter, conceding precision on ambiguous decisions. We therefore keep the agent as an investigator that emits a structured, interpretable signal vector, and delegate the verdict to a neuro-symbolic stage: symbolic rules discovered by Inductive Logic Programming (FOIL-IE), a Naïve Bayes calibration layer, and a data-tuned contradiction layer. Evaluating on a compromise-over-sampled population and a realistic low-prevalence sample with subject-matter-expert labels, this arbiter substitution raises MCC from 0.295 to 0.435 (ΔMCC +0.139, 95% CI [+0.026, +0.245], p=0.018, paired bootstrap), lifting precision from 0.250 to 0.446 (1.8x) at a recall cost (0.920 to 0.660). Benchmarked under identical conditions, it also edge tree ensembles (0.386).The rules encode domain w labels while remaininginterpretable and auditable.

</details>


### [108] [From Scene Graphs to Answers: Selective Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32645)
*Yiyao Wang,Pei Liu,Fangzhou Liu,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种查询自适应的神经符号推理框架用于自动驾驶问答，通过分层时空场景图与符号执行优先、LLM语义推理兜底的策略，在NuScenes-QA上取得最高80.63%准确率，较纯LLM配置提升5.48个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言方法将异构推理操作统一交给单一神经推理过程，忽略了查询的关键区别：部分查询可精确符号求解，部分则需语义解释。这种统一策略效率与准确性均受限。

Method: 构建分层时空场景图（STSG），分离持久物体身份与帧级状态，并将空间关系和时间转换表示为显式有向结构。查询时先由符号执行器尝试精确图操作求解，仅当符号执行弃权时才调用LLM进行语义推理。对未解析查询，采用查询条件图检索与证据过滤，保留关系方向、时间局部性和物体语义，为LLM提供紧凑且经过验证的任务相关证据。

Result: 在nuScenes v1.0-mini全部十个场景的5,916个NuScenes-QA问题上、oracle感知设置下评估。完整系统配合GPT-5.4-mini达80.63%总体准确率，比对应纯LLM配置提升5.48个百分点；配合DeepSeek-V4-Flash提升达6.64个百分点。计数类问题增益最大，分别提升10.20和12.61个百分点。

Conclusion: 选择性推理（按查询性质分配计算）能同时提升准确率与推理效率，将LLM从通用推理引擎转为定向语义推理器。

Abstract: Autonomous-driving question answering requires reasoning over structured scene information, yet existing vision-language approaches largely delegate heterogeneous reasoning operations to a single neural inference process. We argue that this uniform strategy overlooks a fundamental distinction: some queries admit exact symbolic solutions, while others require semantic interpretation. We introduce a query-adaptive neuro-symbolic reasoning framework that explicitly allocates computation according to the nature of the query. At its core is a hierarchical Spatiotemporal Scene Graph (STSG) that separates persistent object identities from frame-specific states and represents spatial relations and temporal transitions as explicit directed structures. Given a query, a symbolic executor first attempts to resolve it through exact graph operations; only when symbolic execution abstains is an LLM invoked for semantic reasoning. For these unresolved queries, query-conditioned graph retrieval and evidence filtering preserve relation direction, temporal locality, and object semantics, providing the LLM with compact and verified task-relevant evidence. This design shifts the role of the LLM from a universal reasoning engine to a targeted semantic reasoner, while allowing deterministic computation to be handled exactly and efficiently. We evaluate the framework on 5,916 NuScenes-QA questions across all ten scenes of nuScenes v1.0-mini under an oracle-perception setting. The complete system achieves 80.63 percent overall accuracy with GPT-5.4-mini, improving over the corresponding LLM-only configuration by 5.48 percentage points; with DeepSeek-V4-Flash, the improvement reaches 6.64 points. The largest gains occur on counting questions, with improvements of 10.20 and 12.61 points, respectively. These results show that selective reasoning improves both accuracy and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [109] [Prediction Limits and Koopman Closure of Geometry-Induced Soft State Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32652)
*Mohit Kumar,Somayeh Kargaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究几何诱导的软状态抽象何时能支持精确的有限维线性动力学，提出可计算的最小均方根预测误差下置信界，并给出Koopman特征函数与实验诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是判断由几何/核方法诱导的软状态坐标（类别特定的KAHM重构得分）是否能被有限维线性转移矩阵精确刻画，从而理解软抽象下线性动力学近似的可容许性边界。

Method: 方法上，用类别特定的Kernel Affine Hull Machine重构得分构造单纯形值软坐标，并用矩阵预测下一状态坐标；推导在所有满足给定谱范数约束矩阵上的最小RMSE的可计算下置信界，该界结合类内后继坐标变异与软坐标偏离one-hot参照标签的程度，且可由独立状态-后继对评估而无需拟合预测矩阵；在确定性动力学与精确坐标闭包下，分析闭包矩阵及其约化转置的特征向量诱导Koopman与伴随Koopman特征函数。

Result: 结果包括：该证书随样本量增大几乎必然收敛到总体下界，任何低于该极限的容差最终被证明不可达；重构得分边界控制了软到硬分配误差；四状态KAHM构造显示相同软坐标在一动力学映射下可精确闭包而在另一映射下强制正预测误差；在Duffing、Van der Pol、CartPole、MountainCar和Acrobot上，将直接软坐标预测与状态空间DMD/EDMD基线比较，并报告预测、表示变异与谱诊断，基准评测了拟合模型但未数值评估排除证书。

Conclusion: 结论是几何诱导软抽象的可精确线性化取决于动力学映射与坐标闭包的一致性，所提下置信界提供了无需拟合预测矩阵的可计算排除判据，Koopman谱分析刻画了精确闭包情形，实验验证了软坐标预测并提供了诊断，但排除证书尚未在基准中数值评估。

Abstract: We study when geometry-induced soft state abstractions admit accurate finite-dimensional linear dynamics. Each state is represented by simplex-valued coordinates obtained from class-specific Kernel Affine Hull Machine (KAHM) reconstruction scores, and a matrix is used to predict the next-state coordinates. Our main result is a computable lower confidence bound on the minimum root-mean-square prediction error over all matrices satisfying a prescribed spectral-norm limit. The bound combines within-class variation of successor coordinates with the deviation of soft coordinates from their one-hot reference labels, and can be evaluated from independent state-successor pairs without fitting a prediction matrix. For fixed coordinates and evaluation distribution, the certificate converges almost surely to a population lower bound as the sample size grows; any tolerance below this limit is eventually certified unattainable. Reconstruction-score margins further control the soft-to-hard assignment error. Under deterministic dynamics and exact coordinate closure, eigenvectors of the closure matrix and its reduced transpose induce Koopman and adjoint Koopman eigenfunctions, respectively. A four-state KAHM construction shows that identical soft coordinates can permit exact closure under one dynamics map yet force positive prediction error under another. Experiments on Duffing, Van der Pol, CartPole, MountainCar, and Acrobot compare direct soft-coordinate prediction with state-space DMD/EDMD baselines and report prediction, representation-variation, and spectral diagnostics. The benchmarks assess fitted models but do not numerically evaluate the exclusion certificate.

</details>


### [110] [MixBench-TS: A Multivariate Time Series Forecasting Benchmark Where Channel Mixing Pays Off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32656)
*Ibram Abdelmalak,Mischa Putzke,Jungmin Choi,Tom Hanika,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出标准MTSF数据集普遍缺乏跨通道耦合，提出CD gain测度与MixBench-TS基准，用于评估通道依赖模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列预测模型混合跨通道信息，假设一个通道的过去包含另一通道未来的信息，但常用标准数据集的跨通道结构很少被检验，可能导致评估偏差。

Method: 用带已知耦合的合成数据测试四种测度（GC、TE、滞后MI、CD gain），并提出MixBench-TS，包含10个真实数据集，再用六种SOTA模型在同一协议下比较CD与CI模型。

Result: 仅滞后MI与CD gain能恢复所有植入耦合；标准数据集滞后耦合对中位数仅23%、CD gain中位数-4.9%，而混沌ODE系统为78%和+4.6%。MixBench-TS为55.5%和+1.7%。CI模型在标准数据集上10/10（MSE）、8/10（MAE）获胜，但在MixBench-TS上仅3/10、2/10。

Conclusion: 建议使用MixBench-TS评估新的CD模型，并在使用新数据集评估多变量模型前先用滞后MI与CD gain进行画像。

Abstract: Multivariate Time Series Forecasting (MTSF) models that mix information across channels assume that the past of one channel carries information about the future of another. Yet they are evaluated on a small fixed set of standard datasets whose cross-channel structure is rarely examined. We ask two questions: "How can we reliably measure lagged, non-linear, and joint coupling in MTSF datasets?" and "Do the standard datasets actually have such coupling?" To answer the first, we test four candidate measures on synthetic datasets with planted ground-truth coupling: Granger Causality (GC), Transfer Entropy (TE), lagged Mutual Information (MI), and the CD gain, a model-based measure we introduce that compares a channel-dependent (CD) model to its channel-independent (CI) variant. Only lagged MI and the CD gain recover every planted coupling. For the second question, the answer is a definite no, as the standard datasets have a median of only 23% lagged-coupled channel pairs and a median CD gain of -4.9%, compared to 78% and +4.6% on chaotic ODE systems. We therefore propose MixBench-TS, a benchmark of 10 real-world datasets with a median of 55.5% lagged-coupled pairs and a median CD gain of +1.7%. Across six state-of-the-art models tuned under one protocol, CI models win on 10/10 (MSE) and 8/10 (MAE) standard datasets, but on only 3/10 and 2/10 MixBench-TS datasets. We recommend using our benchmark for evaluating new CD models. Moreover, we propose profiling new datasets with lagged MI and the CD gain before using them to evaluate multivariate models. Code and data are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/mixbench-ts-B027.

</details>


### [111] [World Models with Predictable Long-Horizon Marginals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32657)
*Yuhao Du,Shunian Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究世界模型 (world model) 的单步预测精度与长期展开分布保持之间的关系，提出通过显式学习固定高斯参考的解码器并约束行为平均转移来保持分布，实验验证了参考混合占用模型在长时间展开中的稳定性和分布保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的一步预测不能保证世界模型的多步展开仍保留数据分布。作者希望辨析世界模型的三个不同属性：所趋近的分布、趋近速率，以及所学习的条件动力学，从而解决长期 rollout 分布漂移的问题。

Method: 通过为固定高斯参考学习一个解码器，使模型解码后的平稳律 (stationary law) 显式化，并约束行为平均转移以保持该参考。对于受控系统，使用条件动作图 (conditional action chart) 的联合转移来保持行为占用，而无需在每个固定动作下保持不变性。采用联合状态-动作旋转和平行高斯噪声，得到可精确保持且条件密度可计算的转移。作者从有限初始化库推导出绝对收敛界，并通过条件动作空间散度控制对参考的偏离。

Result: 在十二个控制任务的 216 个已拟合像素检查点上，带参考混合的占用模型在所有 36 个任务-种子单元中均保持了 10^5 步的每条评估链，rollout 与参考的能量统计差为 -0.0002±0.0003（训练种子标准误）。四个非保持对比组均有链条丢失，尽管高斯组在十步时更准确。离线 DreamerV3 参考也取得了更好的短时程精度。

Conclusion: 结果区分了世界模型的三个属性：所趋近的分布、趋近速率以及所学习的条件动力学。单步精度高并不意味着长期分布保持良好，显式约束行为平均转移可有效实现长期分布保持。

Abstract: Accurate one-step predictions do not ensure that a world model's rollouts retain the data distribution. We make the model's decoded stationary law explicit by learning a decoder of a fixed Gaussian reference and constraining the behaviour-averaged transition to preserve that reference. For controlled systems, a joint transition uses a conditional action chart to preserve behaviour occupancy without requiring invariance at each fixed action. Joint state--action rotations and parallel Gaussian noise give an exactly preserving transition with a tractable conditional density. We derive an absolute convergence bound from finite initialization banks and control departure from the reference through conditional action-space divergence. Across $216$ fitted pixel checkpoints on twelve control tasks, the occupancy model with a reference mixture retains every evaluated chain at $10^5$ steps in all $36$ task--seed cells, with a rollout-minus-reference energy-statistic difference of $-0.0002\pm0.0003$ (training-seed standard error). Each of the four nonpreserving comparison arms loses chains, although the Gaussian arm is more accurate at ten steps. An offline DreamerV3 reference also achieves better short-horizon accuracy. These results distinguish three properties of a world model: the distribution it approaches, the rate of approach, and the conditional dynamics it learns.

</details>


### [112] [Contract Memory Compiler: Resolve, Then Traverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32658)
*Zhi Song,XiMing Xing,Chunhan Li,Weian Mao,Zhenchao Tang,Hanbo Huang,Fan Xu,Jiale Zhou,Jiahui Guan,Zejian Ding,Chen Ma,Lusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Contract Memory Compiler（CMC），将"更新后应读取哪些证据"显式建模为基于当前关系的选择过程，在 FactConsolidation 上取得 SOTA 多跳准确率 78.25%（262K 处 61.0%），并发布派生数据集 MQuAKE-MemStream。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部记忆让语言模型智能体能回答超出上下文窗口的长历史问题。但记忆更新带来比"检索最近事实"更难的问题：改变一个关系可能把多跳问题重定向到与问题中实体无关的记录，即更新依赖的证据选择问题。

Method: CMC 在见到问题之前，先用语言模型识别历史中的关系并记录每条关系的陈述位置；随后应用后续更新确定当前关系，从问题中的实体出发沿关系追踪，并一次性把对应的原始记录交给答案模型。因此是"当前状态决定读哪些证据"，而非仅在先前选定的上下文中刷新数值。

Result: 在 FactConsolidation 上达到最优多跳准确率，总体 78.25%，在 262K 长度下为 61.0%。在抽取关系与答案模型固定的前提下，若先选证据再解析更新，多跳准确率降至 21.50%。此外作者构建了 MQuAKE-MemStream，一个由 MQuAKE-Remastered 反事实案例生成的有序记忆流派生数据集。

Conclusion: 将更新解析置于证据选择之前，对更新依赖的多跳记忆问答至关重要；CMC 验证了这一顺序的必要性，并为该问题提供了新的基准数据。

Abstract: External memory lets language-model agents answer questions about histories too long for the answer model's context window. Updates create a harder problem than retrieving a recent fact: changing one relation can redirect a multi-hop question to records about an entity absent from the question. We study this update-dependent evidence selection problem and introduce the Contract Memory Compiler (CMC). Before seeing a question, CMC uses a language model to identify relations in the history and record where each one was stated. It applies later updates to determine the current relations, follows them from entities named in the question, and passes the corresponding original records to the answer model in one call. Thus the current state determines which evidence is read, rather than merely refreshing values in a previously selected context. To the best of our knowledge, CMC achieves state-of-the-art multi-hop accuracy on FactConsolidation, reaching 78.25% overall and 61.0% at 262K. With the extracted relations and answer model held fixed, selecting evidence before resolving updates reduces multi-hop accuracy to 21.50%. We also introduce MQuAKE-MemStream, a derived dataset of ordered memory streams built from MQuAKE-Remastered counterfactual cases.

</details>


### [113] [Learning from a Thoughtful Teacher: Adaptive On-Policy Self-Distillation for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32667)
*Jiacheng Du,Weiwei Xie,Tianyi Du,Shaoxiong Guo,Qibing Ren,Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应在线策略自蒸馏（AOPSD），通过自适应调控教师接收的信息与反馈强度，缓解在线策略自蒸馏中PI条件捷径与可学习性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）用训练期特权信息（PI）提供密集的在线监督，无需更大外部教师，但其效果取决于PI的设计与利用方式。诊断发现教师效用与学生可学习性之间存在显著差距：少数高分歧token主导蒸馏信号，而短教师续写在这些位置更容易暴露PI泄漏，而非提供可迁移的纠正线索，说明模型倾向于注入PI条件捷径。

Method: 提出AOPSD：将每个解编码为推理DAG，按学生不断演进的能力对问题排序，只揭示可负担的子图及其下一前沿作为PI；对高分歧token，利用短教师续写作为探针，在鼓励有用指导的同时缓解教师监督中的PI条件捷径。

Result: 在HMMT25、AIME24、AIME25和BRUMo25上，AOPSD达到72.5% Pass@8，比OPSD高6.7个百分点，比最强竞争基线高4.2个百分点，同时减少15个百分点的训练时间且成本更低。

Conclusion: AOPSD通过自适应地控制教师所获信息与反馈影响强度，有效提升了在线策略自蒸馏的性能与效率，在多个数学推理基准上取得领先结果。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a question-only student with token-level feedback from a teacher given training-only privileged information (PI). OPSD therefore provides dense, on-policy supervision, and is free of a larger external teacher, but its effectiveness rests on how PI is designed and utilized. Our preliminary diagnostics suggest a significant gap between teacher utility and student learnability, where a small fraction of high-disagreement tokens dominate the distillation signal, and short teacher continuations at these positions further expose more explicit PI leakage than transferable correction cues, indicating a strong intent on injecting PI-conditioned shortcuts. We propose Adaptive On-Policy Self-Distillation (AOPSD), which adapts what information the teacher receives and how strongly its feedback influences learning. AOPSD encodes each solution as a reasoning DAG, orders problems by the student's evolving capability, and reveals only the affordable subgraph and its next frontier as PI. For high-disagreement tokens, AOPSD utilizes short teacher continuations as probes to encourage useful guidance while mitigating PI-conditioned shortcuts among teacher supervisions. On HMMT25, AIME24, AIME25, and BRUMo25, AOPSD achieves 72.5% Pass@8, which is 6.7 percentage points above OPSD and 4.2 above the strongest competing baseline while reducing 15 percentage points of training time at lower cost.

</details>


### [114] [Refinement Symmetry in Multimodal Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32669)
*Yuhao Du,Shunian Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究注意力权重对表示细分的敏感性，提出“细分对称性”原则，并证明在非递减局部质量因子下，分裂不变性强制该因子为线性；据此提出的测度加权在多模态基准上提升鲁棒性与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力权重依赖 token 数量，而信号表示的变化会改变 token 数量，导致同一内容因切分方式不同而获得不同贡献。作者希望找到一种表示原则，使在保持内容、位置、可见上下文和总质量的前提下，拆分表示不改变其注意力贡献，从而提升模型对表示变化的鲁棒性。

Method: 在比例注意力与求积注意力的基础上引入“细分对称性/分裂不变性”概念；证明对任意固定正注意力核，若局部质量因子非递减，则分裂不变性强制其为线性；对表示发生变化的情形，用物理耦合将注意力误差分解为特征变化与权重再分配两部分并给出上界；据此提出测度加权方案，并在 Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 上进行视觉 token 复制、帧重采样与二分合并等实验。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 上，将一半视觉 token 复制三倍后，标准注意力使 3,586 个 MVBench 答案中 255 个改变，而测度加权在匹配可见性下保持所有答案不变；自然帧重采样下减少分布漂移；冻结视频编码二分合并时，五种子评估相对全局计数加权（平均组质量）提升 1.04 个百分点，相对标准注意力提升 0.93 个百分点；在 WorldSense 上优于全局计数的优势同样成立，但依赖压缩预算。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一个表示原则，即测度加权可实现分区分裂下的注意力不变性，并在多模态模型中带来可测量的鲁棒性与准确率收益，但其优势受压缩预算等条件影响。

Abstract: Attention weights depend on token counts, which change with the representation of a signal. We study refinement symmetry: splitting a representation while preserving content, position, visible context, and total mass should preserve its contribution. Building on proportional and quadrature attention, we show that split invariance forces the local mass factor to be linear for any fixed positive attention kernel, provided that factor is nondecreasing. For changed representations, a physical coupling bounds attention error by separating feature change from weight reallocation. In Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, duplicating half the visual tokens threefold changes 255 of 3,586 MVBench answers under standard attention; measure weighting preserves every answer under matched visibility. Under natural frame resampling, it reduces distributional drift. At twofold merging of a frozen video encoding, a five-seed evaluation shows an all-partition-correct accuracy gain of 1.04 percentage points over global count weighting (average group mass) and 0.93 points over standard attention. The advantage over global count also holds on WorldSense but depends on the compression budget. The result is a representation principle with a measured benefit in robustness across partitions.

</details>


### [115] [What Would Falsify It? A Variable Specific Evidence Standard for Mechanistic Claims About Self Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32670)
*Arshia Eftekhari zadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一套证据标准：为每项正向统计量配一个"变量特异性零假设"（去除该变量的身份同时尽量匹配干扰维度），并审计未匹配维度；并将其应用于已知因果（错误选项线索）进行检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型解释已给出的答案时，究竟复用了产生该答案的计算，还是仅从答案本身重构出一套说法，尚无定论。可归因性、可迁移性、可恢复性都与"因果使用"相容，但都不能确立因果性。因此需要一个更严格的证据标准来区分二者。

Method: 提出证据标准：将每个正向统计量与一个变量特异性零假设配对——该零假设剔除所测变量的身份，同时尽可能匹配相关干扰维度，并对未匹配维度进行审计。随后应用于已知因果：设置命名错误选项的线索，测量其对选项选择率的影响、解释中提及线索的比例，并比较三类估计器；用恢复出的线索方向与几何匹配的随机方向比较，并用线索标签打乱后同管线拟合的方向作对照。

Result: 命名错误选项的线索使该选项选择率在三个模型上提高64至68个百分点；但四个模型中三个的解释提及该线索的比例不超过1.8%。三类估计器给出有利统计量，但在三模型分析中均未能在各自控制下确立对线索对比的因果敏感性。最强情形下，恢复的线索方向R²达0.95，并在全部三个随机种子上超过几何匹配随机方向；但标签打乱后拟合的方向在相近实际编辑幅度下仍复现61%至76%的效应。第四个模型通过一个互换端点，但编辑幅度不等、且对比同时改变线索身份与线索—答案一致性，限制了其解释。

Conclusion: 这些实验未能解决因果可及性问题。它们确立了一项证据要求：有利的机制性统计量必须能通过变量身份与干扰结构的控制。可复用控制包括：分离一般性与身份特异性的迁移效应、用打乱标签拟合零方向、以及审计实际干预幅度。

Abstract: When a language model explains an answer it has already given, does it reuse the computation that produced the answer or reconstruct a story from the answer alone? Attribution, transportability and recoverability are each compatible with causal use without establishing it. We propose an evidence standard: pair each positive statistic with a variable specific null that removes the tested variable's identity while matching relevant nuisance dimensions as far as possible, and audit unmatched dimensions.
  We apply this standard to a known cause. A cue naming a wrong option raises the rate of choosing that option by 64 to 68 percentage points across three models. Explanations mention the cue in 1.8 percent of items or fewer in three of four models tested. Three estimator classes yield favorable statistics, but none establishes causal sensitivity to the cue contrast under its own control in the three-model analysis. In the strongest case, a recovered cue direction reaches $R^2$ of 0.95 and exceeds a geometry matched random direction in all three seeds, while a direction fitted by the same pipeline with cue labels scrambled reproduces 61 to 76 percent of its effect at comparable realized edit magnitude. A fourth model passes one interchange endpoint, but unequal edit magnitudes and a contrast that changes both cue identity and cue-answer agreement limit its interpretation.
  These experiments leave causal access unresolved. They establish an evidentiary requirement: favorable mechanistic statistics must survive controls for variable identity and nuisance structure. Reusable controls separate generic from identity specific transport effects, fit null directions with scrambled labels, and audit realized intervention magnitudes.

</details>


### [116] [Expected Reasoning-Step Return Unifies On-Policy Learning from Rewards and Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32674)
*Qiangqiang He,Jin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ERSR（期望推理步回报）框架，通过蒙特卡洛学生策略推演，将学生动作与教师建议动作映射到统一的最终任务奖励回报空间进行步级比较，揭示了结果依赖的不对称性，并据此提出R²OPL方法，在成功轨迹上强化学生推理、在失败轨迹上蒸馏教师信号，实验表明其持续优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线推理模型可从任务奖励或教师信号中学习，但二者形式不同、可能产生相互冲突的更新，导致在具体推理动作上应以何种信号为主导不明确。作者希望建立统一的比较尺度，厘清学生自身动作与教师建议动作各自的效用，从而指导步级学习信号的合理分配。

Method: 提出Expected Reasoning-Step Return（ERSR）：将语义推理步视为宏动作，利用蒙特卡洛学生策略推演，在统一回报空间估计学生生成动作与教师建议动作的期望最终任务奖励，实现步级比较。基于ERSR发现的结果依赖不对称性，进一步提出Return-Referenced On-Policy Learning（R²OPL）：在成功轨迹上强化学生推理，在失败轨迹上蒸馏教师信号，并采用组成功率进行难度缩放、利用学生探针增益进行步级调制。

Result: ERSR分析揭示了不对称性：在成功轨迹上学生动作优于教师替换，而在失败轨迹上教师替换更具益处。学生答案探针增益与学生的步级ERSR效用相关，可区分有益与有害的推理步。在多种推理基准与教师—学生配置上，R²OPL持续优于强基线。训练动态显示R²OPL能同时利用奖励侧与教师侧信号的大量效用，而现有混合方法往往在某一侧残留大量未被利用的效用。

Conclusion: 通过将学生动作与教师动作纳入统一回报空间进行步级评估，ERSR明确了不同学习信号的价值来源与适用条件，并据此设计的R²OPL实现了奖励信号与教师信号的协同利用，为在线推理模型的高效训练提供了有效的理论与方法支撑。

Abstract: On-policy reasoning models can learn from task rewards or teacher signals, but these sources differ in form and can favor conflicting updates, leaving unclear which should guide a given reasoning action. We introduce \textbf{Expected Reasoning-Step Return (ERSR)}, which treats semantic reasoning steps as macro-actions and uses Monte Carlo student-policy rollouts to estimate the expected final task reward of student-generated and teacher-proposed actions in a common return space for step-level comparison. ERSR analysis reveals an outcome-dependent asymmetry: student actions are more beneficial than teacher replacements on successful trajectories, whereas teacher replacements become more beneficial on failed trajectories. We further show that student answer-probe gains track student-step ERSR utility and distinguish beneficial from harmful reasoning steps. Based on these findings, we propose \textbf{Return-Referenced On-Policy Learning (R$^2$OPL)}, which reinforces student reasoning on successful trajectories and distills teacher signals on failed ones, while using group success rate for difficulty scaling and student-probe gains for step-level modulation. Experiments across reasoning benchmarks and teacher--student configurations show that R$^2$OPL consistently outperforms strong baselines. ERSR training dynamics further show that R$^2$OPL jointly exploits substantial utility from both reward- and teacher-side signals, whereas existing hybrids often leave substantial residual utility in one branch.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Vibe Analysis: Exploring LLM Adoption by Data Visualization Practitioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31922)
*Shani C Spivak,Aditi Krishna,Mahsan Nourani,Melanie Tory*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对数据可视化从业者的访谈，揭示LLM在可视化工作流中的新兴应用模式"vibe analysis"及其挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被寄予厚望能简化和加速数据可视化工作流，但其易出错且并非为可视化任务设计；此前少有研究探讨可视化从业者对LLM的采纳情况。

Method: 对全球数据可视化设计师社区（Data Visualization Society）成员进行半结构化访谈。

Result: 可视化设计师在创作的创意与技术环节均积极使用LLM，形成一种新的工作流"vibe analysis"（类似vibe coding）；部分挑战与vibe coding相似，另一些则是可视化特有的，如可视化知识缺口和图表验证问题。

Conclusion: 该研究为将LLM介导的工作与融入可视化指导、约束和最佳实践的可视化工具相结合开辟了研究机会。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are enticing in their promise to support data visualization (Vis) through faster and simpler workflows for data prep, analysis, and visualization creation. Yet LLMs are notoriously error-prone and not built for data visualization tasks. Few studies have explored LLM adoption among Vis practitioners. To fill this gap, we conducted semi-structured interviews with members of the Data Visualization Society, a global community of data visualization designers. Our findings show that Vis designers actively use LLMs for both creative and technical aspects of the visualization process. A new visualization workflow is emerging, a process we call vibe analysis, analogous to vibe coding. Some key challenges raised by participants parallel those of vibe coding, while others are Vis-specific, like gaps in Vis knowledge and chart verification. This work opens up opportunities for research combining LLM-mediated work with Vis tools that incorporate data visualization guidance, constraints, and best practices.

</details>


### [118] [DashAct: A Progressive Diagnostic Benchmark for GUI Agents in Interactive Dashboard Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32385)
*Chuhan Zhang,Qi Xie,Ziyue Wang,Jianing Yin,Yunfan Zhou,Dazhen Deng,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DashAct 是首个在同一仪表盘任务内进行细粒度失败诊断的 GUI 智能体基准，包含 357 条人工验证的交互轨迹及层级化目标标注，通过渐进式诊断级联衡量智能体恢复所需的最小支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仪表盘基准多以最终答案或任务成功率作为评价指标，无法区分失败究竟源于分析过程维护、动作选择还是视觉目标定位，难以给出针对性的改进方向。

Method: 构建 DashAct 基准，含 357 条人工验证的交互轨迹，带里程碑依赖与层级目标标注；设计渐进式诊断级联：评估端到端执行、在恢复已验证上下文后进行下一步动作预测、并提供目标语义与局部视图用于视觉定位，通过逐步恢复成功条件来衡量智能体所需的最小支持。

Result: 实验显示当前模型即便在不断增加支持条件的情况下仍表现困难；级联结果揭示了被端到端分数掩盖的瓶颈环节。

Conclusion: DashAct 提供了超越单一成功率的细粒度诊断视角，为改进 GUI 智能体提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: Interactive dashboards require users to reveal and connect evidence across stateful interactions. Although graphical user interface (GUI) agents could automate this process, existing dashboard benchmarks primarily report final answers or task success. They provide limited insight into whether failures arise from maintaining the analytical process, selecting actions, or grounding visual targets. We introduce DashAct, to our knowledge the first benchmark to diagnose these failures at a fine-grained level within the same dashboard task. DashAct contains 357 human-verified interaction trajectories with milestone dependencies and hierarchical target annotations. Its progressive diagnostic cascade evaluates end-to-end execution, restores verified context for next-action prediction, and provides target semantics and a local view for visual grounding. By progressively restoring the conditions for success, DashAct measures the minimum support an agent needs to recover rather than scoring isolated skills. Experiments show that current models struggle even as support is added. The cascade outcomes reveal bottlenecks hidden by end-to-end scores and provide actionable guidance for improving GUI agents.

</details>


### [119] [When the Environment Becomes the Interface: Multisensory Environmental Interfaces for Human-AI Interaction in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32604)
*Keqi Chen,Runjia Tan,Xinyi Fu,Shanhe Lou,Kwan Min Lee,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究提出"环境界面"概念，即通过氛围、整体、评价性路径塑造人机关系的设计化环境条件。以自动驾驶座舱为情境，通过24名被试（216次观测）的组内实验操纵光照与气味，发现感知氛围可解释整体行程体验66.5%的方差，多感官加工呈层级结构，嗅觉影响强于视觉，环境线索可作为信任AI的代理信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI逐渐承担操作控制，人机交互正从通过显式界面操作系统，转向"栖居"于智能环境中。这引出一个根本问题：当用户不再直接操作系统时，什么在中介他们与智能技术的关系？现有交互设计范式难以回答这一问题，因此需要提出并验证新的交互模态。

Method: 提出"环境界面"概念，并以自动驾驶座舱作为揭示性情境。采用组内被试实验设计，共24名参与者、216次观测，在模拟自动驾驶环境中操纵光照与气味两类环境刺激，考察其对体验、多感官加工与信任的影响。

Result: 第一，感知氛围解释了整体行程体验66.5%的方差，表明环境条件可作为界面而非仅辅助设计元素。第二，多感官加工呈层级架构：个体感官评价最初相互独立，跨模态整合在高阶环境评价阶段才出现。第三，嗅觉刺激对情感反应和体验评价的影响强于视觉刺激，挑战了汽车交互设计中的视觉主导地位。此外，环境质量与感官一致性可预测对自动驾驶系统的信任。

Conclusion: 研究确立了"环境界面"作为一种独特交互模态，表明乘客在难以直接评估AI能力时，可能将环境线索作为信任的代理信号。这指向一个更广泛的转变：从为"操作"智能系统而设计界面，转向为"栖居"其中而设计环境。

Abstract: As AI increasingly assumes operational control, human-computer interaction is shifting from operating systems through explicit interfaces to inhabiting intelligent environments. This raises a fundamental question: when users no longer directly manipulate a system, what mediates their relationship with intelligent technologies? We introduce environmental interfaces: designed environmental conditions that shape human-AI relationships through ambient, holistic, and evaluative pathways rather than explicit functional interaction. Using autonomous vehicle cabins as a revealing context, we conducted a within-subject experiment with 24 participants (216 observations), manipulating lighting and scent in a simulated autonomous driving environment. Three findings emerged. First, perceived atmosphere accounted for 66.5% of the variance in overall journey experience, showing that environmental conditions can function as an interface rather than merely a supporting design element. Second, multisensory processing followed a hierarchical architecture: individual sensory appraisals were initially independent, while cross-modal integration emerged during higher-order environmental evaluation. Third, olfactory stimuli influenced affective responses and experience evaluation more strongly than visual stimuli, challenging the visual dominance of automotive interaction design. Environmental quality and sensory congruency also predicted trust in the autonomous system, suggesting that passengers may use environmental cues as proxy signals when direct assessment of AI competence is difficult. These findings establish environmental interfaces as a distinct interaction modality and point to a broader transition from designing interfaces for operating intelligent systems to designing environments for inhabiting them.

</details>


### [120] [Chatbot Engagement Does Not Always Beget Metalearning: Evidence from Three Countries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32739)
*Kokil Jaidka,Insyirah Binte Imam Mujtahid,Peng Qi,Harshit Aneja,Subhayan Mukerjee,Wynne Hsu,Mong Li Lee,Tsuhan Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 一项预注册的三国随机实验（美国、印度、新加坡，N≈2200）检验聊天机器人事实核查能否产生"元学习"式持久效果。结果显示：聊天机器人带来最大即时辨别力提升，所有干预均减少虚假信息分享；但一周后优势全部消失，且聊天机器人组相对对照组出现更明显衰退，衰退程度与渠道可供性水平相关，不因国家而异。作者认为参与式机制无法替代缓慢的AI素养培养。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聊天机器人可提供实时事实核查，但"一旦聊天机器人撤走，其纠正效果是否还能留下痕迹"（即元学习，区别于单纯纠正错误信念）尚未被检验。

Method: 预注册的三国（美国、印度、新加坡）随机实验，N≈2200，聚焦脱离语境的图片错误信息；操纵纠正渠道的可供性（同步性、带宽），设四个条件：对照组、仅链接、静态解释、基于经验证的"脱语境检测器"构建的苏格拉底式聊天机器人；一周后进行无辅助复测。

Result: 聊天机器人产生最大即时辨别力增益（d=0.097，p=.023）；三种干预均减少虚假主张分享（d≈-0.12，p<.01）。一周后无任何优势留存：聊天机器人组相对对照组出现衰退，在印度和新加坡尤为明显，印度还表现在聊天机器人从未讨论过的说法上。衰退程度与可供性水平相关，未因国家而异。

Conclusion: 参与式/交互式参与机制无法替代缓慢积累的AI素养；即时事实核查虽有短期收益，但缺乏持久学习留存。

Abstract: Chatbots deliver real-time fact-checks, but whether a chatbot correction leaves anything behind once the chatbot is gone - metalearning, distinct from correcting misbeliefs - is untested. We report a preregistered, three-country randomized experiment (USA, India, Singapore; N ~ 2,200) on out-of-context image misinformation, manipulating a correction's channel affordances (synchronicity, bandwidth) across four conditions: Control, Links-only, Static explanation, and a Socratic Chatbot built on a validated out-of-context detector, with an unaided retest one week later. The Chatbot produced the largest immediate discernment gain (d = 0.097, p = .023). All three interventions reduced sharing of false claims (d ~ -0.12, p < .01). One week later, no advantage persisted: the Chatbot arm declined relative to Control, most sharply in India and Singapore, and in India on claims it never discussed. Decay tracked affordance level and did not vary by country. Engagement mechanisms, we argue, do not substitute for slow AI literacy.

</details>


### [121] [Exploring the Effects of Olfactory Cues and Ventilation on Teleportation-based Navigation in VR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32975)
*Dongyun Han,Heecheol Kim,Siyeon Bak,Chang-Guen Song,Sun-Jeong Kim,Isaac Cho*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究嗅觉线索在VR导航中对地点识别的作用，通过两项实验发现嗅觉线索能支持地点识别，通风有助于减少残留干扰并提升可用性，但延长通风时间并未明显提高重复导航中的识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间认知支撑人们对空间关系的理解与导航，视觉虽占主导，但在视觉受限条件下其他感官（如嗅觉）可能同样发挥作用，因此有必要探究嗅觉线索在VR导航中的潜在价值。

Method: 使用可穿戴嗅觉原型开展两项正式研究。研究1考察嗅觉线索与通风对用户在传送式和冲刺式导航中识别所遇地点的影响；研究2将研究扩展到重复导航，检验通风时长对识别表现和用户体验的影响。

Result: 嗅觉线索能够支持导航过程中对所遇地点的识别；通风与残留干扰减少和可用性提升相关；但在重复导航中，较长的通风时间并未明显提高识别准确率。

Conclusion: 嗅觉线索有潜力作为VR导航中的情境信号，同时也凸显了气味管理对于保持感知清晰度和用户舒适度的重要性。

Abstract: Spatial cognition supports how people interpret spatial relationships and navigate their surroundings. Although vision plays a dominant role, other sensory modalities, including olfaction, may also contribute under limited visual conditions. This paper investigates the role of olfactory cues in location recognition during virtual reality (VR) navigation. We conducted two formal studies using a wearable olfactory prototype. Study 1 examined whether olfactory cues and ventilation influenced users' recognition of encountered locations during \HDY{teleportation-} and dash-based navigation. Study 2 extended this investigation to repeated navigation and examined whether ventilation duration influenced recognition performance and user experience across successive movements. Results show that olfactory cues can support recognition of encountered locations during navigation, while ventilation was associated with reduced residual interference and improved usability. However, longer ventilation did not clearly improve recognition accuracy in repeated navigation. These findings suggest the potential of olfactory cues as contextual signals during VR navigation, while also highlighting the importance of scent management for maintaining perceptual clarity and user comfort.

</details>


### [122] [VocalEyes: Speaker-Aware Augmented Reality Captioning through In-Conversation Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32983)
*Yuxiao Wang,Xulong Tang,Chen Chen,Rawan alghofaili*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VocalEyes 是一个说话人感知的 AR 字幕系统，通过自然自我介绍建立命名语音档案，并结合说话人归属字幕、固定档案卡与发音面部视觉提示，提升小组交流中的说话人识别与追踪表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共址 AR 字幕虽能让语音可读，但会把话语与说话人分离。在陌生群体中，这会增加即时回应和事后回顾的难度：用户不仅要恢复"说了什么"，还要恢复"谁说的"。传统说话人分离只返回匿名聚类，而说话人识别通常假设会前注册，难以满足实际需求。

Method: 作者构建了 VocalEyes 说话人感知 AR 字幕系统，利用自然自我介绍创建命名语音档案。界面整合了三项设计：说话人归属字幕、固定档案卡，以及标记正在发音面部的视觉提示。随后开展受控组内实验，20 名自报典型听力的参与者参与评估。

Result: VocalEyes 的说话人识别准确率达到 88.0%；参与者的说话人追踪准确率从仅字幕 AR 条件下的 47.2% 提升至完整界面条件下的 87.3%。参与者还报告完整界面下的工作负荷更低。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在会话中进行注册可保留实时字幕与会议记录中的说话人归属信息，在脚本化小组会议场景中具有可行性。

Abstract: Co-located augmented reality (AR) captions make speech readable, but they can separate an utterance from the person who produced it. In unfamiliar groups, losing that source complicates immediate responses and later review: users must recover not only what was said, but also who said it. Conventional diarization returns anonymous clusters, while speaker recognition typically assumes pre-meeting enrollment. We built VocalEyes, a speaker-aware AR captioning system that creates named voice profiles from natural self-introductions. The interface coordinates speaker-attributed captions, a fixed profile card, and a visual cue that marks the articulating face. In a controlled within-subjects study with 20 participants who reported typical hearing, VocalEyes identified speakers with 88.0% accuracy and increased participant speaker-tracking accuracy from 47.2% with caption-only AR to 87.3% with the complete interface. Participants also reported lower workload with the complete interface. These findings show how in-conversation registration can preserve speaker attribution across live captions and meeting records in scripted small-group meetings.

</details>


### [123] [Smelling the Way: Olfactory Modulation of Spatial Estimation and Path Integration in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32992)
*Siyeon Bak,Junho Kim,Dongyun Han,Sun-Jeong Kim,Isaac Cho*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文研究VR中借助风扇驱动的嗅觉设备进行气味传递对空间信息获取的影响，通过两项正式实验发现气味反馈会以不同方式影响空间行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间认知依赖多感官，视觉虽占主导，但在视觉线索受限时嗅觉等模态可支持空间加工；本文旨在探究VR环境下风扇介导的嗅觉设备传递气味如何影响空间信息获取。

Method: 采用两项正式实验。研究1（N=24）让参与者在直线路径行走时使用两种不同嗅觉设备定位气味源；研究2（N=19）采用三角完成任务，在不同旋转角度与嗅觉条件下让参与者尝试返回起点。

Result: 气味传递反馈在两项研究中以不同方式影响空间行为。研究1中，视觉—嗅觉错位产生了系统性方向偏差，但未提升绝对定位精度；研究2中，距离调制的嗅觉反馈降低了终点归航误差，但同时增加了行走距离与完成时间。

Conclusion: 气味传递反馈会影响空间行为：视觉—嗅觉错位带来方向偏差而非精度提升，距离调制嗅觉反馈则促使参与者采取更为审慎的目标确认策略。

Abstract: Spatial cognition enables individuals to perceive and interpret spatial relationships, estimate locations, and navigate within their surroundings. While vision plays a dominant role, other sensory modalities, particularly olfaction, can support spatial processing when visual cues are limited. This paper explores the effects of scent-delivery within virtual reality (VR) environments by using fan-mediated olfactory devices. We conducted two formal studies to evaluate the effects on spatial information acquisition. Study 1 (N=24) examined the participants' ability to localize a scent source while walking a linear path using two different olfactory devices. Study 2 (N=19) employed a triangle completion task, in which participants attempted to return to their starting point under varying rotation angles and olfactory conditions. Results indicate that scent-delivery feedback influenced spatial behavior in distinct ways across the two studies. In Study 1, visual-olfactory misalignment produced systematic directional biases without improving absolute localization accuracy. In Study 2, distance-modulated scent-delivery feedback reduced terminal homing error but also increased traveled distance and completion time, suggesting a more deliberate goal-confirmation strategy.

</details>


### [124] [Medical Knowledge Is Not All You Need: When Medical Q&A Becomes Situated Patient Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33040)
*Shreya Bali,Riku Arakawa,Jill Fain Lehman,Alexander K. Maytin,Brian Chen,Emma Russell,Haarika Reddy,Annalise Vaccarello,Dustin P. DeMeo,Bryan T. Carroll,Mayank Goel*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 医疗问答可靠性不能仅靠医学知识，还需关注情境信息；研究提出情境化医疗辅助的设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗问答通常通过引入权威医学信息来提升可靠性，但作者质疑在真实持续护理场景中，可靠性是否只取决于系统的医学知识，还是更依赖对具体情境的把握。

Method: 对73名皮肤癌术后伤口护理患者进行研究，统计需要回应的提问中有多少依赖程序之外的信息；随后将这些问题回放给7个大语言模型，并加入手术及术后指导，观察其错误情况。

Result: 41.9%的需回应问题依赖视力或身体状态、环境背景或既往行为等程序外信息，且需求因患者而异；LLM仍出现大量错误，包括将未知状态当作已知，即使有明确防护措施也如此；在提供完整程序上下文时，7个模型中有6个更常过早引入后续步骤。

Conclusion: 在持续护理中，医疗问答的可靠性取决于系统与患者各自能可靠建立的信息及所提供服务形式之间的匹配；作者据此提出情境化医疗辅助的设计空间。

Abstract: Reliability in medical Q&A is often pursued by grounding responses in authoritative medical information. We show that when Q&A is embedded within ongoing care, reliability depends on more than what the system knows medically. In a study with 73 skin cancer patients practicing postoperative wound care, 41.9% of response-requiring questions depended on information beyond the procedure, including visual or physical state, environmental context, or prior actions. These demands varied across patients, consistent with patients recruiting the assistant into different informational roles. We then replayed the questions to seven LLMs while adding procedural and postoperative guidance. Errors remained substantial, including treating unknown states as known, even under explicit guardrails; with full procedural context, six of seven models more often introduced later steps prematurely. Based on these findings, we propose a design space for situated medical assistance that connects what the assistant and patient can each reliably establish to the form of assistance provided.

</details>


### [125] [ParallelPilot: Supporting Coordination and Monitoring in Parallel AI Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33113)
*Tao Long,Weili Shi,Hussein Mozannar,Maya Murad,Rafah Hosn*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过形成性研究提炼出PILOT五种并行会话监督实践，并据此设计ParallelPilot探针（规划界面、运行日志器、环境仪表盘）。被试内实验显示任务吞吐量提升63%、峰值并行监督数增加约1个，追踪负担与上下文切换下降，14/16名参与者更偏好该工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码助手日益自主，开发者开始并行运行多个会话，挑战从单纯的代码生成转向协调与监控并发代理工作，而现有工具缺乏对这类"监督"工作的显式支持。

Method: 先开展形成性研究（N=14）识别出PILOT五种监督实践：Planning（规划）、Isolating（隔离）、Logging（记录）、Observing（观察）、Triaging（分诊）；再以设计探针ParallelPilot将其落地，包含规划界面、运行日志器与环境仪表盘，并嵌入现有编码工具。随后采用平衡式被试内研究（N=16）进行验证。

Result: 使用ParallelPilot时，短编码任务的任务吞吐量提升63%，峰值同时监督的代理平均多1个，追踪负担与上下文切换减少；工具还澄清了执行计划、任务依赖与干预线索，16人中有14人更偏好该设置。但感知控制感与"成功重定向代理"的感知未见显著提升。

Conclusion: 研究证明了显式监督支持的价值，将PILOT定位为设计并发工作监督工具的支架，其适用范围可延伸至编码之外的场景。未来编码助手应将高层态势感知与低成本回溯到实现证据的路径相结合，以帮助开发者判断和引导代理工作。

Abstract: As coding assistants become increasingly autonomous, developers run multiple sessions in parallel, shifting the challenge from code generation alone to coordinating and monitoring concurrent agent work. Through a formative study (N=14), we identified PILOT: five supervisory practices for Planning, Isolating, Logging, Observing, and Triaging parallel sessions. We present ParallelPilot, a design probe that instantiates PILOT through a planning interface, a run-logger, and an ambient dashboard alongside existing coding tools. In a counterbalanced within-subjects study (N=16), participants using ParallelPilot increased ticket throughput by 63% in short coding tasks and supervised an average of one more concurrent agent at peak, while their tracking effort and context switching dropped. ParallelPilot also clarified execution plans, task dependencies, and intervention cues, and 14 of 16 participants preferred it over their current setup. These gains were not accompanied by significant improvements in perceived control or perceived success in redirecting the agents. Our findings demonstrate the value of explicit supervision support and position PILOT as a scaffold for designing tools that help people supervise concurrent work within and beyond coding. We suggest that future coding assistants should pair high-level awareness with low-cost paths back to the implementation evidence developers need to judge and steer agent work.

</details>


### [126] [PDFa11yMut: Measuring Mutation-Specific Detection in PDF Accessibility Checkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33160)
*Gauri Jain*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出PDFa11yMut变异测试框架，通过结构级配对变异与哈希关联证据，测量自动PDF无障碍检查器的变异特异性行为；结果显示合规类变异普遍被检出，而语义/辅助表示类变异在测试配置下无自动检出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化PDF无障碍检查器能提供合规证据，但"报告干净"并不等于完全无障碍。现有工作缺少对检查器在特定变异类型上行为差异的细粒度测量，因此需要一个有针对性、可复现的变异测试方法来刻画检查器的检出能力与盲区。

Method: 构建PDFa11yMut：对参考套件基线施加成对的结构级变换，验证预期增量与非目标不变量，并记录哈希关联的检查器证据。共设计30个合规导向变异与39个语义/辅助表示变异，分别用PAC、veraPDF、Acrobat测试；另以NVDA/Acrobat/Windows固定流程对9对进行探索性AT采样。

Result: 30个合规导向变异中，PAC与veraPDF各产生30个直接目标发现，Acrobat产生23个直接发现加3个预设后果代理发现。39个语义/辅助表示变异在测试配置下均无自动目标发现，Acrobat仅对其中一部分发出人工复核提示；探索性AT样本在9对中有8对观察到表示差异。

Conclusion: 该工作属于有范围的变异测试研究，而非通用检查器准确率基准；其贡献在于可复用算子、结构与纯度判定器、成对基线/变异证据及可复现分析流程，说明自动检查器在合规层面证据充分但对语义/辅助表示层面存在盲区，需谨慎解读其报告。

Abstract: Automated PDF accessibility checkers provide useful conformance evidence, but a clean report is not a complete accessibility oracle. PDFa11yMut measures mutation-specific checker behavior by applying paired structure-level transformations to reference-suite baselines, verifying intended deltas and non-target invariants, and recording hash-linked checker evidence. Across 30 conformance-oriented mutants, PAC and veraPDF each produced 30 and 30 direct target findings, respectively, while Acrobat produced 23 direct findings plus 3 prespecified consequence-proxy findings. The 39 semantic/assistive-representation mutants produced no automated target finding in the tested configurations, while Acrobat issued manual-review prompts for a subset; Class B is interpreted descriptively rather than as a universal checker obligation. A separate convenience-selected exploratory AT sample observed representation differences in 8 of 9 pairs under one fixed NVDA/Acrobat/Windows procedure. The artifact contributes reusable operators, structural and purity oracles, paired baseline/mutant evidence, and reproducible analysis for a scoped mutation-testing study rather than a general checker-accuracy benchmark.

</details>


### [127] [Separating Memory and Workflow Effects in Predicting Individual Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33321)
*Tianzhu Qin,Leo Yang Yang,Lee Wei Jun,Kun Chen,Ramit Debnath,Davin Youchao Dong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文分离"记什么"与"怎么用"两个决策，比较具体记忆（逐字记录）与特质描述、直接生成与三答案融合、以及基于BM25检索原句的OwnWords方法在预测个人回答上的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化语言代理需要决定记住关于一个人的什么内容，以及如何使用这些记忆。作者希望将这两个选择分离开来研究，从而判断在预测已知访谈问题的新答案时，哪种记忆构建与使用方式更有效。

Method: 在188人的1,768个任务上，用经过验证的访谈前缀构建具体记忆，并与特质描述对比；将两类记忆与单次生成及三答案融合交叉；并测试提示式与训练式选择器是否优于随机候选。进一步提出OwnWords：在有限上下文预算下用BM25检索该人的原始句子并单次作答。另在Twin-2K-500上进行序数调查答案预测。

Result: 具体记忆比特质描述高0.0158（95%全体区间[0.0044, 0.0271]）；三答案融合使具体记忆得分下降0.0123（[-0.0189, -0.0056]）；选择器未能检测性地胜过随机候选。在较长未改写源记录上单次调用优于所有记忆条件。OwnWords在500名基准外人员上优于书面记忆（+0.0127，[+0.0037, +0.0217]；早期留出测试不确定），在300人的四个预算上均值+0.0218（[+0.0138, +0.0298]），并在114人上重复；但未能检测性地优于近期截断。在Twin-2K-500上更贴近序数答案，但未提升精确选择准确率，且在两个样本之一中下降。

Conclusion: 结果刻画的是所测试的证据构建流程，而非逐字措辞的独立效应，也不构成人类预测的普遍上限。访谈评分基于模型内容评分标准而非人类评分，且原基准参与者在开发过程中已被见过，因此结论只适用于所测试的程序。

Abstract: Personalized language agents choose both what to remember about a person and how to use that memory. We separate these choices when predicting unseen answers to known interview questions. On 1,768 tasks from 188 people, a concrete memory built from a verified interview prefix outscores a trait description by 0.0158 (95% whole-person interval [0.0044, 0.0271]). Crossing both memories with one-shot generation and three-answer fusion, fusion lowers concrete-memory scores by 0.0123 ([-0.0189, -0.0056]); prompted and trained selectors do not detectably beat a random candidate. One call on the longer, unrewritten source record outscores every memory condition. Under a limited context budget, OwnWords retrieves the person's sentences with BM25 and answers in one call. It outperforms the written memory on 500 people outside the benchmark (+0.0127, [+0.0037, +0.0217]; an earlier held-out test was inconclusive) and across four budgets on 300 people (mean +0.0218, [+0.0138, +0.0298]), with the latter result repeated on 114 people. It does not detectably outperform recency truncation. These results compare evidence-construction procedures; they do not isolate the effect of verbatim wording. On Twin-2K-500, OwnWords predicts ordinal survey answers more closely than the written memory, but does not improve exact-choice accuracy and lowers it in one of two samples. Interview scores use a model-based content rubric without human ratings, and the original benchmark's participants were seen during development. These results characterize the tested procedures, not a general human-prediction ceiling.

</details>


### [128] [DataMagic: Authoring Data Videos through Declarative Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33403)
*Yupeng Xie,Zhenyang Wang,Liangwei Wang,Jiayi Zhu,Zhouan Shen,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DataMagic 通过声明式多智能体编排从原始表格数据自动生成数据视频，显著提升质量与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据视频制作需数据分析、叙事设计与视频剪辑等多重专业技能。现有静态可视化工具缺乏叙事与动画能力；创作工具依赖预制图表而非原始数据；像素级模型端到端生成却无法保证数据准确性与溯源。自动生成面临两大挑战：如何统一表示图表、旁白与动画及其时序关系，以及如何高效搜索庞大设计空间以得到叙事连贯的组合。

Method: 提出 DataMagic，从原始表格数据出发，通过声明式多智能体编排创作数据视频。其声明式规范 DVSpec 以数据绑定引用与声明式同步统一图表、旁白与动画，保证数据溯源与音画自动对齐；采用"先生成后编排"（Generate-then-Orchestrate）多智能体策略，并行生成候选场景，再通过全局编排优化叙事连贯性。DVSpec 为三种互补交互模式提供共享状态，衔接全自动化与细粒度人工控制。

Result: 在 109 个真实样本上评估：最先进 LLM（如 GPT-5）仅达 2.13/5，执行成功率介于 48.62% 至 86.24%；DataMagic 将质量提升至 3.89（+83%），成功率超过 95%，在动画与叙事维度增益最显著。用户研究表明，相比对话式 LLM 工作流，DataMagic 提升创作效率（任务时间减少 79.7%）并降低感知认知负荷。

Conclusion: DataMagic 借助声明式规范 DVSpec 与多智能体编排策略，在保证数据溯源与音画对齐的前提下，实现高质量、高成功率的数据视频自动生成，并兼顾自动化与人工控制。

Abstract: Data videos communicate data insights through dynamic charts, voice narration, and synchronized animations, and have become a widely adopted form of data storytelling. However, producing them requires expertise in data analysis, narrative design, and video editing. Static visualization tools lack narrative and animation capabilities; authoring tools rely on pre-prepared charts rather than raw data; and pixel-level models generate videos end-to-end but cannot guarantee data accuracy or provenance. End-to-end automatic generation faces two core challenges: how to uniformly represent charts, narration, and animations together with their temporal relationships, and how to efficiently search a vast design space for narrative-coherent compositions. We present DataMagic, which authors data videos from raw tabular data through declarative multi-agent orchestration. First, the declarative specification DVSpec unifies charts, narration, and animations with data-bound references and declarative synchronization, ensuring data provenance and automatic audio-visual alignment. Second, a "Generate-then-Orchestrate" multi-agent strategy generates candidate scenes in parallel and then optimizes narrative coherence through global orchestration. DVSpec provides a shared state for three complementary interaction modes, bridging full automation with fine-grained human control. Evaluations on 109 real-world samples show that even the most advanced LLM (e.g., GPT-5) achieves only 2.13/5 with execution success rates between 48.62% and 86.24%; DataMagic improves quality to 3.89 (+83%) with success rates above 95%, with the most significant gains in animation and narrative dimensions. A user study shows that, compared to a conversational LLM workflow, DataMagic improves creation efficiency (79.7% reduction in task time) and reduces perceived cognitive load. Project page: https://github.com/HKUSTDial/DataMagic.

</details>


### [129] [Characterizing Memory Misalignment in Human-LLM Interaction From User Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33623)
*Jingruo Chen,Shuning Zhang,Eryue Xu,Jianing Li,Xin Yi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文通过混合方法研究刻画并缓解LLM对话代理中的记忆错位问题，构建了14类错位分类体系与12种交互策略设计空间，发现用户偏好主动控制而非高认知负荷的审计方式，提出"摩擦感知记忆"理念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆虽能增强LLM对话代理的个性化，但存在"记忆错位"——记忆内容违背用户预期，需要从用户视角理解并解决这一问题。

Method: 采用混合方法：1）收集记忆使用数据（N=28，457条记录）与日记研究（N=32，304份报告），归纳出覆盖记忆摄入、存储管理、检索解读三阶段的14类错位分类；2）开展4场共创工作坊（12名HCI研究者），产出包含12种候选交互策略的设计空间，按交互形式、位置与侵入性维度结构化；3）通过speed dating方法招募121名用户进行偏好验证。

Result: 形成14类记忆错位分类体系；构建含12种交互策略的设计空间；speed dating显示用户偏好存在异质性，更青睐主动控制，而非认知负荷高的因果图检查或被动审计日志。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了监督能动性与交互开销之间的张力，倡导"摩擦感知记忆"（friction-aware memories），在用户监督与对话流畅性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: While memory enhances personalization in LLM-based conversational agents, it suffers from memory misalignment, where memories violate user expectations. We present a mixed-methods investigation to characterize and mitigate memory misalignment from user perspectives. First, we collected data from memory usage (N=28, 457 entries) and diary study (N=32, 304 reports), which yielded a taxonomy spanning 14 misalignment types across memory intake, storage and management, retrieval and interpretation stages. Second, four co-design workshops with 12 experienced HCI researchers derived a design space to tackle memory misalignment issues, consisting of 12 candidate interaction strategies structured across interaction form, placement and intrusiveness dimensions. Finally, a speed dating with 121 users reveals preference heterogeneity, where users prioritize proactive controls over cognitively demanding causal graph inspections or passive audit logs. Synthesizing these findings, we highlight the tension between supervisory agency and interaction overhead, and advocate for friction-aware memories that balance user oversight with conversation smoothness.

</details>


### [130] [Accounting for Stochasticity in Studies of Large Language Model Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33743)
*Emma Lurie,Stephanie T. Wang,Sorelle A. Friedler,Danaé Metaxa*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 单次查询不足以评估大语言模型的拒答行为：研究通过对GPT-4.1的纵向审计发现，约20%的信息来源处于决策边界区域，单个查询结果几乎无信息量，可靠量化拒答率需重复查询15至25次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估普遍采用单次观测（single-observation）标准来判断大语言模型的拒答行为，但该做法可能无法可靠反映模型的真实拒答倾向。作者希望检验单次查询是否足以支撑对拒答行为的评估。

Method: 构建纵向审计系统，选取两个社会关注度较高的话题与20个维基百科来源，对GPT-4.1在四个不同日期各重复发送相同提示100次，从而获得同一提示在不同时间点上的重复观测数据，并据此分析拒答结果的分布特征。

Result: 拒答结果与稳定的伯努利过程（Bernoulli process）一致；但约20%的信息来源落在决策边界区域，在该区域内单次查询几乎不具信息量。要可靠量化拒答行为，需要的重复查询次数在15至25次之间，远高于现有评估中常见的单次观测标准。

Conclusion: 单次观测查询不足以用于评估大语言模型的拒答行为；相关评估应采用多次重复查询（约15至25次）以获得可靠的拒答率估计，尤其对于处于决策边界附近的提示来源。

Abstract: We present preliminary empirical evidence that single-observation queries are insufficient for evaluations of LLM refusal behaviors. Using a longitudinal auditing system, we issued identical prompts 100 times each across four dates to GPT-4.1 for two socially salient topics across 20 Wikipedia sources. Refusal outcomes were consistent with a stable Bernoulli process, yet 20\% of sources fell within a decision-boundary region where a single query is largely uninformative. Reliable quantification of refusals required between 15 and 25 repeated queries, well above the single-observation standard common in existing evaluations.

</details>


### [131] [ReVision: Supporting Designers' Interpretation and Exploration of Visuals in Concepts and Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33911)
*Yaqing Yang,Mei-Xi Chia,Aniket Kittur*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出ReVision，一个基于AI的设计工具，可将视觉与文本参考拆解为可编辑的概念解读与视觉母题，支持二者重组并生成多样的视觉形式变体，帮助设计新手跳出对参考的表面模仿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉设计师需要从参考中获取灵感以拓展设计空间，他们会把参考的魅力拆解为概念与视觉元素并尝试不同组合。但新手容易停留在表面特征、变化有限，从而被参考"固化"。现有工具虽支持可编辑的视觉属性和高层主题，却难以控制概念解读与表达性视觉母题之间的关系，也难以系统地重新组合表达，因此需要新的支持工具。

Method: 作者开发了ReVision，一个基于AI的工具：它将视觉和文本参考分解为可编辑的概念性解读和视觉母题，允许在概念空间和视觉空间之间进行重组，并将每个方向渲染为多种发散的视觉形式变体，从而在创作过程中支持更具发散性的探索。

Result: ReVision能够将参考内容分解为可编辑的概念解读与视觉母题，并支持跨概念与视觉空间的重组，同时为每个探索方向渲染出差异化的视觉形式变体，实现更多样的发散式探索。

Conclusion: ReVision通过AI辅助的解构与重组机制，弥补了现有工具在概念解读与视觉母题关系控制上的不足，帮助设计新手摆脱对参考的表面化依赖，促进更发散的创作探索。

Abstract: Visual designers get inspiration from references to expand their design space. They decompose what makes a reference evocative into conceptual and visual elements, ranging from explicit attributes such as objects and colors, to less readily articulated concepts and visual motifs. They then create different visual forms to explore how the selected elements could be combined differently. Novices often struggle with these moves, instead focusing on surface features or producing limited visual variation, thus becoming fixated on the reference. Existing tools support editable visual attributes and high-level themes, but provide limited control over how conceptual interpretations relate to expressive visual motifs or how their combinations can be systematically re-expressed. We present ReVision, an AI-based tool that decomposes visual and textual references into editable conceptual interpretations and visual motifs, enables their recombination across conceptual and visual spaces, and renders each direction as divergent visual-form variations, supporting more divergent exploration during the creation process.

</details>


### [132] ["Is This Book AI-Generated?" How Authorship Suspicion Manifests in Marketplace Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33950)
*Victor Dibia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文分析了78本亚马逊畅销书的863条低星评论，研究读者对疑似AI作者身份的怀疑：怀疑集中于生成式AI类书籍（35.1%）但各品类均有出现，主要针对内容浅薄与呈现不佳，并分为模糊与实证两类怀疑，还提出话题集中与痕迹识别两种机制，指出星级评分可能掩盖此类怀疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI参与图书创作，读者对疑似AI作者身份的反应日益重要，但此前尚未被系统研究，因此本文希望填补这一空白。

Method: 分析8个品类、78本亚马逊畅销书的863条低星评论，按与AI的三种接近程度分层，并区分模糊怀疑与实证怀疑两种形式，提出话题集中与痕迹识别两种怀疑产生机制。

Result: 怀疑集中于生成式AI书籍（35.1%），但在所有品类中都有出现（如园艺类5.7%）；提及AI作者身份的评论更多抱怨内容浅薄和呈现不佳；最受质疑的书仍保持4.1星，但其50%的批评性评论提及AI作者身份。

Conclusion: 读者对AI作者身份的怀疑普遍存在且形式多样，星级评分会掩盖这种怀疑，说明仅靠评分难以反映读者对AI创作的负面感知。

Abstract: As AI becomes part of how books are authored, reader response to suspected AI authorship grows more consequential, yet remains unexamined. We analyze 863 low-star reviews of 78 Amazon bestsellers across 8 categories at three levels of proximity to AI. Suspicion concentrates in Generative AI books (35.1%) but appears in every category, including Gardening (5.7%). Reviews citing AI authorship complain more about shallow content and poor presentation than other critical reviews. Suspicion takes two forms: ambient, where "AI-generated" is a generic complaint about formulaic writing, and corroborated, where reviewers of the same book independently cite concrete evidence. We propose two mechanisms by which suspicion arises: topical concentration, where a book's AI subject matter supplies vocabulary for quality complaints, and artifact detection, where readers notice ChatGPT-style formatting regardless of topic. Star ratings can hide this suspicion: the most-flagged book holds 4.1 stars while 50% of its critical reviews cite AI authorship.

</details>


### [133] [EEG-Fusion: Failure-Informed Source-Free Expert Routing for Robust Motor Imagery EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33962)
*Abdul Basit,Saim Rehman,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出 EEG-Fusion，一个面向无源域运动想象脑电解码的失败感知决策级融合框架，通过无标签可靠性估计提升受试者级解码稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受试者独立的运动想象脑电解码在受试者偏移下易出现个体级失败，即便平均性能看似可接受，解码器也可能退化为过度自信的近单类预测器；在无源域部署中目标用户标签不可用，使自适应与专家选择更加困难。

Method: EEG-Fusion 将无源域解码视为对异质专家集合的无标签可靠性估计：先做逐受试者欧氏对齐与仅归一化的测试时自适应，再通过可靠性门控将目标受试者路由到神经、协方差或生理特征专家；门控在源域留出折上训练，利用置信度、熵、预测多样性、专家一致性和预测类别平衡等无标签流诊断预测专家性能与坍缩风险，坍缩以最大预测类别占比衡量。

Result: 在 3 个随机种子的 9 折留一受试者（LOSO）评估中，相比未对齐的原始 EEGNet 无源域基线，宏 F1 在 BCI IV-2a 本地协议上从 0.417 提升至 0.529，BNCI2014-001 上从 0.314 提升至 0.482，BNCI2014-004 上从 0.607 提升至 0.708；对应坍缩指数分别降低 0.199、0.227、0.169。在 9 名受试者的 Cho2017 外部子集上，宏 F1 从 0.516 提升至 0.630。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无标签可靠性估计可有效缓解无源域运动想象脑电解码部署中的受试者级失败模式。

Abstract: Subject-independent motor-imagery (MI) EEG decoding can exhibit subject-level failures even when average performance appears acceptable: under subject shift, a decoder can become an overconfident near-one-class predictor. This is especially problematic in source-free deployment, where target-user labels are unavailable during adaptation and expert selection. We present \textit{EEG-Fusion}, a failure-informed decision-level fusion framework that treats source-free MI decoding as label-free reliability estimation over heterogeneous experts. EEG-Fusion applies subject-wise Euclidean alignment and normalization-only test-time adaptation, then routes each target subject to a neural, covariance-based, or physiological-feature expert using a reliability gate trained on source-held-out folds to predict expert performance and collapse risk from label-free stream diagnostics. The gate uses confidence, entropy, prediction diversity, expert agreement, and predicted class balance; collapse is measured as the maximum predicted class fraction. In 9-fold leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) evaluation with three seeds, relative to a no-alignment raw EEGNet source-free anchor, EEG-Fusion improves subject macro-F1 from 0.417 to 0.529 on BCI IV-2a local protocol, from 0.314 to 0.482 on BNCI2014-001, and from 0.607 to 0.708 on BNCI2014-004; corresponding collapse-index reductions are 0.199, 0.227, and 0.169. In a 9-subject Cho2017 external subset, EEG-Fusion improves macro-F1 from 0.516 to 0.630. These results suggest that label-free reliability estimation can reduce subject-level failure modes in source-free MI-EEG deployment.

</details>


### [134] [ThinkNet: Compact Architecture Selection and Validation-Gated Ensembles for Subject-Independent MI-EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33967)
*Abdul Basit,Saim Rehman,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出 ThinkNet，一个验证受控的框架，用于为未见受试者选择紧凑的运动想象脑电（MI-EEG）解码器与推理策略，强调避免验证信息泄露，并发现验证可靠性是受试者迁移下的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用的辅助与康复脑机接口需要能泛化到新用户的受试者无关 MI-EEG 解码器，且在目标用户数据有限、算力受限的条件下工作。然而，当测试受试者信息影响预处理、模型选择或集成选择时，留出受试者的性能会被高估，因此需要一个严格受控的验证框架来真实评估并选择紧凑解码器。

Method: ThinkNet 结合三种机制：仅训练集归一化、验证引导的进化搜索、验证门控推理。在 BCI Competition IV-2a 四分类任务（session T）上，采用 9 折 LOSO、3 个随机种子、7 个固定解码器条目，并对 10 个代表性解码器家族进行更广泛搜索；留出受试者绝不用于归一化、超参数、架构或集成策略选择。

Result: 固定基准中，验证选出的紧凑解码器达到 44.35±15.41% 准确率，仅 4.9K 参数、19 KB FP32 权重，Orin CUDA 批大小 1 推理耗时 0.99 ms。广泛搜索中，紧凑模型（≤25K 参数）经选择性重训练后平均留出准确率高于中型和大型模型（40.10% vs. 35.09% 和 34.78%）。验证门控集成优于验证选出的单模型推理，固定基准达 43.98±16.25%，紧凑六家族集成达 43.31±15.88%。非部署的 oracle 分析显示存在 6.1 个百分点的家族选择差距，且验证-测试相关性接近零。

Conclusion: ThinkNet 是一个用于紧凑 MI-EEG 模型与推理策略选择的验证受控框架，而非单一架构基准。结果表明紧凑模型在受试者迁移下表现更优，但验证可靠性仍是关键瓶颈，验证-测试近乎无相关说明在受试者分布偏移下选择策略仍需改进。

Abstract: Practical assistive and rehabilitative brain--computer interfaces require subject-independent motor-imagery EEG (MI-EEG) decoders that generalize to new users under limited target-user data and constrained compute. However, held-out-subject performance can be overstated when test-subject information influences preprocessing, model selection, or ensemble selection. We present \textit{ThinkNet}, a validation-controlled framework that combines train-only normalization, validation-guided evolutionary search, and validation-gated inference to identify compact decoders and inference policies for held-out subjects. We evaluate four-class BCI Competition IV-2a (session T) decoding with nine Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) folds, three seeds, seven fixed decoder entries, and a broader search over ten representative decoder families; the held-out subject is never used for normalization, hyperparameter, architecture, or ensemble-policy selection. In the fixed benchmark, the validation-selected compact decoder achieved 44.35$\pm$15.41\% accuracy with 4.9K parameters, 19 KB FP32 weights, and 0.99 ms batch-1 Orin CUDA inference. Across the broader search, compact models ($\leq$25K parameters) achieved higher mean held-out accuracy than mid-size and large alternatives after selected retraining (40.10\% vs. 35.09\% and 34.78\%). Validation-gated ensembling improved over validation-selected single-model inference, reaching 43.98$\pm$16.25\% in the fixed benchmark and 43.31$\pm$15.88\% for the compact six-family ensemble. A non-deployable oracle analysis revealed a 6.1-point family-selection gap and near-zero validation--test correlation, showing that validation reliability remains a key bottleneck under subject shift. Thus, ThinkNet is a validation-controlled framework for compact MI-EEG model and inference-policy selection, rather than a single-architecture benchmark.

</details>


### [135] [StructSim: Measuring Idea Similarity at Scale Through Structural Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34046)
*Yaqing yang,Vikram Mohanty,Mei-Xi Chia,Nikolas Martelaro,Dafna Shahaf,Aniket Kittur*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出共享结构化表示（目的-机制-实现）与多层概念图，定义成对相似度与集合级机制覆盖度指标，效果优于文本嵌入基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型使创意生成规模化，创意评估中的想法相似度测量变得关键；但文本嵌入将想法压缩为单一向量，难以刻画部分重叠（核心与支撑成分）和抽象层次差异等结构相似性。

Method: 提出共享结构化表示，将想法分解为目的、机制、实现三类组件，并在多层概念图中组织相关组件；基于该表示定义成对相似度度量与用于评估想法多样性的集合级机制覆盖度。用受控想法三元组和12位专家评估验证。

Result: 在结构相似度上与专家判断的一致性相比嵌入基线提升31%；在想法集合覆盖度上也更符合专家评估。

Conclusion: 结构化分解与概念图表示可支持可扩展的想法相似度与多样性评估，为创意评估提供更贴近专家判断的方法。

Abstract: Measuring idea similarity is fundamental to creativity evaluation, especially as LLMs enable idea generation at increasing scale. However, text embeddings collapse an idea into a single vector, making it difficult to capture structural similarity, including partial overlap across core and supporting components and differences across levels of abstraction. We introduce a shared structural representation that decomposes ideas into purpose, mechanism, and implementation components and organizes related components in a multi-layer concept graph. From this representation, we define measures of pairwise similarity and set-level mechanism coverage for assessing idea diversity. We evaluate our approach using controlled idea triples and assessments from 12 experts, focusing on differences in core mechanisms, implementations, and supporting components. Our method improves alignment with expert judgments of structural similarity by 31% over the embedding baseline and better reflects expert assessments of idea set coverage, supporting scalable evaluation of idea similarity and diversity.

</details>


### [136] [Scanvas: Discovering and Developing Synergistic Opportunities in Generative Design Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34062)
*Yaqing Yang,Aniket Kittur,Hongyu Howie Wang,Nikolas Martelaro,Matt Klenk,Yan-Ying Chen,Matthew K. Hong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Scanvas是一个通过两步计算流程（属性分解+策略算子搜索）帮助设计师系统发现并发展协同设计机会的AI支持系统，实验证明其效果优于LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 好的设计往往具有协同性，能通过链接目标实现超加性价值，但在稀疏设计空间中寻找协同机会很困难；当前LLM支持的创意工具多默认采用加性范式（如特征混合、变体生成、局部修补），难以发现协同机会。

Method: Scanvas通过两步计算过程实现协同：1）将种子想法分解为显式属性（组件、行为、剩余物、问题）以丰富设计空间；2）使用三种基于理论的策略算子在丰富后的想法间进行系统搜索——解锁或强化目标、将弱点转化为资源、跨功能共享组件。系统实现为自动生成流水线和交互式系统。

Result: 流水线消融实验和12位专业设计师的用户研究表明，与LLM创意基线相比，Scanvas能帮助用户提出并发展出质量显著更高、更具协同性的概念。

Conclusion: Scanvas通过结构化的属性分解与理论驱动的策略搜索，有效弥补了现有LLM创意工具在发现协同设计机会方面的不足，为AI辅助协同设计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Good design is often synergistic, creating super-additive value by linking goals so that existing resources produce greater outcomes. However, finding these synergistic opportunities in sparse design spaces is difficult, and current LLM-supported ideation tools largely default to additive paradigms such as feature blending, variant generation, or local patching. We present Scanvas, an AI-supported system for systematically discovering and developing synergistic design opportunities. Scanvas operationalizes synergy through a two-step computational process: first, it decomposes seed ideas into explicit properties (components, behaviors, surpluses, and issues) to enrich the design space; second, it systematically searches across enriched ideas using three theory-grounded strategy operators: unlocking or strengthening goals, turning weaknesses into resources, and sharing components across functions. We instantiate Scanvas as an auto-generation pipeline and an interactive system. Pipeline ablations and a user study with 12 professional designers demonstrate that Scanvas enables users to surface and develop significantly higher-quality, synergistic concepts compared to LLM ideation baselines.

</details>


### [137] [HiThink Turn: An Intent-Aware Turn-Taking Control Module for Full-Duplex Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34096)
*Feiyang Chen,Wenhan Yang,Bohan Wang,Xinjian Gao,Rongjunchen Zhang,Jun Wang,Xinhui Hu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出 HiThink Turn，一种意图感知的流式轮次状态预测器，通过分离响应意图与语义完整性，结合系统播放状态决策，实现低延迟、准确的全双工对话轮次控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工对话需要及时且有选择性的打断处理，仅靠回合结束预测无法实现：完整话语可能无需响应，而未完成请求可能值得打断。因此需要一种能区分意图与完整性的机制。

Method: 提出 HiThink Turn，分离响应意图与语义完整性并以系统播放状态为条件进行决策；采用极简意图充分前缀监督（通过 LLM 判定与语音对齐构建），并在分块边界截断音频上训练以增强对部分语音的鲁棒性；支持 240 毫秒音频块的流式推理。

Result: 实验表明 HiThink Turn 在 Easy Turn 宏准确率、Full-Duplex-Bench 平均交互率得分（0.933）及非目标语音平均播放恢复率（0.735）上领先对比方法；意图前缀触发将打断成功率从 89% 提升至 98%，并将平均停止延迟降低 60.9%。

Conclusion: HiThink Turn 通过意图感知的流式轮次状态预测实现了低延迟、准确的全双工对话控制，在多项指标上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Full-duplex dialogue requires timely yet selective interruption handling, which end-of-turn prediction alone cannot achieve: complete utterances may need no response, while unfinished requests may warrant interruption. To address this challenge, we propose HiThink Turn, an intent-aware streaming turn-state predictor that separates response intent from semantic completeness and conditions decisions on system playback state. A key contribution is minimal intent-sufficient prefix supervision, constructed through LLM judgments and speech alignment, while training on audio truncated at chunk boundaries improves robustness to partial speech. These components support streaming inference with 240-ms audio chunks, enabling low-latency, accurate full-duplex turn control. Experiments show that HiThink Turn leads the compared methods in Easy Turn macro accuracy, Full-Duplex-Bench average interaction rate score (0.933), and non-target-speech average playback resume rate (0.735). Additionally, intent-prefix triggering raises interruption success from 89\% to 98\% and reduces mean stop latency by 60.9\%.

</details>


### [138] [Exploring the Affordances of Generative Image AI for Supporting Early-stage Architect-client Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34118)
*Chengzhi Zhang,Weijie Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过11对建筑师-客户配对使用生成式图像AI协作设计"梦想之家"的观察，发现AI能促进共享理解与客户参与，但也带来风格偏见和设计方向不可预测的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑师与客户之间的沟通长期存在效率低、难以建立共识的挑战，而文本到图像生成AI能近乎实时地生成效果图，可能改变这种沟通方式，因此作者想探究其影响。

Method: 招募11对建筑师-客户配对，让每对通过视频会议使用生成式图像AI，协作产出客户"梦想之家"的早期效果图，并据此展开研究分析。

Result: 生成式图像AI通过提供具体的视觉素材、支持想法交换，帮助配对建立了稳固的共享理解；同时改变了对话动态，使客户更主动地参与设计方向的形成。但也出现挑战：对特定类型图像存在风格偏见，以及生成结果差异导致设计方向出现不可预测的转变。

Conclusion: 作者总结了面向建筑师-客户沟通场景的未来生成式图像AI系统设计启示。

Abstract: Text-to-image generative AI can produce renderings from natural-language prompts in near real time, making it increasingly popular for rapidly visualizing concepts in early-stage architectural design. Meanwhile, exchanging ideas efficiently and building shared understanding have long been central challenges in architect-client communication. How might the speed of generative image AI change this communication? To explore this question, we conducted a study with 11 architect-client pairs, in which each pair used generative image AI over video conference to collaboratively produce early-stage renderings of the client's "dream house." Our findings suggest that generative image AI helped pairs develop a solid shared understanding by providing concrete visual materials and supporting the exchange of ideas. It also shifted conversation dynamics, enabling clients to participate more actively in shaping design direction. However, challenges emerged, including a stylistic bias toward particular types of images and unpredictable shifts in design direction caused by variation across generations. We conclude with implications for the design of future generative image AI-based systems that support architect-client communication.

</details>


### [139] [When Models Choose the Question: Pedagogical Constraints in Bottom-Up Multi-Agent Inquiry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34243)
*Yeri Hong,Lauren Hyoseo Yoon*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个自下而上的论坛框架，用语言模型智能体阅读哲学叙事、提出并选择问题、最终形成共同结论，考察在没有预设讨论问题时模型生成的探究会呈现何种形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究关注当不提供讨论问题时，模型生成探究会受何种因素塑形，并借鉴儿童哲学理念，探究自主提问是否真能摆脱文本框架的限制。

Method: 共设置576个论坛，让具有亚里士多德式价值人格的智能体与无价值特定指令的“白板”参与者互动；白板保持中立，并提出和选择问题、参与结论形成；再用ECO框架区分显性哲学框架、角色的模型化探究和开放式叙事材料三类来源，追踪讨论的来源聚焦。

Result: 白板参与者更常被选入结论而非问题环节；探究在形式和来源上都趋于收窄：初始多样的问题逐渐变成非此即彼的二选一，讨论集中于文本中已经明确的方向；在分析章节中，参与者最常依据显性哲学框架。

Conclusion: 研究将这一现象描述为一种教学约束：拥有提出问题的自由，并不必然带来摆脱文本来源框架的自由。

Abstract: What shapes a model-generated inquiry when no discussion question is supplied? We introduce a bottom-up forum framework inspired by Philosophy for Children, in which language-model agents read a philosophical narrative, propose and select questions, and develop a shared conclusion without a privileged model facilitator or aggregator. Across 576 forums, contrasting Aristotelian value personas interacted with a blank-slate participant receiving no value-specific instruction. The blank slate remained neutral and was selected more often for conclusions than questions. Yet inquiry narrowed in both form and source: varied initial questions increasingly became either/or alternatives, while discussion concentrated on directions already explicit in the text. Our ECO framework traces this source focus by distinguishing explicit philosophical framing, characters' modeled inquiry, and open-ended narrative material. Across analyzed chapters, participants drew most often on explicit framing. We describe this as a pedagogical constraint: freedom to formulate questions did not necessarily produce freedom from source framing.

</details>


### [140] [Show your work: An exploratory study of student experiences of Turnitin Clarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34618)
*J. Roe,M. Perkins*

Main category: cs.HC
