TL;DR: 该探索性质性研究以期望-确认理论为框架，考察7名英国大学生在无引导条件下使用Turnitin Clarity的期望与体验，发现整体呈现"混合确认"：功能期望基本满足，被监视感持续存在，AI助手界限引发分歧，接受度取决于练习时间、双向透明与数据治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程捕获平台被厂商宣传为公平措施而非检测工具，但其对学生（必须在此类平台中写作的人）的可接受性基本未被检验，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 探索性质性研究。7名英国大学生在无平台引导（onboarding）的情况下参加使用前焦点小组；其中6人随后在五天内完成一次不计分的500字写作任务，之后7人全部参加使用后焦点小组。数据采用反身性主题分析，并以期望-确认理论作为统摄框架。

Result: 整体呈现"混合确认"。功能期望大多得到满足；预期的被监视感在使用后依然存在，对部分人因感知公平而缓解，对另一些人则加剧自我监控。有边界的AI助手造成分歧：其限制被欢迎（划定可接受使用范围），但部分人感到削弱了对作品的拥有权，并担心会压平产出。参与者权衡观察成本与公平收益，要求透明度双向运行；多人表示已因预期被指控AI滥用而"防御性写作"。

Conclusion: 学生对过程技术的接受是有条件的，取决于练习时间、双向透明与明确的数据治理。参与者质疑单一线性文档能否代表其混乱、多模态的写作过程。过程捕获并非外在于其所记录的写作，而是重组了它本欲观察的实践本身。

Abstract: Process-capture platforms promise to make the writing behind an assessed artefact visible, and vendors increasingly market them as a fairness measure rather than a detection tool. Their acceptability to the students who must write within them, however, is largely untested. This exploratory qualitative study examines how students' expectations of one such platform, Turnitin Clarity, compared with their experience of using it. Seven students at a UK university, given no onboarding to the platform, took part in pre-use focus groups. Six then completed an unassessed 500-word writing task over five days, before all seven returned for a post-use focus group. Data was analysed using reflexive thematic analysis, with Expectation-Confirmation Theory as an orienting frame.
  The overall pattern was one of mixed confirmation. Participants' functional expectations were largely met, and the sense of being watched that they had anticipated persisted after use: for some it was offset by perceived fairness, while for others it heightened self-monitoring. The bounded AI assistant divided them: its limits were welcomed for marking out acceptable use, but some felt it weakened their ownership of the work and worried it would flatten what they produced. Across both phases, participants weighed the costs of observation against perceived gains in fairness and asked for transparency to run in both directions. Several described already writing defensively in anticipation of accusations of AI misuse. Acceptance of process technologies was reported as being conditional on practice time, two-way transparency, and explicit data governance. Participants also questioned whether a single linear document can represent a writing process they described as messy and multimodal. Across these accounts, process capture did not sit outside the writing it recorded but reorganised the practice it set out to observe.

</details>


### [141] [AI Tools Adoption across the Double Diamond Workflow: Phase, Mode, and Barriers in Designer Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34655)
*Sepideh Tajarmakan,Khashayar Hojjati Emami*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 一项涵盖43个国家443名设计师的调查显示，79.7%在双钻流程至少一个阶段确认使用AI，但采用呈部分化，平均覆盖2.89/4个阶段，且保留早期阶段、舍弃后期阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计师采用AI的速度超过了为其构建的工具的跟进速度，因此需要了解AI在真实设计工作流各阶段中的使用情况。该研究旨在提供首批按阶段细分的调查数据之一。

Method: 对43个国家443名设计师进行问卷调查，考察其在双钻工作流四个阶段（发现、定义、开发、交付）中报告的AI使用情况。

Result: 79.7%的设计师至少在1个阶段确认使用AI；使用覆盖平均为4个阶段中的2.89个，且集中于早期阶段。工具选择随各阶段主导活动而变：对话式工具主导发现与定义，AI原生图像生成主导开发，交付阶段呈混合特征。AI原生与嵌入式AI在不同阶段达到峰值，指向两种人机协作模式：从零生成与在现有软件内精修。使用强度下降源于参与设计师减少而非单次使用缩减。采用者与非采用者仅在“感知有用性”一个维度上有差异。

Conclusion: 设计师对AI的采用是部分性、阶段选择性的，工具类型与协作模式随工作流阶段分化；采用差异主要由感知有用性驱动。

Abstract: Designers are adopting AI faster than the tools built for them can keep up. This survey of 443 designers across 43 countries, among the first phase-disaggregated accounts of its kind, examined reported AI use across the four phases of the Double Diamond workflow (Discover, Define, Develop, Deliver). 79.7 percent reported confirmed AI use in at least one phase, but engagement was typically partial, spanning a mean of 2.89 of 4 phases, with adopters retaining the earliest phases and dropping the latest. Tool choice tracked each phase's dominant activity: conversational tools drove Discover and Define, AI-native image generation took over Develop, and Deliver showed a hybrid profile. AI-native and embedded AI use peaked in different phases, pointing to two distinct modes of human-AI collaboration: generating from scratch versus refining within existing software. Use intensity declined through fewer designers engaging, not scaled-back use. Adopters and non-adopters differed on one dimension: perceived usefulness.

</details>


### [142] ["Black Mirror?": Public Sensemaking of AI-Powered Lifelogging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34950)
*Ying Ma,Jarod Govers,Le Fang,Shuning Zhang,Yongquan 'Owen' Hu,Xin Yi,Jorge Goncalves*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究以Looki L1为案例，分析中英文社交媒体评论（N=5,053），考察AI生活记录设备入市初期公众的意义建构，揭示用户如何理解AI介导记忆的社会、道德与政治意涵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的生活记录可穿戴设备正成为新型消费设备，将日常体验转化为可搜索的AI记忆档案，但其社会、道德与政治意涵尚不明确，尤其在市场进入初期的公众理解过程缺乏实证研究。

Method: 以Looki L1为实证切入点，采集大规模中英文社交媒体话语（N=5,053条评论），结合主题聚类与归纳式主题分析。

Result: 用户普遍援引反乌托邦监控想象、表达隐私顺从与旁观者顾虑，并同时讨论辅助价值与消费逻辑。英文评论更常关注人际权力、证据用途与黑客焦虑；中文评论更常聚焦劳动剥削、治理监控与技术必然性。

Conclusion: AI介导记忆的公众意义建构存在跨语境共性（监控想象、隐私顾虑）与文化差异（英文偏人际与安全，中文偏劳动与治理），为理解此类设备的伦理与社会接受提供早期实证依据。

Abstract: AI-powered lifelogging wearables are emerging as a new class of consumer devices that transform everyday experience into searchable, AI-curated memory archives. We study early public sensemaking around these systems at the moment of their market entry, using the Looki L1 as an empirical lens. Analysing large-scale Chinese-language and English-language social media discourse (N = 5,053 comments), we combine topic clustering with inductive thematic analysis to examine how users interpret the social, moral, and political implications of AI-mediated memory. Across contexts, users reference dystopian surveillance imaginaries, express privacy resignation and bystander concerns, and debate assistive value alongside consumer logics. English-language comments more often framed these devices through interpersonal power, evidentiary use, and hacking anxieties, while Chinese-language comments more often foregrounded labour exploitation, governance surveillance, and technological inevitability.

</details>


### [143] [From Early Participation to Later Completion: Evidence from a Large-Scale Self-Paced Learning Programme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34953)
*Sakshi Sharma,Pavani Ayinampudi,Aditya B. M. V.,Jinal Gupta,Prakash Hegade,Rohit Sharma,Meenakshi V,S. R. S. Iyengar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 早期参与积分可预测学习者在无积分自学课程中的后续参与情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模学习项目会产生参与记录，参与积分常用于记录和鼓励参与，但其价值可能超出获积分活动本身。现有评估多局限于实施游戏化元素的活动或环境内，缺乏证据说明早期参与积分是否包含关于后续、且在积分系统之外的参与信息。

Method: 使用某大规模远程软件技能提升实习项目中876名学习者的匿名记录，将来自直播出勤和投票回答的参与积分与后续自学课程完成情况进行对比分析。主分析采用第一周至少获得1分的438名学习者，无积分分析保留全队列。

Result: 第一周参与积分区分后续完成自学课程的学习者，AUC为0.89，到第四周升至0.95。自学课程序列的两个阶段均观察到相似AUC；第一周无积分识别未开始或未完成自学课程的学习者，精确率为95%。

Conclusion: 早期参与积分能为产生积分活动之外的后续参与提供信息，有助于大规模学习项目在学习进行中及时识别可能需要关注的学习者，但不应将参与积分视为整体学习者投入度的衡量指标。

Abstract: Large-scale learning programmes generate records that make learner participation observable across different activities. Participation points are commonly used to record and encourage such participation, but their value may extend beyond the activities for which points are awarded. Existing evaluations often examine gamification outcomes within the activities or learning environments in which the game elements are implemented, providing limited evidence about whether early participation points contain information about later participation outside the points system. This study examines whether early participation points can provide information about learners' later participation in a self-paced learning track that does not award participation points. Using anonymised records from 876 learners in a large-scale remote software-upskilling internship, we examined participation points generated from live-session attendance and poll responses against later self-paced course completion. The primary analysis used the 438 learners who earned at least one point during the first week, while the full cohort was retained for the no-point analysis. Week-one participation points distinguished learners who later completed a self-paced course with an AUC of 0.89, increasing to 0.95 by the fourth week. Similar AUCs were observed at both stages of the self-paced course sequence, while the absence of week-one points identified learners who did not start or did not complete a self-paced course with 95% precision. These findings indicate that early participation points can provide information about later participation outside the activities that generate the points. Such information can help large-scale learning programmes identify learners who may require timely attention while learning is still in progress, without treating participation points as a measure of overall learner engagement.

</details>


### [144] [Toward a Culturally Adapted Chinese Language Agent: A Wizard-of-Oz Study of Nonverbal Behavior in Chinese-German Intercultural Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35150)
*Siddhant Jain,Anna Lea Reinwarth,Dimitra Tsovaltzi,Rafael Math,Julia Renner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一项Wizard-of-Oz研究设计与实时系统，用于采集母语中文者对德语学习者违反社会规范时的多模态反应数据，为面向中文学习的文化适应型对话智能体奠定语料与方法基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功的跨文化交际不仅需要语法能力，还需要对文化嵌入的社会规范的敏感度。对德语母语的汉语学习者而言，这种敏感度至关重要，但现有语言学习智能体支持不足，且缺乏母语者真实反应的实证数据支撑。

Method: 采用Wizard-of-Oz（绿野仙踪）研究设计并搭建实时系统：由Live Link面部捕捉驱动的超写实MetaHuman虚拟形象、MediaPipe上半身追踪、供操作者实时选择行为的wizard控制台，以及跨智能体与学习者两路数据流的同步多模态记录。提出基于心理学理论的分层标注框架，覆盖不可观测的社会情绪反应、规范解读、言语及可观察行为，以及未来监督目标。与文化和教学专家共同开发四个具生态效度的互动场景。

Result: 构建了可实时运行的多模态数据采集系统，并能产出经分层标注的语料，覆盖面部、上身动作与行为选择等多通道反应，数据在智能体流与学习者流之间同步记录。

Conclusion: 该研究为采集规范违背后的多模态行为数据提供了方法与技术基础，所形成语料可支持未来文化解读与行为生成模型的训练，并推动面向中文学习的文化适应型对话智能体发展。

Abstract: Successful intercultural communication requires more than grammatical competence. It demands sensitivity to culturally embedded social norms whose violation triggers subtle but meaningful nonverbal responses. For German learners of Mandarin Chinese, acquiring this sensitivity is critical yet poorly supported by existing language-learning agents. We present a Wizard-of-Oz (WoZ) study design and supporting real-time system for collecting multimodal behavioral data from native Chinese speakers reacting to social norm violations by German learners. The system features a photorealistic MetaHuman avatar driven by Live Link face capture and MediaPipe upper-body tracking, a wizard console for real-time behavior selection, and synchronized multimodal logging across agent and learner streams. A layered annotation framework, based on psychological theory and covering non-observable socioemotional reactions, norm interpretation, verbal, and observable behavior thereof, and future supervision targets enables the corpus to support training of future automated cultural interpretation and behavior generation models. Four ecologically valid interaction scenarios, developed with cultural and pedagogical experts, provide the methodological and technical foundation for a culturally adapted conversational agent for Chinese language learning.

</details>


### [145] [The Future of Visualization Dashboards in the Age of Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35170)
*Vaishali Dhanoa,Duosi Dai,Gabriela Molina Léon,Eduard Gröller,Niklas Elmqvist*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对14个国家16位专家的访谈，研究发现生成式AI将改变而非取代仪表盘：仪表盘仍将用于常规监控与报告，但创作职责将转向需求定义、内容筛选与结果评估，同时带来验证、维护和共享理解等方面的新挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI使仪表盘创建更加容易，这引发了对仪表盘未来以及其创建者和使用者角色的疑问。论文希望了解专家对生成式AI如何影响仪表盘实践与预期的看法。

Method: 访谈研究：对来自14个国家的16位专家进行访谈，了解他们在仪表盘方面的实践与对生成式AI的期望。

Result: 几乎所有专家都预期仪表盘会继续存在，用于重复性问题、监控和报告；预期会出现自适应视图以及语言、图形控件和手势的结合，并强调交互在人类探索与理解中的作用；作者职责将转向需求规范、生成内容的策划与输出评估；同时也担忧验证负担、用户理解、维护、个性化削弱共享理解等问题。

Conclusion: 论文提出关于验证、终端用户教育、仪表盘泛滥与腐化、组织指导、自适应、新颖可视化以及AI生成内容问责等七个研究与实践机会，并将仪表盘演进与维持其使用所需的人力和组织工作联系起来。

Abstract: Generative AI promises easier dashboard creation, raising questions about the future of dashboards and the people who create and use them. We interviewed 16 experts based in 14 countries about their practices and expectations. Almost all expected dashboards to persist for recurring questions, monitoring, and reporting. They anticipated adaptive views and combinations of language, graphical controls, and gestures, while emphasizing interaction as part of human exploration and understanding. Participants expected authors' responsibilities to shift toward specifying requirements, curating generated work, and evaluating outputs, with design knowledge and communication remaining important. Easier creation also raised concerns about validation effort, users' understanding, maintenance, and personalization weakening shared understanding. We discuss seven opportunities for research and practice concerning validation, end-user education, dashboard proliferation and rot, organizational guidance, adaptation, novel visualizations, and accountability for AI-generated content. Our findings connect dashboard evolution with the human and organizational work needed to sustain their use.

</details>


### [146] [Alignment Games: A Framework for Conceptual Repair in Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35197)
*Hari Subramonyam,Maneesh Agrawala,Sean Follmer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出"对齐游戏"（Alignment Games）框架，用于在人与AI交互过程中显现并修复任务相关的概念错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念在使用中的含义受情境、任务、目标和先验知识影响，不同协作者对同一任务的理解可能不同（如"给五岁孩子做的视觉吸引海报"），这种任务相关的差异会阻碍协作，需要被显性化并修复。

Method: 基于情境化概念化理论，用相关属性、值、关系、约束和优先级来刻画任务特定的概念框架；定义对齐动作，干预情境、解释情境的推理过程或由此产生的结果框架；通过对齐动作的组合形成修复序列。

Result: 通过教育内容生成、创意编程和议论文写作等示例，展示了这些对齐动作如何组合为修复序列，并由此推导出在运行时支持"任务充分的概念对齐"的设计原则。

Conclusion: 该框架可帮助在人类与AI交互中识别和修复概念错位，为运行时实现任务充分的概念对齐提供了设计原则。

Abstract: The meaning of a concept in use is shaped by the situation, task, goals, and prior knowledge. For example, a request to make a poster "visually appealing for a five-year-old" might evoke bright colors and cartoon imagery for one collaborator, but less text, bold shapes, and visual simplicity for another. We call such task-relevant differences conceptual misalignment. We introduce Alignment Games, a framework for making these differences visible and repairable during human-AI interaction. Drawing on theories of situated conceptualization, we characterize task-specific conceptual frames in terms of relevant attributes, values, relations, constraints, and priorities. We then define alignment moves that intervene on the situation, the reasoning used to interpret it, or the resulting frame. Through examples from educational content generation, creative coding, and argumentative writing, we show how these moves can be composed into repair sequences and derive design principles for supporting task-sufficient conceptual alignment at runtime.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Replay in the Silent Degrees of Freedom: Continual Learning Without an Offline Phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31630)
*Zhang Yanhai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无需离线阶段、仅靠局部生物约束规则即可实现在线巩固的持续学习方法，通过隔离规则、不应期轮转、稳态压力与相对新颖性门控，在k-WTA动力学下将回放更新限制于隐藏突触，实现当前输入计算不变而过去记忆写入未用自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于回放的持续学习几乎都依赖专门的离线阶段或与输入流交错回放，而大脑还能在清醒时通过局部睡眠（单个回路的短暂使用依赖性停歇期）进行巩固。论文追问：一个由局部、生物约束规则训练的网络，能否完全不依赖离线阶段实现巩固。

Method: 提出三条规则协同作用：（1）隔离规则，在k-winner-take-all动力学下将回放更新限制在相对当前输入不可见的隐藏突触，优化器状态仅在掩码内推进；（2）不应期轮转规则，使刚发放的单元在下一轮竞争中缺席，扩大可巩固集合；（3）稳态压力与相对新颖性门控，决定回放爆发何时触发、轮转何时运行。整体上反转了非干扰式持续学习的通常方向：保持当前输入的隐藏计算不变，同时将过去记忆写入当前批次未使用的自由度。

Result: 在类增量split-MNIST上无需离线阶段达到91.6±0.3%，在两个留出划分上达到或超过最佳离线夜间调度，与DER++持平并高于experience replay、ER-ACE、A-GEM及未掩码局部回放；单次通过时以91.8%领先DER++的90.1%（后者夜间降至76.9%）。小缓冲区时优势最大，大缓冲区时让位于反向传播参照方法。在split CIFAR-10上领先离线排练和experience replay，但落后于ER-ACE和DER++。轮转贡献了大部分增益，隔离提供了不变性保证。

Conclusion: 轮转带来主要增益，隔离额外提供不变性保证，且机制不局限于局部规则：在相同调度下，具有k-WTA隐藏层的反向传播网络同样能从轮转中获益，隔离在其之上仍是免费的。

Abstract: Replay-based continual learning almost always consolidates in a dedicated offline phase or by interleaving replayed samples with the input stream, whereas brains also consolidate during wakefulness through local sleep, brief use-dependent off-periods of individual circuits. We ask whether a network trained by local, biologically constrained rules can consolidate with no offline phase at all. An isolation rule confines replay updates to hidden synapses invisible to the current input under k-winner-take-all dynamics, with optimiser state advanced only inside the mask; a refractory rotation rule makes units that have just fired sit out the next competition, widening the consolidable set; a homeostatic pressure and a relative-novelty gate decide when replay bursts fire and when rotation runs. This inverts the usual direction of non-interfering continual learning: the hidden computation on the current input is held invariant (exactly on the proven channels, and for all but 0.3% of waking samples per update elsewhere) while past memories are written into the degrees of freedom the current batch leaves unused. On class-incremental split-MNIST the system reaches 91.6+-0.3% with no offline phase, at or above the best offline-night schedule on two held-out splits, tied with DER++ and above experience replay, ER-ACE, A-GEM and unmasked local replay; in a single pass it leads DER++ (91.8% against 90.1%) while the night falls to 76.9%. The advantage is largest at small buffers and gives way to the backpropagation references at large ones; on split CIFAR-10 the system leads offline rehearsal and experience replay but trails ER-ACE and DER++. Rotation carries most of the gain; isolation adds the invariance guarantee. The mechanism is not tied to the local rule: under the same schedule a backpropagation network with k-WTA hidden layers gains from rotation, and isolation is again free on top of it.

</details>


### [148] [OMP-MoE: Efficient Expert Pruning for Mixture-of-Experts LLMs via Orthogonal Matching Pursuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31631)
*Dezhi Li,Lujun Li,Qiyuan Zhu,Hao Gu,Bei Liu,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OMP-MoE，一个免训练的MoE大模型专家剪枝压缩框架，将剪枝建模为稀疏信号重构，用正交匹配追踪与注水策略实现高效专家选择与跨层分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽有利于大模型高效扩展，但内存需求巨大，部署困难。现有剪枝方法要么搜索成本过高，要么忽视专家之间的动态依赖关系。

Method: 免训练压缩框架：1) 将单个专家贡献视为字典原子，用正交匹配追踪(OMP)以线性复杂度贪心选择最小化重构误差的专家；2) 用注水策略优化跨层专家分配，兼顾重构质量与路由稳定性；3) 提出OMP-MoE†自适应推理机制，基于能量预测动态调整专家激活。

Result: 在Qwen、DeepSeek-V2、GPT-OSS、Mixtral等MoE模型上，25-50%剪枝率下均优于现有方法。Qwen3-30B-A3B在50%压缩下保留93.3%原性能，搜索速度提升33倍，推理加速1.55倍。

Conclusion: OMP-MoE通过稀疏重构视角统一解决MoE专家冗余问题，实现低搜索成本、高压缩率与性能保持，为MoE大模型部署提供高效免训练方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models enable efficient scaling of large language models but face critical deployment challenges due to massive memory requirements. Existing pruning methods either incur prohibitive search costs or neglect the dynamic interdependencies between experts. To address these challenges, we present OMP-MoE, a novel training-free compression framework for reducing expert redundancy in MoE-based LLMs. Based on observations of expert contribution patterns, we reformulate the pruning problem as a sparse signal reconstruction task solved through Orthogonal Matching Pursuit. Specifically, our method first treats individual expert contributions as dictionary atoms and selects experts that greedily minimize reconstruction error with linear computational complexity. Then, we optimize cross-layer expert allocation through a water-filling strategy that accounts for both reconstruction quality and routing stability. Finally, we introduce OMP-MoE†, an adaptive inference mechanism that dynamically adjusts expert activation based on energy prediction. Comprehensive experiments on Qwen, DeepSeek-V2, GPT-OSS, and Mixtral MoE demonstrate consistent improvements over existing methods at 25-50% pruning ratios. For Qwen3-30B-A3B at 50% compression, we retain 93.3% of original performance, achieving 33$\times$ faster search and 1.55$\times$ inference speedup. Codes will be available after acceptance.

</details>


### [149] [EEGAgentBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Short- and Long-Horizon EEG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31632)
*Huyu Wu,Weining Weng,Yuchen Liu,Yiqiang Chen,Yang Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出EEGAgentBench，一个统一的基准，用于系统评估LLM智能体在短时与长时脑电分析中的知识推理、工具使用与工作流构建能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电分析正从短片段分类走向长时程解读，需迭代证据积累、多步推理与专用工具协同。现有LLM智能体评测碎片化、任务与时间跨度有限、协议不统一，缺乏对智能体推理、工具使用和工作流构建的全面评估，因此需要统一基准填补空白。

Method: EEGAgentBench涵盖六类代表性脑电应用（从知识问答到睡眠分期），信号时长2秒至近23小时，预测目标包括类别标签、事件区间与epoch级序列，支持知识推理、短时解读、长时事件检测与序列理解四类评估。基准提供10个确定性脑电分析工具，仅暴露任务相关信号测量，要求智能体自主选工具、迭代累积证据并构建多步工作流。共评测15个模型家族、29个前沿LLM。

Result: EEGAgentBench能有效区分超出模型规模与推理成本之外的智能体能力差异；结果显示当前LLM智能体在长时程脑电分析中存在显著局限，尤其体现在持续证据积累与多步推理方面。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了统一、跨时间跨度的脑电分析智能体评测基准，揭示了现有LLM智能体在长时程推理与证据整合上的不足，为后续智能体能力提升指明方向。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) analysis is evolving from short-segment classification toward long-horizon interpretation that demands iterative evidence accumulation, multi-step reasoning, and coordinated use of specialized signal-processing tools. Although large language models (LLMs) have recently shown promise as autonomous agents for EEG analysis, existing EEG agentic evaluations remain fragmented, covering limited tasks over narrow temporal horizons with inconsistent protocols, and providing no comprehensive assessment of agents' reasoning, tool-use, and workflow construction capabilities. To address this gap, we propose \textbf{EEGAgentBench}, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating LLM agents on short- and long-horizon EEG analysis. EEGAgentBench spans six representative EEG applications ranging from knowledge question answering to sleep staging. It encompasses signal durations from 2 seconds to nearly 23 hours, with prediction targets ranging from class labels to event intervals and epoch-level sequences. This design supports unified evaluation across knowledge reasoning, short-horizon interpretation, long-horizon event detection, and sequential understanding. The benchmark further provides 10 deterministic EEG analysis tools that expose only task-relevant signal measurements. Agents must therefore select tools autonomously, accumulate evidence iteratively, and construct multi-step workflows. For evaluation, we benchmark 29 frontier LLMs from 15 model families. Results demonstrate that EEGAgentBench effectively distinguishes agent capabilities beyond model scale and inference cost, while revealing substantial limitations of current LLM agents in long-horizon EEG analysis, particularly in sustained evidence accumulation and multi-step reasoning.

</details>


### [150] [Enhancing generalization in endwall film cooling prediction: Incorporating the superposition principle into transformer-based neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31633)
*Qineng Wang,Liming Song,Tianyuan Liu,Zhendong Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理增强神经算子框架SDNO，分两阶段预测涡轮端壁气膜冷却温度场，训练用1-5孔样本即可泛化预测10-20孔布局，精度优于全监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡轮端壁气膜冷却布局设计需快速准确预测温度场，但气膜孔数量可变导致传统模型泛化能力差，难以预测训练分布外的孔数布局。

Method: 受气膜冷却叠加原理启发，构建叠加式深度神经算子(SDNO)，将温度场预测分两阶段：第一阶段用基于Transformer的Calculate Net对随机分配气膜孔的各子区域预测温度场；第二阶段用Super Net将各子区域温度场叠加为整体布局温度场。同时用对孔位置敏感的有符号距离函数(SDF)编码气膜孔位置，替代直接像素化冷却轮廓。

Result: 模型仅在1-5个可变位置气膜孔样本上训练，即可准确预测训练中未见的10-20孔布局的气膜冷却效率，且相比全监督基线预测精度更高。

Conclusion: SDNO框架有效提升了变孔数涡轮端壁气膜冷却布局的泛化预测能力，验证了该物理增强神经算子方法的有效性。

Abstract: In this study, a physics-enhanced neural operator framework is proposed to enhance the generalization prediction ability of the cooling layout of a turbine endwall with variable number of film holes. Specifically, inspired by the film cooling superposition principle, we propose a film cooling prediction model, namely superposition-based deep neural operator (SDNO), that divides the endwall temperature field prediction into two stages. In the first stage, the cooling layout of a turbine endwall is divided into several sub-parts with randomly assigned film holes, and a Transformer-based neural operator network, namely Calculate Net, is designed to predict the temperature field of each sub-part. Then, in the second stage, another neural operator network, i.e., Super Net, is trained to combine the temperature fields predicted by Calculate Net for each sub-part and obtain the superposed temperature field of the full cooling layout. Additionally, instead of directly taking the film cooling contours as pixel plots, a signed distance function (SDF) which is sensitive to the variable locations of cooling holes, is designed to encode the location information of cooling holes. Furthermore, the proposed endwall film cooling prediction model is trained with the samples that changing the number of film holes from 1-5 with variable locations. Then, the trained prediction shows excellent generalization prediction ability, which can accurately predict the film effectiveness of the cooling layout with 10-20 film cooling holes that are unseen in the training samples. The proposed SDNO also improves prediction accuracy relative to the fully supervised baseline. With the above, the effectiveness of our proposed prediction model has been well demonstrated.

</details>


### [151] [Symmetry-quotient Flatness and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31634)
*Taiki Miyagawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在对称商空间中建立理论链路：商线性稳定性⇒商平坦性⇒输入光滑性⇒泛化，为神经网络参数在函数保持对称下的平坦性与泛化提供不变性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准平坦性度量在原始参数空间中定义，对正缩放等函数保持对称不具不变性，而平坦性常与泛化相关、SGD也常被认为偏向平坦解，因此需要对称感知的商空间理论。

Method: 在神经网络参数的商空间上，针对平方损失和具备函数保持群作用的模型，将商平坦性定义为经验损失在正则商流形上Hessian的迹；通过商空间版的平坦到光滑论证连接商平坦性与输入光滑性；证明线性化SGD动力学的一步均方商线性稳定性蕴含显式商平坦界（依赖批大小与学习率），并推广至更高阶张量矩；在局部覆盖与有界性假设下推导总体泛化界。

Result: 建立了quotient线性稳定性⇒quotient平坦性⇒输入光滑性⇒泛化的定理级链路；给出由批大小和学习率表示的显式商平坦界；在局部覆盖与有界性假设下得到以商平坦性、进而以商线性稳定性表达的总体泛化界。

Conclusion: 通过商空间视角，平坦性与稳定性可在函数保持对称下被不变地刻画，并可作为泛化的可控度量，为理解SGD隐式偏置与泛化提供理论基础。

Abstract: This paper develops a theorem-level pipeline in symmetry-quotient settings: quotient linear stability implies quotient flatness, quotient flatness implies input smoothness, and input smoothness yields generalization under local covering assumptions. Flatness is often associated with generalization, and Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) is frequently viewed as implicitly biased toward flat solutions. However, standard flatness measures are typically defined in the raw parameter space and are therefore not invariant under function-preserving symmetries such as positive rescaling. We develop a symmetry-aware theory of quotient flatness, quotient linear stability, input smoothness, and generalization on quotient spaces of neural-network parameters. For square loss and models equipped with function-preserving group actions, we define quotient flatness as the trace of the Hessian of the empirical loss on the regular quotient manifold. We show that quotient flatness controls input smoothness through a quotient-space analogue of the flatness-to-smoothness argument. We also prove that one-step mean-square quotient linear stability of the linearized SGD dynamics implies an explicit quotient-flatness bound in terms of the batch size and learning rate, and extend this analysis to higher-order tensor moments. Finally, under local covering and boundedness assumptions, we derive population generalization bounds in terms of quotient flatness and, consequently, in terms of quotient linear stability.

</details>


### [152] [What Next-Event Accuracy Cannot See: Closed-Loop Evaluation of Emergency Department Trajectory Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31635)
*Zhen Xuen Brandon Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EDSim-Bench，用425,028条MIMIC-IV-ED就诊记录系统量化临床轨迹模拟器在自回归生成中的误差累积问题，发现next-event准确率无法反映rollout表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床轨迹模型通常用观测历史上的next-event准确率评估，但模拟时必须基于自身生成的事件做条件预测，误差会累积；该问题在序列建模中已知，却未被系统量化于临床轨迹模拟器。

Method: 构建EDSim-Bench，基于425,028条MIMIC-IV-ED就诊并在MC-MED外部复现；从留出前缀出发生成整段就诊，从终止、事件组成、时序、条件保真度、占用预测五方面评估，并以仅用训练集的order-3 n-gram为基线；比较Transformer、GRU、LSTM及其训练监督方式。

Result: 尽管next-event准确率相差在0.001以内，三种神经网络在rollout下差异巨大：某Transformer配方终止分在不同种子下从0.43到0.96，分歧达n-gram的4.2至137倍；无前缀训练神经网络在终止与事件组成上接近n-gram。对所有可监督位置监督比仅监督前缀末位分歧低1-2个数量级，且在模型扩展、时间偏移、外部站点下持续。最佳模型生成的就诊长度约为观测的一半，占用预测上模型排名出现反转。

Conclusion: next-event准确率不足以评估临床轨迹模拟器，需在多种子、多rollout准则与下游任务上进行闭环评估。

Abstract: Clinical trajectory models are usually evaluated by next-event accuracy on observed histories. Simulation is different: models must condition on their own generated events, allowing errors to compound. Although this problem is well known in sequence modelling, it has not been systematically quantified for clinical trajectory simulators. We developed EDSim-Bench to evaluate this failure mode using 425,028 MIMIC-IV-ED stays, with external replication on MC-MED, and release the evaluation protocol and scoring code. Starting from held-out visit prefixes, models generate the remainder of each visit and are evaluated on termination, event composition, timing, conditional fidelity, and occupancy forecasting, with a train-only order-3 n-gram as a reference baseline. Despite next-event accuracies within 0.001, three neural architectures behaved very differently under rollout. Across seeds, one Transformer recipe ranged from 0.43 to 0.96 in termination score and from 4.2- to 137-fold the divergence of the n-gram; no prefix-trained neural model approached the n-gram on termination or event composition. Inference-time interventions improved termination but did not jointly recover composition and timing. Supervising every eligible sequence position rather than only the final prefix position was associated with one to two orders of magnitude lower divergence across Transformer, GRU, and LSTM models, with the pattern persisting under model scaling, temporal shift, and external-site evaluation. Nevertheless, even the best model generated visits approximately half as long as observed, and model rankings reversed on occupancy forecasting, a downstream quantity relevant to bed management. These results show that next-event accuracy is insufficient to evaluate clinical trajectory simulators and motivate closed-loop evaluation across seeds, rollout criteria, and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [153] [Grounding Vision-Language Models in Driving Semantics: A Multi-Dataset Predicate Framework for Explainable Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31636)
*Mohamed Chouai,Fazli Faruk Okumus,Stefan Kugele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性多数据集谓词框架，从可测量的几何、运动学、时间、地图和交通控制证据中推导驾驶场景语义，并在nuPlan和nuScenes上构建统一的谓词知识图谱，实现可验证、可追溯的场景语义表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型日益用于驾驶场景理解，但其输出所表达的语义关系往往难以与真实交通状况进行核验，缺乏可验证性和可追溯性。

Method: 采用确定性多数据集谓词框架，从几何、运动学、时间、地图与交通控制等可测量证据推导场景语义；数据集特定接口仅用于恢复场景信息，谓词定义在nuPlan和nuScenes间保持不变，并物化为统一的谓词知识图谱。

Result: 在每数据集200个场景的人工标注谓词关系上语义验证，nuPlan宏F1为0.94，nuScenes为0.93，共享谓词跨数据集平均差异0.02；用冻结的LLaVA-OneVision-7B在九个NuPlanQA子任务评估，谓词接地在七个子任务中取得最高准确率，包括交通灯（53.2%→71.5%）、态势评估（76.2%→86.1%）、动作推荐（82.9%→89.0%）；天气/光照基本不变（89.4% vs 88.8%），与缺乏相应谓词一致；仅谓词KG输入在九个任务中的八个优于仅元数据输入。

Conclusion: 确定性谓词提供了一致且可追溯的语义表示，在oracle接地条件下能够降低谓词词汇覆盖范围内推理任务对视觉输入的依赖。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used for driving-scene understanding, yet the semantic relations expressed in their outputs are often difficult to verify against the underlying traffic situation. This paper introduces a deterministic multi-dataset predicate framework that derives driving-scene semantics from measurable geometric, kinematic, temporal, map, and traffic-control evidence. Dataset-specific interfaces are used only to recover the required scene information, while predicate definitions remain unchanged across nuPlan and nuScenes and are materialised in a common Predicate Knowledge Graph. Quantitative semantic validation against manually annotated predicate relations on 200 scenarios from each dataset yields macro F1 scores of 0.94 on nuPlan and 0.93 on nuScenes, with an average cross-dataset difference of 0.02 across the shared predicates. The Predicate KG is further evaluated using a frozen LLaVA-OneVision-7B model on the nine NuPlanQA subtasks. Predicate grounding achieves the highest accuracy among the evaluated visual-input conditions in seven of nine NuPlanQA subtasks, including Traffic Light (53.2% to 71.5%), Situation Assessment (76.2% to 86.1%), and Action Recommendation (82.9% to 89.0%). Weather/Lighting remains essentially unchanged (89.4% vs. 88.8%), consistent with the absence of corresponding predicates, while Predicate KG only input outperforms metadata-only input in eight of nine subtasks. The results show that deterministic predicates provide a consistent and traceable semantic representation and, under oracle grounding, can reduce visual dependence for reasoning tasks covered by the predicate vocabulary.

</details>


### [154] [FIDAL: Diversity-Aware Federated Active Learning Under Real-World Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31637)
*David Dueñas Gaviria,Shadi Albarqouni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FIDAL，一个开放集联邦主动学习框架，整合校准的全局-局部证据不确定性、支持集多样性加权与自适应OOD拒绝，在三个多中心医学影像基准上超越检测器类开放集方法约12个百分点，并实现最佳精度-ID纯度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临标注成本高、域偏移和类别不平衡问题，尤其是无关的分布外（OOD）样本会稀释已标注数据。现有主动学习方法仅关注分布内（ID）数据的不确定性或多样性，忽略了联邦临床场景中的未知样本，因此需要同时解决ID样本查询与OOD样本拒绝的问题。

Method: FIDAL结合三部分：1）校准的全局-局部证据不确定性；2）支持集多样性加权；3）自适应OOD拒绝。拒绝门控通过Otsu准则对每个客户端、每轮的基座模型高斯覆盖信号进行阈值处理，无需手工调参即可查询高信息量的ID样本并排除无关离群点。

Result: 在皮肤病学、组织病理学和乳腺摄影（含自然伪影）三个多中心医学影像基准的真实开放集场景中，FIDAL在平衡准确率上比检测器类开放集方法高出约12个百分点，是唯一在三个基准的精度-ID纯度Pareto前沿上的方法。相同查询预算下，其OOD样本标注量比所有精度匹配基线少至少1.3倍，乳腺摄影基准上估计节省7-29小时专家阅片时间；仅标注部分数据池即可达到或超过全监督性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在医学开放集联邦主动学习中整合不确定性、多样性与OOD拒绝具有重要价值。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training across institutions without centralizing data, yet high annotation costs, domain shifts, and class imbalance remain major obstacles, especially when irrelevant out-of-distribution (OOD) samples dilute the labeled data. Existing active learning methods target uncertainty or diversity within in-distribution (ID) data and overlook unknown samples in federated clinical settings. We propose FIDAL, an open-set federated active learning framework that combines calibrated global-local evidential uncertainty, support-set diversity weighting, and adaptive OOD rejection. The rejection gate thresholds a foundation-model Gaussian-coverage signal per client and per round with Otsu's criterion, so that highly informative ID samples are queried while irrelevant outliers are excluded without any hand-tuned threshold. Evaluated on three multi-center medical imaging benchmarks (dermatology, histopathology, and mammography with organically occurring artifacts) in realistic open-set scenarios, FIDAL outperforms detector-based open-set methods by up to about 12 percentage points of balanced accuracy and is the only method on the accuracy-ID purity Pareto front of all three benchmarks. At an equal query budget it spends at least 1.3 times fewer annotations on OOD samples than every accuracy-matched baseline, saving an estimated 7-29 hours of expert reading on the mammography benchmark. By labeling only a fraction of the data pool, it matches or exceeds fully supervised performance across modalities. These results highlight the value of integrating uncertainty, diversity, and OOD rejection in open-set federated active learning for medicine.

</details>


### [155] [Energy-aware frugal Bayesian optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31638)
*Gaston Plat,Paul Saves,Nathalie Bartoli,Thierry Lefebvre,Joseph Morlier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在贝叶斯优化框架中引入计算能耗足迹指标，引导模型参数设置兼顾预测性能与计算节约，实验显示可改善收敛与能耗之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代设计优化框架过度追求预测精度而忽视计算开销，导致大规模架构与多学科设计优化问题难以处理，即使使用样本高效的贝叶斯优化器也收效有限。

Method: 在贝叶斯优化框架内引入一个量化计算能耗足迹的度量指标，用以引导模型的参数设置，使配置在性能与节约之间取得平衡。

Result: 计算机实验揭示了最优收敛与底层能耗足迹之间存在的权衡关系，并且有时能够同时获得更优的极值点和更低的能耗。

Conclusion: 将能耗足迹纳入贝叶斯优化目标可有效平衡性能与计算成本，为高开销优化问题提供更节能的解决途径。

Abstract: Modern design optimization frameworks aim first and foremost for models with the most accurate predictions without balancing computational overhead. It remains a reason why scaled architecture and multidisciplinary design optimization problems are difficult to address, even with sample-efficient Bayesian optimizers. In this paper, a metric quantifying the computational energy footprint is introduced within a Bayesian optimization framework to guide the parameter setting of a model towards configurations that balance both performance and frugality. The computer experiments highlighted existing tradeoffs between optimum convergence and the underlying energy footprint, and sometimes resulted in both a better-found optimum and lower energy consumption.

</details>


### [156] [When Does Domain Adaptation Help on Physical Vibration Sensors? A Held-Out-Bearing Study of Neural-Operator and Convolutional Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31639)
*Kumbha Nagaswetha,Rabi Pathak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在留出轴承（held-out-bearing）协议下重新评估滚动轴承故障诊断的域适应任务，发现仅源域迁移在转速变化时准确率仅0.36，远低于常报的99%；表示形式（阶次域 vs 频域）而非对齐方法才是决定迁移成败的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究报告的99%以上准确率通常在同一物理轴承同时出现在训练和测试集的划分下取得，存在数据泄漏，不能反映真实的工况迁移能力。作者希望用更严格的留出轴承协议，考察域适应在该任务中的真实表现，并厘清决定迁移效果的因素。

Method: 采用留出轴承协议，将每个轴承单元完整分配给训练或测试集；以计算阶次跟踪（将故障频率固定到与转速无关的固定轴阶）作为受控的表示变换；对比傅里叶神经算子（FNO）与特征维度匹配的卷积网络在频域和阶次域下的源域迁移；并使用相同的归一化RBF-MMD损失进行无监督对齐，比较频域与阶次域的效果，另加少量目标标签预算实验；第二个数据集的留出单元为故障直径。

Result: 转速变化下仅源域迁移仅0.36（目标监督上限0.97）；FNO将源域迁移从0.36提升到0.61，而匹配维度的卷积网络在两种表示下都接近随机水平；相同RBF-MMD损失无目标标签时，算子在频域达0.71，在阶次域达0.95，距目标监督上限仅0.02，且在每一留出轴承折上均超过0.86；表示正确后增加少量标签收益很小。第二个数据集表明同一协议暴露了即使目标监督模型也难以避免的失败。

Conclusion: 在该任务中，决定域适应能否奏效的是输入表示，而非对齐方法；阶次域表示使无监督对齐接近目标监督上限，且对标签需求大幅降低；更严格的留出协议能揭示被高准确率掩盖的真实迁移困难。

Abstract: Diagnosing rolling-element bearing faults from vibration is a canonical physical-sensing task and a widely used benchmark for domain adaptation under operating-condition shift. Accuracies above 99 percent are commonly reported, but under evaluation splits that place the same physical bearing in both training and test. We revisit the task under a held-out-bearing protocol, assigning every bearing unit entirely to either the training or the test set, and find that source-only transfer is far weaker than such numbers suggest: on a change of shaft speed it reaches only $0.36$, against a target-supervised ceiling of 0.97. We then study what governs transfer. Treating computed order tracking, a shaft-angle resampling that places fault frequencies at fixed shaft orders independent of running speed, as a controlled change of representation, we find that a Fourier Neural Operator raises source-only transfer from $0.36$ to $0.61$ on the speed shift, where the fault peaks move, while a convolutional network of matched feature dimension stays near chance in both representations. The representation also decides whether unsupervised alignment can work: with the same normalized RBF-MMD loss and no target labels, the operator reaches 0.71 in the frequency domain but 0.95 in the order domain, within 0.02 of the target-supervised ceiling and above $0.86$ on every held-out bearing fold. Once the representation is right, a small label budget adds little. These results indicate that, for this task, the input representation rather than the alignment method decides whether adaptation helps. A second dataset, whose held-out units are fault diameters rather than bearings, shows that the same protocol exposes failures that even a target-supervised model cannot avoid.

</details>


### [157] [Measure Learning at Steady State: A BIRD-SQL Formula 1 Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31640)
*Manoj Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文把ICL视为完整学习系统，在更长的共享世界调度（174题的BIRD-SQL F1任务）上评估，用"稳态学习"（后40题相对基线的差距）替代短视界增益，并把分数拆为探索效率、任务奖励和交付成本。结果显示无界ICL虽节省后期探测（4.6-5.6降至0.95），但命中提升有限，上下文膨胀至约95k tokens、成本近乎翻倍，因此短视界增益会低估探测节省并掩盖成本反转，无界ICL并非理想的学习机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有Continual Learning Bench以短视界增益对比重置基线进行评估，并认为朴素的full-context ICL在受测记忆中最强。但短视界评估可能遗漏长期成本与部署代价的变化，因此需要把ICL作为一个整体学习系统，在更长、共享世界的调度下重新衡量其真实表现。

Method: 将ICL视为单一学习系统，在更长的共享世界调度上打分：以174道BIRD-SQL F1题目中预先设定的后40题为后期窗口，用相对基线的差距定义稳态学习；并把总分拆解为探索效率（SQL探测次数）、任务奖励（命中数）和交付成本（API美元开销与上下文规模）三个维度评估。

Result: 在gpt-5.6-luna上，后期探测次数从4.6-5.6降至0.95，但命中数仅小幅上升；ICL上下文增长到约95k tokens，成本约翻倍，出现明显的成本反转。

Conclusion: 短视界增益低估了后期探测节省，却遗漏了成本反转这一关键现象；因此无界ICL并不是理想的学习机制，需要引入具备成本约束、可持续的替代学习机制。

Abstract: Continual Learning Bench scores learning as short-horizon gain versus a reset baseline and finds naive full-context ICL strongest among the memories it tested. We treat ICL as one learning system and score it on a longer shared-world schedule. Steady-state learning is the gap versus baseline on a pre-set late window (last 40 of 174 BIRD-SQL formula-1 questions). We split the score into exploration efficiency (SQL probes), task reward (hits), and delivery cost (API dollars and context size). On gpt-5.6-luna, late probes fall from 4.6-5.6 to 0.95 while hits rise only modestly and ICL context grows to about 95k tokens with cost roughly doubling. Short-horizon gain understates the late probe saving and misses the cost inversion, so we find that unbounded ICL is a poor candidate for the learning mechanism.

</details>


### [158] [Product-Aware Deterministic Rounding for Quantized Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31641)
*Piyush Sao,Narasinga Miniskar,Pedro Valero-Lara,Keita Teranishi,Sudip Seal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究量化中确定性的乘积感知舍入：通过零空间约简与条件期望补齐，在多项式时间内逼近最优乘积误差，并给出静态权重的精确度量与NP难结论，实验验证显著优于最近舍入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量舍入决策会通过矩阵乘法相互耦合，传统的逐元素舍入（如round-to-nearest）只优化单个标量误差，忽略对整体乘积的影响。论文希望在缩放、裁剪边界与网格固定后，研究确定性的、考虑乘积效果的舍入方案，以降低矩阵乘法的整体乘积误差。

Method: 1) 动态激活舍入：用零空间约简保持松弛后的乘积不变，仅保留至多r个分数决策（r为活跃的间隙加权权重块的秩）；再用条件期望补齐，得到确定性多项式时间算法。2) 静态可复用权重：给出精确期望乘积损失度量——在输出偏置固定时为非中心输入二阶矩，自由重校准偏置时为中心化协方差；并证明精确优化即使秩为1也是NP难。3) 提出裁剪感知的初始化方法。

Result: 动态舍入算法的平方乘积误差上界为OPT_dyn + rν_max²/4（ν_max为该块最大行范数）。在K=1024、r=16的平衡块中，条件期望补齐的抖动归一化中位误差为0.010，而最近舍入为0.899；裁剪感知初始化在10%裁剪下将中位归一化误差降低43.4倍。留出Digits实验中，保留输入均值或校正输出偏置在全部四种位宽与校准规模设置下均改善中位乘积误差，优于最近舍入。

Conclusion: 乘积感知的舍入可通过零空间约简与条件期望补齐在多项式时间内有效实现，误差接近最优且大幅优于逐元素最近舍入；静态权重的最优舍入本质上困难（NP难），但偏置校准等简单机制即可显著提升，验证了考虑乘积耦合的舍入设计对量化矩阵乘法的价值。

Abstract: Scalar rounding decisions interact through matrix multiplication. We study deterministic
  product-aware rounding after scales, clipping bounds, and grids are fixed, with each active
  scalar choosing between adjacent levels. For dynamic activation rounding, null-space
  reduction preserves the relaxed product while leaving at most $r$ fractional decisions, where
  $r$ is the rank of the active gap-weighted weight block. Conditional-expectation completion
  gives a deterministic polynomial-time algorithm with squared product error at most $
  \mathrm{OPT}_{\mathrm{dyn}}+rν_{\max}^2/4$, where $\mathrm{OPT}_{\mathrm{dyn}}$ is the best
  admissible error and $ν_{\max}$ is the largest row norm of that block. For reusable static
  weights, the exact expected product-loss metric is the uncentered input second moment with
  fixed output bias; free bias recalibration yields the centered covariance. Exact optimization
  is NP-hard even at rank one. In balanced blocks with $K=1024$ and $r=16$, conditional-
  expectation completion attains a dither-normalized median error of $0.010$, compared with
  $0.899$ for round-to-nearest. Clipping-aware initialization reduces median normalized error
  by a factor of $43.4$ at ten-percent clipping. Held-out Digits experiments show that
  retaining the input mean or correcting the output bias improves median product error over
  round-to-nearest in all four tested bit-width and calibration-size settings.

</details>


### [159] [Information Design Against Gaming and Learning Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31643)
*Madhava Gaikwad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 部署带弃权选项的二分类器时，弃权策略的选择取决于对手类型：面对博弈型对手应弃权于边界附近，但面对学习型对手这种策略反而最糟。两种自然防御（固定率弃权与近边界弃权）在Blackwell意义下不可比较，且重构边界所需查询数分别为tildeΘ(d/eps)与Θ(d log(1/eps))。研究刻画了两者间的帕累托前沿，并在七个任务上验证了结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署带弃权选项的二分类器时，主体必须决定对哪些查询弃权，而正确选择取决于对手性质。博弈型对手已知分类器并试图跨边界操纵特征，学习型对手则不知道分类器、会从弃权信号中推断边界位置。作者希望分析这两种相冲突的防御目标之间的张力，并给出可量化的权衡。

Method: 作者从理论角度比较两种自然防御：固定率弃权和近边界弃权，利用Blackwell可比较性证明二者不可相互模拟。针对学习型对手，推导重构边界所需查询数的上界与下界：固定率弃权为tildeΘ(d/eps)，近边界弃权为Θ(d log(1/eps))，其中d为分类器族的VC维。进一步刻画两种防御目标之间的帕累托前沿，并在七个二分类任务（表格、图像和语言模型特征输入）上做实证验证，比较标签加反事实访问与仅标签访问的查询效率。

Result: 理论上证明两种防御在Blackwell意义下不可比较，无法通过后处理相互模拟；重构边界的查询复杂度分别为tildeΘ(d/eps)（固定率）和Θ(d log(1/eps))（近边界），且前者是在某些查询分布上的最坏情形下界，任何重构算法都无法在这些分布上缩小差距。实验在七个二分类任务上同时验证了两种速率：标签加反事实访问相比已发表的仅标签基线，最多可用少200倍的查询重构边界。

Conclusion: 弃权策略的优劣完全取决于对手模型，不存在普遍最优的单一防御。固定率弃权与近边界弃权在信息获取效率上存在本质差异，且可在帕累托前沿上进行权衡。该结果为设计针对不同对手的弃权机制提供了理论依据和实证支持。

Abstract: A principal who deploys a binary classifier with an abstention option must decide which queries the mechanism abstains on. The right choice depends on the adversary. A gaming adversary already knows the classifier and tries to manipulate features across the boundary, so the principal does best by abstaining on queries close to that boundary. The same boundary-localizing rule is the worst possible choice against a learning adversary who does not know the classifier: each abstention now tells the adversary that the boundary is nearby, which is enough to drive a binary search. We analyze this tension. The two natural defenses, abstaining at a fixed rate and abstaining near the boundary, are Blackwell-incomparable: neither can be simulated by post-processing the other's responses. The number of queries needed to reconstruct the boundary to error $\eps$ is $\tildeΘ(d/\eps)$ under the first defense and $Θ(d \log(1/\eps))$ under the second, where $d$ is the VC dimension of the classifier family and $\tildeΘ$ suppresses factors polylogarithmic in $d$ and $1/\eps$. The first rate is a worst case over query distributions; no reconstruction algorithm can close the gap at the distributions that attain it. We characterize the Pareto frontier between the two defense objectives, and confirm both rates on seven binary-classification tasks spanning tabular, image, and language-model-feature inputs: label-plus-counterfactual access extracts the boundary with up to $200\times$ fewer queries than a published label-only baseline.

</details>


### [160] [MaD-RL: Matching Distributions for Calibrating LLMs with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31644)
*Sourabh Kulkarni,Ksheeraj Sai Vepuri,Basar Demir,Jason Bohrer,Emily Shen,Jianfa Chen,Nan Jiang,Ankit Jain,Harihar Subramanyam,Mannat Singh,Chirag Nagpal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于强化学习的分布匹配（Distribution Matching）框架，通过设计不同散度（KL、JS等）的奖励函数，控制模型输出潜在类别属性的分布，从而解决后训练中输出多样性下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流RL后训练方法（如GRPO）以最大化期望奖励为目标，会导致策略概率集中于单一模式、降低输出多样性。而在合成数据生成、公平性约束满足、策略探索等场景中，需要控制输出分布而非仅最大化奖励。现有的熵正则化和采样温度方法效果有限，仅作用于token空间且只能趋向均匀分布。

Method: 提出通用的RL分布匹配框架，使模型输出的潜在类别属性分布匹配指定目标分布。指出已有工作是分布匹配在L2散度下的特例，并进一步提出KL散度和Jensen-Shannon散度对应的奖励函数，并给出理论依据。

Result: 实证表明GRPO等主流后训练方法会降低输出多样性；所提方法在数学推理和编程任务上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 分布匹配为控制和调节模型输出分布提供了通用且理论支撑的RL框架，分散度设计比传统熵正则化和温度调节更灵活有效。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is widely used in language-model post-training to maximize rewards assigned to individual model outputs, such as scores from binary verifiers or reward models trained on human feedback. However, applications such as synthetic-data generation, fairness-related constraint satisfaction, and policy exploration require controlling the distribution of outputs across model generations rather than only maximizing expected reward. We propose a general RL-based framework for \textit{Distribution Matching} allowing matching the distribution of a latent categorical attribute of model outputs to a specified target distribution. Empirically, we demonstrate that dominant post-training recipes such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) reduce output diversity by concentrating policy probability towards a single mode. Entropy regularization and sampling temperature can improve the spread of the distribution but have constrained effectiveness, limited to apply only in token space and toward uniform distributions. We show that prior work in this area is a specific case of Distribution Matching involving the $L_2$ divergence. We then propose reward functions for other divergences such as KL and Jensen-Shannon and motivate them with theoretical justification. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on a set of experiments involving mathematical reasoning and programming.

</details>


### [161] [STAR: Adaptive Spatial-Temporal Normalization for Unified Microservice Incident Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31645)
*Xinhua Miao,Linyu Zhu,Bowei Yang,Zhengong Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR框架，通过时空自适应归一化机制学习鲁棒的微服务多模态表征，统一支持异常检测、故障定级与根因定位任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模微服务系统的自动化事件管理依赖指标、日志、追踪等多模态可观测数据的学习表征。现有自监督框架虽能统一建模异常检测、故障定级与根因定位，但难以应对非平稳时序动态和异构服务依赖结构带来的挑战。

Method: STAR引入两个紧密耦合的机制：时间自适应归一化（TAN），利用多尺度时间上下文动态归一化多变量时间序列；空间自适应归一化（SAN），在服务依赖图上进行结构感知的归一化。与以往将归一化视为静态或任务无关的方法不同，STAR将其建模为可学习、上下文条件化的变换，与微服务系统的内在特性对齐，并将得到的自适应表征集成到统一自监督框架中，实现端到端无监督的AD、FT、RCL支持。

Result: 在两个真实微服务基准上的大量实验表明，STAR持续优于所有最先进基线，在三个任务上均取得显著且稳定的提升。

Conclusion: 实验结果凸显自适应归一化是复杂软件系统中实现鲁棒多模态表征学习的一种有原则且有效的机制。

Abstract: Automated incident management in large-scale microservice systems relies on learning robust representations from multimodal observability data, including metrics, logs, and traces. Although recent self-supervised frameworks enable unified modeling for anomaly detection (AD), failure triage (FT), and root cause localization (RCL), they often struggle with non-stationary temporal dynamics and heterogeneous service dependency structures. In this paper, we propose STAR, a Spatial-Temporal Adaptive Representation learning framework that explicitly addresses these challenges through adaptive normalizations. STAR introduces two tightly coupled mechanisms: Temporal Adaptive Normalization (TAN), which dynamically normalizes multivariate time series using multi-scale temporal context, and Spatial Adaptive Normalization (SAN), which performs structure-aware normalization over service dependency graphs. Unlike prior methods that treat normalization as static or task-agnostic, STAR formulates it as a learnable, context-conditioned transformation aligned with the intrinsic properties of microservice systems. The resulting adaptive representations are integrated into a unified self-supervised framework, enabling end-to-end unsupervised support for AD, FT, and RCL tasks. Extensive experiments on two real-world microservice benchmarks demonstrate that STAR consistently outperforms all state-of-the-art baselines, yielding significant and stable improvements across all three tasks. Our results highlight adaptive normalization as a principled and effective mechanism for robust multimodal representation learning in complex software systems.

</details>


### [162] [Typed Temporal Interaction Features for Simulation-Backed Forecasting of Open-Source Game Release Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31647)
*Shayma Alkobaisi,Anas Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GAMEQUALGRAPH-Pilot，一个带类型化时序特征的流水线，用于预测开源视频游戏30天内的质量事件，但评估基于模拟数据而非真实游戏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源视频游戏质量取决于代码、资产、配置、测试、贡献者和议题工作流之间的交互关系，而传统缺陷预测器通常将这些关系扁平化或直接忽略。作者希望研究发布级别的质量事件预测，并保留这些类型化交互信息。

Method: 构建GAMEQUALGRAPH-Pilot流水线，包含类型化时序特征、校准风险估计和考虑工作量（effort-aware）的排序。由于可获取的OS-SGameBench材料缺乏人工审核的发布日期和爆发标签，评估采用模拟方式：五个随机种子世界，每个含120个项目、24个发布版本，采用项目不相交验证和未来跨项目测试。另进行消融实验及缺失性、漂移、引擎、项目规模、告警阈值和归因等受控分析。

Result: AUPRC为0.520，AUROC为0.673，Brier分数为0.207，在20%测试预算下考虑工作量的召回率为29.68%。最接近的本地对比方法Static-Hetero-Reimpl的AUPRC为0.522，差值为-0.002，经Holm校正后不具统计显著性。推理延遲为每发布0.023毫秒。

Conclusion: 结果支持所提协议的可复现性，但不支持其部署有效性。在期刊投稿或实际应用前，仍需完成真实OSGameBench发布重建、分层标签审核和官方图模型对比。这一边界有助于保护研究诚信并支持可信评估。

Abstract: Open-source video-game quality depends on inter-actions among code, assets, configuration, tests, contributors, and issue workflows, yet conventional defect predictors usually flatten or omit these relations. We investigate release-level forecasting of a quality incident within thirty days using GAMEQUALGRAPH-Pilot, a typed temporal feature pipeline with calibrated risk estimates and effort-aware ranking. Because the accessible OS-SGameBench materials do not provide manually audited release dates and outbreak labels, the executed evaluation is explicitly simulation-backed rather than an empirical claim about real games. Five seeded worlds each contain 120 projects and 24 releases, with project-disjoint validation and future cross-project testing. The pilot obtains an AUPRC of 0.520, AUROC of 0.673, Brier score of 0.207, and 29.68% effort-aware recall at a twenty-percent testing budget. Its closest local comparator, Static-Hetero-Reimpl, reaches 0.522 AUPRC; the -0.002 difference is not statistically significant after Holm correction. Inference requires 0.023 milliseconds per release in the measured environment. Ablations and controlled missingness, drift, engine, project-size, alert-threshold, and attribution analyses expose where typed interactions help and where they fail. Results support the reproducibility of the proposed protocol, not deployment effectiveness. Real OSSGameBench release reconstruction, stratified label audits, and official graph-model comparisons remain mandatory before journal submission or operational use in practice. This boundary protects research integrity and supports credible evaluation.

</details>


### [163] [Energy Vision--Language--Action: A Controlled Multimodal Benchmark for Intent-Conditioned Residential Energy Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31648)
*Lyes Saad Saoud,Oualid Doukhi,Ehsan Reihani,Saeed Sepasi,Deok Jin Lee,Moussa Ayyash,Reza Ghorbani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EVLA（Energy Vision-Language-Action），一个面向意图条件化住宅能源管理的受控多模态基准，将电池调度建模为多模态轨迹预测问题，并开展初步实验揭示语言通路与视觉通路贡献的不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型主要在机器人领域被研究，即把视觉观测与语言指令映射为物理动作。作者希望将这一范式迁移到住宅能源管理场景，并提供一个可控基准来研究语义意图与潜在上下文如何影响能源动作预测。

Method: EVLA 将电池调度定义为多模态轨迹预测：输入为 RGB 能量场表示、数值运行状态、自然语言目标，输出为 16 步电池动作轨迹（由有限时域采样的参考生成器产生）。数据源自公共住宅电力负荷，价格、荷电状态、室内温度、时刻为生成的基准元数据。隐藏运行工况仅通过能量场纹理编码，实现数值状态固定下的配对视觉变化。439,203 个基础窗口 × 3 种隐藏工况 × 5 种语言目标 = 6,588,045 个多模态实例。初步研究在固定子集（5,000 训练 / 500 验证 / 500 测试）上评估 36 种配置、3 个随机种子。

Result: MobileNet 系列对比中，移除处理后语言使轨迹 MSE 从 0.3856 ± 0.0039 升至 0.8628 ± 0.0001；而移除视觉得到 0.3843 ± 0.0013，与完整模型相当。结果显示出明显的模态使用不对称性：语言通路与预测质量强相关，而当前 RGB 通路在整体误差上未带来优势。

Conclusion: 这些结果刻画的仅限于固定试点子集与所执行的协议，而非全基准训练。EVLA 为研究语义意图与潜在上下文如何影响住宅能源动作预测提供了可控的实验环境。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are studied mainly in robotics, where visual observations and language instructions are mapped to physical actions. This paper introduces Energy Vision-Language-Action (EVLA), a controlled multimodal benchmark for intent-conditioned residential energy management. EVLA frames battery scheduling as a multimodal trajectory-prediction problem in which an RGB energy-field representation, a numerical operating state, and a natural-language objective are mapped to a 16-step battery-action trajectory generated by a finite-horizon sampling-based reference generator. Source windows are derived from public residential electrical-load data, while electricity price, battery state of charge, indoor temperature, and time of day are generated benchmark metadata. A hidden operating regime is encoded only through energy-field texture, enabling paired visual changes while the explicit numerical state is fixed. Crossing 439,203 retained base windows with three hidden regimes and five language objectives yields 6,588,045 multimodal instances. An initial study evaluates 36 configurations over three training seeds using fixed subsets of 5,000 training, 500 validation, and 500 test instances. In the MobileNet-family comparison, removing processed language increases trajectory mean-squared error from 0.3856 +/- 0.0039 to 0.8628 +/- 0.0001, whereas removing vision yields 0.3843 +/- 0.0013, comparable to the full model. The results show strong asymmetry in modality use: the processed-language pathway is strongly associated with prediction quality, while the current RGB pathway provides no aggregate error advantage. These results characterize the fixed pilot subset and executed protocol rather than full-benchmark training. EVLA provides a controlled setting for studying how semantic intent and latent context influence residential energy-action prediction.

</details>


### [164] [From Phase Transition to Systemic Failure: A Decoupled Analytics Framework for GNN Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31656)
*Shuai Yan,Dan Peng,Jie Li,Ke Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建合成同配图回归基准，分离标签噪声与特征分布偏移，通过41种配置、410次运行评估代表性GNN的表现，发现模型对中度标签噪声较宽容，而对严重分布偏移极为敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据质量是GNN在实际图挖掘任务中可靠部署的主要瓶颈，其中标签噪声与特征分布偏移是两类常见但本质不同的问题，缺乏在可控条件下分别研究其影响的基准。

Method: 构建可分别操控标签噪声与特征分布偏移的合成同配图回归基准，设置41种配置、进行410次运行，评估代表性GNN模型在不同噪声与偏移条件下的行为。

Result: 加性标签损坏下性能在较宽噪声范围内相对稳定，约50%噪声比附近出现转折区后急剧下降；极端特征分布偏移下所有模型显著退化，测试MSE增加48至316倍，相关性下降73%至89%。

Conclusion: 在当前可控设定下，GNN对中度标签扰动远比严重分布不匹配更为宽容；研究提供了理解数据质量影响GNN图挖掘系统的受控经验基线，并为面向部署的监控与模型维护提供实践启示。

Abstract: Data quality is a major bottleneck for the reliable deployment of graph neural networks (GNNs) in real-world graph mining tasks. Among various sources of degradation, label noise and feature distribution shift (hereafter referred to as distribution shift) are two common yet fundamentally different challenges. To study their effects under controlled conditions, this paper constructs a synthetic homophilic graph regression benchmark in which the two factors can be manipulated separately. A total of 41 configurations and 410 runs are conducted to evaluate the behavior of representative GNN models under varying noise and shift conditions. The results show two distinct patterns. First, under additive label corruption, performance remains relatively stable over a broad range of noise settings and begins to deteriorate sharply only after an observed transition region around the 50 percent noise ratio. Second, under extreme feature distribution shift, all tested models suffer substantial degradation, with test MSE increasing by 48 times to 316 times and correlation dropping by 73 percent to 89 percent. These findings suggest that, in the present controlled setting, GNNs are considerably more tolerant to moderate label perturbation than to severe distribution mismatch. The study provides a controlled empirical baseline for understanding how data quality affects GNN-based graph mining systems and offers practical implications for deployment-oriented monitoring and model maintenance.

</details>


### [165] [Cross-Material Support Transfer for Core-Loss Prediction Under Waveform Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31659)
*Cong Yao,Chunye Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 磁芯损耗建模存在"表征-部署"波形失配，材料D的梯形波样本稀缺。作者证明问题本质是协变量偏移下信息缺失而非类别不平衡，提出MIST方法：通过FiLM引入材料身份、重加权稀缺材料损失、不对材料D做微调，联合五个材料训练一个2784参数预测器，将材料D p95从20.39%降至12.38%，梯形波类p95从37.4%降至15.16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功率磁材料通常在正弦波和三角波上表征，但实际变换器使磁芯暴露于梯形波、PWM型磁通轨迹，损失模型必须预测训练数据最稀薄之处。MagNet Challenge最终测试中，材料D的梯形波占测试集16.4%却仅占训练集1.4%，最佳提交p95停滞在15.9%，为五种材料中最差。作者想厘清这一障碍的根源并寻找解决方案。

Method: 1) 受控实验反驳类别不平衡解读：四种标准补救措施均失败，将梯形波比例提升至测试集水平反而使精度下降。2) 提出MIST（材料身份支持迁移）：在全部五种材料上联合训练一个2784参数预测器；通过FiLM（特征级线性调制）引入材料身份；对稀缺材料的真实标签损失进行重加权；材料D不做微调，避免其无梯形波训练集的偏差被重新引入。

Result: MIST将材料D的五种子p95从20.39±2.03%降至12.38±0.92%，梯形波类p95从37.4±8.8%降至15.16±1.69%，以六分之一的参数量且无微调阶段超越最佳提交；在匹配容量下移除材料身份会使误差膨胀一个数量级。

Conclusion: 障碍源于协变量偏移下的信息缺失，缺失的支持可以从同类材料借取而非外推；稀缺材料应与其同类材料联合表征。

Abstract: Power magnetic materials are characterized on the sinusoidal and triangular waveforms that excitation hardware conveniently produces, whereas deployed converters expose cores to trapezoidal, PWM-shaped flux trajectories, so loss models must predict exactly where their training data are thinnest. The final test of the MagNet Challenge embeds a deliberately extreme instance of this characterization-deployment mismatch: for material D, trapezoids form 16.4% of the test set but only 1.4% of the training set. The 95th-percentile relative error, hereafter p95, of the best submission, built on sequential transfer learning, stalled at 15.9%, the worst among the five materials. This paper shows that the obstacle is missing information under covariate shift rather than class imbalance, and that the missing support can be borrowed from sibling materials instead of being extrapolated. Controlled experiments first refute the imbalance reading: four standard remedies fail, and raising the trapezoidal share to the test-set level degrades accuracy further. The proposed material-identity support transfer, MIST, then trains one 2784-parameter predictor jointly on all five challenge materials. Material identity enters through feature-wise linear modulation, or FiLM, the scarce material's true-label loss is reweighted, and material D receives no fine-tuning, so that the bias of its trapezoid-free training set is never re-installed. MIST lowers the five-seed material-D p95 from 20.39+/-2.03% to 12.38+/-0.92% and the trapezoidal-class p95 from 37.4+/-8.8% to 15.16+/-1.69%, surpassing the best submission with one-sixth of its parameters and no fine-tuning stage; removing material identity at matched capacity inflates the error by an order of magnitude. These results argue that scarce materials should be characterized jointly with their siblings.

</details>


### [166] [Beyond the Graph: An Adaptive Meta-Learner Fuses Explainability, Weather, and Dynamics for Robust Bus ETA Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31667)
*Pratham Payra,Jagadish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HYB(nm)自适应混合集成框架，动态融合五种互补模型来预测公交ETA，在加尔各答三条线路（4000+趟次）数据上实现高鲁棒性和领先精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的公交ETA预测对城市出行、乘客满意度和公交效率至关重要，但现有模型难以应对非线性时空动态、数据稀疏以及天气等因素的影响。

Method: 提出HYB(nm)自适应混合集成框架，通过元学习器根据实时情境动态融合五种互补模型：历史基线MST-AV、周期性时间模式分析GDRN-DFT、非线性动力学Koopman神经算子KOOP-NET、天气融合特征工程神经网络FENN，以及实时图卷积网络MGCN。架构可扩展为HYB(k)。

Result: 在加尔各答三条公交线路、超过4000趟次的GPS与天气数据上评估：HYB(2)鲁棒性优越，精度可与领先图神经网络媲美，并在不同预测时间跨度和运行条件下实现稳定性与效率的平衡。

Conclusion: HYB(k)可扩展架构为公交机构提供从经济单模型到定制高保真混合模型的灵活工具，推动可预测且公平的城市交通发展。

Abstract: Accurate bus Estimated Time of Arrival (ETA) prediction is vital for urban mobility, passenger satisfaction, and transit efficiency, yet existing models falter against nonlinear spatiotemporal dynamics, data sparsity, and factors such as weather. This paper proposes HYB(nm), an adaptive hybrid ensemble framework that dynamically fuses five complementary models - a historical baseline (MST-AV), periodical temporal pattern analysis (GDRN-DFT), Koopman Neural Operators for nonlinear dynamics (KOOP-NET), weather-integrated feature-engineered neural networks (FENN), and real-time graph convolutional networks (MGCN) - via a meta-learner attuned to real-time context. Evaluated on GPS and weather data from three Kolkata bus routes comprising more than 4,000 trips, the framework leverages the individual strengths of its components (for example, the low-latency explainability of MST-AV, the weather resilience of FENN, and the network-dynamics capture of MGCN) to deliver the superior robustness of HYB(2), state-of-the-art accuracy rivalling leading graph neural networks, and balanced trade-offs in stability and efficiency across prediction horizons and operating conditions. The extensible HYB(k) architecture equips transit agencies with flexible tools, ranging from economical single models to tailored high-fidelity hybrids, advancing predictive, equitable urban transport.

</details>


### [167] [NanoForecast v0.5: Competitive Time Series Forecasting Through Training Pipeline Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31669)
*Gautam Kishore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NanoForecast v0.5 是一个 6.5M 参数的时间序列预测模型，在不改变架构的前提下通过修正训练流程，性能追平 31 倍规模的 TimesFM（200M）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索小型模型能否通过修正训练流程而非扩大架构规模，达到与大型模型相当的预测性能。

Method: 保持 v0.3 架构不变，修正 loss 作用域处理、张量形状对齐，并扩大数据增强覆盖范围后重新训练。

Result: 在固定协议下 MASE 从 3.030 降至 1.704（降低 43.8%）。在全部三个 ETT 数据集（0.676/1.110/0.287 vs 0.705/1.360/0.545）和汇率数据集（4.317 vs 4.383）上均优于 TimesFM；但在高基数电力与交通数据集上 TimesFM 保持明显领先。对 PatchTST（15M+ 参数）在三个 ETT 集合上全胜。

Conclusion: 训练约 12 小时（单张 T4 GPU），推理无需 GPU（基于 Apple M4 CPU）。代码、预训练权重与评估框架以 Apache 2.0 开源。

Abstract: We present NanoForecast v0.5, a 6.5M-parameter forecaster that competes with models 31x its size (TimesFM, 200M parameters) after training pipeline fixes and no architecture change. Retraining the v0.3 architecture with corrected loss-scope handling, tensor shape alignment, and wider augmentation coverage cuts overall Mean Absolute Scaled Error by 43.8% under one fixed protocol (MASE 3.030 to 1.704) on the same data and compute budget. NanoForecast v0.5 beats TimesFM on all three ETT datasets (MASE 0.676/1.110/0.287 vs. 0.705/1.360/0.545) and on exchange rate (4.317 vs. 4.383); TimesFM keeps a clear lead on the high-cardinality electricity and traffic sets. Against PatchTST (15M+ parameters, official configuration), v0.5 wins all three ETT sets. Training takes about 12 hours on a single cloud GPU (NVIDIA T4, Google Colab) and inference needs no GPU (measurements in this paper are on an Apple M4 CPU). We release all code, pretrained checkpoints, and evaluation framework under Apache 2.0 at https://github.com/eulogik/NanoForecast

</details>


### [168] [Active Causal Discovery Benchmark: Evaluating LLM Agents Under Budgeted Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31675)
*Sagar Deb,Devam Shah,Ashwanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ACDB基准，用于评测LLM智能体在预算受限干预下恢复因果图结构的能力，当前结果显示LLM尚未解决该任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缺乏一个基于结构因果模型（SCM）、可分离地评估骨架恢复、DAG恢复与干预效率的标准化环境，以判断LLM智能体能否在观测与预算受限硬干预下真正恢复因果结构。

Method: ACDB将线性高斯世界生成器与固定的"观察-干预-提交"API配对，并设计三层评分契约，分别衡量骨架恢复、DAG恢复和干预效率；在六级难度阶梯上进行对比实验，包括PC+贪心主动定向启发式、Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5.4以及结构盲随机DAG基线和密度探测。

Result: PC+贪心主动定向启发式是最强非预言机方法（有向F1 42.7%，SHD 4.79），优于Claude Sonnet 4.6（31.7%，7.25）与GPT-5.4（22.9%，9.27）。精确率-召回率分解显示：PC高精确率但欠承诺，LLM过度承诺且精确率较低，统计工具接入往往增加弃权而非有效干预。结构盲随机DAG基线达23.6%有向F1，密度探测将其降至16.9%。

Conclusion: 当前结果应被视为基准审计与校准报告，而非LLM已解决主动因果发现的证据；密度探测结果推动v1校准。

Abstract: We introduce the Active Causal Discovery Benchmark (ACDB), an SCM-grounded environment for evaluating whether LLM agents recover causal graph structure from observations and budget-constrained hard interventions. ACDB pairs a linear-Gaussian world generator with a fixed observe-intervene-submit API and a three-layer scoring contract that separates skeleton recovery, DAG recovery, and intervention efficiency. On the current six-level ladder, PC with a greedy active orientation heuristic is the strongest non-oracle method (directed F1 42.7%, SHD 4.79), ahead of Claude Sonnet 4.6 raw active (31.7%, 7.25) and GPT-5.4 raw active (22.9%, 9.27). The most informative diagnostic is the precision-recall decomposition: PC under-commits with high precision, LLMs over-commit with lower precision, and statistical-tool access often increases abstention rather than useful intervention. A structure-blind random DAG baseline reaches 23.6% directed F1 on this dense v0 ladder; a density probe lowers this floor to 16.9%, motivating the v1 calibration pass. The current results should therefore be read as a benchmark audit and calibration report, not as evidence that current LLMs solve active causal discovery.

</details>


### [169] [When Keywords Drop but Classifiers Hold: Soft Refusals under KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31678)
*Kang Chen,Xiuze Zhou,Hong Chen,Yuanguo Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究KV cache压缩（保留任务精度）是否会破坏关键词式拒答监控与更强拒答分类器之间的一致性。在200条有害提示+长填充上下文的配对协议下，Qwen2.5-3B上关键词拒答率从98.0%降至80.5%，而分类器拒答率保持99.0%-99.5%，MMLU精度不变，人类标注更贴近分类器，说明关键词率会误判为"安全性下降"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV cache压缩已广泛用于内存受限的长上下文LLM推理，而部署系统通常在生成后用关键词过滤或学习分类器来判定拒答。这类监控器本应反映模型在真实服务条件下是否拒绝了有害请求，但目前尚不清楚：在保持任务精度的匹配压缩条件下，轻量级词法监控器与更强拒答分类器之间的一致性是否仍能维持。因此需要检验压缩是否会让"安全审计信号"失真。

Method: 采用配对协议：n=200条有害提示，均配以长填充上下文。每条提示在共享prefill后分别以完整保留（full retention）和匹配淘汰（matched eviction）两种方式各生成一次回答。同一批回复由关键词启发式、HarmBench Llama-2-13B分类器、辅助LLM评判器打分，并对分歧样本进行人工标注。测试模型为Qwen2.5-3B，并对比短填充与配对SnapKV的条件。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-3B上，关键词拒答率从98.0%降至80.5%（McNemar p~1e-8），而分类器拒答率几乎保持上限（99.0%-99.5%），MMLU精度无变化（50.0%）；人工标签主要跟随分类器，符合"软拒答"（soft refusals）的解释。该差距并非普遍存在：在短填充和配对SnapKV下差距减弱。

Conclusion: KV cache压缩下关键词监控与分类器监控会出现系统性分歧，关键词率下降并不等于安全性真正下降，而是软拒答导致的监控假象。因此压缩场景下的安全审计不应仅依赖关键词拒答率，而应采用多个与具体服务上下文相匹配的评判器进行综合评估。

Abstract: KV cache compression is widely used for long context LLM inference under memory constraints, while deployed systems typically score refusals after generation with keyword filters or learned classifiers. Such monitors are intended to indicate whether a model declined a harmful request under the serving regime actually used. However, it remains unclear whether matched compression that preserves task accuracy also preserves agreement between lightweight lexical monitors and stronger refusal classifiers. We study this with a paired protocol on n=200 harmful prompts with a long filler context: each prompt is answered once under full retention and once under matched eviction after a shared prefill, and the same replies are scored by keyword heuristics, the HarmBench Llama-2-13B classifier, an auxiliary LLM judge, and humans on disagreements. On Qwen2.5-3B, keyword refusal falls from 98.0% to 80.5% (McNemar p~1e-8) while classifier refusal stays near ceiling (99.0%-99.5%) and MMLU accuracy is unchanged (50.0%); human labels predominantly follow the classifier, consistent with soft refusals. The gap is not universal and weakens under short fillers and paired SnapKV, so safety auditing under compression should rely on several judges matched to the serving context rather than on keyword rates alone.

</details>


### [170] [Does Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture Pretraining Help Time Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31680)
*Yutong Feng,Bowen Liao,See Kiong Ng,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对一种JEPA实例进行了跨9种骨干网络、11个基准（含时序与时空预测）的大规模评估，发现其预训练收益随骨干网络差异显著，既有稳定提升也有稳定退化，且该现象跨任务族普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA作为时间序列自监督预训练范式虽具前景，但现有证据对其收益评价不一，且多数研究仅测试单一或少量架构，无法判断JEPA预训练是否可靠，还是高度依赖下游模型。

Method: 对某一种JEPA实例开展大规模评估，覆盖9种骨干网络与11个基准任务，涵盖时序预测与时空预测两个任务族，进行跨架构的系统性对比。

Result: 该JEPA实例的收益在不同骨干网络间差异显著：部分架构获得一致的性能提升，另一些则出现一致的性能退化，即使在同一数据集上也是如此；该模式在两个任务族中均成立。

Conclusion: JEPA预训练收益的可变性是该实例的普遍性质，而非数据集特有的偶然现象，实践中选择骨干网络时需将这一因素纳入考量。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPA) have emerged as a promising self-supervised pretraining paradigm for time series, learning representations by predicting target embeddings in latent space rather than reconstructing raw signals. Yet evidence on their benefits remains mixed, and most studies test only a single backbone or a narrow set of architectures, leaving unclear whether JEPA pretraining is a reliable improvement or one that depends heavily on the downstream model. We address this gap through a large scale evaluation of one JEPA instantiation across nine backbones and eleven benchmarks spanning temporal and spatio-temporal forecasting, the most extensive cross architecture assessment of JEPA for time series to date. We find that the benefit of this instantiation varies sharply across backbones, producing consistent gains for some architectures and consistent degradation for others, even on the same dataset. This pattern holds across both task families, indicating the variability is a general property of this instantiation rather than a dataset specific artifact worth accounting for when choosing a backbone in practice.

</details>


### [171] [Autonomous Research Project Management as an Agent Skill: A Case Study in Exact Spectral Spatial Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31683)
*Alexander Chen,Jeffrey Meng,Bram Hoex,Tong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作展示了一个由智能体技能在消费级硬件上完成的端到端自主机器学习研究演示，用基于FFT的核岭回归求解器处理NOAA Kaplan SST v2月度异常场，并反思自主研究的治理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证智能体能否在消费级硬件上自主完成完整的长周期机器学习研究流程，并探讨自主研究的可信度与治理方式。

Method: 由DeepSeek V4 Flash在DeepSeek Harness（DSH）中通过智能体技能套件编排执行；将长周期状态解耦为基于文件的任务（epic/issue）追踪基底，共74个子智能体会话；以FFT核岭回归（KRR）求解规则空间网格，数据为2005年NOAA Kaplan SST v2月度异常场，网格36×72；在仅CPU硬件（Apple M2 Pro）上运行。

Result: 求解耗时78.7秒，峰值内存1.57 GB；智能体展现出闭环科研韧性：将两个失败假设评审门退回文献检索、修复bootstrap索引错误，全程仅需4次人工介入。

Conclusion: 科学可信度需要可审查的状态、可证伪的评审门以及对负面结果的透明报告；呼吁机器学习社区采用智能体可访问的结构化格式而非静态PDF文稿。

Abstract: This work presents an end-to-end demonstration of autonomous machine learning research conducted by an agent skill on consumer hardware. The demonstration evaluates an FFT-based Kernel Ridge Regression (KRR) solver for regular spatial grids using 2005 monthly NOAA Kaplan SST v2 anomaly fields on a $36 \times 72$ grid. This was autonomously executed by DeepSeek V4 Flash, orchestrated by our agent skill suite within DeepSeek Harness (DSH). Experiments were executed on CPU-only hardware (Apple M2 Pro; 78.7 s solver time, 1.57 GB peak RSS). Long-horizon state was decoupled into a file-based epic- and issue-tracking substrate. Across 74 sub-agent sessions, the agent demonstrated closed-loop scientific resilience: routing two failed hypothesis review gates back to literature retrieval, patching bootstrap indexing bugs, and executing with only four discrete human steering events. Finally, we reflect on autonomous research governance, arguing that scientific credibility requires inspectable state, falsifiable review gates, and transparent reporting of negative results, urging the machine learning community to favour agent-accessible structured formats over static PDF manuscripts.

</details>


### [172] [What does FFN compression change downstream? Same-state causal restoration in diffusion language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31685)
*Shaurya Omar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Same-State Causal Restoration（SSR），通过在压缩模型到达的相同当前输入上恢复原始FFN并测量轨迹变化，首次直接测量DLM压缩中移除FFN计算的同输入闭环效应，发现压缩侧状态比局部NMSE更能预测下游影响，仅恢复4个transitions即可挽回89.9%损失精度并保留约36.8%的MAC节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）支持灵活并行生成，但迭代去噪计算代价高昂，促使压缩越来越激进。现有压缩目标主要衡量压缩计算在局部上逼近原始计算的程度，然而局部误差无法揭示哪些被移除的计算对下游去噪轨迹真正重要。因此需要一种能够直接测量被移除FFN计算对下游影响的因果方法。

Method: 提出Same-State Causal Restoration（SSR）：在压缩模型实际到达的精确当前输入上恢复原始FFN，并测量由此引起的轨迹变化，从而直接测量同当前输入下的闭环效应。方法在LLaDA-8B-Instruct和Dream-v0-Instruct-7B上验证，采用无需任务标签的校准，冻结单一恢复窗口用于留出推理，并与等预算的局部误差基线对比。

Result: 在固定去噪阶段，压缩侧状态对下游效应的排序显著优于局部NMSE；受控干预显示修正结构比幅度更重要。在激进的LLaDA压缩下，仅恢复四个transitions即可挽回89.9%的损失精度，同时保留约36.8%的整模型MAC节省，并优于等预算的局部误差基线。Dream实验进一步表明，恢复稠密行为与修复最终任务是两种不同结果。

Conclusion: SSR首次实现了DLM压缩中被移除FFN计算在同当前输入下闭环效应的直接测量，证明压缩侧状态在预测下游影响方面优于局部误差指标，并能在低恢复预算下高效挽回精度，同时揭示恢复稠密行为与修复最终任务并不等价。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) enable flexible, parallel generation, but their iterative denoising remains computationally expensive, motivating increasingly aggressive compression. Existing compression objectives largely measure how well compressed computation approximates the original locally, but local error does not reveal which removed computations actually matter to the downstream denoising trajectory. We introduce Same-State Causal Restoration (SSR), which restores the original FFN on the exact current input reached by the compressed model and measures how the resulting trajectory changes. To our knowledge, this is the first direct measurement of the same-current-input closed-loop effect of removed FFN computation in DLM compression. Across LLaDA-8B-Instruct and Dream-v0-Instruct-7B, compressed-side state ranks this downstream effect substantially better than local NMSE at fixed denoising phase, while controlled interventions show that correction structure matters beyond magnitude. Using task-label-free calibration, SSR freezes a single restoration window for held-out inference. Under aggressive LLaDA compression, restoring only four transitions recovers 89.9% of the lost accuracy while retaining an estimated 36.8% whole-model MAC saving and outperforming an equal-budget local-error baseline. Dream further shows that restoring dense behavior and repairing the final task are distinct outcomes.

</details>


### [173] [3-D Emissions Mapping and Social Cost Estimation for US Domestic Aviation at West Coast Hubs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31686)
*Hesam Shafiei Nia,Don MacKenzie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过物理信息自编码器重建美国西海岸枢纽航班轨迹，构建三维航空排放地图，评估区域环境与机场周边健康影响及社会成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有航空排放清单缺乏准确的轨迹数据，难以支持高分辨率的社会成本与健康影响评估，因此需要重建航班轨迹以生成精细的排放地图。

Method: 采用物理信息自编码器（AE）处理2025年1月美国西海岸枢纽的ADS-B轨迹记录；编码器结合CNN、Bi-GRU和带跳跃连接的3D CNN，解码器为TCN，并与基线AE及三次样条插值进行对比。排放通过EUROCONTROL BADA性能表与ICAO EEDB排放指数映射，并用BFFM2进行高度修正。

Result: 所提AE模型在5%至50%缺失率下均优于TCN-AE和三次样条插值。货币化结果显示，NOx在直接气候强迫中产生轻微净冷却效应，但占货币化空气质量与健康成本的99.7%，尽管其质量占比不足CO2的0.4%，是健康成本的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 本研究首次将基于AE的轨迹重建、时空特征分离编码与基于高度的排放建模结合，生成用于空气质量、气候影响和人群暴露评估的区域航空排放清单，为美国国内航空的环境影响评估和机场周边健康政策提供量化依据。

Abstract: Existing aviation emissions inventories lack accurate trajectory data for high-resolution social cost and health impact assessment. This paper develops a 3-D emissions map by reconstructing flight trajectories for US west coast hubs to estimate regional environmental and near-airport health impacts. A physics-informed autoencoder (AE) is applied to ADS-B trajectory records for January 2025 covering US west coast hubs. The encoder combines a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN), a Bi-GRU, and a 3-D CNN with skip connection; the decoder is a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN). It is benchmarked against a baseline-AE and cubic spline interpolation. Emissions are mapped via EUROCONTROL Base of Aircraft Data (BADA) performance tables and ICAO Engine Emissions Databank (EEDB) emission indices, with altitude corrections via Boeing Fuel Flow Method 2 (BFFM2). Social costs are quantified for all flight phases, with health impacts assessed for Landing and Takeoff cycles within 50 km of each hub. The proposed AE model outperforms both a TCN-AE and cubic spline interpolation across 5% to 50% missing rates. Monetizing the emissions inventory shows NOx produces a small net cooling effect in direct climate forcing, while accounting for 99.7% of monetized air-quality and health cost despite being under 0.4% of CO2 by mass, making it the dominant health-cost driver. To our knowledge, this is among the first studies combining AE-based trajectory reconstruction with separate spatial-temporal feature encoding and altitude-based emissions modeling to produce a regional aviation emissions inventory for air quality, climate impact and population exposure. The resulting emissions map and social cost estimates provide quantitative context for environmental impact assessment and near-airport health policy evaluation for US domestic aviation.

</details>


### [174] [Same Probe, Different Numbers: Are Activation Probes Robust to Inference-Time Numerical Non-Determinism?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31796)
*Alizishaan Khatri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测量了推理配置（批量大小与数值精度）变化对激活探针的影响，发现探针本身稳定，但聚合准确率会低估判定翻转率2至9倍，建议用逐样本一致率评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活探针（activation probes）越来越多用于部署中的大模型监控，但探针通常在某一推理配置下训练，却在实际服务栈的任意批量大小与数值精度下使用。由于GPU核心并非批量不变、浮点格式舍入方式不同，部署时的激活值并不等于训练时的激活值，因此需要量化这种配置失配对探针可靠性的真实代价。

Method: 在Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen3-8B与Gemma-3-4B上，覆盖批量大小4、8、16与float32、bfloat16、float16配置，训练768个探针，每对配置之间做迁移评估，共1,392次迁移，并逐样本比较判定结果；同时测量激活的相对L2扰动幅度。

Result: 在prompt场景下准确率跨迁移波动不超过0.47个百分点，仅0.076%判定翻转，float32仅变批量时201,960个判定无一改变。解码阶段翻转率升至2.8%，但生成token一致的样本仅0.12-0.15%翻转，token不一致的样本翻转率达12.9%，说明主因是文本而非算术。bfloat16下改变批量会使2.1%样本的首个生成token翻转，第20个token时25%样本落在不同token上。翻转是对称的，Cohen's kappa高于0.94，AUROC变化不超过0.05。激活扰动幅度与格式舍入相当，bfloat16批量变化带来的相对L2中位数为1e-2，约为float16的8倍。
