Conclusion: 探针能吸收这些扰动，而模型自身的下一token argmax却不能。激活监控的鲁棒性评估应报告逐样本一致率而非聚合准确率，区分表征噪声与输入变化，并明确说明服务配置。

Abstract: Activation probes are increasingly used to monitor LLMs in deployment. A probe is typically trained under one inference configuration, then used under whatever batch size and numerical precision the serving stack uses. Because common GPU kernels are not batch-invariant and floating-point formats round differently, the activations seen at deployment are not the ones the probe was trained on. We measure what that costs for Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen3-8B and Gemma-3-4B across batch sizes 4, 8 and 16 and float32, bfloat16 and float16, training 768 probes on one configuration, evaluating each on every other, and comparing verdicts example by example. Probes are stable, but aggregate accuracy is the wrong instrument for showing it: it understates how many verdicts change by a factor of two to nine. At the prompt, accuracy never moves by more than 0.47 percentage points across 1,392 transfers and only 0.076% of verdicts change; under float32 with only the batch size varied, none of 201,960 verdicts change. During decoding the flip rate rises to 2.8%, but rows whose realised tokens matched flip in only 0.12-0.15% of cases, while rows whose tokens diverged flip in 12.9%: the cause is the text, not the arithmetic. A bfloat16 batch-size change flips the first generated token for 2.1% of rows and leaves 25% on different tokens by token 20. Flips are symmetric, Cohen's kappa stays above 0.94, and AUROC moves by at most 0.05 points. Underneath, activations move about as much as the format's rounding: a bfloat16 batch-size change perturbs them by a median relative L2 of 1e-2, roughly 8x the float16 figure. Probes absorb this; the model's own next-token argmax does not. Robustness evaluations of activation monitors should report per-example agreement rather than aggregate accuracy, separate representational noise from input change, and state the serving configuration.

</details>


### [175] [Handwritten Digit Leakage from Smartphone Motion Sensors Across Unseen Users and Phone Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32117)
*Constantino Álvarez Casado,Erkka Rantahalvari,Matteo Pedone,Matti Matilainen,Manuel Lage Cañellas,Le Nguyen,Simo Hosio,Olli Silvén,Miguel Bordallo López*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在假设手写区间已知的前提下，利用智能手机运动传感器信号可跨用户、跨机型预测手写数字，但存在采集顺序捷径，隐私风险结论受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机运动传感器在支持交互应用的同时，其读数可能泄露超出设计用途的触摸屏输入信息，因此需研究手写数字是否能被跨用户、跨设备预测。

Method: 基于481名参与者、19,628条HuMIdb记录的10类分类任务，在已知绘制区间假设下，比较手工特征+经典机器学习、MiniRocket核方法，以及紧凑传感器patch transformer；使用加速度计、线性加速度、陀螺仪和重力信号进行建模。

Result: Transformer在75名未见参与者上达到57.74%准确率和82.64% top-3准确率；在9款未见手机上、3个随机种子下，未见参与者准确率为58.77±0.95%。低运动记录仍具信息量，准确率不随运动强度单调变化；对比预训练、数据增强和派生信号未带来一致增益。

Conclusion: 在假定分割条件下，数字可跨熟悉用户和手机型号被预测；但采集顺序捷径限制了对实际隐私暴露风险的推断。代码已开源。

Abstract: Smartphone motion sensors support interactive applications, but their readings may also reveal touchscreen input beyond their intended use. Assuming known drawing intervals, we study whether handwritten digits remain predictable across users and devices, as a 10-class problem on 19,628 HuMIdb recordings from 481 participants. We compare handcrafted features with classical machine learning algorithms, MiniRocket kernels, and a compact sensor patch transformer on accelerometer, linear acceleration, gyroscope, and gravity signals. The transformer achieves 57.74\% accuracy and 82.64\% top-3 accuracy on 75 unseen participants, and 58.77$\pm$0.95\% over 3 seeds for unseen participants on 9 unseen phone models. Low motion recordings remain informative, accuracy is not monotonic in motion level, and the tested contrastive pretraining, augmentation, and derived signals give no consistent gains. Digits are thus predictable beyond familiar users and phone models under assumed segmentation, while acquisition-order shortcuts limit conclusions about practical privacy exposure. Code available at: https://github.com/Arritmic/motion-digit-leakage.

</details>


### [176] [seq2cause: One Autoregressive Backbone, Four Causal Discovery Tasks in Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31801)
*Hugo Math*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Seq2Cause统一框架，借助预训练自回归模型作为摊销条件独立性检验引擎，在单一冻结骨干下同时解决事件因果推断的四种结构不同情境，并建立预测-因果对偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统（车辆、患者、基因组）产生离散事件序列，其核心问题是因果性的：哪些事件导致哪些事件，哪些导致故障或疾病等高层结果。该问题沿两个轴分解——依赖类型（事件→事件 vs 事件→结果）与因果范围（单序列 vs 群体），形成四种结构不同、可识别条件各异的情境。现有方法均假设多流结构且词汇量低，最多只覆盖其中一种情境，且无法扩展到数百以上事件类型。

Method: Seq2Cause通过一个共享原语统一四种情境：将预训练自回归模型改造为摊销条件独立性检验引擎，无需任务特定再训练。作者建立预测-因果对偶性，使模型的超额交叉熵同时约束四种情境下的因果识别误差。

Result: 在非线性SCM（词汇量达8,000种类型）和真实车辆诊断日志（29K事件类型、474种故障结果）上，Seq2Cause是首个用单一冻结骨干在大规模下填充全部四种情境的方法。现有方法在此设定下或不适用、或不准确、或计算上不可行。

Conclusion: Seq2Cause以单一预训练自回归模型为共享原语，统一处理四种因果推断情境，并通过预测-因果对偶性让下一词预测的提升免费收紧因果保证，在真实大规模事件序列上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Complex systems such as vehicles, patients, or genomes emit discrete event sequences whose operative question is causal, not predictive: which events cause which other events, and which cause higher-level outcomes such as failures or diseases? This question decomposes along two axes -- dependency type (event $\to$ event vs.\ event $\to$ outcome) and causal scope (single sequence vs.\ population) -- yielding four structurally distinct regimes with different identifiability conditions. No existing method addresses more than one, because all assume multi-stream structure with low vocabulary, and none scales beyond a few hundred event types.
  We present \textsc{Seq2Cause}, a unified framework that resolves all four regimes through a single shared primitive: a pretrained autoregressive model repurposed as an amortized conditional independence testing engine requiring no task-specific retraining. We establish a prediction--causality duality: the model's excess cross-entropy simultaneously bounds causal identification error across all four regimes, so that every improvement in next-token prediction tightens causal guarantees for free.
  On nonlinear SCMs (vocabularies up to $8{,}000$ types) and real-world vehicle diagnostic logs ($29$K event types, $474$ failure outcomes), \textsc{Seq2Cause} is the first method to populate all four regimes at scale with a single frozen backbone. Existing methods are either inapplicable, inaccurate, or computationally intractable in this setting.

</details>


### [177] [Medium-Term Multi-Resolution Electric Load Forecasting using Economic Data and Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31806)
*Lindas Eloi,Goude Yannig,Ciais Philippe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨社会经济数据对中期电力负荷预测（1-48个月，月/日分辨率）的影响，使用表格基础模型（FM）和特征选择流程，发现精选经济协变量可将预测技能提升20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中期负荷预测（MTLF）介于长期（依赖经济预测）与短期（依赖天气、日历、自回归）之间，需要兼具外推能力和波动性建模能力。但经济指标能否帮助MTLF、在何种预测期限和分辨率下有帮助仍不清楚，因此需要研究社会经济数据对中期预测的影响。

Method: 构建覆盖20年观测的数据集，包含电力负荷、天气变量以及经济特征（消费价格、生产指数、电动汽车数量、就业等）。采用表格基础模型（Foundation Model），并使用新的特征选择流程，构建具有不同特征子集的专家模型集成，以避免噪声数据。通过特征重要性和上下文重要性分析模型的可解释性。

Result: 精选经济协变量在2015-2025年间使预测技能提升20%，该提升在各类预测期限和分辨率下保持稳定，月分辨率MAPE限制在4%，日分辨率限制在5%。FM在利用的上下文方面存在局限，暗示可通过缩减上下文节省计算成本；经济预测变量的特征重要性随预测期限延长而增加。

Conclusion: 在MTLF中纳入经济数据可弥合与长期负荷预测（LTLF）之间的差距，实现无缝预测。

Abstract: Accurate medium-term, from a few months to a few years, electricity load forecasts are crucial for informed decision-making in power plant maintenance scheduling, load dispatch and price settlement. Being comprised between Long-Term Load Forecasting (LTLF) which uses mostly economic projections and appliances development scenarios, and Short-Term Load Forecasting (STLF) driven by weather, calendar and autoregressive patterns, Medium-Term Load Forecasting (MTLF) requires both extrapolation capabilities and variability modeling. Yet, it remains unclear if MTLF can benefit from economic indicators, and especially at which forecast horizon and resolution. To address these challenges we investigated the impact of socioeconomic data on predictions issued 1 month and up to 48 months in advance for France at monthly and daily resolution using a tabular Foundation Model (FM). A dataset covering 20 years of observations of electricity load, weather variables and economic features such as consumer price and production indices, electric vehicle counts or employment is created for the study. To avoid noisy data, we used a new feature selection pipeline, creating ensemble of expert models with diverse feature subsets, to demonstrate that selected economic covariates improve forecast skill by 20% over 2015-2025. This enhancement is steady across lead times and resolutions limiting the Mean Absolute Percentage Error to 4% for monthly granularity and 5% for daily granularity. Explainability of the models is investigated through feature and context importance. Results showed that the FM is limited in the context it leverages pointing towards potential computational savings with a reduced context, while feature importance of economic predictors grows with the forecast horizon. This suggests that including economic data in MTLF could bridge the gap with LTLF leading to seamless forecasts.

</details>


### [178] [Relational Compression: A Framework for Relational Fidelity in Constrained Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31816)
*Yaniv Shulman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"关系压缩"的统一框架，把保真内容从元素本身转移到元素间的关系结构上，并以有限码字碰撞作为一种具体实现，建立了其与谱稀疏化、图摘要、相似性保持表示和关系蒸馏等方法的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当有价值的信息存在于元素之间的关系而非元素本身时，传统的压缩表示应当保留什么？现有图摘要、谱稀疏化、相似性保持表示与关系蒸馏等方法各自独立发展，缺乏统一的形式化语言来描述它们在重构假设与资源约束上的差异。

Method: 作者在经典"信源—描述—重构"意义下形式化关系压缩，但以关系结构本身作为承载保真的内容。每个实例需指定：信源关系、保留的描述、重构或评估的关系、保真准则以及受约束的资源。在此接口下统一放置并区分已有方法，同时提出有限码字碰撞作为具体实现，用同码字概率导出关系几何。

Result: 同码字概率导出的关系几何将成对的对齐与分离同聚合的 Rényi-2 占据度以及类别分配的球面几何联系起来，并与平方欧氏质心重构、归一化图关联与割具有精确的目标对应。图与图像实验展示了有限码字族内互补的两条路径：图与教师定义的关系要求直接作用于相等或碰撞，而重构则通过联合解码器发挥作用。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，不同的关系要求可以在一个统一的受约束表示框架内被形式化并加以检验，为关系型压缩提供了共同的理论基础与可操作的实现路径。

Abstract: What should a compressed representation preserve when the information of interest lies in relationships among elements rather than in the elements themselves? We formulate relational compression in the classical source-description-reconstruction sense, but with relational structure itself as the fidelity-bearing content. Each instance specifies the source relation, retained description, reconstructed or evaluated relation, fidelity criterion, and constrained resource. We use this interface to situate selected methods from graph summarization, spectral sparsification, similarity-preserving representation, and relational distillation within a common formulation while keeping their different reconstruction and resource assumptions explicit. We develop finite-codeword collision as one concrete realization. Same-codeword probability yields a relational geometry linking pair-specific alignment and separation to aggregate Rényi-2 occupancy and the spherical geometry of categorical assignments, with exact objective correspondences to squared-Euclidean centroid reconstruction and normalized graph association and cut. Graph and image studies illustrate complementary routes within the finite-codeword family: graph- and teacher-defined relational requirements act directly on equality or collision, while reconstruction acts through a joint decoder. Together, these results illustrate how distinct relational requirements can be formulated and tested within a common constrained-representation framework.

</details>


### [179] [Averaged Mirror Descent and Dual Gradient Methods: Convergent Algorithms for Entropic Gromov-Wasserstein Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31848)
*Joanna Mark,Gabriel Rioux,Riccardo Passegger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Averaged Mirror Descent (AMD) 方法，平均连续 MD 步，并证明其对任意代价函数的收敛性；同时给出固定步长对偶梯度法对任意代价收敛的结论，兼顾不精确迭代，实验显示 AMD 与对偶梯度法在经典 MD 失效的例子上仍能收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) 距离可基于内在结构度量异质数据集间的差异并寻找最优对齐，但其计算昂贵，常采用熵正则化得到 EGW 问题。当前最流行的 EGW 求解器 Mirror Descent (MD) 虽被广泛使用，其收敛性却仅在受限的代价函数类上得到证明；另一种对偶梯度法虽适用于一般代价，但步长选择依赖于正则化参数。本文旨在同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出 Averaged Mirror Descent (AMD)，即对连续的 MD 步进行平均，并证明其对任意代价函数收敛；进一步证明固定步长的对偶梯度法（虽迭代更复杂）对任意代价亦收敛；两种方法均考虑了实际中不可避免的不精确迭代。

Result: 在各种设置下比较了这些方法的经验性能，特别地，在经典 MD 失效的例子上，AMD 与对偶梯度法均能收敛。

Conclusion: AMD 与固定步长对偶梯度法在任意代价函数下均具有收敛保证，且对不精确迭代稳健，为 EGW 问题提供了比经典 MD 更可靠的求解方案。

Abstract: The Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) distance measures the discrepancy between metric measure (mm) spaces and identifies optimal alignments between them based solely on their intrinsic structure. Since it identifies isomorphic mm spaces, it provides a natural notion of distance for heterogeneous datasets which may admit isomorphic representations. In order to accelerate computation of GW distances, many practitioners employ entropic regularization to obtain an Entropic GW (EGW) problem. The most popular EGW solver is the Mirror Descent (MD) algorithm, which reduces EGW computations to an iterative process where an entropic optimal transport (EOT) problem is solved at each iteration. Despite its widespread use, the convergence of MD for this problem has only been established for restricted classes of costs. On the other hand, a recently proposed dual gradient method is available for general costs, but requires a choice of step size which depends on the regularization parameter. To address these two issues, we introduce Averaged Mirror Descent (AMD), which averages consecutive MD steps, and prove its convergence for arbitrary costs. Then, we establish that the dual gradient method with a fixed step size also converges for arbitrary costs at the cost of a more complicated iteration. In both cases, we also account for inexact iterations which are inescapable in practice. We compare the empirical performance of these methods across various settings and, in particular, show that AMD and the dual gradient method both converge on an example where classical MD fails.

</details>


### [180] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Equity Trading: Benchmarking Actor-Critic Methods with Forward Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31870)
*Bicheng Wang,Xinyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以20只标普500大盘股2000–2020年数据为样本，比较A2C、PPO、DDPG、TD3、SAC五种深度强化学习交易方法与监督式价格预测基线，评估收益、风险及前瞻式再训练的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 股票市场噪声大、非平稳且仅部分可预测，持续盈利的交易策略难以构建，因此需要系统比较端到端深度强化学习方法与传统价格预测基线在收益与风险上的表现差异。

Method: 采用日频数据，覆盖20只S&P 500大盘股（2000–2020），加入趋势跟踪技术指标与对数min-max缩放；训练期为2000–2018，回测期为2019–2020。对A2C、PPO、DDPG、TD3、SAC五种actor-critic型DRL方法分别进行单次训练与前瞻式再训练（在每个测试窗口前用全部可用数据重新训练），并与监督式价格预测基线对比。

Result: DDPG年化收益最高（55.5%）、Sharpe比率1.38、alpha 0.22，但市场beta也最高（1.24）。TD3与SAC风险-收益更均衡，Sharpe分别为1.37和1.33，最大回撤约25%。前瞻式再训练提升A2C、PPO和SAC表现，TD3基本不变，而DDPG年化收益从55.5%降至29.8%，显示TD3对超参数更稳健。预测基线最大回撤最小（9.6%）、beta最低（0.31）。

Conclusion: 端到端DRL策略在收益上更具优势，但风险暴露更高；预测驱动的策略收益较低却风险更小，二者之间存在明显的收益-风险权衡。DDPG在高收益同时伴随高beta和对再训练敏感，TD3与SAC在稳健性与风险控制上更具吸引力。

Abstract: Consistently profitable trading is difficult because equity markets are noisy, non-stationary, and only partially predictable from historical data. We benchmark five deep reinforcement learning (DRL) actor-critic methods: A2C, PPO, DDPG, TD3, and SAC, that learn trading actions end-to-end from market states, and compare them with a supervised price-forecasting baseline. Using daily data for 20 large-capitalization S&P 500 stocks from 2000 to 2020, enriched with trend-following technical indicators and log min-max scaling, we train on 2000-2018 and backtest on 2019-2020. Each agent is evaluated both when trained once and under forward retraining, in which it is retrained on all data available before each successive test window. DDPG achieves the highest annual return (55.5%), Sharpe ratio (1.38), and alpha (0.22), but also the highest market beta (1.24). TD3 and SAC offer a better risk-return balance, with Sharpe ratios of 1.37 and 1.33 and maximum drawdowns of about 25%. Forward retraining improves A2C, PPO, and SAC, leaves TD3 essentially unchanged, and reduces DDPG's annual return from 55.5% to 29.8%, consistent with TD3's greater robustness to hyperparameters. The forecasting baseline has the smallest maximum drawdown (9.6%) and the lowest beta (0.31), underscoring a trade-off between the higher returns of end-to-end DRL and the lower risk of forecast-driven strategies.

</details>


### [181] [TemporalGraphLLM: Temporal Graph Neural Networks with Large Language Models for Dynamic Text-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31881)
*Moran Beladev,Or Eitan,Gilad Katz,Lior Rokach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TemporalGraphLLM 框架，将时序图神经网络与 LLM 结合，用于动态文本属性图上的推理，在边分类、链接预测和边文本生成任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态文本属性图（DTAGs）中节点、边和文本属性随时间演化，广泛用于社交网络、引文图、知识图谱等场景。现有方法难以同时建模图结构的时间演化和文本属性的语义丰富性：TGNN 能捕捉演化关系但缺乏文本上下文推理，LLM 擅长文本理解但在时序图结构化推理上存在不足。因此需要弥合两者之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出 TemporalGraphLLM，可整合任意时序 GNN 与 LLM。采用图-时间感知的指令微调，并通过新颖的时序 GNN 注入机制，用图嵌入替换专门的附加 token，从而在 LLM 框架内有效利用预训练 TGNN。

Result: 在真实动态图数据集上的大量评估表明，该方法在边分类、链接预测和基于边的文本生成任务上均达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 研究凸显了 LLM 与 TGNN 的协同潜力，为演化图上的学习开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Dynamic text-attributed graphs (DTAGs), where nodes, edges, and textual attributes evolve over time, are crucial in applications such as social networks, citation graphs, and knowledge graphs. However, existing approaches struggle to jointly model the temporal evolution of graph structures and the semantic richness of textual attributes. While Temporal Graph Neural Networks (TGNNs) capture evolving node relationships, they often lack contextual text reasoning. Conversely, Large Language Models (LLMs) excel in textual understanding but struggle with structured graph reasoning in temporal settings. To bridge this gap, we propose TemporalGraphLLM, a novel framework that can integrate any temporal GNN with an LLM for enhanced reasoning in DTAGs. Our approach fine-tunes LLMs using graph-time-aware instruction tuning and novel temporal GNNs injection to replace dedicated added tokens with graph embeddings. TemporalGraphLLM effectively leverages pretrained TGNNs within an LLM framework to achieve state-of-the-art performance on edge classification, link prediction, and edge-based text generation tasks. Extensive evaluation on real-world dynamic graph datasets demonstrates state-of-the-art performance. Our findings highlight the synergistic potential of LLMs and TGNNs, opening new directions for learning on evolving graphs.

</details>


### [182] [DOHF: Online Diffusion Fine-tuning with Doob's $h$-transform Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31882)
*Zhengyi Guo,Jiayuan Sheng,Wenpin Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DOHF，将Doob h-变换转化为实用在线扩散微调算法，用最优性权重估计归一化局部校正并蒸馏进生成模型，在无需额外网络评估下适配黑盒/不可微奖励，并在三类场景中提升对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于奖励的扩散微调面临两大实际困难：理想结果样本稀少，且条件校正的估计代价高昂。

Method: 提出Diffusion Online h-guidance Fine-tuning（DOHF）：为生成样本分配最优性权重；在当前rollout策略下估计归一化局部校正∇log h；将该校正直接蒸馏进生成模型。理论上用统一的h-变换视角刻画population-optimal DiffusionNFT更新及各类classifier-free guidance方法。

Result: 框架可在不增加额外网络评估的情况下兼容黑盒与不可微奖励；在三种实验场景中显示出对齐效果的提升。

Conclusion: 通过低成本估计与迭代蒸馏来适配概率条件化，可改进统计采样与视觉生成中的生成式学习。

Abstract: Reward-based diffusion fine-tuning faces practical challenges when desirable outcomes are rare or conditioning corrections are costly to estimate. In this work, we propose Diffusion Online $h$-guidance Fine-tuning (DOHF), which turns Doob's $h$-transform into a practical online training algorithm. DOHF assigns optimality weights to generated samples, estimates the normalized local correction $\nabla\log h$ under the current rollout policy, and distills it directly into the generative model. Theoretically, we characterize the population-optimal DiffusionNFT update as well as the various classfier free guidance methods through a unified $h$-transform perspective. Methodologically, our framework accommodates black-box and non-differentiable rewards without additional network evaluations. We further show improved alignments under three empirical scenarios. Our work demonstrates how adapting probabilistic conditioning through inexpensive estimation and iterative distillation can improve generative learning across statistical sampling and visual generation.

</details>


### [183] [FARE: Deep Reinforcement Learning For Fair Exposure Constrained Uncertainty Aware Financial Content Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31890)
*Arundeep Chinta,Lucas Vinh Tran,Jay Katukuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出FARE，一个不确定性感知的排序执行层，将SOV约束排序建模为深度强化学习问题，在保证公平曝光的同时最小化点击损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融服务中的内容个性化需保证多样化产品的公平曝光，既受合同义务驱动，也要避免高CTR内容主导的"富者愈富"现象。SOV约束要求每个内容类别在顶部位置获得目标曝光比例，但现有基于CTR模型的再排序层未能显式处理CTR预测不确定性。

Method: 将SOV约束排序类比为算法金融中的约束交易执行，建模为深度强化学习问题。显式将CTR预测不确定性纳入智能体状态空间与策略设计，使高不确定性预测可进行更大排序调整。提出模块化执行层FARE，无需重训底层模型即可将任意黑盒CTR预测转化为SOV公平排序，包括不确定性加权比例控制策略FARE-PC及学习型神经策略FARE-ES和FARE-PPO。

Result: 不确定性感知方法能显著降低SOV相对公平目标的偏差，同时最小化参与度损失。在合成数据上，无梯度进化策略优于策略梯度方法；而在KuaiRand-Pure数据集上，两者优劣关系发生反转。

Conclusion: 显式建模CTR预测不确定性有助于在公平约束与参与度之间取得更好平衡，FARE作为模块化执行层具有通用性和实用性，但不同策略的适用性依赖数据特性。

Abstract: Content personalization systems in financial services must ensure fair exposure across diverse offerings-a requirement driven by contractual obligations and the need to prevent "rich-get-richer" dynamics where content with high click-through rate (CTR) dominates while other relevant products receive minimal visibility. Share of Voice (SOV) constraints, which guarantee each content category a target fraction of top-position exposure, address this by promoting product diversity and balanced user discovery. While re-ranking layers atop CTR models are common in practice, we propose two key novelties: (1) framing SOV-constrained ranking as a deep reinforcement learning problem analogous to constrained trade execution in algorithmic finance, and (2) explicitly incorporating CTR prediction uncertainty into the agent's state space and policy design-enabling larger ranking adjustments for high-uncertainty predictions where deviation from CTR-optimal ordering is less costly. We introduce FARE (Fair Ranking Executor), a modular uncertainty-aware execution layer that translates any black-box CTR model's predictions into SOV-fair rankings without retraining the underlying model. Our uncertainty-weighted proportional control policy (FARE-PC) and learned neural policies (FARE-ES, FARE-PPO) demonstrate that uncertainty-aware approaches can substantially reduce SOV deviation from fairness targets while minimizing engagement loss, with gradient-free evolution strategies outperforming policy gradient methods on synthetic data and the ordering reversing on KuaiRand-Pure.

</details>


### [184] [CyberWorld: World Models for Sample-Efficient Autonomous Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31893)
*Ryozo Masukawa,Sanggeon Yun,Raheeb Hassan,Hyunwoo Oh,SungHeon Jeong,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CyberWorld，一种 Dreamer 风格的世界模型框架，从向量、图、文本和多模态表示中学习潜在网络动态，探索"网络世界模型中的世界应为何物"这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主网络防御方法多为无模型强化学习，需要大量环境交互。世界模型在机器人领域通过想象轨迹优化策略，显著提升样本效率，但将其扩展到网络安全面临核心问题：网络世界模型中的"世界"应如何定义。

Method: 提出 CyberWorld 框架，采用 Dreamer 风格的世界建模方式，从被防御网络的向量、图、文本和多模态四种表示中学习潜在网络动态，并通过想象轨迹优化策略。

Result: 在全部四种可评分的 CyberWheel 攻击策略中，基于图的 CyberWorld 变体在 3.6k–15.8k 环境步后超越策略无关基线，而无模型 PPO 需数百万步。图结构在拓扑依赖攻击下鲁棒性更强，更简单表示在整体性能上仍有竞争力。成功运行中，网络规模从 15 增至 100 主机时，达到基线所需回合数近似恒定。

Conclusion: 学习到的网络动态可作为自主防御的样本高效且可扩展的基础，世界表示是影响鲁棒性与可扩展性的核心设计维度。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning has become a prominent approach to autonomous cyber defense. Existing methods are predominantly model-free and consequently require extensive environment interaction. World models provide an alternative by learning predictive dynamics and optimizing policies through imagined trajectories, yielding substantial gains in sample efficiency in robotics and embodied control. Extending this paradigm to cybersecurity raises a fundamental question: what should constitute the "world" in a cyber world model? We introduce CyberWorld, a Dreamer-style world modeling framework that learns latent cyber dynamics from vector, graph, textual, and multimodal representations of the defended network. Across all four scoreable CyberWheel attack strategies, the graph-based CyberWorld variant exceeds a strategy-agnostic control after 3.6k-15.8k environment steps, compared with millions of steps required by model-free PPO. Across representation choices, graph structure provides greater robustness under topology-dependent attacks, while simpler representations remain competitive in overall performance. Among successful runs, the number of episodes required to reach the control remains approximately constant as network size increases from 15 to 100 hosts. These results establish learned cyber dynamics as a sample-efficient and scalable basis for autonomous defense, and identify world representation as a central design axis for robustness and scalability.

</details>


### [185] [Understanding the Synergy between SFT, RLVR, and OPD in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31900)
*Emre Can Acikgoz,Yang Li,Zeyu Leo Liu,Srijan Bansal,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出大模型后训练的多阶段流程（SFT、RLVR、OPD）各阶段存在耦合效应，某阶段即使提升当前模型，也可能削弱下一阶段的效果；通过Qwen3在数学与科学推理上的受控实验，揭示了OPD有效性取决于师生兼容性而非教师规模，并提出教师适配与学生热身可将OPD平均准确率从29.2%提升至43.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM后训练常将SFT、RLVR与在线策略蒸馏（OPD）组合成多阶段流水线，但这些阶段通常被孤立地设计和评估。作者希望揭示这种组合具有实质性影响：某个能改进当前模型的阶段，可能反而降低下一阶段的学习效率。因此需要系统研究各阶段之间的相互影响与"学习接口"效应，而非只关注单阶段的增益。

Method: 使用Qwen3模型在数学与科学推理任务上进行受控实验。首先在2倍至53倍参数量比例的九个师生对上刻画OPD特性，分析其有效性与师生兼容性的关系。随后考察OPD前后阶段对兼容性的重塑：(1)对比简短SFT热身与RLVR强化后的学生进行蒸馏的效果；(2)用RLVR适配教师并观察下游OPD准确率变化；(3)在相同准确率下比较OPD与SFT为下游RLVR提供的初始化质量及其随RL算力扩展的表现。

Result: OPD有效性取决于师生兼容性而非单纯的教师规模。简短SFT热身可提升后续OPD，而经RLVR强化的学生从同一教师蒸馏时反而退化；用RLVR适配教师能按其所增加的能力比例提升下游OPD准确率。结合教师适配与学生热身，可在相同蒸馏步数（含额外预备训练）下将OPD平均准确率从29.2%升至43.8%，相对提升50%。此外，在准确率相当时，OPD为下游RLVR留下的初始化强于SFT，且该差距随RL算力扩展而扩大。

Conclusion: 多阶段后训练中，每个阶段的选择不应只考虑其自身带来的能力增益，还应考虑它为下一阶段创造的学习接口。阶段之间存在显著的耦合与相互影响，孤立评估会低估或误判整体流水线的效果，应面向后续阶段进行阶段设计。

Abstract: Modern LLM post-training composes supervised fine-tuning (SFT), reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), and on-policy distillation (OPD) into multi-stage pipelines, yet these stages are typically designed and evaluated in isolation. We show that this composition is consequential: a stage that improves the current model can make the next stage less effective. Through controlled experiments with Qwen3 models on math and science reasoning, we first characterize OPD across nine student-teacher pairs spanning 2x to 53x parameter ratios and show that OPD effectiveness depends on student-teacher compatibility rather than teacher scale alone. The surrounding stages of OPD reshape this compatibility in three ways: (1) A brief SFT warm-up improves subsequent OPD, while an RLVR-strengthened student regresses under distillation from the same teacher. (2) Adapting the teacher with RLVR raises downstream OPD accuracy in proportion to the capability it adds. Following these two interventions, we find that combining teacher adaptation and student warm-up alone raise average OPD accuracy from 29.2\% to 43.8\% (50\% relative improvement) after the same number of distillation steps, with additional preparatory training. (3) At comparable accuracy, OPD leaves a stronger initialization for downstream RLVR than SFT, with a gap that widens as RL compute scales. Our results suggest that each post-training stage should be chosen not only for the capability it adds, but for the learning interface it creates for the next stage.

</details>


### [186] [ROTE: Benchmarking Neural Memorization on Complexity-Controlled Symbolic Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31918)
*Xinye Chen,Stefan Güttel,Mohammad Mozaffari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ROTE基准协议，用LZW压缩调控序列复杂度，评估多种神经架构的符号记忆能力与规则外推，揭示记忆质量、rollout稳定性与计算开销之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作缺乏对神经架构符号记忆能力的系统性评估手段，尤其是缺乏以可控复杂度序列为基准、统一训练与评估流程的协议，难以比较不同架构在记忆与外推规则上的表现。

Method: 提出ROTE（RollOut Testing of Exact memorization）协议：使用LZW压缩调控序列复杂度；各架构统一训练为相同有限上下文条件预测器；评估采用teacher-forced单步预测与对保留符号的闭环rollout；覆盖门控循环、最小循环、注意力及混合循环-注意力模型，并保留其原生计算特性；除标准预测指标外，报告归一化字符串距离、训练时间、内存占用与参数量，并进行LZW复杂度扫描。

Result: 在LZW复杂度扫描下，各架构在记忆质量、rollout稳定性与计算开销方面表现出不同取舍，建立了算法序列复杂度与神经架构记忆容量之间的联系。

Conclusion: ROTE为神经序列模型的符号记忆与规则外推提供了可复现的评估基准，揭示复杂度与记忆容量间的关联及多维度权衡，代码开源。

Abstract: We introduce ROTE (RollOut Testing of Exact memorization), a benchmarking protocol for evaluating symbolic memorization of neural architectures. We study memorization and the extension of symbolic rules in neural sequence models by using sequences whose complexity is regulated by Lempel--Ziv--Welch (LZW) compression. Under ROTE, each architecture is trained as the same finite-context conditional predictor and is evaluated using teacher-forced one-step prediction as well as closed-loop rollout on the withheld symbols. Following a shared prediction-and-rollout evaluation routine, the benchmark evaluates gated recurrent, minimal recurrent, attention-based, and hybrid recurrent-attention models with their native computational characteristics preserved. Beyond standard predictive metrics, the benchmark reports normalized string distances, training time, memory usage, and parameter count across an LZW-complexity sweep. The study establishes a connection between the complexity of algorithmic sequences and the memorization capacity of neural architectures, revealing the trade-offs involving memorization quality, rollout stability, and computational expense. Our software and reproducible experimental code can be obtained from https://github.com/nla-group/rote.

</details>


### [187] [Resource-Aware Federated Mixture-of-Experts with Adaptive Pruning for Onboard Learning in LEO Satellite Constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31932)
*Mohamed Shaaban,Mohamed Elmahallawy,Marius Bernahrndt,Tobias Hecking*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出COSMIC-FL资源感知联邦学习框架，利用MoE架构与结构化剪枝实现LEO卫星在轨高效学习，通信/计算/能耗最多降低80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星需在轨学习（如灾害响应、环境监测），但传统联邦学习假设的资源超出受限星载平台能力，且往往需将原始影像传回地面站，因此需要资源感知的联邦学习方案。

Method: COSMIC-FL引入两种互补MoE架构：Sliced设计共享骨干表示并激活任务特定通道子集；Modular设计用轻量门控将输入路由至物理分离的专家网络。通过语义类别-专家映射，使每颗卫星仅训练、更新与通信与本地数据相关的专家路径。进一步引入分阶段优化与三种结构化剪枝：服务端剪枝、客户端固定比例剪枝（均值投票聚合）、基于聚合重要性与MAD差距准则的自适应客户端逐层剪枝。

Result: 在六个图像分类基准的高度非独立同分布设置下，COSMIC-FL保持有竞争力精度，相较SOTA联邦学习方法在通信、计算与能耗上最多降低80%；并在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上验证了真实嵌入式部署约束下的效率提升。

Conclusion: 语义专家路由与结构化剪枝协同，使计算与模型稀疏性自适应匹配数据语义与层级重要性，在异构空间平台上实现精度-效率的良好权衡。

Abstract: Low-Earth-orbit (LEO) satellites are increasingly expected to perform onboard learning for applications such as disaster response and environmental monitoring. However, conventional federated learning (FL) is ill-suited to onboard satellite learning, as it assumes computational, memory, and communication resources beyond the capabilities of resource-constrained LEO platforms, often necessitating the transmission of raw imagery to ground stations. We present COSMIC-FL, a resource-aware FL framework for efficient onboard learning in LEO satellite constellations. COSMIC-FL introduces two complementary Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures: a Sliced design that shares backbone representations while activating task-specific channel subsets, and a Modular design that employs lightweight gating to route inputs to physically separated expert networks. A semantic class-to-expert mapping enables each satellite to train, update, and communicate only the expert paths relevant to its local data. To further improve efficiency, COSMIC-FL integrates staged optimization with three structured pruning strategies: server-side pruning, client-side fixed-ratio pruning with mean-vote aggregation, and adaptive client-side per-layer pruning based on aggregated importance and a MAD-based gap criterion. Combined with semantic expert routing, these techniques jointly adapt computation and model sparsity to both data semantics and layer importance, yielding a favourable accuracy--efficiency trade-off for heterogeneous space platforms. Experiments on six image classification benchmarks under highly non-i.i.d. settings show that COSMIC-FL maintains competitive accuracy while reducing communication, computation, and energy consumption by up to 80% over SOTA FL methods. We further validate COSMIC-FL on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, confirming its efficiency gains under realistic embedded deployment constraints.

</details>


### [188] [Cache-Aware Conv3D Lowering Across Embedded World-Model Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31938)
*Jiaming Zhang,Wu Yang,Shuai Tao,Wulong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种"缓存感知 lowering"方法，将因果 Conv3D 调用重写为批量空间 Conv2D 操作，在不修改预训练权重的情况下大幅加速生成式世界模型的 VAE 解码与整体推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型可为具身规划提供视觉推演，但其在边缘设备上的可行性不仅取决于模型本身，还取决于执行运行时如何表示其算子。现有部署往往需要针对每个模块和运行时状态做大量 AOT 特化，代价高，因此需要一种轻量的运行时优化手段。

Method: 提出缓存感知 lowering：将受支持的因果 Conv3D 调用表达为批量空间 Conv2D 操作，同时保持预训练权重、时间缓存语义、卷积参数、偏置放置和输出布局不变；在 64-GB NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin 上对完整 Cosmos3-Edge 图像到视频流水线进行验证，并与完全特化的 TensorRT 进行对比，还做了同潜变量下的 BF16/FP32 有限精度评估。

Result: 在 Jetson AGX Orin 上，VAE 解码加速约 7 倍，完整生成延迟降低 2 倍以上，重复解码评估保持完整快速路径覆盖、无回退；同样的 lowering 也提升 Cosmos3-Nano，并迁移到架构不同的 LingBot-World 的 Wan2.1 VAE。与全特化 TensorRT 相比，TensorRT 在稳态下还能再快 1.36 倍，但需要显著更多的逐模块、逐运行时状态 AOT 特化。

Conclusion: 缓存感知 lowering 是一种轻量级运行时优化，可在不修改学习模型本身的前提下，恢复大部分可获得的解码器加速，为生成式世界模型在边缘设备上的部署提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Generative world models can provide visual rollouts for embodied planning, yet their feasibility on edge devices depends not only on the learned model but also on how the execution runtime represents its operations. We introduce a cache-aware lowering that expresses supported causal Conv3D calls as batched spatial Conv2D operations while preserving pretrained weights, temporal-cache semantics, convolution parameters, bias placement, and output layout. Across the complete Cosmos3-Edge image-to-video pipeline on a 64-GB NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, the proposed route accelerates VAE decoding by approximately $7\times$ and reduces complete-generation latency by more than $2\times$, while repeated decoder evaluations maintain complete fast-path coverage without fallbacks. The unchanged lowering also improves Cosmos3-Nano and transfers to LingBot-World's architecturally distinct Wan2.1 VAE. A clean-device comparison against fully specialized TensorRT shows that TensorRT provides a further $1.36\times$ steady-state improvement, but requires substantially greater per-module and per-runtime-state AOT specialization. Same-latent BF16 and FP32 evaluations characterize the finite-precision differences introduced by the alternative execution order. Together, these results position cache-aware lowering as a lightweight runtime optimization that recovers most of the available decoder acceleration without modifying the learned models themselves.

</details>


### [189] [Simple Extensions of Single-Objective Acquisition Functions and Hedge Strategies for Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31940)
*Haris Moazam Sheikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于超体积变换的框架，将标准单目标采集函数直接扩展到多目标贝叶斯优化，并扩展到多目标对冲策略，方法简单却能与复杂MOBO方法匹敌或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）通常依赖专门的采集函数或标量化方案来显式处理非偏好目标间的权衡，复杂度高。作者质疑这种复杂性是否必要，希望用更简单的方式实现有效的MOBO。

Method: 提出一个通过超体积（hypervolume）变换将标准单目标采集函数直接扩展到多目标场景的框架；并将原本仅用于单目标优化的采集函数对冲（hedge）策略扩展到多目标情形。方法对现有贝叶斯优化流程改动极小，无需专门的多目标公式。

Result: 在多种合成和真实多目标基准上评估，所提方法尽管简单，在优化性能和样本效率上一致地达到或超过更复杂的当前最优MOBO方法。

Conclusion: 有效的多目标贝叶斯优化可以通过复用并谨慎扩展成熟的单目标采集策略来实现，为现有方法提供更简单、更灵活的替代方案。

Abstract: Multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) is commonly approached through specialized acquisition functions or scalarization schemes designed to explicitly account for trade-offs among non-preferential objectives. In this work, we show that such complexity might be unnecessary. We propose a framework that extends standard single-objective acquisition functions directly to the multi-objective setting through a hypervolume-based transformation. We further extend hedge strategies for acquisition functions, which are typically used only in single-objective optimization, to the multi-objective regime. Our approach requires minimal modification to existing Bayesian optimization pipelines and avoids the need for bespoke multi-objective formulations. We demonstrate how a broad class of commonly used single-objective acquisition functions and hedge strategies can be adapted in a principled manner to handle multiple objectives, while preserving their intuitive interpretation and computational efficiency. Empirically, we evaluate the proposed methods across a range of synthetic and real-world multi-objective benchmarks. Despite their simplicity, our extensions consistently match or outperform more complex state-of-the-art MOBO methods in terms of optimization performance and sample efficiency. These results suggest that effective multi-objective Bayesian optimization can be achieved by reusing and carefully extending well-established single-objective acquisition strategies, offering a simpler and more flexible alternative to existing approaches.

</details>


### [190] [On-Policy Attention Linearization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31947)
*Arian Raje,Anupam Nayak,Anthony Fei,Akaash Parthasarathy,Mohamed Abdelfattah,Gauri Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OPAL（On-Policy Attention Linearization），通过在策略蒸馏让混合注意力学生模型采样自身长上下文轨迹并接受冻结全注意力教师的密集监督，解决了线性注意力压缩导致的误差累积问题，仅用 3B tokens 即大幅恢复长上下文与推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合 transformer 用线性注意力替换大部分 softmax 注意力，可显著降低内存，但现有从全注意力教师蒸馏得到的模型在长上下文检索与思考模式推理任务上常崩溃。原因是线性注意力层需将上下文压缩进固定大小状态，误差在长序列上累积，而离策略蒸馏从未教学生如何从这种漂移中恢复，任务序列越长越困难。

Method: 提出 On-Policy Attention Linearization（OPAL）：让混合注意力的学生模型自行采样长上下文轨迹（在策略），并接受来自冻结的全注意力教师的密集监督，从而学会从自身生成的漂移中恢复。应用于 Qwen3-4B 与 MiMo-7B-RL-0530，无需 SFT 或 RLVR。

Result: 仅用 3B 训练 tokens：常识推理恢复全注意力性能的 87–94%；NIAH 检索恢复 100%；数学推理恢复 83–93%。相比最强先前线性化方法（检索仅恢复 68%、数学推理绝对平均准确率仅 21.6%），OPAL 完全恢复检索且数学达到 67.6–72.2%。

Conclusion: 在策略蒸馏能有效缓解线性注意力的误差累积问题，使混合注意力学生模型在长上下文检索和推理上接近甚至达到全注意力教师水平，且训练代价低、无需 SFT 或 RLVR。

Abstract: Hybrid transformer architectures that replace most softmax attention layers with linear attention offer transformer-level quality at a fraction of the memory cost. Rather than pretraining such models, a growing body of work distills them from already trained full-attention transformers. However, these distilled models often collapse on long-context retrieval and reasoning tasks, particularly when operating in thinking mode, where the efficiency gains of hybrid architectures matter most. Since linear attention layers must compress context into a fixed-size state, their errors compound over long sequences. As off-policy distillation never teaches the student model to recover from this drift, tasks that necessitate longer sequence lengths become especially challenging. We introduce On-Policy Attention Linearization (OPAL) in which the hybrid attention student samples its own long-context trajectories and receives dense supervision from the frozen full-attention teacher. Applying OPAL to Qwen3-4B and MiMo-7B-RL-0530, we recover $87$--$94\%$ of full-attention performance on commonsense reasoning, $100\%$ on needle-in-a-haystack (NIAH) retrieval, and $83$--$93\%$ on mathematical reasoning with only 3B training tokens. We achieve these results without supervised fine-tuning (SFT) or reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). Compared with the strongest prior linearization method, which recovers $68\%$ of its teacher's retrieval performance and $21.6\%$ absolute average mathematical reasoning accuracy, OPAL fully recovers retrieval and achieves $67.6$--$72.2\%$ on math reasoning.

</details>


### [191] [Model-Agnostic Online Certificate-Driven Calibration for Time Series Forecasting Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31960)
*Chenfeng Huang,Zixuan Ma,George Michailidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出模型无关的在线鞅PAC-Bayes框架，在时间依赖与分布漂移下给出有限样本certificate，并用作在线校准的正则化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列OOD泛化中，部署动态常因协变量漂移、概念漂移和时间依赖性而偏离训练条件。现有PAC-Bayes域适应分析依赖独立采样与分布稳定性假设，这些假设在时间序列中因序列相关和非平稳漂移而被违反，导致certificate失效。

Method: 将目标风险分解为源风险项、源-目标失配项和复杂度项；用鞅集中不等式替代独立样本集中，构造适应损失尺度与可预测变差的在线鞅PAC-Bayes有限样本certificate。以该certificate作为在线校准的代理正则化器，在固定预测骨干上训练门控残差贝叶斯头，产生纠正更新，门关闭时回退骨干预测。在线校准结合源风险锚、后验漂移惩罚和由目标窗口计算的时间自适应失配项，遵循先预测后更新协议。

Result: 在卷积、注意力及大语言模型预测器上实验，在协变量漂移和概念漂移下均显示稳定性与精度提升。

Conclusion: 该框架为时间序列OOD泛化提供了在时间依赖和分布漂移下可计算的有限样本保证，并通过门控在线校准有效提升多种预测器的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Time series out-of-distribution generalization requires forecasters to remain reliable when deployment dynamics differ from training conditions due to covariate shift, concept shift, and temporal dependence. Probably Approximately Correct Bayesian domain adaptation provides computable certificates by decomposing target risk into a source risk term, a source-to-target mismatch term, and a complexity term, but standard analyses rely on independent sampling and distributional stability, assumptions that are violated in time series by serial dependence and nonstationary shift. We propose a model-agnostic online martingale Probably Approximately Correct Bayesian framework that yields finite-sample certificates under temporal dependence and distribution shift. The certificate replaces independent-sample concentration with martingale concentration that adapts to loss scale and predictable variation. We use the certificate as a surrogate regularizer for online calibration by training a gated residual Bayesian head on top of a fixed forecasting backbone, producing a corrective update that reverts to the backbone prediction when the gate is closed. Online calibration combines a source risk anchor, a posterior-shift penalty, and a time-adaptive mismatch term computed from target windows observed before forecasting. It follows a predict-then-update protocol in which outcomes become available only after forecasting and are used to update subsequent predictions. Experiments across convolutional, attention-based, and large language model-based forecasters show improved stability and accuracy under covariate and concept shift.

</details>


### [192] [Model Casting and Low-Parameter Gating: Towards More Sparsely Activated FFNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31975)
*Maria Lomeli,Antoine Groudiev,Matthijs Douze,Loïc Cabannes,Pierre Emmanuel Mazaré,François Fleuret,Maximilian Beck,Gergely Szilvasy,Naila Murray,Hervé Jégou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 model casting 与 LoPA Gating 两种方法，通过稀疏化 FFN 激活降低推理 FLOPs，突破 3x 加速上限，在 GPU 上实现 3.31x 实际加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈网络（FFN）计算量大，现有剪枝方法（如 top-p、TEAL）加速效果有限，且标准门控机制存在 3x 的加速上限，难以进一步提升推理效率。

Method: 提出 model casting：训练中期稀疏化 FFN 激活，推理时仅计算门控矩阵输出，跳过另两个矩阵计算。进一步提出 LoPA Gating，一种低 FLOPs 的门控矩阵参数化方式，通过为门控矩阵分配更少 FLOPs 和参数来突破 3x 上限。涵盖两种场景：对预训练模型进行诱导稀疏激活的 casting，以及从零开始用 LoPA 训练。

Result: 在同等质量下，LoPA Casting 实现 3.2x FLOP 加速，而 top-p 与 TEAL 最多仅 1.6x。使用专用 kernel 后，在 90% 稀疏度下 GPU 上实际加速达 3.31x，突破标准门控 3x 上限；RELU-fication 则停滞在 80% 稀疏度以下。

Conclusion: model casting 与 LoPA Gating 显著优于现有剪枝方案和常规 RELU-fication，能有效突破 FFN 加速的理论上限，并在 CPU 和 GPU 上均带来实际加速。

Abstract: This paper introduces model casting, a mid-training recipe that drastically sparsifies the activations within the Feed-Forward Network (FFN) layer. With this strategy, at inference time, we first compute the output of the gating matrix and, thanks to its high sparsity, we avoid computations with the two other matrices, reducing the FLOP count by up to 3x. While this theoretical speedup is an upper bound, model casting translates into significant speedups both on CPU and GPU.
  We then introduce LoPA Gating, a new FFN design that increases the maximum theoretical speedup. It is a low-FLOPs parameterization of the gating matrix that overcomes the 3x cap by allocating fewer FLOPs and parameters to the gating matrix, compared to the two other FFN matrices that are sparsely activated.
  We consider two cases: (i) we cast a pre-trained model with a sparsity inducing activation; (ii) we train with LoPA from scratch. In all settings, we significantly outperform existing pruning solutions and regular RELU-fication. For instance, at matched quality, we achieve a 3.2x FLOP speedup with LoPA Casting, against 1.6x at best for competing methods top-p and TEAL. Using dedicated kernels, we achieve an actual 3.31x speed-up on GPU at 90% sparsity, past the 3x ceiling of standard gating; RELU-fication, meanwhile, plateaus below 80% sparsity.

</details>


### [193] [Understanding the Subspace Stabilization of the Hessian and Gradient Covariance Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31983)
*Fangshuo Liao,Anastasios Kyrillidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络训练中Hessian矩阵顶部子空间的稳定现象，提出新的不稳定性度量，并通过梯度协方差矩阵的类间-类内分解，给出Hessian顶部子空间的近似显式形式及稳定性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作通过逐步Hessian顶部子空间的重叠度衡量稳定性，并用训练后期参数变化减小来解释该现象。但作者认为该解释依赖于参数变化幅度，需提出与参数变化幅度无关的新度量，以更本质地刻画子空间稳定现象。

Method: 定义新的子空间演化不稳定性度量；观察梯度协方差矩阵顶部子空间与Hessian具有相似性质；对梯度协方差矩阵进行类间与类内分解，导出可近似Hessian及梯度协方差矩阵顶部(C-1)子空间的显式形式；在梯度流框架下分析该近似的慢演化。

Result: 所识别的显式形式能近乎完美地近似Hessian与梯度协方差矩阵的顶部(C-1)子空间；该近似的慢演化源于Hessian矩阵离群特征值与主体特征值之间的分离。

Conclusion: Hessian顶部子空间的稳定现象可由Hessian离群特征值与主体特征值的分离解释，而非仅依赖参数变化幅度减小。该工作为二阶信息在神经网络训练中的稳定结构提供了新度量与理论解释。

Abstract: The phenomenon of the top subspace stabilization of the Hessian matrix is an surprising and critical aspect in study of the second-order information of neural network training. Prior work argues that the top subspace of the Hessian stabilizes by measuring the overlap between the top subspaces of the step-wise Hessian, and explains this stabilization with diminishing parameter change in the late phase of training. In this paper, we define a new instability metric for the subspace evolution, and use it to detect subspace stabilization that is independent of the magnitude of parameter change. In the meantime, we observe that the gradient covariance matrix has a similar property of its top subspace to the Hessian. By using a between-class and within-class decomposition of the gradient covariance matrix, we identify an explicit form that gives a near-perfect approximation of the top-$(C-1)$ subspace of the Hessian and the gradient covariance matrix. In the gradient flow set-up, we show that the slow evolution of the idenfied approximation is due to the separation between the outlier and the bulk eigenvalues of the Hessian matrix, thus providing an explanation to the phenomenon of the top subspace stabilization of the Hessian matrix.

</details>


### [194] [Lagrangian and Hamiltonian Neural Networks With a Dissipative System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31988)
*V. Rayamajhi,J. Singal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了拉格朗日神经网络与哈密顿神经网络在含时耗散系统上的适用性，以带阻尼的一维谐振子为对象，并与无阻尼情形对比，发现两种方法均能预测阻尼系统的物理行为并不同程度地"学习"到潜在的拉格朗日量与哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉格朗日神经网络与哈密顿神经网络此前主要被验证于无阻尼等无显式时间依赖的系统，其在耗散、显含时间依赖系统上的适用性尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对带阻尼的一维单分量谐振子构建拉格朗日与哈密顿神经网络模型，并与无阻尼系统进行对照实验，考察模型对系统行为的预测能力以及对潜在拉格朗日量、哈密顿量的学习效果。

Result: 拉格朗日方法与哈密顿方法均能预测带阻尼系统的经验物理行为，并能在不同程度上有效学习到其背后的拉格朗日量与哈密顿量，与无阻尼情形此前的结果一致。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了拉格朗日与哈密顿力学的一些重要性质，尤其是那些在无显式时间依赖系统中不显现的性质。

Abstract: We investigate the applicability of Lagrangian and Hamiltonian Neural Network models to a dissipative system that has explicit time dependence in its Lagrangian, Hamiltonian, and total energy. To do so we consider these neural network models for simulated systems of a harmonic one-dimensional, one-component oscillator with damping, as well as without damping for comparison. We find that both the Lagrangian and Hamiltonian approaches are able to predict the empirical physical behavior of the damped oscillator systems and to effectively ``learn'' to varying degrees the underlying Lagrangians and Hamiltonians, as has previously been shown to be the case with undamped oscillator systems. These investigations elucidate important properties of Lagrangian and Hamiltonian mechanics, including properties that are not manifest when considering systems without explicit time dependence.

</details>


### [195] [Mechanistic Interpretability Reveals Shared Causal Subspaces in Brain-to-Speech Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31992)
*Maryam Maghsoudi,Ayushi Mishra,Sanghamitra Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文利用机制可解释性中的激活修补方法，研究语音解码器内部哪些神经元承载跨模态信息，发现跨模态收益来自小规模神经元群组而非单个神经元，且早期阶段具模态特异性、后期阶段存在共享表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从脑活动解码隐性语音（如默念或想象语音）比解码发声语音更困难。跨模态迁移是一种有前景的补救手段，但解码器内部如何表征和处理不同语音形式的脑活动仍不清楚，尤其是不清楚哪些内部神经元携带跨模态信息、这些神经元是否在不同语音形式间共享。

Method: 采用机制可解释性方法，使用同一批句子在发声、默念和想象三种条件下的记录构建输入对，进行激活修补。具体做法是将某一句子在一种条件下的解码器内部活动插入到解码器对同句子另一条件的处理过程中，并测量解码准确率的变化。

Result: 没有任何单个神经元能驱动这种跨模态收益；收益来自小规模神经元群组，其中发声语音是最有用的来源。这些群组在解码器早期阶段基本具有条件特异性，而在后期阶段出现重叠。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，可通过训练目标鼓励学习主要来自发声数据的共享后期表征，从而构建更节省数据的隐性语音解码器。

Abstract: Decoding covert speech, such as mimed or imagined, from brain activity is harder than decoding vocalized speech. Cross-modal transfer, where information from one speech form helps decode another, is a promising remedy; yet how a decoder internally represents and processes brain activity from different speech forms remains unclear. In this work, we ask: which internal neurons of a decoder carry cross-modal information, and are these neurons shared across different speech forms? To answer these questions, we leverage mechanistic interpretability, using recordings of the same sentences in vocalized, mimed, and imagined input pairs for activation patching. We insert the decoder's internal activity for a sentence in one condition into its processing of the same sentence in another and measure the change in decoding accuracy. We find that no single neuron drives this benefit; instead, it arises from small groups of neurons, with vocalized speech as the most useful source. These groups are largely condition-specific in the early stage of the decoder but overlap in the later stage. These findings point toward more data-efficient covert speech decoders through training objectives that encourage shared later-stage representations learned mainly from vocalized data.

</details>


### [196] [Can Circuit Alignment Predict OOD Generalization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31996)
*Ayan Banerjee,Abhra Chaudhuri,Josep Llados,Umapada Pal,Anjan Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Circuit Alignment Score (CAS)，无需目标域数据、仅凭模型权重即可预测OOD泛化，且理论上可证明一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表征相似性指标（CKA、SVCCA、RSA）比较激活而非预测泛化，且可证明对分布偏移所诱发的计算图结构重路由不敏感，无法用于OOD泛化预测。

Method: CAS通过图核比较跨域类别特定电路，并分解为同类一致性与跨类混淆两项；将CAS构造为域分布上的Lebesgue积分，用蒙特卡洛估计恢复学习器的OOD精度排序。

Result: 在PACS上48个学习器中，CAS与OOD精度秩相关达0.88，优于CKA(0.58)、SVCCA(0.23)、RSA(0.14)；其他基准及与数据依赖方法对比也呈现类似趋势；成对反转误差以O(1/M)速率趋于零。

Conclusion: CAS是首个可证明一致的分布鲁棒性预测器，既不需要目标域数据也不需要标签。

Abstract: Can out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization be predicted from a trained model's weights alone, without any target-domain data? Existing representational similarity metrics (CKA, SVCCA, RSA) compare activations rather than forecast generalization. We show they are provably insensitive to structural rerouting in the computational graph, the very change distribution shift induces. We close this gap with the Circuit Alignment Score (CAS), which compares class-specific circuits across domains via graph kernels, decomposed into same-class coherence and cross-class confusion. Casting CAS as a Lebesgue integral over the domain distribution, we prove its Monte Carlo estimate recovers the ground-truth ranking of learners by OOD accuracy, with pairwise inversion error vanishing at rate $O(1/M)$, where $M$ is the number of sampled domains. Across $48$ learners on PACS, CAS attains $0.88$ rank correlation with OOD accuracy, versus $0.58$ (CKA), $0.23$ (SVCCA), and $0.14$ (RSA), with similar trends on other benchmarks and even against data-dependent methods, making it the first provably consistent predictor of distributional robustness requiring neither target-domain data nor labels. The code is available at: https://github.com/ayanban011/ACE

</details>


### [197] [Integrating Language Models into Listened and Imagined Speech Decoding from MEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31997)
*Maryam Maghsoudi,Sai Samrat Kankanala,Shihab A. Shamma,Sriram Ganapathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型对MEG神经解码的帮助，并发现其对想象言语解码的增益大于聆听言语解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 想象言语解码是脑机接口的重要目标，但神经响应弱、信噪比低、想象言语数据集稀缺，使其极具挑战。语言模型能提供强上下文线索，但其对神经解码的具体帮助程度及在聆听与想象言语间的差异尚不清楚。

Method: 使用配对的聆听-想象MEG数据集，在两个阶段引入语言模型信息：一是训练对比式神经解码器，将MEG表征与声学及上下文语言表征对齐；二是在推理阶段提出神经约束束搜索框架，将神经证据与语言模型的下一个词概率结合。

Result: 对比式神经解码器提升了跨被试的聆听与想象言语单词解码性能。神经约束束搜索下，想象言语解码从语言模型获益更多：神经证据与语言模型证据的最佳平衡向语言模型偏移，且相对仅神经解码的增益更大。

Conclusion: 语言先验在神经证据较弱时最有用，因此对想象言语脑机接口尤其有价值。

Abstract: Decoding imagined speech is an important goal for brain-computer interfaces but remains challenging due to weak neural responses, low signal-to-noise ratio, and limited imagined-speech datasets. Language models provide strong contextual cues for text prediction, but how much they can help neural decoding and whether their contribution differs for decoding perceived and imagined speech remains unclear. To investigate this, we use a paired listened-imagined MEG dataset and incorporate language-model information at two stages. First, we train a contrastive neural decoder that aligns MEG representations with acoustic and contextual language representations, improving cross-subject word decoding for both listened and imagined speech. Second, at inference, we introduce a neural-constrained beam-search framework that combines neural evidence with language-model next-word probabilities. We find that imagined-speech decoding benefits more from the language model than listened-speech decoding. For Imagined speech, the best-performing balance between neural and language-model evidence shifts toward the language model, and the gain over neural-only decoding is larger. Together, these results suggest that language priors are most useful when neural evidence is weaker, making them particularly valuable for imagined-speech BCIs.

</details>


### [198] [VC Dimension and Expressivity of Real-Valued Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31999)
*Gavin Dooley,Andy Yang,Yijia Jessica Zhu,David Chiang,Peter Cholak,Anand Pillay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究softmax注意力多层Transformer在实数域上的表达能力，给出VC维与split VC维的上界（O(n^4)与O(n^6)）及下界（Ω(n)），并由此推导对称函数表达能力的界限与实数位访问的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于Transformer能力与局限的结果往往对Transformer的定义施加了诸多限制，本文希望在极少附加假设下（softmax注意力、多层、实数运算）研究其表达能力，获得更一般、更本质的结论。

Method: 借助实几何（real geometry）中的结果，对softmax注意力多层Transformer的VC维和split VC维进行上界分析，并构造具体Transformer实例来证明对应的下界；再将结论应用于对称函数空间和实数位访问问题。

Result: 得到VC维上界O(n^4)、split VC维上界O(n^6)（n为输入长度），并构造出达到Ω(n)下界的Transformer。据此证明：在对称（置换不变）函数类中，Transformer可一致表达单符号字母表上的所有函数、非一致表达双符号字母表上的所有函数，但即使非一致也无法表达六符号字母表上的某些函数；同时证明Transformer访问实数十进制位数的限制。

Conclusion: 在极弱假设下，softmax注意力多层Transformer的容量受VC维约束，其对称函数表达能力随字母表规模增加而急剧下降，且可访问的实数精度有限，这些结果刻画了Transformer表达能力的根本界限。

Abstract: Whereas previous results on abilities and limitations of transformers have restricted the definition of transformers in various ways, here we study softmax-attention, multi-layer transformers operating on real values, with very few additional assumptions. Applying results from real geometry, we obtain upper bounds on the VC dimension and split VC dimension of such transformers ($O(n^4)$ and $O(n^6)$, respectively, where $n$ is the input length). Conversely, we also construct specific transformers witnessing lower bounds on these quantities ($Ω(n)$ in each case). These results have some notable consequences. For example, within the class of symmetric (permutation-invariant) functions, we show that transformers can uniformly express all functions over an alphabet of one symbol and non-uniformly express all functions over an alphabet of two symbols, but cannot (even non-uniformly) express some functions over an alphabet of six symbols. We also prove limitations on how many bits of a real number a transformer can access.

</details>


### [199] [Is invariance all you need for algorithmic fairness? Removing demographic information can create new bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32004)
*Aditya Parikh,Eike Petersen,Stella Frank,Enzo Ferrante,Melanie Ganz,Aasa Feragen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从理论与实证两方面论证：强制人口统计表征不变性（demographic invariance）不仅不能有效缓解偏见，反而可能妨碍偏见缓解并制造新的偏见，因此它既非理想也非公平的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人口统计信息在内部表征中的编码常被视为算法偏见的风险因素，人口统计表征不变性常被推崇为理想状态。但作者认为，当人口统计特征与目标标签相关时，一定程度的编码是必要的，盲目追求不变性可能存在问题。

Method: 作者区分了边际表征不变性与类别条件表征不变性，并从数学上证明二者分别对应标准群体公平概念中的人口统计均等与机会均等。随后在五个表格数据集和两个胸部X光影像数据集上，从理论和实证两方面评估两种不变性类型对预测性能和公平性的影响。

Result: 结果表明，强制人口统计不变性会妨碍偏见缓解，甚至产生新的偏见；理论与实证结果一致支持"人口统计表征不变性既不可取也不足以保证公平"这一论点。

Conclusion: 人口统计表征不变性既不是理想目标，也不是实现公平的充分条件；研究者与从业者应重新审视将表征不变性作为公平性解决方案的做法。

Abstract: Encoded demographic information in internal model representations is a commonly assumed risk factor for algorithmic bias, with demographic representation invariance often being touted as the ideal state. However, while demographic shortcut learning is a genuine threat, some degree of encoding is necessary when demographics correlate with target labels. Here, we show, mathematically and empirically, that enforcing demographic invariance can actually hamper bias mitigation and even create new biases. We distinguish marginal from class-conditional representation invariance, and show that they imply the standard group fairness notions of demographic parity and equalized odds, respectively. We evaluate the effects on predictive performance and fairness of enforcing both invariance types, both theoretically and empirically across five tabular and two chest X-ray imaging datasets. Our findings support our mathematical argument that demographic representation invariance is neither desirable nor sufficient for fairness.

</details>


### [200] [Human Activity Recognition via Ultra-Wideband Data: A Framework for Dimensionality Reduction, Pattern Discovery, and Predictive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32008)
*Nahid Sahel Gozin,Reza Sedaghat,Prathap Siddavaatam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个针对UWB雷达数据的HAR框架，通过两种新型降维方法（CPE-IPCA和PPSA）结合频繁模式挖掘与分类器，实现六类活动的高精度识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UWB雷达数据具有高维、噪声大、复杂和非线性等特点，在资源受限的实时系统中进行人体活动识别（HAR）仍具挑战，需要高效降维并保持高分类精度。

Method: 提出两种降维技术：一是CPE-IPCA，结合聚类、多项式特征扩展与增量PCA，仅用50个成分即可保留100%方差；二是PPSA，在PCA后标准化数据，用80个成分保留99.1%方差。随后用Apriori和FP-Growth挖掘频繁模式，并用随机森林和向量空间模型（VSM）进行分类。

Result: 随机森林在CPE-IPCA上达到100%准确率，在PPSA上达99%；VSM在PPSA上精确率、召回率和F1均为100%，在CPE-IPCA上接近完美（精确率1.00，召回率0.98-1.00，F1为0.99-1.00）。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了快速、可解释且鲁棒的HAR系统，适用于医疗健康、辅助生活和智能环境等场景。

Abstract: Recent advances in sensor technology have enabled more effective human activity recognition (HAR), particularly in real-time systems with limited computational resources. However, Ultra-Wideband (UWB) radar data remain challenging due to high dimensionality, noise, complexity, and nonlinear characteristics. This research proposes a framework to efficiently reduce data size, uncover significant patterns, and classify six activity types (Standff, Liedown, Noactivity, Sit, Stand, and Walk) from UWB signals with high accuracy. Two novel dimensionality reduction techniques are introduced in this paper. The first, Clustered Polynomial Expansion with Incremental PCA (CPE-IPCA), combines clustering and polynomial feature expansion with Incremental PCA, preserving 100% of the variance in only 50 components. The second, Post-PCA Standardization Approach (PPSA), standardizes data after PCA and retains 99.1% of the variance in 80 components, achieving superior compression and computational efficiency compared to conventional nonlinear methods. Frequent patterns are identified using Apriori and FP-Growth, which are then classified with Random Forest and a Vector Space Model (VSM). The framework achieves 100% accuracy with Random Forest on CPE-IPCA and 99% on PPSA, while VSM attains 100% precision, recall, and F1 on PPSA and near-perfect performance on CPE-IPCA (precision 1.00, recall 0.98-1.00, F1 0.99-1.00), demonstrating a fast, interpretable, and robust HAR system suitable for healthcare, assisted living, and smart environments.

</details>


### [201] [Representation Learning for Exact Preimages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32018)
*Konstantin Hess,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRIO 通过径向变换的可逆分解，使前向模型保持表达力的同时，让前像具有显式几何表示，从而兼顾表达性预测、精确前像恢复与前像上的可解全局优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学与工程任务需要逆向推理：给定性能或安全水平，刻画满足该目标的全部输入（前像）并在其上优化。但表达力强的神经网络预测器缺乏显式前像表示，恢复与优化代价高，形成表达性、前像近似精度与下游可解性之间的三重矛盾。

Method: 提出 TRIO 框架，核心是前像因子化：前向模型通过非线性径向变换（含神经网络）保持表达力；在求逆时每个变换简化为单一标量半径，得到简单的几何水平集，形成可复用的显式几何表示；对线性目标给出闭式全局解；并证明通用逼近定理。

Result: 证明 TRIO 能任意逼近任意连续前向映射及其全部可能不连通、非凸的前像；对线性目标可得到闭式全局最优解。

Conclusion: TRIO 在设计上同时实现了表达性前向建模、精确前像恢复与前像上的可解全局下游优化，解决了三向挑战。

Abstract: Modern neural predictors can model highly nonlinear maps, but many scientific and engineering tasks require reasoning in the opposite direction: given a performance or safety level, the goal is to characterize the preimage, that is, the complete set of inputs which meet the desired target level and optimize over that set. For expressive neural predictors, however, such preimages typically have no explicit representation and are expensive to recover or optimize over. This creates a fundamental three-way challenge between expressive forward prediction, accurate preimage approximation, and tractable optimization over the preimage for downstream tasks. We introduce TRIO (tractable representations for preimage learning and inverse optimization), a framework for learning representations that make these objectives compatible by construction. Our key contribution is a preimage factorization: the forward model remains expressive through nonlinear radial transformations (including neural networks), while, under inversion, each transformation reduces to a single scalar radius, which yields simple geometric level sets. This yields an explicit geometric representation that is reusable for downstream optimization over the preimage, and, for linear objectives, we show that this admits a closed-form global solution. We finally prove a universal approximation theorem which shows that TRIO can approximate any continuous forward map and its entire family of potentially disconnected, nonconvex preimages arbitrarily well. Hence, TRIO combines expressive forward modeling, exact preimage recovery, and tractable global downstream optimization over preimages by design.

</details>


### [202] [Interactive Distributionally Robust Multi-Agent Learning with General Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32048)
*Debamita Ghosh,George K. Atia,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RoMEX-φ，一个面向一般和分布鲁棒马尔可夫博弈（DRMG）的无模型框架，结合基于均衡的探索与对偶拟合学习，利用鲁棒多智能体解耦系数（robust MADC）刻画探索复杂度，实现亚线性鲁棒遗憾保证，并在实验中验证了其对转移偏移的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体强化学习中，模型错配是根本性挑战，策略交互会放大转移不确定性。分布鲁棒马尔可夫博弈（DRMG）虽提供理论框架，但现有方法常依赖限制性假设，且难以扩展到大规模状态与联合动作空间。

Method: 研究带通用函数逼近和φ-散度不确定集的一般和DRMG在线学习。提出无模型框架RoMEX-φ，将基于均衡的探索与对偶拟合学习相结合；通过对鲁棒多智能体Bellman算子的函数对偶表示，用中心化经验鲁棒差异从名义交互数据中实现可处理的最坏情况价值估计；并引入robust MADC刻画策略交互与对抗转移不确定性带来的内在探索复杂度。

Result: 建立了由robust MADC支配的亚线性鲁棒遗憾保证，而非显式依赖状态与联合动作空间规模，以内在函数类复杂度替代表格化依赖。在可扩展的一般和DRMG上的数值实验（总变差不确定性）表明，RoMEX-φ比非鲁棒对应方法对转移偏移更具韧性，同时与精确表格鲁棒基线相比仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 该工作为带通用函数逼近的分布鲁棒多智能体强化学习提供了一个可扩展框架。

Abstract: Model misspecification poses a fundamental challenge in multi-agent reinforcement learning, where transition uncertainty can be amplified by strategic interactions among agents. Distributionally robust Markov games (DRMGs) provide a principled framework for addressing such uncertainty, yet existing methods often rely on restrictive assumptions or scale poorly to large state and joint action spaces. We study online learning in general-sum DRMGs with general function approximation and $φ$-divergence uncertainty sets. We propose RoMEX-$φ$, a model-free framework that integrates equilibrium-based exploration with dual fitted learning. Through a functional dual representation of the robust multi-agent Bellman operator, RoMEX-$φ$ enables tractable worst-case value estimation from nominal interaction data using a centered empirical robust discrepancy. We introduce the robust Multi-Agent Decoupling Coefficient (robust MADC) to characterize the intrinsic exploration complexity arising from strategic interactions and adversarial transition uncertainty. We establish sublinear robust regret guarantees governed by the robust MADC rather than explicitly by the state and joint action space sizes, replacing tabular dependence with intrinsic function-class complexity. Numerical experiments on a scalable general-sum DRMG under total variation uncertainty show that RoMEX-$φ$ is substantially more resilient to transition shifts than its non-robust counterpart while remaining competitive with an exact tabular robust baseline. Our results provide a scalable framework for distributionally robust multi-agent reinforcement learning with general function approximation.

</details>


### [203] [Graph Forward Distribution Matching for Molecular Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32056)
*Yihan Zhu,Yuhan Liu,Brett Savoie,Tengfei Luo,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphFDM提出一种基于前向过程的在线强化学习范式，用于图扩散模型的分子逆设计，实现多属性精确控制且保持高化学有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法通过反向采样将图扩散模型微调视为序列决策，用单一终端奖励优化数百个耦合决策，导致训练不稳定、有效性崩塌、属性收益有限。目标是在不牺牲化学有效性的前提下实现多属性精确控制。

Method: GraphFDM在扩散的前向过程中进行优化，利用有效生成样本定义奖励倾斜的目标分布，对图尺寸和分子结构针对每个属性条件联合优化，将强化信号融入监督学习而无需存储反向轨迹。理论推导了唯一最优目标，证明了逐条件改进保证，并揭示标准图扩散固定图尺寸先验导致不可消除的匹配差距。

Result: 在多条件聚合物和小分子生成任务中，GraphFDM在每个目标属性上取得最低MAE，相对最强基线降低最多53.0%，化学有效性高于0.99，并可泛化到分布外属性组合。

Conclusion: GraphFDM通过前向分布匹配范式有效解决了图扩散逆设计中的稳定性与有效性难题，实现了多属性精确控制与良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Achieving precise control over multiple properties without sacrificing chemical validity remains a central challenge in molecular inverse design. Existing reinforcement learning (RL) methods fine-tune graph diffusion models by treating **reverse** sampling as a sequential policy, using a single terminal reward to optimize hundreds of coupled decisions. They often suffer from instability, validity collapse, and limited property gains. We introduce GraphFDM (Graph Forward Distribution Matching), a new online RL paradigm for graph diffusion that performs optimization through the **forward** process. GraphFDM uses valid generations to define a reward-tilted target distribution jointly optimized over graph size and molecular structure for each property condition, incorporating reinforcement signals into supervised learning without storing reverse trajectories. We derive the unique optimal target, prove a condition-wise improvement guarantee, and show that the fixed graph-size prior of standard graph diffusion leaves an irreducible matching gap. In multi-conditional polymer and small-molecule generation, GraphFDM achieves the lowest MAE on every target property, with reductions of up to 53.0\% relative to the strongest baselines and chemical validity above 0.99. It further generalizes to out-of-distribution property combinations.

</details>


### [204] [Depth Laws for the Precision Floor of Trained Neural Networks: Amplification, Residual Scaling, and a Quantization-Aware Training Paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32060)
*Ahmad S. Tarawneh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"精度下限"（precision floor）概念，用一阶理论将其与预测放大G联系起来（η_c=Λ/G），并在MLP、CNN、ViT及9个预训练语言模型上验证深度缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究网络在精度崩塌前需要多少比特，以及这一需求如何随深度增长，旨在建立量化精度与网络深度之间的定量理论关系。

Method: 通过一阶理论推导精度下限η_c=Λ/G，其中Λ在训练架构中约为1.45±14%，G²随深度线性增长且增长速率与残差分支尺度的平方成正比；在PTQ和QAT下测试MLP、CNN、ViT及9个预训练语言模型，并用13个训练架构和不同层数GPT-2进行验证。

Result: 深度指数的预测等式α_PTQ=ρ在13个架构中的12个以及12至48层GPT-2上以95%区间成立；用1/√D缩放残差分支和预归一化可消除深度惩罚，b_c=(α/γ)log₂D+C；QAT存在悖论：噪声感知训练使浅层网络可容忍噪声约翻倍，但增益随深度衰减，深度规律变陡（α_QAT/α_PTQ=1.45-1.47）。

Conclusion: 决策边界、跨层误差抵消和重尾分布均不是精度下限的决定因素，而预测放大G是核心量，由此建立了比特需求随深度增长的理论框架。

Abstract: How many bits does a network need before its accuracy collapses, and how does this grow with depth? We study the precision floor, the perturbation level or bit-width at which accuracy falls halfway to chance, in MLPs, CNNs, Vision Transformers and nine pretrained language models, under post-training quantization (PTQ) and quantization- or noise-aware training (QAT). (i) A first-order theory sets the floor through one full-precision quantity, the predictive amplification $G$: $η_c=Λ/G$, and $G^2$ grows linearly in depth at a rate proportional to the squared residual branch scale. (ii) The predicted equality $α_{PTQ}=ρ$ of depth exponents holds within 95% intervals in twelve of thirteen trained architectures and in GPT-2 from 12 to 48 layers, with $Λ=1.45\pm14\%$ across trained architectures. (iii) Residual branches scaled by $1/\sqrt{D}$ and pre-normalisation remove the depth penalty, and each quantizer turns noise into bits at a rate fixed by its step rule, giving $b_c=(α/γ)\log_2 D+C$. (iv) A QAT paradox: noise-aware training roughly doubles the tolerable noise of shallow networks, but the gain decays with depth, so the depth law steepens ($α_{QAT}/α_{PTQ}=1.45$-$1.47$ on two datasets, ten seeds each). Decision margins, cross-layer error cancellation and heavy tails do not set the floor.

</details>


### [205] [When Should a Human Take Back Control? Optimal Delegation under Turbulent AI Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32083)
*Haoze Yan,Julien Roze,Ved Upadhyay,Unal Tatar,Thibaut Mastrolia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出连续时间框架，用Hawkes自激过程建模AI风险的聚集与级联，将人类监督与委托决策建模为随机控制问题，并提出Hawkes-PPO算法求自适应监督策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署AI系统需决定何时委托、何时让人类介入以监控和缓解AI运行带来的风险。当失败聚集时决策尤为困难：幻觉或有害输出会触发更多错误，形成风险高发时段。现有监督与委托方法虽以历史为条件，但未同时建模事件聚集、监督努力对聚集的抑制，以及与委托决策的联合作用。

Method: 以自激（Hawkes）动态刻画风险事件如何提高后续事件发生的可能性，使事件时序与历史成为决策核心。构建随机控制问题，联合人类行动、监控努力，以及在"人机协作运行"与"完全AI委托"之间的切换，在操作收益、监督成本、AI失败级联及诱发不确定性之间权衡。人的参与被视为风险管理的内生组成部分，策略同时决定何时需要监督及分配多少努力。研究一种松弛的切换形式，并提出Hawkes-PPO——使用观测事件时间指数滤波库的策略梯度方法。

Result: 在合成环境中，该方法取得比固定制度（始终委托或始终监督）更高的风险调整目标，并接近近似全信息oracle。数值模拟展示了级联风险如何影响干预与委托决策。

Conclusion: 将强化学习与AI系统的自适应人类监督相连接，说明切换策略与Hawkes-PPO算法能沿时间高效监控项目，减少风险突发事件的频率与成本。

Abstract: Deploying AI systems requires deciding when to delegate tasks and when humans should intervene to monitor and mitigate risk induced by AI operations. These decisions are challenging when failures cluster: a hallucination or harmful output can trigger further errors, creating periods of elevated risk. We introduce a continuous-time framework for learning adaptive human oversight under such turbulent AI risk. Existing oversight and delegation formulations condition on history but do not model incident clustering, or its suppression by supervision effort, jointly with the delegation decision and this study fixes this gap. The self-exciting dynamics capture how risk events increase the likelihood of subsequent events, making their timing and history central to decision-making. We formulate a stochastic control problem combining human actions, monitoring effort, and switching between human-AI-assisted operation and full AI delegation, balancing operational rewards against oversight costs, and cascading AI-failures and induced uncertainty. Human participation is an endogenous component of risk management: the policy determines both when oversight is needed and how much effort to allocate. We study a relaxed switching formulation and propose Hawkes-PPO, a policy-gradient method that uses a bank of exponential filters of observed incident times. In a synthetic environment it attains a higher risk-adjusted objective than either fixed regime and approaches an approximate full-information oracle. We illustrate our results with numerical simulations by examining how cascade risks influence intervention and delegation, connecting reinforcement learning with adaptive human oversight of AI systems. In particular, we illustrate the benefit of our switching strategy and Hawkes-PPO algorithm to monitor the project efficiently along time, reducing turbulent risks occurrences and costs.

</details>


### [206] [Emergent One-Third Scaling Law as Attention Tries to Concentrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32100)
*Yizhou Liu,Sara Kangaslahti,Jeff Gore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出并验证：任何softmax在学到尖峰分布时，其logit幅度会以1/3幂律增长，并成为训练瓶颈，使损失以1/3幂律衰减；LLM的损失缩放符合该预测，且注意力头（而非语言建模头）是驱动1/3损失缩放的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经缩放定律（训练越久性能越好，呈幂律关系）是当今大语言模型的核心，但其起源仍存争议。已有工作提出幂律可能源自单个softmax头学习尖峰分布时的强非线性，但LLM中存在多个softmax，多个softmax下的行为尚不清楚，因此需要研究多softmax情形下缩放定律的来源与瓶颈。

Method: 通过toy model进行理论分析，考察任意位置的softmax在学习尖峰分布时logit幅度的增长规律；并在真实LLM中确认多个softmax是否学习到尖峰分布，比较LLM损失缩放与预测指数；同时分析logit增长动力学以定位瓶颈所在（注意力头 vs 语言建模头）。

Result: toy model显示：任何学习尖峰分布的softmax，其logit幅度以指数1/3的幂律增长，并成为训练瓶颈，其损失贡献以相同指数1/3幂律衰减；只要至少一个softmax学到尖峰分布，整体损失即服从1/3缩放。LLM中许多softmax确实学到尖峰分布，LLM损失缩放与该1/3预测相符；logit增长动力学表明注意力头（而非语言建模头）是驱动1/3损失缩放的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 注意力机制试图聚焦于特定信息——这正是Transformer的核心——可能同时也是训练神经缩放定律的核心。该工作为LLM中1/3幂律损失缩放提供了统一解释，并将缩放定律的起源定位到softmax学习尖峰分布所导致的logit增长瓶颈。

Abstract: The neural scaling law relating longer training to better performance through a power law is central to today's large language models (LLMs), yet its origin remains debated. One recent proposal is that power laws can emerge from the strong non-linearity of a single softmax head learning peaked distributions. What happens with multiple softmax functions, as in LLMs, is unclear. Here, we show through toy models that any softmax learning peaked distributions, regardless of its position in the model, can develop logit magnitudes that grow in a power law with exponent $1/3$, becoming a training bottleneck whose loss contribution decays as a power law with the same exponent $1/3$. The overall loss therefore obeys $1/3$ scaling whenever at least one softmax learns peaked distributions. We confirm that many softmax functions in LLMs learn peaked distributions and that LLM loss scaling matches this $1/3$ prediction. Moreover, logit growth dynamics reveal that attention heads, rather than the language modeling head, are the bottleneck likely driving the $1/3$ loss scaling in LLMs. Attention trying to concentrate on specific information, which is the heart of Transformers, may therefore also be the heart of the neural scaling law of training.

</details>


### [207] [LLM Unlearning Evaluation with TRIAGE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32103)
*Danial Ataee,Peter Triantafillou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TRIAGE 评估框架，从模型内部（Fisher 信息与 Hessian 对角近似）刻画机器遗忘对参数敏感度、局部曲率及语义相邻知识的影响，并对 12 种遗忘方法、4 个模型、3 个基准进行分类分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评估主要依赖行为基准，只能判断模型是否“看起来遗忘了”，无法说明遗忘如何改变模型内部结构、以及是否波及语义相关的保留知识。

Method: TRIAGE 使用 Fisher 信息与 Hessian 的对角近似度量参数敏感度和局部曲率变化；采用 Forget / Adjacent-Retain / Generic-Retain 三分区量化语义相关知识间的“邻接差距”；再依据变化的幅度与分布，将算法更新分为 no-op、partially localized、collateral dominant、globally destructive 四类。

Result: 在 WMDP、TOFU、MUSE 三个基准上对 12 种遗忘方法和 4 个语言模型评估发现：行为遗忘效果相近的方法，其内部变化和附带损伤模式可能差异显著；这些内部特征还随模型和基准不同而变化，说明遗忘效果并非仅由算法决定。

Conclusion: TRIAGE 可作为现有遗忘基准的补充，从模型内部视角揭示遗忘如何重塑参数空间并影响保留知识，为行为评估提供解释性支持。

Abstract: Large language models can memorize private or harmful information, motivating machine unlearning methods that remove targeted knowledge while preserving other capabilities. However, existing evaluations rely primarily on behavioral benchmarks, which assess \emph{whether} a model appears to forget but provide limited insight into \emph{how} unlearning changes the model or affects related knowledge. We introduce \textit{TRIAGE} (\textit{Tripartite Representation-internal Introspection for Adjacency Gap Evaluation}), a benchmark-agnostic evaluation framework for characterizing these changes. TRIAGE uses diagonal approximations of the Fisher information and Hessian to measure changes in parameter sensitivity and local curvature, and utilizes a Forget / \emph{Adjacent-Retain} / \emph{Generic-Retain} partition to quantify an \emph{adjacency gap} in semantically related knowledge. Based on the magnitude and distribution of these changes, TRIAGE further classifies each algorithm's update as \emph{no-op}, \emph{partially localized}, \emph{collateral dominant}, or \emph{globally destructive}. Across 12 unlearning methods, four language models, and the WMDP, TOFU, and MUSE benchmarks, we find that methods with similar behavioral forgetting can produce substantially different internal changes and patterns of collateral damage. These signatures also vary across models and benchmarks, indicating that the effects of unlearning are not determined solely by the unlearning algorithm. TRIAGE can be applied alongside existing unlearning benchmarks to complement behavioral evaluation with a model-internal view of how unlearning reshapes the model's parameter space and affects retained knowledge.

</details>


### [208] [An Attention-Driven Heterogeneous GNN Model for Credit Card Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32106)
*Kathiresan Jayabalan,Sethuraman Radhakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于SMOTE-Tomek数据平衡技术与异构神经网络（HGNN）的信用卡欺诈检测框架，在Kaggle数据集上取得了99.97%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无现金经济转型使信用卡成为数字交易的核心，但信用卡欺诈风险随之上升。信用卡欺诈检测面临模式不断变化、数据高度不平衡以及需同时识别合法与欺诈交易等难题，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: 采用Kaggle信用卡欺诈检测数据集，针对数据高度不平衡问题，使用SMOTE-Tomek技术进行数据平衡；随后使用异构神经网络（HGNN）模型对平衡后的数据分类。HGNN通过异构图架构表示不同交易，并利用基于注意力机制的消息传递技术捕捉复杂关系、时间因素与用户行为特征。

Result: HGNN模型达到99.97%准确率、99.48% F1分数、99.15%精确率和98.97%召回率。SMOTE-Tomek的集成进一步提升了欺诈交易识别能力，同时降低了假阳性率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明该模型有效，可应用于真实世界的信用卡欺诈检测场景。

Abstract: The global transition to a cashless economy has placed credit cards as the key element of digital transactions, acclaimed for their easy use, speed, and acceptance in most places. However, the growing dependence on this payment method has led to an escalation of the risks associated with credit card (CC) fraud. Detecting this type of fraud is a difficult task because the patterns are constantly changing, there is a data imbalance, and it is necessary to identify the legitimate transactions and the fraud ones at the same time. This study addresses this challenge by proposing a credit card fraud detection (CCFD) framework using a data balancing technique and a deep learning (DL) model. The proposed fraud detection model is trained and evaluated by collecting the dataset called Credit Card Fraud Detection from the Kaggle repository. As the dataset is highly imbalanced, we utilized the Synthetic Minority Oversampling Technique (SMOTE)-Tomek technique to balance the dataset. Further, the balanced dataset is classified using the Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network (HGNN) model. The HGNN model represent various transactions using a heterogeneous graph architecture and by using an attention based message passing technique, it managed to consider the complex relationships, time factors, and user behavior. The integration of SMOTE-Tomek in the model further boosted its capacity to identify fraudulent transactions, while lowering the rate of false positives. The HGNN model attained a 99.97% accuracy, a 99.48% F1-score, a 99.15% precision, and a 98.97% recall. The findings indicates that this model is effective and can be applied to real-world CCFD scenarios.

</details>


### [209] [SAMBAR: Selective Anchoring via Method of Multipliers for Balanced Knowledge Acquisition and Retention in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32108)
*Aayushi Shrivastava,Xunlan Zhou,Hongrui Zhao,Ziyu Chen,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAMBAR，一种无需旧任务演示数据的持续学习方法，通过乘子法约束优化与选择性参数锚定，解决了VLA微调中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型需持续学习新任务，但朴素微调会导致灾难性遗忘，削弱通用能力。现有持续学习方法多依赖重放旧任务数据，而这些演示数据并不总是可得，因此需要一种不依赖重放的持续学习方案。
