A random-feature kernel view explains why exact recovery of each hypervector is unnecessary: inference only needs to preserve the class evidence that determines the prediction. Across ten datasets, SupHDC achieves 1.39x analytical speedup with no average accuracy loss, and up to 2.08x speedup with only a 2.67 percentage-point mean accuracy loss. On a Raspberry Pi~5, it delivers 2.01x measured wall-clock speedup with a 2.26 percentage-point loss in mean prediction accuracy. SupHDC shows that high-dimensional redundancy can be used not only for robustness, but also as capacity for shared inference.

</details>


### [244] [Not All Errors Matter: Decision-Relevant Prediction Error Predicts Planning Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32322)
*Linhao Wang,Yiyan Fan,Dongjin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"决策相关预测误差"（DRPE），指出总预测误差不能可靠反映世界模型的规划能力，误差在不同状态维度上的分配才是关键，并给出等误差评估协议与理论条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通常以预测误差作为训练与评估目标，隐含假设"预测越准，决策越好"。本文证明该假设会失效：总误差相近的模型，若误差落在不同状态维度上，规划表现可差异巨大，因此需要一个与决策相关的误差度量。

Method: 提出决策相关预测误差（DRPE），即只在影响决策的状态维度上度量预测误差；设计等误差（iso-error）评估协议，在保持总误差不变的前提下改变误差分配；在具有已知状态相关性、配备标准规划器的因子化网格世界中进行实验，评估55个受控与学习模型（不同误差水平与分配），并形式化DRPE正确排序模型而总误差无法排序的充分条件。

Result: 总预测误差与规划成功率仅弱相关（Spearman ρ=-0.25），而DRPE预测力强（ρ=-0.84；受控模型族内为-0.98）。总误差仅相差1%的模型，规划成功率可相差60个百分点（97% vs 37%）。相关误差依赖任务，同一总误差下模型排名会跨任务反转。更深层的想象会放大决策相关误差，学习模型在稀有但决策关键的事件上表现出系统性偏差。

Conclusion: 评估世界模型不能只看总预测误差，应关注决策相关状态维度上的误差；DRPE能更准确地预测规划表现，并给出了其有效性的理论条件。

Abstract: World models are typically trained and evaluated by prediction error, assuming that more accurate predictions lead to better decisions. We show that this assumption can fail because models with similar total error can differ substantially in planning performance when their errors occur on different state dimensions. We introduce Decision-Relevant Prediction Error (DRPE), which measures prediction error on the state dimensions that affect decisions. We also develop an iso-error evaluation protocol that varies error allocation while keeping total error fixed. In a factored gridworld with known state relevance and a standardized planner, we evaluate 55 controlled and learned models across different error levels and allocations. Total prediction error is weakly related to planning success (Spearman $ρ=-0.25$), whereas DRPE is strongly predictive ($ρ=-0.84$; $-0.98$ within the controlled family). Models with only a 1\% difference in total error can differ by 60 percentage points in planning success (97\% vs 37\%). The relevant error also depends on the task, with model rankings reversing across tasks at the same total error. Deeper imagination further amplifies decision-relevant errors, while learned models exhibit systematic bias on rare but decision-critical events. We formalize sufficient conditions under which DRPE correctly ranks models and total prediction error cannot.

</details>


### [245] [Active Feature Acquisition With Incomplete Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32325)
*Reza Rezvan,Valter Schütz,Han Wu,Linus Aronsson,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究训练数据不完整情况下的主动特征获取（AFA-ITD），分析缺失数据对特征获取价值的影响并提出三种学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFA需权衡特征成本与预测性能，但已有工作大多假设训练数据完整，这一假设在实践中常被违背。

Method: 在MCAR假设下分析一步与多步获取价值变化，并比较三种从不完整数据中学习的方法：aliasing、filtering和生成式恢复（generative restoration）。

Result: 一步获取值不变，多步获取值可能下降；filtering所需训练样本量随维度指数增长，生成式恢复则随获取预算指数增长。实验显示缺失主要损害利用多步获取的方法，生成式恢复在许多实验中可恢复性能。

Conclusion: 训练数据缺失对AFA的影响集中在多步获取，生成式恢复是有效的应对手段。

Abstract: In many prediction tasks, acquiring all features can be a prohibitively expensive or outright impossible task. Further, in many cases a static subset of features may not be enough to solve the problem sufficiently across various instances. Active Feature Acquisition (AFA) addresses these problems by formalizing the trade-off between feature cost and predictive performance during sequential feature selection. However, prior AFA work largely assumes access to complete training data, an assumption that is often violated in practice. Here we study AFA with Incomplete Training Data (AFA-ITD), showing that under missing completely at random (MCAR) data, one-step acquisition values remain unchanged, whereas multi-step values can decrease. We analyze three approaches to learning from incomplete data: aliasing, filtering, and generative restoration. We show that filtering can require a number of training instances scaling exponentially with the dimension, whereas generative restoration scales exponentially with the acquisition budget. We empirically test our theory on a controlled experiment and across common AFA datasets and find that missingness mainly damages methods that exploit multi-step acquisitions and that generative restoration is able to recover lost performance in many experiments. Code is available at https://github.com/Linusaronsson/AFA-Benchmark/tree/missing-data.

</details>


### [246] [Continual Data Unlearning in Diffusion Models via Transition-based Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32328)
*Sunbeom Jeong,Sehwan Kim,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出持续数据遗忘框架，利用已完成删除的方向性参考约束后续更新，在删除持久性与生成效用间取得更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型数据遗忘需移除特定训练样本影响而不抑制其代表的概念。但删除请求顺序到来时，新请求的更新会损害生成效用并破坏先前删除效果。

Method: 将已完成删除转换作为方向性参考来正则化未来更新：记录同一固定噪声输入上去噪器响应在遗忘前后的变化；采用单侧惩罚，抑制沿记录方向相对于删除后参考的逆转，而不惩罚正交响应变化与超出参考的进展；维护固定容量代表性转换记录库，基于进展对参数更新的局部敏感度进行选择，使记录在惩罚失活时仍能保留。

Result: 实证评估表明，随请求累积，该框架在删除持久性与生成效用之间取得优于现有遗忘基线的平衡，且仅需少量转换内存。

Conclusion: 该持续数据遗忘框架通过方向性单侧惩罚与固定容量记录库，有效缓解顺序删除中的效用退化与遗忘破坏问题。

Abstract: Data unlearning in diffusion models aims to remove the influence of specific training examples without suppressing the broader concepts they represent. However, when deletion requests arrive sequentially, updates for new requests can degrade generative utility and undermine earlier deletions. We propose a continual data unlearning framework that uses completed deletion transitions as directional references to regularize future updates. For each request, we record changes in denoiser responses on the same fixed noisy inputs before and after unlearning. Rather than matching full post-deletion responses, we apply a one-sided penalty that discourages reversal along the recorded directions relative to the post-deletion references, while leaving orthogonal response changes and progress beyond these references unpenalized. To keep storage independent of the number of requests, we maintain a fixed-capacity bank of representative transition records. Records are selected based on the local sensitivity of progress along their recorded directions to parameter updates, allowing them to be retained even when their penalties are inactive. Empirical evaluations show that the proposed framework achieves a better balance between deletion persistence and generative utility than existing unlearning baselines as requests accumulate, using only a small transition memory.

</details>


### [247] [When the Merge Coefficient Stops Mattering: Proximity Regularized Merging for Continual LoRA Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32332)
*Yixuan Liu,Yuhao Sun,Sen Song,Jin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出邻近正则化合并（PRM），在顺序LoRA合并的任务向量训练阶段加入近端惩罚，使任务向量本身更"可合并"，从而在无排练持续学习中提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无排练持续学习使用参数高效适配器时，可视为一系列任务向量写入操作：每个新任务学习低秩适配器并合并进运行模型。作者认为现有方法只关注"写入多少"任务向量，而忽略了任务向量本身是否被训练成易于合并的形式。

Method: 提出PRM（Proximity Regularized Merging），对顺序LoRA合并做最小改动：在任务向量训练时加入近端惩罚，但不改变后续的写入规则。分析手段包括匹配前缀范数控制和近端强度扫描。

Result: 在Base->+Prox诊断中，PRM在多种写入规则、骨干网络和类增量设置下提升AAA；其固定系数变体与基于系数的强基线相当。机制上，近端训练缩小任务向量半径、降低Fisher加权干扰、拓宽系数平台，并揭示稳定性-可塑性权衡。

Conclusion: 顺序LoRA合并的有效性不仅取决于任务向量写入的多少，还取决于任务向量本身是否被训练为可合并的，PRM以极小改动实现了这一目标。

Abstract: Rehearsal-free continual learning with parameter-efficient adapters can be cast as a sequence of task-vector write-in operations: for each new task, a low-rank adapter is learned and merged into a running model. We propose Proximity Regularized Merging (PRM), a minimal modification to sequential LoRA merging that adds a proximal penalty during task-vector training without changing the subsequent write-in rule. PRM acts as a robust task-vector regularizer: in the reported Base->+Prox diagnostics, it improves AAA across multiple write-in rules, backbones, and class-incremental settings, while its fixed-coefficient variant remains competitive with strong coefficient-based baselines. Mechanistically, matched-prefix norm controls and proximal-strength sweeps show that proximal training shrinks the task-vector radius, lowers Fisher-weighted interference, broadens the coefficient plateau, and exposes a stability-plasticity trade-off. Together, these results suggest that the effectiveness of sequential LoRA merging depends not only on how much of a task vector is written in, but also on whether the task vector itself has been trained to be mergeable.

</details>


### [248] [A Comparative Analysis of Attention versus State-Space Models for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32341)
*Enes Arda,Semih Cayci,Atilla Eryilmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"信念几何"统一分析框架，从理论上比较注意力与状态空间模型的表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer与SSM的比较多停留在实证层面，现有理论分析往往针对特定任务或特定架构，缺乏统一、通用的分析工具。

Method: 以广义的上下文线性回归为出发点，用累积贝叶斯遗憾作为度量，抽象出三类能力——证据组装、信念维护、内容寻址，并设计三种案例分别隔离这些能力。

Result: 信念维护上SSM在平稳聚合核下达到最优遗憾；位置组装上SSM具记忆优势；内容寻址上softmax注意力相对sigmoid选择性SSM有指数级宽度优势。基于LLaMA型Transformer与Mamba-2的实验表明结论可超出可解析类与线性回归测试床。

Conclusion: "信念几何"为比较注意力与SSM提供统一理论视角，明确各自优势所在，并被实证验证具有更广泛适用性。

Abstract: Transformers and state-space models (SSMs) are two prominent sequential learning architectures, yet their comparison remains largely empirical and existing theoretical analyses are typically task-specific or architecturally restricted. In this paper, we develop belief geometry, a unified analytical framework for comparing the representational capabilities of broad classes of attention and SSMs. Starting from a generalized formulation of in-context linear regression and using cumulative Bayes regret as our measure, we abstract three capabilities required by many sequential learning problems in our belief geometry: evidence assembly, belief maintenance, and addressing. We then study three cases of our formulation that isolate these capabilities and yield sharp architectural lessons: For belief maintenance, SSMs attain the optimal regret over stationary aggregation kernels; for positional assembly, SSMs have a memory advantage; and for content addressing, softmax attention has an exponential width advantage over sigmoid-selective SSMs. Experiments with LLaMA-type Transformers and Mamba-2 show that these architectural insights extend beyond our analytically tractable classes and linear-regression testbed.

</details>


### [249] [Analog-Friendly Predictive Coding without Activation Derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32350)
*Francesco Innocenti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"激活匹配Bregman预测编码"，用与激活函数匹配的Bregman散度替代标准平方误差能量，消除对激活函数导数的需求，并结合镜像下降实现局部推断与学习规则，实验表明其性能与标准PC及BP相当，同时保留PC的学习动力学特性，更适合模拟硬件实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码（PC）是反向传播（BP）的局部、基于能量的替代方案，其迭代推断动力学适合模拟硬件实现。但标准非线性PC在推断和学习过程中都需评估激活函数的导数，这在物理实现上困难。作者希望找到一种更契合模拟硬件的非线性PC形式，同时保留其关键计算性质。

Method: 提出激活匹配Bregman PC：用与激活函数相匹配的Bregman散度替换标准平方误差能量，从而消除激活函数导数；结合镜像下降进行推断，得到仅需加权求和、局部预测误差、状态积分和激活函数的局部推断与学习规则。

Result: 数字实验中，Bregman PC在分类和生成任务上表现与标准PC和BP相当，同时保留了PC特有的学习动力学，并在稳定的大模型参数化下收敛到BP。

Conclusion: 该工作为非线性PC提供了一种更利于模拟硬件实现的表述，同时保留了其核心计算性质。

Abstract: Predictive coding (PC) is a local, energy-based alternative to backpropagation (BP) whose iterative inference dynamics make it attractive for implementation on analog hardware. However, standard nonlinear PC requires evaluating the derivative of the activation function during both inference and learning, which can be difficult to realise physically. Here, we introduce \textit{activation-matched Bregman PC}, replacing standard squared-error energies with Bregman divergences matched to the activation function. This formulation eliminates activation derivatives and, when combined with inference via mirror descent, yields local inference and learning rules requiring only weighted sums, local prediction errors, state integration, and the activation function. In digital experiments, Bregman PC performs comparably to standard PC and BP on classification and generative tasks, while preserving characteristic learning dynamics of PC and its convergence to BP under stable large-model parameterisations. These results provide a more analog-friendly formulation of nonlinear PC while retaining its key computational properties.

</details>


### [250] [Editable Map-Conditioned Trajectory Generation for Human Mobility Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32360)
*Takayuki Mizuno,Shouji Fujimoto,Mikito Hiruki,Atushi Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"地图条件自回归人类移动生成"任务，用道路栅格作为可编辑条件，在网格局部词表下实现无需重训即可响应地图编辑的移动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础设施干预模拟需要能直接响应地图编辑的移动生成器，但许多数据驱动生成器未将地图作为可编辑条件暴露出来，限制了干预场景下的仿真能力。

Method: 将任务形式化为地图条件自回归生成：道路栅格条件化一个解码器，按一分钟间隔输出31.25米网格单元token，网格局部词表支持留出与本地编辑地图而无需重训或改词表。基于日本石川县874个网格、87,400条手机轨迹从零训练，留出219个网格；实例化ResNet-50视觉前缀与ViT交叉注意力两种配置。

Result: 以正确地图与同划分内打乱地图生成对比留出真实轨迹：在110网格测试集上，ResNet-50配置在60%网格上正确地图生成更接近真实（Hausdorff能量距离，p=0.021），DTW方向一致但不显著（57%，p=0.074）；正确地图密度相关性0.38，打乱地图仅0.01。ViT配置轨迹级敏感性较弱、密度增益较小。示例性移除桥梁的编辑可在不重训下改变生成轨迹。

Conclusion: 结果支持可编辑地图人类移动仿真的可行性。

Abstract: Geospatial simulation of infrastructure interventions requires mobility generators that respond directly to edited maps, yet many data-driven generators do not expose the map as an editable condition. We formulate this task as map-conditioned autoregressive generation of human mobility: a road raster conditions a decoder that emits nominal 31.25 m mesh-cell tokens at one-minute intervals. The mesh-local vocabulary supports held-out and locally edited maps without retraining or vocabulary changes. We instantiate a ResNet-50 visual-prefix configuration and a Vision Transformer (ViT) cross-attention configuration, trained from scratch on 87,400 smartphone-derived trajectories from 874 meshes in Ishikawa Prefecture, Japan; 219 meshes are held out. We evaluate map sensitivity by comparing correct-map and within-split shuffled-map generations with held-out real trajectories. On the 110-mesh test split, for the ResNet-50 configuration, correct-map generations are closer than shuffled-map generations on 60% of meshes under Hausdorff-based energy distance (p = 0.021), while DTW is directional but inconclusive (57%, p = 0.074); correlation with real density is 0.38 with the correct map versus 0.01 with shuffled maps. The ViT configuration shows weaker trajectory-level sensitivity and smaller density gains. An illustrative bridge-removal edit changes generated continuations without retraining. Together, these results support the feasibility of editable-map human-mobility simulation.

</details>


### [251] [Black-Box Auditing of Epistemic Reliability in Multi-Agent Debate Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32361)
*Derui Wang,Zewei Shi,Rayne Holland,Ruoxi Sun,Xingliang Yuan,Jason Xue,Liming Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究争论蒸馏中弱验证器的"认识可靠性退化"现象，并提出ER-Audit两阶段黑盒审计框架，以检测被聚合性能提升掩盖的隐藏任务退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 争论蒸馏通过多智能体争论记录适配较弱的验证器以提升其判断力，但在被监控任务上的增益无法保证其在相关未被监控任务上的可靠性。论文关注"认识可靠性退化"：适配在保持被监控性能的同时，降低了隐藏任务上对正确回答的支持。作者考虑一个对抗性辩论者，其在维护正确被监控回答的同时操纵辩论论据，并追问这种退化是否仅仅反映灾难性遗忘，以及标准评估能否检测到它。

Method: 论文提出ER-Audit，一个两阶段黑盒审计框架，比较适配前后的冻结验证器检查点；并引入两个将受监控与隐藏任务提示配对、共享同一上下文的评估基准。ER-Audit通过评估语义有效的同义改述来搜索非退化的反例；若未找到，则使用独立同义改述进行序贯假设检验。作者推导了非退化概率的随时有效（anytime-valid）下置信界，从而在有限预算内实现数据依赖的停止规则。此外，作者建立了跨固定改述分布的公共下界，并将其扩展到与其混合分布总变差距离有界的分布上。

Result: 实验表明，更高的隐藏任务准确率可以与更多的非退化反例以及更低的非退化下界共存。这种分歧挑战了仅基于广泛灾难性遗忘的解释，并显示审计能够揭示被聚合性能增益所掩盖的选择性隐藏任务退化。

Conclusion: 该工作表明仅凭被监控任务的聚合性能无法保证验证器的认识可靠性，标准评估可能漏检选择性隐藏任务退化；ER-Audit提供了可检测此类退化的黑盒审计方法与理论保证，代码与基准已开源。

Abstract: Debate distillation adapts weaker verifiers using multi-agent debate transcripts to improve their judgement in subsequent debates, but gains on monitored tasks do not establish reliability on related unmonitored tasks. We study epistemic reliability degradation, in which adaptation preserves monitored performance while reducing support for correct responses on hidden tasks. We consider an adversarial debater that manipulates debate arguments while defending the correct monitored response, and ask whether the resulting degradation merely reflects catastrophic forgetting and whether standard evaluation can detect it. To address these questions, we propose ER-Audit, a two-stage black-box auditing framework that compares frozen verifier checkpoints before and after adaptation, and introduce two evaluation benchmarks pairing monitored and hidden task prompts grounded in shared contexts. ER-Audit searches for counterexamples to non-degradation by evaluating semantically valid paraphrases and, if none is found, uses independent paraphrases for sequential hypothesis testing. We derive anytime-valid lower confidence bounds on the non-degradation probability, allowing data-dependent stopping within a finite budget. We further establish a common lower bound across fixed paraphrase distributions and extend it to distributions within a bounded total variation distance of their mixtures. Our experiments show that higher hidden-task accuracy can coexist with more counterexamples to non-degradation and lower non-degradation bounds. This divergence challenges explanations based solely on broad catastrophic forgetting and shows that auditing can uncover selective hidden-task degradation concealed by aggregate performance gains. Our code and benchmarks are available at https://github.com/CSIRO-CQS-AI-alignment-Team/Epistemic-Reliability-Auditor.

</details>


### [252] [DiffPTS: Rethinking Diffusion ELBO for Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32363)
*Weiwei Ye,Dongyuan Li,Hangchen Liu,Haotong Jiang,Yoshihide Sekimoto,Renhe Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视Location-Scale Noise Model下的ELBO，提出统一框架DiffPTS，实现扩散概率预测中各组件端到端优化，在多个基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率时间序列预测需建模复杂时变分布。现有DDPM方法虽引入预训练均值与方差估计器应对分布漂移，但沿用标准DDPM框架，仅考虑ELBO的部分组件，将估计器训练视为独立回归任务，与变分推断框架割裂。

Method: 在Location-Scale Noise Model（LSNM）下重新推导ELBO，发现其自然导出估计器的高斯负对数似然目标，并定义了一个联合训练目标，从而统一了近期面向概率预测的扩散范式。基于此提出DiffPTS通用框架，支持ELBO内所有组件的端到端优化。

Result: 在多个基准上，DiffPTS持续优于近期模型，达到SOTA性能，相比现有基于扩散的方法平均降低CRPS/MSE超过14.53%/16.55%。

Conclusion: 通过LSNM下ELBO的原则性重构，DiffPTS实现了扩散概率预测的端到端训练，验证了统一变分推断视角的有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Probabilistic time series forecasting requires modeling and predicting complex and time-varying distributions. Recently, Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM)-based approaches have shown promise by equipping the dif- fusion process with pretrained mean and variance estimators to accommodate distributional shift. However, these methods typically follow the standard DDPM framework and consider only partial components of the evidence lower bound (ELBO), treating the training of estimators as designed regression tasks separate from the variational inference framework. To address this, we rethink the ELBO under the Location-Scale Noise Model (LSNM) and find that it naturally induces a Gaussian negative log likelihood objective for the estimators and inherently defines a joint training objective that unifies recent diffusion paradigms for probabilistic forecasting. Building on this principled ELBO reformulation, we propose Diff- PTS, a general framework that enables end-to-end optimization of all components within the ELBO. Across multiple benchmarks, DiffPTS consistently outperforms recent models, achieving state-of-the-art performance with an average CRPS/MSE reduction of over 14.53%/16.55% compared to existing diffusion-based methods. The code is available at https://github.com/wwy155/DiffPTS.

</details>


### [253] [Graph Memory: Spectral Associative Memory via Dirichlet Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32365)
*Zhaoyang Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出谱稠密联想记忆（spectral dense associative memory），将经典向量值联想记忆扩展到图结构数据，实现图的存储与检索并保持图几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稠密联想记忆主要处理向量值模式，而许多现代机器学习问题天然具有图结构，需要能处理关系模式、图扩散几何、社区结构与图归纳偏置的记忆机制，因此亟需面向图的联想记忆范式。

Method: 通过由Dirichlet能量与谱范数距离诱导的log-sum-exp能量进行检索，产生存储拉普拉斯矩阵的softmax加权平均，且结果仍为合法图拉普拉斯矩阵。

Result: 证明了指数级存储容量与指数衰减的检索误差；并为图谱量（特征值、特征空间、扩散算子）建立了理论保证；在合成图数据、真实航空网络、蛋白质构象数据与可穿戴传感器数据上展示了稳健的图检索并保持数据图几何。

Conclusion: 该框架为图结构数据提供了新的联想记忆范式，架起了稠密联想记忆与现代图学习及生成式AI之间的桥梁。

Abstract: Dense associative memories have traditionally focused on storing and retrieving vector-valued patterns. Many modern machine learning problems, however, are naturally graph-structured, requiring memory mechanisms for relational patterns, graph diffusion geometries, community structures, and graph-based inductive biases. We propose a spectral dense associative memory for storage and retrieval of graph data, extending the classical vector-valued memories. Retrieval is performed through a log-sum-exp energy induced by Dirichlet energy with spectral norm distances, producing a softmax-weighted average of the stored Laplacians that remains a valid graph Laplacian. We prove exponential storage capacity and exponentially decaying retrieval error. Beyond graph retrieval, we establish theoretical guarantees for spectral quantities central to graph learning, including eigenvalues, eigenspaces, and diffusion operators. Experiments on synthetic graph data, real-world airline network, protein conformation data and wearable sensor data demonstrate robust graph retrieval while preserving the graph geometry of the data. Our framework provides a new associative memory paradigm for graph-structured data and bridges dense associative memory with modern graph learning and generative AI.

</details>


### [254] [STRIDE: State-Transition Representation via Increment Dynamics and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32367)
*Yuchen Xiong,Siming Huang,Jianfeng Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STRIDE框架，将连续预测重构为状态转移预测，通过导数指纹定义状态并学习局部转移函数，在九个基准族上显著优于十四个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将连续时间序列预测问题重新表述为状态转移预测问题，利用转移统计量预测来实现连续概率预测，以提升长时程预测精度。

Method: 用导数指纹定义状态，学习状态转移的局部函数（qpairs）；通过尾随卷积窗口估计局部联合转移频率，其滞后差分构成高维增量轨迹；用POD提取坐标路径，并由带记忆的稀疏动力学联合预测；重组预测并用历史锚定反演恢复未来转移分布，采样状态路径选择局部函数生成连续预测。

Result: 三种子实验在九个基准族上与十四个基线比较；五个独立马尔可夫与隐状态基线覆盖全部230个任务，其中220个完整全时程配对。系统加权后期Energy胜率在190个多步配对上为73.6%–78.5%，对DLinear在72个配对多步任务上为77.3%。在64条独立初始化Aizawa轨迹上，后期Energy相对四个匹配控制降低约24%–56%，四个预设对比均通过Holm校正。

Conclusion: 结果将转移统计量预测与连续概率预测相连接，在匹配的Aizawa研究中展现出显著的长时程预测增益。

Abstract: We introduce STRIDE (State-Transition Representation via Increment Dynamics and Evolution), which defines states through derivative fingerprints and learns local functions for state transitions (qpairs), recasting continuous forecasting as transition prediction. Trailing convolution windows estimate local joint-transition frequencies, whose lagged differences form a high-dimensional increment trajectory. Proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) gives coordinate paths, jointly forecast by sparse dynamics with memory. Recombining their forecasts and applying history-anchored inversion recovers future transition distributions; sampled state paths select local functions to generate continuous forecasts. Three-seed experiments compare STRIDE against fourteen baselines across nine benchmark families. Five independent Markov and hidden-state baselines cover all 230 evaluated tasks, with 220 complete whole-horizon pairs. Against these comparators, system-weighted late-Energy win fractions range from 73.6% to 78.5% on 190 multi-step pairs; against DLinear, the fraction is 77.3% on 72 paired multi-step tasks. Matched controls examine the intermediate representation. On 64 independently initialized Aizawa trajectories, late-Energy reductions against four matched controls range from approximately 24% to 56%, with all four prespecified contrasts passing Holm correction. These results connect transition-statistic prediction to continuous probabilistic forecasting, with substantial long-horizon gains in the matched Aizawa study.

</details>


### [255] [Measurement Boundaries in LLM Financial Agent Evaluation: Fixed-Tape Execution and Multi-Defect Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32379)
*Weicheng Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文通过金融智能体实验框架的两个研究，界定LLM智能体评测中执行性能比较与审计分数可支持的结论边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解读LLM智能体评测的执行性能与审计分数时，需要明确何种对照条件才能支撑相应结论；作者希望厘清执行比较究竟估计了什么，以及目标召回率能捕捉什么。

Method: 研究A在三个共享24天上涨阶段的合成设定上，比较理想化与压力执行下的独立运行，并将存储的响应带在两套执行目标下重放，用十个种子簇评估排名稳定性。研究B修正不完整的答案键，用严格多标签提示下匹配的零、一、二缺陷任务替换旧任务，并在六种审计器与来源组合中检验目标违规召回率。

Result: 研究A中解析出的决策路径仅在450对中的19.8%一致；在固定响应条件下，压力执行使总收益变化-0.0170（95%区间[-0.0230,-0.0117]），约为理想基线的10.4%，且十个种子簇无法分辨模型排名。研究B中，从一缺陷到二缺陷的目标违规召回率下降在六种组合中的五种为正（中位数0.267），三种通过Holm校正；但包含两个目标标签最多的审计器微精确率仅0.149，在98/100个零缺陷任务上仍给出发现，仅21/100次返回精确的双缺陷集合。

Conclusion: 两项研究针对不同限制：执行比较所估计的内容，以及目标召回率所捕捉的内容。总体表明固定条件与诊断性对照限定了单一分数所能支撑的论断范围。

Abstract: What controls are needed to interpret execution performance and audit scores in LLM agent evaluations? We study two limits on these interpretations in a financial agent harness. In Study~A, comparing independent runs under idealized and stressed execution on three synthetic settings that share one 24-day upward phase mixes the execution rule with fresh model responses and portfolio feedback: the parsed decision paths agree in only $19.8\%$ of $450$ pairs. Replaying each stored response tape through both execution destinations gives a narrower result. Conditional on those responses, stressed execution changes total return by $-0.0170$ (95\% interval $[-0.0230,-0.0117]$), or $10.4\%$ of the idealized baseline, and ten seed clusters do not resolve the model ranking. Study~B corrects an incomplete answer key and replaces legacy tasks with matched zero-, one-, and two-defect tasks under an explicit multi-label prompt. The drop in target violation recall from one to two defects is positive in five of six combinations of auditor and source (median $0.267$), with three surviving Holm correction. Yet the auditor that includes both target labels most often has micro-precision $0.149$, emits findings on $98/100$ zero-defect tasks, and returns the exact dual-defect set in only $21/100$ cases. Target recall by itself therefore gives a poor account of audit quality on this construction. The studies address different limits: what an execution comparison estimates, and what target recall captures. Together, they show how fixed conditions and diagnostic controls bound the claims a score can support.

</details>


### [256] [Attribution Without a Second Pass: Inline Per-Sample Gradient Provenance at ~1% Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32380)
*Amit Nautiyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Traceprop 通过在训练反向传播中内联记录投影的逐样本梯度，避免了后验数据归因方法的二次遍历，实现高效且可审计的归因存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实用数据归因方法（TRAK、LoGRA、EK-FAC）是后验的：训练后需对训练集进行第二次遍历以重算逐样本梯度，集成时每个检查点都要重复。这带来了高昂的计算开销。

Method: Traceprop 在训练反向传播时内联记录投影的逐样本梯度，无需额外遍历。采用 Kronecker 分解草图（Kronecker-factored sketch），可从单个被跟踪层扩展到所有层，而无需实体化稠密投影矩阵。

Result: 在单张 NVIDIA L4 上对 GPT-2 和高达 2.8B 的 Pythia 模型进行 LoRA 微调时，内联记录在最后一层块范围仅占 0.30-1.08% 墙钟时间，即使跟踪 Pythia-1B 所有层也低于 1%（0.79%）。相比最接近的内联竞品 LogIX，分解草图在同等存储下便宜 2.0-4.1 倍，且随跟踪范围增长而扩大，在所有测试范围均显著，同时归因质量匹配或超越 LogIX。内联构建归因就绪存储比一次后验遍历便宜 60-242 倍，比五检查点 TRAK 集成便宜 301-1211 倍，记录的梯度与自动微分精确匹配。

Conclusion: Traceprop 显著降低了数据归因的存储与计算成本，同时保持归因质量，并因每个存储梯度携带源文件谱系，同一次遍历还能生成 EU AI Act 第 26 条审计追踪。

Abstract: Data attribution methods used in practice (TRAK, LoGRA, EK-FAC) are post-hoc: after training they make a second pass over the training set to recompute per-sample gradients, repeated per checkpoint when ensembled. Traceprop avoids that pass by recording projected per-sample gradients inline on the training backward pass. A Kronecker-factored sketch scales from a single tracked layer to every layer without materializing a dense projection matrix. On LoRA fine-tunes of GPT-2 and Pythia models up to 2.8B on one NVIDIA L4, inline logging costs 0.30-1.08% of wall-clock time at last-block scope and stays under 1% (0.79%) even when tracking every layer of Pythia-1B. Against LogIX, the closest inline-capable competitor, the factored sketch is 2.0-4.1x cheaper at equal storage, a gap that grows with tracked scope and is significant at every scope tested, while matching or exceeding LogIX's attribution quality at matched storage. Building the attribution-ready store inline is 60-242x cheaper than one post-hoc pass and 301-1211x cheaper than a five-checkpoint TRAK ensemble, with recorded gradients matching autograd exactly. Because each stored gradient carries source-file lineage, the same pass also produces EU AI Act Article 26 audit trails.

</details>


### [257] [TimeES: Probabilistic and Deterministic Time Series Forecasting via Evolutionary Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32384)
*Weiwei Ye,Renhe Jiang,Hangchen Liu,Dongyuan Li,Yoshihide Sekimoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeES框架，基于演化谱理论建模非平稳时间序列的演化与随机性，实现高效的概率与确定性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时间序列本质非平稳，趋势、周期与不确定性随时间演化。现有深度学习方法在傅里叶域中未显式建模谱的演化与随机性，难以准确预测期望轨迹及其不确定性。受演化谱（ES）理论启发提出该工作。

Method: 推导可参数化的演化谱表示，将非平稳随机过程建模转化为由随机变量调制的演化表征学习；利用厄米对称性与谱能量稀疏性进行频率选择，将谱估计复杂度从O(NM)降至O(NK)（K << M/2）；基于简单线性骨干网络构建TimeES。

Result: 在确定性与概率预测任务上均取得一致的最先进性能，且具备高效率和可解释性。

Conclusion: TimeES为概率与确定性时间序列预测提供了一种通用、高效且可解释的框架，验证了演化谱理论在深度学习预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world time series are inherently non-stationary, with trends, periodic patterns, and uncertainty evolving over time. While the Fourier domain offers a natural lens to model time series, current deep learning approaches do not explicitly model evolution and randomness in the Fourier spectra, which limits their ability to accurately predict both the expected trajectory and its uncertainty in non-stationary time series. Motivated by Evolutionary Spectra (ES) theory, we propose TimeES, a general framework that enables probabilistic and deterministic forecasting via the evolutionary spectra theory. Specifically, we derive a parameterizable evolutionary spectra formulation, recasting non-stationary random process modeling as learning an evolving representation modulated by random variables. Furthermore, we reduce the complexity of the estimated spectra from O(NM) to O(NK), where K << M/2, by exploiting Hermitian symmetry and spectral energy sparsity for frequency selection. Based on a simple linear backbone, our proposed TimeES achieves consistent state-of-the-art performance across both deterministic and probabilistic forecasting tasks, with high efficiency and interpretability. Code is available at: https://github.com/wwy155/TimeES.

</details>


### [258] [Using Machine Learning to Investigate Predictors of Fasting Blood Glucose: Insights into Circadian Timing and Age Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32386)
*Viktoriya Bu-Dager,Silvia Cirstea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于NHANES 2017–2020数据，构建可解释机器学习框架预测对数转换的空腹血糖，最终交互增强型XGBoost模型在独立测试集上取得MAE=0.0804、RMSE=0.1148、R²=0.7761的良好表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖调节受损是代谢功能障碍和2型糖尿病的重要诱因。研究旨在利用代谢、激素、生活方式、人口学、营养及昼夜节律等多维变量，开发可解释的机器学习框架来预测空腹血糖，并探索昼夜节律与代谢健康之间的关系。

Method: 合并NHANES 2017–2020多个子数据集，采用防泄漏流程（仅在数据划分后及训练折内进行插补、缩放和独热编码）；使用94个候选预测变量及构造的昼夜节律交互项，评估Elastic Net、LASSO和XGBoost模型；通过MAE、RMSE、R²、校准及SHAP值评估性能。

Result: 最终交互增强型XGBoost模型在独立测试集上表现优异，仅用10个预测变量即达到MAE=0.0804、RMSE=0.1148、R²=0.7761。糖化血红蛋白为主要预测因子，其次为胰岛素、糖尿病诊断、γ-谷氨酰转移酶、年龄、种族和性别。在构造的交互项中，睡眠中点×年龄在重复随机划分分析中被稳定保留，但相对主要血糖预测因子贡献较小。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持进一步探讨昼夜节律与年龄交互作用在代谢健康中的意义，并验证了可解释机器学习框架在血糖预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Impaired glucose regulation is a major contributor to metabolic dysfunction and type 2 diabetes. This study developed an interpretable machine-learning framework to predict log-transformed fasting blood glucose using metabolic, hormonal, lifestyle, demographic, nutritional, and circadian variables from the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey 2017--2020 pre-pandemic dataset. After merging multiple NHANES sub-datasets, data processing used a leakage-resistant pipeline in which imputation, scaling, and one-hot encoding were performed only after dataset splitting and within training folds. Elastic Net, LASSO, and XGBoost models were evaluated using 94 candidate predictors and engineered circadian interaction terms. Performance was assessed using mean absolute error, root mean squared error, coefficient of determination, calibration, and Shapley Additive Explanations. The final interaction-augmented XGBoost model achieved strong performance on the independent test set, with a mean absolute error of 0.0804, a root mean squared error of 0.1148, and a coefficient of determination of 0.7761, using 10 predictors. Glycohemoglobin was the dominant predictor, followed by insulin, diabetes diagnosis, gamma-glutamyl transferase, age, race, and gender. Among the engineered interaction terms, sleep midpoint multiplied by age was consistently retained in repeated random-split analyses, although its contribution remained modest relative to dominant glycaemic predictors. These findings support further investigation of circadian-age interactions in metabolic health.

</details>


### [259] [Recovery-Directed Symbolic Distillation of Neural Likelihoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32409)
*Kianté Fernandez,Xinwei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种符号蒸馏流程，将训练好的神经似然转化为可解释的显式表达式，在保持参数恢复精度的同时大幅提升参数估计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摊销神经似然虽能为解析不可处理或未指定的似然模型提供推断能力，但其黑盒特性限制了可解释性，因此需要将其转化为可解释的形式。

Method: 采用以恢复为导向的目标引导符号回归，使表达式优先保留参数恢复精度而非仅逼近似然函数；候选表达式在留出数据集上评估，并用同时考虑表达式复杂度、参数恢复性能和与学习似然的分布距离的准则进行选择。在扩散决策模型上评估，其解析似然可作为真值。

Result: 恢复导向目标相比标准符号回归目标提升了参数恢复效果；所得符号似然的参数评估速度比神经似然以及（在可用时）精确似然快100倍以上，同时仅带来可控的精度损失，并在经验数据的贝叶斯层次推断中验证了计算优势。

Conclusion: 该流程为将符号蒸馏与现有神经似然估计方法整合提供了轻量接口，并可适配多种基于模拟的推断场景。

Abstract: Amortized neural likelihoods enable computationally expensive inference for models with analytically intractable or unspecified likelihoods, but their black-box nature limits interpretability. We introduce a symbolic distillation pipeline that converts trained neural likelihoods into explicit, interpretable expressions optimized for efficient parameter estimation. Our approach uses a recovery-directed objective to guide symbolic regression toward expressions that preserve parameter-recovery accuracy rather than merely approximating the likelihood function. Candidate expressions are evaluated on held-out datasets and selected using a criterion that jointly accounts for expression complexity, parameter-recovery performance, and distributional distance from the learned likelihood. We evaluate the pipeline on the diffusion decision model, a classical cognitive model, whose analytically tractable likelihood provides ground truth for controlled evaluation. The proposed recovery-directed objective improves parameter recovery over standard symbolic-regression objectives. The resulting symbolic likelihoods enable over 100 times faster parameter evaluation than both neural likelihoods and, when available, the exact likelihood, while maintaining a manageable loss in precision. We further demonstrate these computational benefits in Bayesian hierarchical inference on empirical data. Our pipeline provides a lightweight interface for integrating symbolic distillation with existing neural-likelihood estimation methods and can be adapted to a range of simulation-based inference settings.

</details>


### [260] [HoTS: Homophily-Aware Temperature Scaling for Graph Neural Network Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32426)
*Inwoo Tae,Yoontae Hwang,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究图节点分类中的后处理校准问题，提出同质性感知温度缩放（HoTS），从理论上推导局部同质性与最优温度的反比关系，并在18个基准上取得最优ECE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图节点分类中的置信度（通常为最大预测类概率）需要被校准，用于预测排序、将不确定节点交给人工复核或控制风险。现有的后处理校准器要么使用单一的全局温度，要么使用缺乏原则性结构形式的图感知模块。作者希望明确局部图结构应如何进入节点级校准，并指出仅依赖logit的温度规则不够用：即使两个节点logit相同，只要局部同质性不同，其所需的最优温度也不同。

Method: 作者分析了带有高斯特征和一层线性GCN的群体集中式上下文随机块模型（contextual stochastic block model）。在类均值等距的假设下，推导出类模板分数上的贝叶斯后验是一个温度缩放的softmax，其逆温度由依赖同质性的信号强度决定；在正信号的同质区域，温度近似与归一化局部同质性成反比。基于这一规律，提出HoTS：一种简单的后处理校准器，依据基于熵的logit集中度和估计的局部同质性为每个节点分配正标量温度。HoTS仅含三个温度参数，保持预测类别不变，并从校准数据中学习结构修正的强度。

Result: 在18个节点分类基准、2种GNN骨干网络和8种校准基线上，HoTS取得了最佳的平均期望校准误差（ECE）4.79%、最佳平均排名，以及在选择性分类中最可靠的置信度排序。代码已开源于 https://github.com/inu0104/HoTS。

Conclusion: 局部图结构（局部同质性）应作为节点级温度校准的关键变量，温度随局部同质性升高而降低；由此得到的HoTS是一种有理论依据、参数极少且保持预测类别的后处理校准方法，在多项基准上优于既有校准器。

Abstract: For graph node classification, calibrated class probabilities are needed when confidence scores, usually the maximum predicted class probability, are used to rank predictions, defer uncertain nodes to human review, or control risk. Existing post-hoc calibrators either apply one global temperature or use graph-aware modules without a principled structural form. We study how local graph structure should enter node-level calibration. Our first results show that a logit-only temperature rule is insufficient when nodes with identical logits but different local homophily require different optimal temperatures. We then analyze a population-concentration contextual stochastic block model with Gaussian features and a one-layer linear GCN. Under equidistant class means, the Bayes posterior over class-template scores is a temperature-scaled softmax whose inverse-temperature is governed by a homophily-dependent signal strength. In the positive-signal homophilic regime, the resulting temperature decreases approximately inversely with normalized local homophily. This law motivates Homophily-aware Temperature Scaling (HoTS), a simple post-hoc calibrator that assigns each node a positive scalar temperature from entropy-based logit concentration and estimated local homophily. HoTS has three temperature parameters, preserves the predicted class, and learns the strength of the structural correction from calibration data. Across 18 node-classification benchmarks, two GNN backbones, and eight calibration baselines, HoTS achieves the best mean Expected Calibration Error (ECE) of 4.79%, the best average rank, and the most reliable confidence ranking in selective classification. Code is available at https://github.com/inu0104/HoTS.

</details>
